### [1] [A literature-guided descriptor-based framework for filtering composition search spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31650)
*Lei Zhang,Markus Stricker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种文献引导的基于描述符（descriptor-based）的过滤框架，利用文献训练的词语嵌入（word embedding）模型筛选候选成分空间，平均过滤掉 74.27% 的候选成分，最优值误差平均为 1.93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献蕴含关于材料行为的潜在知识，但多以词语、语境与重复关联的形式存在，而非显式设计原则。由此引出核心问题：如何将大规模科学语料用于材料发现的实际问题。

Method: 针对给定性能指标（performance metric），框架在文献训练的词语嵌入模型中从经过过滤的词表中选取两个描述符，并利用所选描述符为候选成分构建基于帕累托（Pareto-based）的过滤器。

Result: 在所评估的性能指标与成分搜索空间上，框架平均过滤掉 74.27% 的候选成分，相对于实验测量的平均最优值误差为 1.93%。与专家选择及随机选择的描述符相比，依赖性能指标的描述符在保留比例（retained fraction）与最优值误差之间提供了更可控的平衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明，文献衍生的嵌入（literature-derived embeddings）可支持直观且可复现的过滤器，用于缩小候选成分空间，同时保留高性能成分。

Abstract: Scientific literature contains latent knowledge about materials behavior, but much of this knowledge is expressed through words, contexts, and recurring associations rather than explicit design principles. This raises a central question: how can large-scale scientific corpora be used for practical problems in materials discovery? Here, we present a literature-guided descriptor-based filtering framework for reducing composition search spaces. For a given performance metric, the framework selects two descriptors from a filtered vocabulary in a literature-trained word embedding model and uses the selected descriptors to construct a Pareto-based filter for candidate compositions. Across the evaluated performance metrics and composition search spaces, the framework filters out an average of 74.27\% of the candidate compositions, with an average best-value error of 1.93\% relative to experimental measurements. Compared with expert-chosen and random descriptors, our performance metric-dependent descriptors provide a more controlled balance between retained fraction and best-value error. These results show that literature-derived embeddings can support intuitive and reproducible filters for narrowing candidate composition spaces while preserving high-performing compositions.

</details>

### [2] [Interactions and dynamics of metastable triplet-state helium dimer with Ne and Ar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34713)
*Dibyendu Sardar,François Lique*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次对 He₂(a³Σᵤ⁺)+Ne 范德华复合物进行相互作用与碰撞动力学的理论研究，构建了三维克平均势能面并进行完全耦合计算，揭示了精细结构守恒的倾向规则，并与 He₂+Ar 体系对比说明低能散射对势能面与约化质量的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: He₂(a³Σᵤ⁺) 是直接激光冷却与精密测量的候选体系，但其参与的相互作用与碰撞在超冷及冷能域尚未被研究，亟需理论刻画以支撑未来实验。

Method: 对基电子态 X³A′ 采用自旋限制耦合簇方法（包括单、双及非迭代三重激发）计算相互作用势；构建三维振动平均势能面；进行包含 He₂(a³Σᵤ⁺) 精细结构能级的完全密耦合（close-coupling）散射计算，并对比 He₂+Ar 体系。

Result: 相互作用势阱极浅，深度为 4.612 cm⁻¹；低能散射分析给出了与准束缚态（quasi-bound states）相关的共振特征，并建立了精细结构守恒跃迁的倾向规则（propensity rule）；He₂+Ar 体系呈现更丰富的共振结构。

Conclusion: 所计算的势能面与散射性质为 He₂+Ne 范德华复合物的描述提供了可靠基准，并可能有助于解释未来的实验光谱与碰撞测量；低能散射对相互作用势与约化质量（reduced mass）高度敏感。

Abstract: Helium dimer in its metastable triplet electronic state, He$_2(a~^3Σ_u^+)$, is a promising candidate for direct laser cooling and precision measurements. Interactions and collisions involving He$_2(a~^3Σ_u^+)$ remain unexplored in ultracold and cold energy domains. Here, we present the first theoretical investigation of intermolecular interactions and collision dynamics of He$_2(a~^3Σ_u^+)$+Ne van der Waals complex. For the ground electronic state, $X~^3\mathrm{A}'$, we employ a spin-restricted coupled cluster method with single, double, and noniterative triple excitations. The resulting interaction potential exhibits an extremely shallow well with a depth of 4.612 cm$^{-1}$. We construct a three-dimensional vibrationally averaged potential energy surface. Using this surface, we perform full close-coupling calculations including fine-structure levels of He$_2(a~^3Σ_u^+)$ to investigate collisional excitation and quenching induced by Ne. We analyze the low-energy scattering dynamics and model resonant features associated with quasi-bound states supported by interaction potential. Propensity rule for fine-structure conserving transitions is established. We additionally consider He$_2$+Ar collisions and compare their energy dependence and resonance structure with those of He$_2$+Ne. The He$_2$+Ar system exhibits a richer resonance structure, highlighting the sensitivity of low-energy scattering to interaction potential and reduced mass. Calculated PES and scattering properties provide a reliable benchmark for description of He$_2$+Ne van der Waals complex and may be useful for interpretation of future experimental spectra and collision measurements.

</details>

### [3] [Does a Second-Class Primary Constraint Generate a Gauge Transformation? Electromagnetisms and Gravities, Massless and Massive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31809)
*J. Brian Pitts*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文论证：若按“第一类约束（first-class constraints）各自生成规范变换（gauge transformations）”的标准理解，则Proca有质量电磁学中的第二类主约束（second-class primary constraint）同样可视为生成规范变换，从而使“个体生成”观点面临困境，而规范生成元（gauge generator）观点则可回避该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束哈密顿动力学（constrained Hamiltonian dynamics）中对于第一类约束如何生成规范变换存在两种观点：各自独立生成，或仅通过Rosenfeld-Anderson-Bergmann-Castellani规范生成元（gauge generator）G的特定组合生成。近期工作表明，分别对待第一类约束会导致Proca非规范理论与Stueckelberg规范有质量电磁学之间的可观测量不等价，因此有必要澄清两类约束与规范变换的关系。

Method: 采用理论论证方式，将规范生成元G的判据（保持Hamilton方程、至多改变正则作用量一个边界项，且Hamilton方程为该作用量的Euler-Lagrange方程）作为参照标准，并将其与麦克斯韦理论（Maxwell's theory）中第一类主约束、Proca有质量电磁学（Proca's massive electromagnetism）中第二类主约束，以及广义相对论（General Relativity）与其有质量自旋2（massive spin 2）亲属理论的对应约束进行类比分析。

Result: 论证显示：在“麦克斯韦理论中第一类主约束生成规范变换”的同等意义上，Proca理论中的第二类主约束也生成规范变换；类似地，广义相对论的各种有质量自旋2亲属理论中的第二类主约束所生成的规范变换，与GR中相应第一类主约束所生成的程度相当。此结果与“第二类约束不生成规范变换”的普遍共识形成张力。

Conclusion: “第一类约束通常各自（individually）生成规范变换”的观点面临一个规范生成元观点所不具有的难题（puzzle）。因此，以规范生成元G作为规范变换来源的视角在概念一致性上更具优势。

Abstract: In constrained Hamiltonian dynamics there are two views regarding how first-class constraints generate gauge transformations: individually or only in a certain combination, the Rosenfeld-Anderson-Bergmann-Castellani gauge generator. This gauge generator $G$ preserves Hamilton's equations and changes the canonical action at most by a boundary term; Hamiltonian's equations are the Euler-Lagrange equations for the canonical action. Hence the canonical formalism is equivalent to the Lagrangian formalism, and indeed subsumed within it (in important examples) with many canonical momenta serving as auxiliary fields. $G$ generates transformations basically equivalent to the usual 4-dimensional Lagrangian expressions, such as a 4-gradient in electromagnetism or a space-time coordinate transformation (on-shell) in General Relativity. It has been shown recently that separate first-class constraints lead to inequivalent observables between Proca non-gauge and Stueckelberg gauge massive electromagnetism. There is, however, widespread agreement that second-class constraints do not generate gauge transformations.
  Here it is shown that in such a sense as the first-class primary constraint in Maxwell's theory generates a gauge transformation, the second-class primary constraint in Proca's massive electromagnetism also generates a gauge transformation. Likewise the second-class primary constraints in various massive spin $2$ relatives of General Relativity generate as much of a gauge transformation as do the corresponding first-class primary constraints in GR. Hence the view that first-class constraints typically generate gauge transformations _individually_ faces a puzzle not faced by the gauge generator view.

</details>

### [4] [Knowledge-Driven XRD Phase Identification via Multi-View Retrieval and Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31888)
*Doaa Mohamed,Markus Stricker*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个面向X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction, XRD）物相分析的多决策框架（multi-decision framework），融合表征学习、相似性检索与可解释决策支持，在共享参考数据库与潜在空间（latent space）中输出排序化的候选物相列表及置信度。在合成数据集上，晶系分类（crystal system classification）准确率达98.85%，空间群预测（space group prediction）达95.82%，并在真实扰动下保持稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XRD是确定晶态材料物相组成与结构的实验技术，但图谱解析困难，尤其在高通量材料发现（high-throughput materials discovery）中需表征大量新型材料且缺乏参考图谱。机器学习被日益用于加速与自动化该分析，以降低人工解析带来的误差。

Method: 框架在统一参考数据库中整合表征学习（representation learning）、相似性检索（similarity-based retrieval）与可解释决策支持。采用卷积自编码器（convolutional autoencoder）学习XRD图谱的紧凑潜在表征，在保持结构相似性的同时对实验噪声与测量条件变化保持稳健。推理阶段应用互补决策机制：潜在空间分类与检索、基于积分梯度（Integrated Gradients）的解释引导相似性，以及基于成分的相似性搜索；多路径结果在共享潜在空间内聚合并输出最终预测与置信度。

Result: 在合成数据集（synthetic datasets）上表现出较强预测性能：测试集晶系分类准确率98.85%，空间群预测准确率95.82%，并在真实扰动条件下保持稳健性。

Conclusion: 该框架超越单标签预测（single-label prediction），转向类似专家实践的排序化、可解释物相分析，支持高通量与探索性材料发现场景下可靠且对分析人员友好的晶相与结构识别。

Abstract: X-ray diffraction (XRD) is a experimental technique for determining the phase composition and structure of crystalline materials. However, interpreting XRD patterns is challenging, particularly in high-throughput materials discovery, where many novel materials may need to be characterized and no reference patterns are available. Consequently, machine learning is increasingly used to accelerate and automate the analysis while reducing errors associated with human interpretation. We propose a multi-decision framework for XRD phase analysis that integrates representation learning, similarity-based retrieval, and explainable decision support within a unified reference database. A convolutional autoencoder learns compact latent representations of XRD patterns that preserve structural similarity while remaining robust to variations arising from experimental noise and measurement conditions. By integrating multiple decision pathways within a shared latent space, the framework moves beyond single-label prediction toward ranked and interpretable phase analysis that mirrors expert practice. During inference, complementary decision mechanisms are applied, including latent-space classification and retrieval, explanation-guided similarity using Integrated Gradients, and composition-based similarity search. These mechanisms generate ranked candidate phase lists that are aggregated into a final prediction with an associated confidence score. Experiments on synthetic datasets demonstrate strong predictive performance, achieving 98.85\,\% accuracy for crystal system classification and 95.82\,\% accuracy for space group prediction on the test set, while maintaining robustness under realistic perturbations. The framework supports reliable, analyst-friendly identification of crystal phases and structures in high-throughput and exploratory materials discovery settings.

</details>


### [5] [The Young-E(3) Tensor Product Decomposition for Rotation and Permutation Equivariant Cluster Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31895)
*James M. Goff,Aidan P. Thompson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 Young--E(3)（YE3T）张量积基，可同时表达旋转对称性与置换对称性，将 ACE 基作为特例包含，生成完备正交基并提升机器学习原子间势的速度与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有固定晶格团簇展开（cluster expansions, CE）与原子团簇展开（atomic cluster expansions, ACE）在对称性表达上存在局限，且传统做法先构造超完备的旋转与置换不变基再进行后验约简，效率不足。

Method: 在 Young 子群稳定的载体（Young subgroup-stabilized carriers）上执行 Schur--Weyl 分解，并对旋转与置换表示进行联合耦合，从而生成任意 $S_N\times SO(3)$ 张量积载体，构建任意张量积秩与角向、径向内容的完备正交联合适配基。

Result: YE3T 基包含 ACE 基作为子集（对应置换对称性特征仅限全对称载体的特例），无需超完备步骤即可直接给出完备正交基；其导出的联合不可约旋转-置换等变基在速度与精度上均超越现有旋转适配展开，定义了机器学习原子间势的新 Pareto 前沿。

Conclusion: 置换对称性特征的可调性使该基同时适用于原子尺度与电子结构模拟，并可严格扩展至超出置换对称特征的置换特征。

Abstract: A generalization of fixed-lattice cluster expansions (CE) and atomic cluster expansions (ACE) is presented that expresses both rotation and permutation symmetries. By performing Schur--Weyl decomposition in Young subgroup-stabilized carriers, followed by joint coupling of rotation and permutation representations, arbitrary $S_N\times SO(3)$ tensor product carriers are generated. This allows us to resolve complete, orthonormal joint rotation and permutation-adapted bases for arbitrary tensor product ranks and arbitrary angular and radial tensor product content. We show that this very general Young--E(3) (YE3T) tensor product basis contains the ACE basis as a subset, corresponding to the special case where permutation symmetry character is restricted to the fully symmetric carrier. Rather than constructing an overcomplete rotation and permutation-invariant basis and reducing it \textit{a posteriori}, Barthelemy \textit{et al.} recently demonstrated scaling benefits by not constructing an overcomplete basis. YE3T directly produces a complete orthonormal basis without an overcomplete step and rigorously extends to permutation characters beyond permutation-symmetric features. The YE3T decomposition yields new joint irreducible rotation- and permutation-equivariant basis sets that surpass existing rotation-adapted expansions in both speed and accuracy, defining a new Pareto front for machine-learned interatomic potentials. We show that the tunability of the permutation symmetry character makes the basis useful for both atomistic and electronic-structure simulations.

</details>


### [6] [Jahn-Teller Distortion-Driven Lattice Parameter Tunability in Coherent High-Entropy Oxide Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31899)
*Dhiya Srikanth,Joseph Petruska,Matthew Furst,Kaylin Lamaute,Jon-Paul Maria,Saeed S. I. Almishal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过制备0-20% Cu梯度的外延高熵氧化物薄膜，揭示Cu浓度与Jahn-Teller畸变对晶格常数（lattice parameter）的调控机制，并发现该调控随生长温度呈现不同趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清Cu浓度与Jahn-Teller畸变（Jahn-Teller distortions）如何控制MgO衬底上赝形（pseudomorphic）MgCoNiCuZnO衍生高熵氧化物（high-entropy oxide）薄膜的面外晶格常数，并厘清其与Co价态变化的相对贡献。

Method: 制备覆盖0-20% Cu的九种组分薄膜，用X射线荧光（X-ray fluorescence）与衍射（diffraction）验证组分与物相，采用脉冲激光沉积（pulsed laser deposition）在多个衬底温度下生长。

Result: 在500 °C下，晶格常数随Cu含量近似线性增大；在300 °C下则呈非线性减小，等摩尔MgCoNiCuZnO在两温度区间晶格常数差异达2.6%。该变化归因于Cu驱动的Jahn-Teller畸变与Co价态变化之间的温度依赖耦合。

Conclusion: 既有工作将Co价态视为MgCoNiCuZnO晶格随温度响应的主导因素，本研究则确立Cu的Jahn-Teller畸变为同等重要的结构调控参数。

Abstract: Here, we isolate how Cu concentration and Jahn-Teller distortions control the out-of-plane lattice parameter of pseudomorphic MgCoNiCuZnO-derived high-entropy oxide films on MgO. We prepare nine compositions spanning 0-20% Cu, verify target composition and phase by X-ray fluorescence and diffraction, and grow films by pulsed laser deposition across multiple substrate temperatures. At 500 °C, the lattice parameter increases nearly linearly with Cu content; at 300°C, it decreases nonlinearly, producing a 2.6% difference between temperature regimes for equimolar MgCoNiCuZnO at our growth conditions. We attribute this change in lattice parameter to temperature-dependent coupling between Cu-driven Jahn-Teller distortions and Co valence change. While prior work identifies Co valence as the primary driver of the MgCoNiCuZnO lattice response with temperature; our results establish Cu Jahn-Teller distortion as an equally important structural control parameter.

</details>


### [7] [Temperature Dependent Evolution of the Electronic Structure in EuZn2As2 across the Neel Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32121)
*Milo Sprague,Anup Pradhan Sakhya,Barun Ghosh,Mazharul Islam Mondal,Arun K. Kumay,Himanshu Sheokand,Kapil Gope,Tetiana Romanova,Dariusz Kaczorowski,Arun Bansil,Madhab Neupane*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）化合物 EuZn2As2 中观察到接近奈尔温度（Néel temperature, TN = 19 K）的显著电阻率异常（resistivity anomaly），并通过磁场抑制该异常；结合角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）与第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，指出该输运行为源于自旋散射（spin-scattering）而非费米面重构（Fermi surface reconstruction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁阻材料（magnetoresistive materials）对磁性存储与自旋电子学（spintronics）器件发展意义重大；近年来 EuX2Pn2 类反铁磁化合物因其异常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect）行为及 TN 附近的显著电阻异常而受到密集关注，但其微观起源仍存争议，涉及短程铁磁涨落（short-ranged ferromagnetic fluctuations）、磁极化子（magnetic polaron）形成、倾斜自旋构型（canted spin configurations）及随温度变化的金属-绝缘体转变（metal-insulator transitions）等多种解释。

Method: 报告 EuZn2As2 中 TN = 19 K 附近显著电阻率异常的观测结果，并施加面内（in plane）与面外（out of plane）磁场以证明该异常的抑制；同时采用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）与第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）计算相结合的方式研究温度依赖的电子结构。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Phonon--magnon coupling has been extensively studied in ferromagnets, whereas its microscopic mechanisms in antiferromagnets remain less understood. Here, we combine first-principles calculations with spin--lattice dynamics to investigate phonon--magnon interactions and their impact on thermal transport in antiferromagnetic NiO and MnO, which are put in contrast to the ferromagnetic EuO with an identical crystal structure. Green--Kubo and spectral energy density analyses show that spin dynamics reduce phonon thermal conductivity and lifetimes, with the strongest effects in MnO. A simplified phase-space model further illustrates that, for comparable phonon and magnon energy scales, the antiferromagnetic case can support a larger phonon--magnon scattering phase space than the ferromagnetic case, suggesting a greater number of kinematically allowed scattering channels. ### [14] [Piezo-Hall effect in $\mathrm{RbCr_2Se_2O}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33026)
*San-Dong Guo,Shi-Hao Zhang,Feng-Ren Fan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出并阐释了“压霍尔效应（piezo-Hall effect）”：磁性材料在无应变时因磁空间群（magnetic space group, MSG）禁止净磁矩而抑制反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect, AHE），施加应变后转变为允许净磁矩的新磁空间群，从而诱导 AHE 出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反常霍尔效应对理解磁性材料中的自旋轨道耦合与磁输运具有基础意义，而应变与压电效应可显著调控材料性质。将 AHE 与应变两种要素结合，可望拓展对二者耦合行为的认识。

Method: 以 d 波交替磁金属（d-wave altermagnetic metal）为例，对压霍尔效应进行详细分析，并选取具体材料 RbCr₂Se₂O 开展第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）加以验证。

Result: 在沿 x 与 y 方向施加相同单轴应变时，反常霍尔电导（anomalous Hall conductivity）表现出符号相反但大小相等的特征。

Conclusion: 除压霍尔效应外，可相应提出压磁光效应（piezo-magneto-optical effect）；若以电场调制替代应变调控，则可提出电霍尔效应（electro-Hall effect）与电磁光效应（electro-magneto-optical effect）；温度亦可作为外场引入温度霍尔效应（temperature Hall effect）。该工作推进了对应变与物理性质耦合的理解。

Abstract: The anomalous Hall effect (AHE) is of fundamental importance for understanding spin-orbit coupling and magnetic transport in magnetic materials. Meanwhile, strain can significantly modify material properties, and the piezoelectric effect stands as a well-known representative. The piezo-Hall effect combines the two physical ingredients of AHE and strain. Specifically, the piezo-Hall effect describes a physical process in which the magnetic space group (MSG) of a magnetic material prohibits a net magnetic moment and suppresses the AHE in the pristine state but transforms into a new MSG upon strain application that permits a net magnetic moment, thereby enabling the emergence of the AHE. The underlying mechanisms cover strain-induced magnetic electronic state transition and strain tuning of magnetization direction. We take the $d$-wave altermagnetic metal as an example to analyze the piezo-Hall effect in detail, with first-principles calculations carried out on the specific material $\mathrm{RbCr_2Se_2O}$ to verify this scenario. A notable result is that under identical uniaxial strain applied along the $x$ and $y$ directions, the anomalous Hall conductivity exhibits opposite signs but equal magnitudes. Similarly, the piezo-magneto-optical effect can also be proposed, and if strain tuning is replaced by electric-field modulation, the electro-Hall effect and electro-magneto-optical effect can be also put forward accordingly. Even temperature can serve as an external field to introduce the temperature Hall effect. Our work advances the understanding of the coupling between strain and physical properties.

</details>


### [15] [Unifying Magnetic, Electrical, and Thermoelectric Responses in a Non-Collinear Antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33046)
*André José,Adrielson Dias,Carlos Eduardo,Giovanna Celly,Luiza Paffer,José Araújo,José Laurentino,Monalisa Cavalcante,José Holanda*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共线反铁磁体（non-collinear antiferromagnet）因其复杂自旋构型与界面对称性，为调控磁、电、热响应提供了非常规途径。传统交换偏置（exchange bias）的理解主要局限于共线反铁磁体系，本文旨在检验该界面效应能否拓展至非共线体系并呈现多物理量一致性。

Method: 构建 10 nm IrMn$_3$ 与 5 nm 坡莫合金（permalloy, Py）耦合的异质结，并分别采用纵向磁光克尔效应（longitudinal magneto-optical Kerr effect, MOKE）、各向异性磁电阻（anisotropic magnetoresistance, AMR）与反常能斯特效应（anomalous Nernst effect, ANE）三种独立手段进行测量。

Result: 三种测量独立地揭示了同一约 50 Oe 的单向场偏移，并表现出一致的余弦角度依赖（cosine angular dependence）。该对称性在磁化反转（magnetization reversal）、电荷输运（charge transport）与热致电压产生（thermally driven voltage generation）中均一致体现。

Conclusion: 结果表明 IrMn$_3$/Py 具有相干的多功能响应，将交换偏置的传统认识拓展至共线反铁磁体系之外，并显示非共线反铁磁体可作为自旋电子学（spintronics）与自旋热电子学（spin-caloritronics）器件中耦合磁、电、热功能的平台。

Abstract: Non-collinear antiferromagnets offer unconventional routes for controlling magnetic, electrical, and thermoelectric responses through their complex spin configurations and interfacial symmetry. Here, we detect exceptional interfacial properties arising from a non-collinear antiferromagnet by demonstrating a robust and quantitatively consistent exchange-bias response in an IrMn$_3$/Py heterostructure. The system consists of a 10 nm IrMn$_3$ layer coupled to a 5 nm permalloy (Py) film. Longitudinal magneto-optical Kerr effect (MOKE), anisotropic magnetoresistance (AMR), and anomalous Nernst effect (ANE) measurements independently reveal the same unidirectional field shift of approximately 50 Oe, together with a consistent cosine angular dependence. The simultaneous observation of these signatures establishes a direct correspondence between the interfacial magnetic symmetry imposed by the non-collinear antiferromagnet and the responses of the adjacent ferromagnet. This symmetry is manifested consistently in magnetization reversal, charge transport, and thermally driven voltage generation, demonstrating that the exchange-bias imprint is not restricted to a single experimental observable. Our results reveal a coherent multifunctional response of IrMn$_3$/Py and extend the conventional understanding of exchange bias beyond collinear antiferromagnetic systems. More broadly, they demonstrate the potential of non-collinear antiferromagnets as platforms for coupling magnetic, electrical, and thermoelectric functionalities in spintronic and spin-caloritronic devices.

</details>


### [16] [Let CSP Be Your ANCHOR: Adaptive Crystal Search over Frozen Structure Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33407)
*Emma Lei Hovmand,Jonas Elsborg,Melih Kandemir,Arghya Bhowmik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文主张将从头晶体生成（de novo crystal generation, DNG）中的“组成搜索”与“结构生成”解耦：以冻结的晶体结构预测（crystal structure prediction, CSP）模型作为物理先验（physical prior），在其之上用多目标强化学习策略搜索组成，并辅以连续自适应新颖性（continuous adaptive novelty, CAN）评分。实验显示，用 ANCHOR 策略替代 DNG 组成可将 MSUN 从 11.4% 提升至 47.6%、SUN 从 1.1% 提升至 22.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNG 模型以同一组权重同时决定在组成空间中搜索何处以及如何生成结构，作者认为这种耦合不利于材料发现（materials discovery）。论文主张应分离两种职能：CSP 模型作为物理先验应通过似然训练（likelihood training）改进，而包括新颖性（novelty）在内的奖励应作用于对组成的搜索过程。

Method: 提出 ANCHOR，即围绕冻结 CSP 模型、以多目标奖励（multi-objective rewards）训练的 GRPO 组成策略（composition policy）；并提出连续自适应新颖性（CAN），对已知结构与不断增长的发现历史给出分级新颖性评分。在固定评估器下以冻结 CSP 模型作为固定标尺（fixed ruler），检验适应应作用于何处，并与直接在相同奖励上微调 DNG 模型进行对比。

Result: 在相同 CSP 骨干（backbone）上以 ANCHOR 策略替换 DNG 组成，MSUN 由 11.4% 升至 47.6%，SUN 由 1.1% 升至 22.1%，公式唯一性（formula uniqueness）为 99.9%。直接在相同奖励上微调 DNG 模型仅移动其组成边缘分布（composition marginal），未提升其位于凸包上（on-hull）比例。即使仅将稳定性奖励路由至 ANCHOR 的 CSP 骨干，相对于冻结骨干约使 SUN 减半，而基于搜索中发现结构的似然训练可改进 CSP 骨干。在 MatterGen 评估流程下，ANCHOR 将当前最优 MSUN 从 29.2% 提升至 41.3%，无需重训练即可迁移至另外两个 CSP 骨干，蒸馏进 Crystalite-CSP 后达 47.1%。

Conclusion: 将组成搜索与结构生成分离、并让奖励作用于组成策略，能显著提升发现性能，且策略与新颖性度量可跨 CSP 骨干迁移。作者亦指出，与其他针对势能（potential）优化的模型一样，其 on-hull 率取决于该势能本身。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation (DNG) models decide where to search in composition space and how to generate structures with one set of weights. We argue that discovery is better served by separating the two. A crystal structure prediction (CSP) model is a physical prior that should be improved by likelihood training, while rewards, including novelty measured against the search's own history, should act on a search over compositions. We introduce ANCHOR, a GRPO composition policy trained with multi-objective rewards around a frozen CSP model, and continuous adaptive novelty (CAN), a graded novelty score against known structures and a growing discovery history. Using the frozen CSP model as a fixed ruler under one evaluator, we test where adaptation should act. Replacing DNG compositions with ANCHOR's policy on the same CSP backbone raises MSUN from 11.4% to 47.6% and SUN from 1.1% to 22.1% at 99.9% formula uniqueness. Fine-tuning DNG models directly on the same rewards instead moves their composition marginal without raising their on-hull fraction. We show that KL-regularized fine-tuning of a DNG model can only reweight chemistry the pretrained model already supports by a bounded factor, while unregularized DNG fine-tunes move toward known or less stable chemistry. Even a stability-only reward routed into ANCHOR's CSP backbone roughly halves SUN relative to the frozen backbone, whereas likelihood training on structures found during search can improve a CSP backbone. Under MatterGen's evaluation pipeline, ANCHOR raises state-of-the-art MSUN from 29.2% to 41.3%, transfers without retraining to two further CSP backbones, and reaches 47.1% after distillation into Crystalite-CSP. As with any model optimised against a potential, its on-hull rate depends on that potential.

</details>


### [17] [Benchmark of First-Principles Titanium K-Edge X-Ray Absorption Spectral Simulations on Titanium-containing Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33776)
*Chuntian Cao,Joshua J. Kas,Karol Dyro,Bruce Ravel,John Vinson,Deyu Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究以九种常见钛化合物为对象，将第一性原理（first-principles）模拟的 Ti K 边 X 射线吸收近边结构（XANES）与实验数据系统对标，量化了四极激发、热无序与多体摇出等效应的作用，并建立一个可推广的高保真 XANES 数据库生成流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X 射线吸收谱（XAS）虽为元素特异性的局域结构与电子性质探针，但其定量分析困难，依赖准确的第一性原理谱学模拟。现有模拟对各类物理效应的处理尚不明确，因此需要系统评估哪些效应为准确再现实验谱所必需。

Method: 对九种常见钛化合物的 Ti K 边 XANES 进行第一性原理模拟，并与实验数据对标；系统考察四极激发（quadrupole excitations）、热无序（thermal disorder）与多体摇出（many-body shake-up）等关键物理效应，同时对核心空穴末态采用能带理论（band-theory）处理。

Result: 四极激发与热无序对准确再现前边（pre-edge）特征不可或缺，多体摇出则显著影响主边与后边（main- and post-edge）区域的谱形。计入上述效应并结合能带理论核心空穴处理，模拟谱在多数体系中与实验高度一致（相似度评分较高）。例外为 BaTiO$_3$ 在 4980 eV 处的肩峰（shoulder peak）在模拟中基本缺失。

Conclusion: BaTiO$_3$ 肩峰的缺失表明，捕捉 Ba 4f 轨道关联效应需要比半局域密度泛函理论（semi-local density functional theory）更准确的电子结构理论，且氧空位（oxygen vacancies）等缺陷也可能对该肩峰有贡献。除具体钛体系外，本工作建立了生成高保真 Ti K 边 XANES 数据库的稳健工作流，其框架可推广至广泛材料体系。

Abstract: X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) is a powerful, element-specific probe for investigating the local structural and electronic properties of materials. However, quantitative analysis remains challenging, necessitating accurate first-principles spectral simulations. In this study, we benchmark first-principles simulations of Ti K-edge X-ray absorption near-edge structure (XANES) against experimental data for nine common titanium compounds. We systematically investigate key physical effects, including quadrupole excitations, thermal disorder, and many-body shake-up. Our results demonstrate that quadrupole excitations and thermal disorder are essential for capturing accurate pre-edge features, while many-body shake-up effects significantly influence the spectral shape of the main- and post-edge regions. By incorporating these effects alongside a band-theory treatment of the core-hole final state, our simulated spectra achieve excellent agreement with experimental data for most of the systems, as evidenced by high similarity scores. The shoulder peak in BaTiO$_3$ at 4980 eV in the experiment is largely missing in simulation. Further analysis shows that more accurate electronic structure theory than semi-local density functional theory is required to capture the correlation effects of the Ba $4f$ orbitals and that defects, such as oxygen vacancies, may also contribute to the shoulder. Beyond tackling specific titanium material systems, this work establishes a robust workflow for generating high-fidelity Ti K-edge XANES databases for titanium compounds, providing a framework that can be generalized to a broad range of materials.

</details>


### [18] [Democratizing Atomistic Simulation Workflows for the AI Era with the Quantum Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33823)
*Andrew S. Rosen,Naisargi Goyal,Brad Ayers,Vineet Bansal,Julia H. Baratta,Samuel M. Blau,Yuan Chiang,Sihoon Choi,Orion Archer Cohen,Blake Dallmann,Tom Demeyere,Will Engler,Yue-Wen Fang,Isabella Furrick,Eliu Huerta,Honghui Kim,Hironori Kondo,Anup Kumar,Jaehong Kwon,Osman Mamun,Charles B. Musgrave,Hananeh Oliaei,Aryan Saha,Davide Sarpa,Benjamin X. Shi,Yuliang Shi,Xing Wang,Robert B. Wexler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: QuAcc 是一个面向原子尺度模拟（atomistic simulation）的开源工作流库（workflow library），重点支持量子力学计算。其核心设计在于将领域科学逻辑与工作流引擎（workflow engine）解耦，使工作流可用普通 Python 函数编写并在多个引擎上运行而无需修改源码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习（machine learning）与人工智能（artificial intelligence）时代，原子尺度模拟工作流日益重要，但新工作流的开发与贡献门槛较高，且基础机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials, MLIPs）的性能评估易受方法学漂移（methodological drift）影响。QuAcc 旨在提供灵活且易用的框架以应对这些问题。

Method: QuAcc 提供预定义工作流配方（workflow recipes），覆盖第一性原理电子结构（first-principles electronic-structure）方法、半经验与紧束缚（semiempirical and tight-binding）方法、经典势（classical potentials）以及基础 MLIPs。其设计将领域特定科学逻辑与用于编排和执行计算的工作流引擎分离；工作流以普通 Python 函数编写，可在多个受支持引擎上执行而无需修改底层源码。此外，QuAcc 提供统一平台以生成与目标模型一致的从头算（ab initio）参考计算。

Result: QuAcc 能够在不修改底层源码的情况下，用多个受支持的工作流引擎执行同一工作流，从而降低开发与贡献新工作流的门槛。其统一平台有助于缓解评估基础 MLIPs 时的算法漂移，使模型性能评估更具一致性。

Conclusion: QuAcc 是一个灵活且易用的原子尺度模拟工作流框架，适应机器学习与人工智能时代的模拟需求。其科学逻辑与工作流引擎解耦的设计，以及统一评估基础 MLIPs 的能力，构成该框架的核心价值。

Abstract: We present the Quantum Accelerator (QuAcc), an open-source workflow library for atomistic simulations with an emphasis on quantum-mechanical calculations. QuAcc provides predefined workflow recipes spanning first-principles electronic-structure methods, semiempirical and tight-binding approaches, classical potentials, and foundation machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs). A central design feature of QuAcc is its separation of domain-specific scientific logic from the workflow engine used to orchestrate and execute calculations. Workflows are written as ordinary Python functions and can be executed with multiple supported workflow engines without modifying the underlying source code, lowering the barrier to developing and contributing new workflows. QuAcc also streamlines the evaluation of foundation MLIPs by providing a unified platform for generating ab initio reference calculations consistent with the model of interest, mitigating methodological drift when assessing model performance. Together, these features make QuAcc a flexible and accessible framework for atomistic simulation workflows that have become central to the current era of machine learning and artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [19] [Correlation Between Dopant Atom Evaporation Field and Measured Site Preference in Atom Probe Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34008)
*Kasper A. Hunnestad,Constantinos Hatzoglou,Frida H. Danmo,Zewu Yan,Edith Bourret,Francois Vurpillot,Antonius T. J. van Helvoort,Sverre Selbach,Dennis Meier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以Zr掺杂ErMnO₃为模型体系，首次揭示原子探针层析（APT）中掺杂原子位置与场蒸发（field evaporation）条件的关系，发现基体与掺杂原子均存在显著优先保留（preferential retention）效应，会影响掺杂位点判定并导致误判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂氧化物（complex oxides）性质可通过化学掺杂精确调控，但低掺杂水平下的原子尺度掺杂分析日益困难；APT虽具备单原子级化学灵敏度与空间分辨率，却需先明确原子特异性场蒸发过程才能可靠提取信息。

Method: 以Zr掺杂ErMnO₃作为模型体系，结合原子探针层析（atom probe tomography, APT）测量与场蒸发模拟（field-evaporation simulations），分析掺杂原子的APT测量位置及场蒸发条件。

Result: 分析发现基体与掺杂原子均存在显著的优先保留（preferential retention）现象，强烈影响掺杂位点判定并可能导致错误解释；该保留效应由内禀参数（掺杂原子的蒸发场、浓度）与外禀参数（分析温度）共同决定。

Conclusion: 研究结果对固体体系中单个掺杂原子的APT分析以及原子尺度场蒸发动力学（field evaporation dynamics）的理解具有重要意义。

Abstract: Complex oxides possess a wide range of electric, magnetic, and optical properties that can be precisely tuned by chemical doping. The atomic-scale analysis of the property-controlling dopants, however, becomes increasingly difficult towards low doping levels. Atom probe tomography (APT) offers chemical sensitivity and spatial resolution to image individual dopant atoms down to a few parts per million. To reliably extract such information, detailed knowledge about the atom-specific field evaporation processes is required. Here we demonstrate a first insight into the APT-measured atomic position of dopant atoms and the field evaporation conditions, using Zr-doped ErMnO3 as a model system. Our analysis reveals a substantial preferential retention of both matrix and dopant atoms which strongly affects the dopant site determination and can lead to an incorrect interpretation. The retention effect is determined by intrinsic and extrinsic parameters, such as the dopant's evaporation field and concentration and the analysis temperature, respectively, as we explain based on field-evaporation simulations. Our results are important for the APT-based analysis of individual dopant atoms in solid systems and the understanding of field evaporation dynamics at the atomic level in general.

</details>


### [20] [Native implementation of the machine-learned Skala exchange-correlation functional in CP2K: Unified one-centre reconstruction for molecular and condensed-phase calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34055)
*Johann Pototschnig,Franz Pöschel,Jürg Hutter,Thomas D. Kühne*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将机器学习得到的 Skala 交换关联泛函（exchange-correlation functional, XC）原生实现于 CP2K，采用 GPW/GAPW 方法并保持分子级基准精度，在凝聚相能量与带隙预测上表现良好，但出现结构过结合现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是将此前分子层面的 Skala 泛函拓展至周期性凝聚相电子结构计算，并借助周期性多体参考数据支持后续发展。

Method: 在 CP2K 中以 GPW 与 GAPW 原生实现，采用单中心联合重构密度、密度梯度与动能密度以保留混合梯度项与非局域耦合；引入 XC 专用 GAPW 表示，并包含布里渊区采样与点群对称性约化。

Result: 在足够灵活的轨道基下保持分子基准精度；全电子 Skala-D3(BJ) 对晶体 CO2、NH3、尿素相对扩散蒙特卡洛（diffusion Monte Carlo, DMC）的 MAE 为 1.54 kJ mol^-1；DMC-ICE13 十三相绝对晶格能 MAE 为 1.16 kJ mol^-1、十二个相对冰 Ih 能量 MAE 为 0.82 kJ mol^-1；15 材料带隙 MAE 为 0.43 eV。

Conclusion: 该框架将分子 Skala 与凝聚相电子结构相连接，可用于周期性多体参考数据的后续开发；但 LC10 基准显示平衡晶格常数被系统性低估，存在结构过结合问题。

Abstract: We implement the machine-learned Skala exchange-correlation (XC) functional natively in CP2K using its Gaussian and plane-wave (GPW) and Gaussian and augmented-plane-wave (GAPW) methods. A joint one-centre reconstruction of density, density gradients, and kinetic-energy density before functional evaluation preserves mixed gradient terms and nonlocal couplings between smooth and atom-local contributions in all-electron and pseudopotential calculations. An XC-specific GAPW representation resolves rapidly varying local contributions on atom-centred grids, reducing plane-wave requirements for pseudopotential calculations. The implementation includes Brillouin-zone sampling and point-group symmetry reduction. With sufficiently flexible orbital bases, it retains the molecular benchmark accuracy of our earlier GauXC formulation. All-electron Skala-D3(BJ) calculations yield a mean absolute error (MAE) of 1.54 kJ mol$^{-1}$ against diffusion Monte Carlo for crystalline CO$_2$, NH$_3$, and urea, spanning dispersion, quadrupolar electrostatics, and hydrogen bonding. For all thirteen DMC-ICE13 phases, the MAEs are 1.16 kJ mol$^{-1}$ for absolute lattice energies and 0.82 kJ mol$^{-1}$ for the twelve relative energies to ice Ih. At fixed experimental geometries, all-electron/mixed-core calculations give a band-gap MAE of 0.43 eV on a 15-material set, substantially below commonly used semilocal functionals and comparable to widely used hybrid functionals. The LC10 benchmark nevertheless reveals systematically underestimated equilibrium lattice constants, indicating structural overbinding. This framework connects molecular Skala to condensed-phase electronic structure and enables future development using periodic many-body reference data.

</details>


### [21] [Nanometer-scale operando electric field mapping in oxide junctions by correlative STEM-EBIC and 4D-STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34087)
*Yining Xie,Eoin Moynihan,Xingyao Li,Richard Beanland,Aditya Singh,Marin Alexe,Mingmin Yang,Ana Sanchez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合操作态扫描透射电镜电子束诱导电流（STEM-EBIC）与四维扫描透射电镜（4D-STEM），在纳米尺度定量重建氧化物肖特基结（Schottky junction）的偏压依赖电场空间分布，揭示其偏离理想肖特基耗尽模型的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解氧化物界面局域电场是连接界面静电学（interfacial electrostatics）与器件功能的关键，但传统电学测量仅能间接推断电场。该研究旨在实现操作条件下氧化物异质结电场的定量纳米尺度成像。

Method: 以 La0.67Sr0.33MnO3/Nb:SrTiO3（LSMO/NSTO）为模型体系，在 MEMS 偏压平台上制备电子透明结并验证其保持整流输运行为；结合操作态 STEM-EBIC 与 4D-STEM 重建偏压依赖电场分布，其中 STEM-EBIC 用于弥补定量 4D-STEM 场成像在异质结中的若干局限。

Result: 重建的电场剖面显示耗尽区内存在显著非线性衰减，并延伸穿透至 LSMO 层，直接揭示氧化物肖特基结偏离理想传统肖特基耗尽模型，提供非经典界面静电学（non-classical interfacial electrostatics）的实验特征。

Conclusion: 该关联方法可在工作状态下对氧化物异质结进行定量纳米尺度电场成像，为连接界面静电学与宏观输运及指导氧化物器件设计提供依据。

Abstract: Understanding local electric fields at oxide interfaces is essential for linking interfacial electrostatics to device functionality, yet conventional electrical measurements infer these fields only indirectly. Here, we combine operando scanning transmission electron microscope electron-beam-induced current (STEM-EBIC) with four-dimensional-STEM (4D-STEM) to quantitatively reconstruct the bias-dependent electric field spatial distribution across an oxide Schottky junction at nanometer scale. Using La0.67Sr0.33MnO3/Nb:SrTiO3 (LSMO/NSTO) as a model system, we fabricate an electron-transparent junction on a MEMS biasing platform and verify that it retains the rectifying transport behavior. STEM-EBIC provides complementary information addressing several key limitations in quantitative 4D-STEM field mapping for heterojunctions. The reconstructed electric field profiles show a pronounced nonlinear decay within the depletion region and an extended penetration into the LSMO. These results directly reveal the deviations of oxides Schottky junction from the ideal conventional Schottky depletion model, providing experimental signatures of non-classical interfacial electrostatics. Our correlative approach enables quantitative nanoscale electric field mapping in operating oxide heterojunctions, providing a basis for linking interfacial electrostatics to macroscopic transport and guiding oxide devices design.

</details>


### [22] [Finite-temperature grain boundary phases, transformations, and diagrams in alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34131)
*Flynn Walsh,Timofey Frolov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种同时采样复杂界面全部微观自由度的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟方法，可对多组分材料的有限温度晶界结构、相变与相图进行严格预测。

Here we show that wavelength-resolved magneto-optical Kerr spectroscopy of a proximity-coupled MoSe$_2$/Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ van der Waals heterostructure resolves magnetic symmetry breaking across the A- and B-exciton manifolds. Moreover, characteristic features of the chemical composition of the Si oxide and Si nitride phases in two-phase Si oxynitrides are revealed. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子间势（interatomic potentials）是材料原子尺度建模的核心工具，而 ACE 通常以 one-hot 表示编码化学自由度，导致化学分层（chemically stratified）模型。晶格上的构型团簇展开所用的替代化学表示尚未在原子间势中被评估。

Abstract: Interatomic potentials are central tools in the atomistic modeling of materials. The atomic cluster expansion (ACE) parameterizes such potentials from ab initio data, conventionally encoding the chemical degrees of freedom with a one-hot representation that yields chemically stratified models. Here we revisit the multicomponent ACE for an arbitrary chemical site basis. We then establish an exact analytical mapping between a fitted linear ACE and the average-atom potential that describes a perfectly random alloy. We benchmark potentials built on the occupational, Chebyshev, and conventional ACE bases against solute binding and vacancy formation energies in Mg-Nd, and against the mixing enthalpies of the Mo-Nb and Cr-W solid solutions. When training data are scarce, the occupational basis converges fastest and offers the best control over targeted material properties, while the conventional and Chebyshev bases face challenges in reproducing these properties. The occupational basis likewise yields the most reliable average-atom description of disordered alloy thermodynamics. In the large-data limit the three bases perform identically. The chemical basis is therefore a design choice that governs data efficiency. Its explicit treatment opens a route to average-atom potentials for the thermodynamic, mechanical, and kinetic properties of concentrated alloys.

</details>

By optimizing the design for better carrier capture efficiency and tuning the emission wavelength to the O-band, we show ways of improving the carrier confinement. Absorption spectroscopy on the finalized structure confirms substantial energy separation between the quantum dot ground and excited states, effectively suppressing thermal carrier escape even at elevated temperatures. These results highlight critical design for improving carrier capture and retention in long-wavelength quantum dot lasers.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: A fundamental issue with first principles electronic structure calculation is the requirement for magnetic initialisations, which are usually derived from experimental results. In this paper, we present a new and computationally inexpensive method to predict magnetic structures from first principles, based upon symmetry analysis and a non-magnetic band structure only. We demonstrate that this method of creating initialisations successfully reproduces the known magnetic ground state for a wide class of magnetic materials, including ferromagnetic BCC Fe, antiferromagnetic L1$_0$ PtMn, and frustrated antiferromagnetic L1$_2$ IrMn$_3$, while also predicting non-magnetic materials to remain non-magnetic.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruddlesden-Popper 相具有高度可调且天然分层的结构，其极化可通过杂化非本征铁电机制（hybrid improper ferroelectric mechanism）产生，由此形成多个序参量（如八面体旋转、极性畸变与应变）相互作用的复杂畴壁结构，值得深入探究。

Method: 采用群论（group theory）与机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）相结合的方法，对 Ca3Ti2O7 在 {100}、{110} 和 {001} 赝四方面内的畴壁进行映射；并引入电荷感知（charge-aware）训练框架，结合参考玻恩有效电荷（reference Born effective charges）降低预测误差。

Result: 所训练的势函数（potential）能够复现所有考察壁类型与取向下的密度泛函理论（DFT）序参量与极化分布；电荷感知训练框架结合参考玻恩有效电荷使畴壁形成能的预测误差降低 70%，表明建模对称破缺界面（symmetry-broken interfaces）需纳入长程静电作用。

Conclusion: MLIP 能以接近 DFT 的精度识别原子尺度的最小能量路径（minimum energy pathways），并揭示用于极化翻转（polarization switching）的低能反极性（antipolar）构型。

Abstract: Ruddlesden-Popper phases are highly tunable and naturally layered structures, in which polarization can arise via a hybrid improper ferroelectric mechanism. In this work, we explore the rich set of DW structures in prototypical Ca3Ti2O7, mapping out the DWs in the {100}, {110} and {001} pseudo-tetragonal planes using group theory and machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs). Finally, the MLIP is used to identify minimum energy pathways at the atomic scale with nearly the precision of DFT calculations, revealing a low-energy antipolar configuration for polarization switching.

The calculated Mulliken populations reveal pressure-induced depletion of Zr s and p-states, accompanied by an increase of the d-state population, while the density of states indicates enhanced p-d hybridization under compression. These results provide experimental and computational evidence for pressure-induced s,p-d electronic redistribution in zirconium and link this effect to the previously reported anomalous structural evolution of the compressed amorphous alloy.

*Sofiia Kosar,Anil R. Pininti,Vladyslav Hnapovskyi,José P. Jurado,Subhashri Mannar,Lorenzo Mardegan,Anand S. Subbiah,Frédéric Laquai,Stefaan De Wolf*

Abstract: The scalable fabrication of high-quality, large-area perovskite thin films is hindered by microscopic inhomogeneities, particularly residual compositional impurities formed during processing. Films with higher impurity density show accelerated early degradation, establishing this parameter as an early-warning metric for stability screening. Extending this framework to degradation tracking further reveals coupled photo- and thermo-chemical contributions to impurity-mediated instability. Overall, this work establishes a practical chemically-validated diagnostic imaging framework for rapid pre-screening of perovskite films to enable stable and scalable PSCs.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 搭建自制的超高压（ultra-high vacuum, UHV）拉曼平台，并集成便携式拉曼真空传输箱（Raman vacuum suitcase, RVS），该装置具备温度控制与精确气体定量投加功能，可在明确的热学与化学环境下防止样品接触空气并实现原位测量。

Result: 利用该平台监测了7原子宽与9原子宽扶手椅型石墨烯纳米带（7- and 9-AGNRs）的表面合成过程，量化了7-AGNR拉曼模式在162–748 K宽温区内的演变，并分辨出受控O2暴露引起的化学变化，其结果与反应性锯齿（zigzag）位点的氧化相一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合RVS的UHV拉曼光谱（UHV Raman spectroscopy）可作为在受控环境下获取低维量子材料本征振动特征的有效途径。

Abstract: Atomically precise graphene nanoribbons (GNRs) exhibit tunable electronic and magnetic properties governed by edge topology and finite-size effects, which make them versatile platforms for next-generation electronic and spintronic applications. However, the unpaired pi-electrons responsible for their magnetic properties simultaneously make them highly susceptible to chemical degradation under ambient conditions. This intrinsic reactivity poses a central experimental challenge: accessing vibrational and electronic signatures of air-sensitive GNRs during synthesis and under controlled environments without breaking vacuum. Once the material has been exposed to air, standard characterization techniques would probe oxidized or chemically modified species rather than the pristine form. Here, we overcome this limitation by developing a home-built ultra-high vacuum (UHV) Raman platform designed to preserve sample integrity by preventing air exposure and to enable in situ investigation of material properties. The portable Raman vacuum suitcase (RVS) integrates temperature control and precise gas dosing, allowing direct monitoring of growth kinetics, lattice dynamics, and reactive-edge responses under well-defined thermal and chemical environments. Using this platform, we monitor the on-surface synthesis of 7- and 9-atom-wide armchair GNRs (7- and 9-AGNRs), quantify the evolution of 7-AGNR Raman modes over a wide temperature range (162-748 K), and resolve chemical changes upon controlled O2 exposure that are consistent with oxidation at the reactive zigzag sites. These results establish UHV Raman spectroscopy with the RVS as a route to accessing the intrinsic vibrational signatures of low-dimensional quantum materials under controlled environments.

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作构建了一个机器学习框架，基于 Materials Project 的结构、组分与电子信息识别二维材料磁序并预测磁化强度，分类平衡准确率达 93.6%，磁化强度平均绝对误差为 0.686 μB/formula unit，并对候选材料开展了磁织构与类斯格明子（skyrmion-like）特征的模拟分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维（2D）材料中的奇异磁序（exotic magnetic orders）对高能效自旋电子学（spintronic）应用具有重要价值，但实现稳健的高温范德华反铁磁体（van der Waals antiferromagnets）与拓扑磁态（topological magnetic states）仍具挑战，因而需要高效的磁序识别与磁化强度预测手段。

Method: 构建固定长度描述符（descriptors）以支持两个互补任务：铁磁（FM）与反铁磁（AFM）分类及定量磁化强度预测。分类采用在结构衍生描述符上训练的 LightGBM 模型（未显式引入磁性描述符），并使用按化学体系分组的五折交叉验证（five-fold cross-validation）以减少化学泄漏，配合超参数与分类阈值优化。磁化强度预测采用残差多层感知机（residual multilayer perceptron），输入为组分、结构、电子及任务特定的初始态描述符，并引入对数目标变换与稳健加权损失，最终集成三个独立训练的模型。

Result: 在独立测试集上，分类器取得 93.6% 的平衡准确率（balanced accuracy）；磁化强度预测模型达到 0.686 μB/formula unit 的平均绝对误差（mean absolute error）。对选定磁性候选材料，通过归一化磁化强度剖面（normalized magnetization profiles）与拓扑荷（topological charge）开展模拟磁成像与相位重建分析，以研究磁织构及类斯格明子特征。

Conclusion: 该框架为筛选二维磁性材料并优先选择面向反铁磁与拓扑自旋电子学应用的候选材料提供了高效途径。

Abstract: Exotic magnetic orders in two-dimensional (2D) materials are attracting huge interest for energy-efficient spintronic applications, yet realizing robust high-temperature van der Waals antiferromagnets and topological magnetic states remains challenging. In this work, we develop a machine-learning framework for identifying magnetic orders and predicting magnetization using structural, compositional, and electronic information derived from the Materials Project. Fixed-length descriptors are constructed for two complementary tasks: ferromagnetic (FM) versus antiferromagnetic (AFM) classification and quantitative magnetization prediction. Magnetic order is classified using a LightGBM model trained on structure-derived descriptors without explicitly including magnetic descriptors. Five-fold cross-validation grouped by chemical system is used to reduce chemical leakage, together with hyperparameter and classification-threshold optimization. On an isolated test set, the classifier achieves a balanced accuracy of 93.6% in the testing set. Magnetization is predicted using a residual multilayer perceptron with compositional, structural, electronic, and task-specific initial-state descriptors. A logarithmic target transformation and robust weighted loss are used to account for the broad magnetization distribution, while three independently trained models are combined into a final ensemble. The model achieves a mean absolute error of 0.686 μB/formula unit. For selected magnetic candidates, simulated magnetic imaging and phase-reconstruction analysis are further used to investigate magnetization textures and skyrmion-like features through normalized magnetization profiles and topological charge. This framework provides an efficient approach for screening 2D magnetic materials and prioritizing candidates for antiferromagnetic and topological spintronic applications.

</details>


### [33] [Density functional perturbation theory of meta-generalized gradient approximations using algorithmic differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35572)
*Bruno Ploumhans,Niklas Frederik Schmitz,Michael F. Herbst*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作为依赖密度 ρ 与动能密度 τ 的交换关联泛函（exchange-correlation functional）实现了密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT），并用算法微分（AD）替代繁琐的二阶导数推导。作者将 AD-DFPT 应用于 ZnO 与 BaTiO3 的响应性质计算，并用于训练神经网络 meta-GGA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面波密度泛函理论（DFPT）中，对同时依赖密度 ρ 与动能密度 τ 的交换关联泛函（如 meta-GGA）求导需要繁琐的交换关联二阶能量导数表达式，构成实现障碍。该工作旨在绕开这一推导难题，并扩展至更广的非局域参数化情形。

Method: 将交换关联二阶导数重构为交换关联势的雅可比-向量积（Jacobian-vector product），并借助算法微分（AD）技术求值。该方法与作者此前开发的 AD-DFPT 框架 [N. F. Schmitz et al., npj Comput. Mater. 12, 6 (2026)] 集成，从而可获取任意基态量对任意微扰的导数。

Result: 对 ZnO 与 BaTiO3 计算了一系列响应性质，结果显示近期的 r2SCAN01 meta-GGA 泛函总体优于 LDA 与 PBE。此外，利用 AD-DFPT 梯度优化了一个神经网络 meta-GGA，使其自洽地重现体硅（bulk silicon）的杂化 DFT 参考密度。

Conclusion: 结果表明 AD-DFPT 是在 meta-GGA 层级计算密度泛函理论导数（DFT derivatives）的多用途途径，既适用于常见响应性质，也适用于新型交换关联泛函基于梯度的训练所需的非常规导数。

Abstract: Density functional perturbation theory (DFPT) is an established framework for the computation of derivatives in plane-wave density functional theory. We present an implementation of DFPT for exchange-correlation (XC) functionals $E_\mathrm{xc}(ρ,τ)$ that incorporate an explicit dependence on both the density $ρ$ and the kinetic energy density $τ$. This covers the popular class of semilocal meta-generalized gradient approximations (meta-GGAs) as well as broader nonlocal parametrizations. We sidestep the derivation of cumbersome XC second energy derivative expressions by recasting these derivatives as a Jacobian-vector product of the XC potentials, which we evaluate with algorithmic differentiation (AD) techniques. Integration with our previously developed AD-DFPT framework [N. F. Schmitz et al., npj Comput. Mater. 12, 6 (2026)] provides access to derivatives of arbitrary ground state quantities with respect to arbitrary perturbations. We employ AD-DFPT to compute a range of response properties for ZnO and BaTiO3, and find that the recent r2SCAN01 meta-GGA functional generally outperforms LDA and PBE. Finally, we showcase the optimization of a neural-network meta-GGA to self-consistently reproduce hybrid-DFT reference densities of bulk silicon, using AD-DFPT gradients. Overall, these results establish AD-DFPT as a versatile route for computing DFT derivatives at the meta-GGA level, be they common response properties or the unusual derivatives required for the gradient-based training of novel XC functionals.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BaxZr1-xTiO3（0.15 < x < 0.25）陶瓷兼具高介电常数、低漏电流和低损耗角正切，适用于可调电容器、多层陶瓷电容器与压电驱动器，并因 Zr4+ 等价取代 Ti4+ 引起的局域化学应变而呈现弛豫特性。然而，据作者所知，x~0.2 组分的热释电性质尚未被研究，本文即为填补该空白。

Result: 热波探测显示显著的热释电响应，且相对陶瓷两个相反表面可呈强不对称性；该不对称性与响应轮廓显著依赖电场循环历史。室温 XRD 表明宏观四方性（tetragonality）近乎缺失，仅 (200) 峰存在微弱不对称，指示富 Ti 极性纳米区（polar nanoregions）内部存在小的四方性。升温时 714 cm-1 拉曼带相对强度下降，表明 30–40 °C 间存在弥散的铁电-顺电相变（ferroelectric-paraelectric phase transition）。介电常数温度关系遵循幂指数为 1.4 的修正 Curie-Weiss 定律。

Conclusion: 综合结果表明 BaTi0.8Zr0.2O3 陶瓷中长程有序铁电态与弛豫态共存，并可能存在非遍历弛豫型极性态。

Abstract: BaxZr1-xTiO3 ceramics with 0.15 < x < 0.25, which have high dielectric permittivity, small leakage currents and low dissipation factor, are promising materials for tunable capacitor devices, multilayered ceramic capacitors and piezoelectric actuators. These materials reveal relaxor properties due to the local chemical strains caused by the isovalent substitution of Ti4+ ions by Zr4+ ions with a larger ionic size. However, to the best of our knowledge, the pyroelectric properties of BaxZr1-xTiO3 ceramics with x~0.2 have not been studied. To fill the gap in knowledge, we perform the pyroelectric thermo-wave probing of BaZr0.2Ti0.8O3 ceramics prepared by the solid-state synthesis. Results of the thermo-wave probing of the most interesting polar states, namely polarized, depolarized, relaxed and restored polarized states, reveal the pronounced pyroelectric response that can be strongly asymmetric with respect to the opposite surfaces of the ceramics. The asymmetry and profiles of pyroelectric response depend significantly on the pre-history of the electric field cycling indicating possible non-ergodic relaxor-type polar states in the ceramic sample. X-ray diffraction spectrum, recorded at room temperature, reveals the virtual absence of the macroscopic tetragonality and the weak asymmetry of the (200) peak, which indicate the small tetragonality inside the Ti-enriched polar nanoregions. Decrease of the relative intensity of the Raman band at 714 cm-1 occurring upon heating evidences the diffuse ferroelectric-paraelectric phase transition between 30 C and 40 C. The temperature dependence of the dielectric permittivity obeys modified Curie-Weiss law with power 1.4, indicating that both ordered ferroelectric and relaxor states coexist in the BaTi0.8Zr0.2O3 ceramics.

</details>

*Timothy Draher,Nolan Bitner,Vasileios Niaouris,Claire E. McDermott,David Czaplewski,Supratik Guha,David D. Awschalom,F. Joseph Heremans,Alan M. Dibos*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SiC中的钒色心具备电信O波段发射、亚微秒光学寿命与秒级自旋弛豫时间，是量子网络节点（quantum network nodes）的有力候选，但高折射率SiC宿主中的光子提取效率（photon extraction）低，制约其应用。

Result: 在原生态系综中观测到Purcell增强的光致发光，瞬态光谱烧孔（transient spectral hole burning）线宽为50 MHz、时间尺度为微秒；在稀薄注入样品中隔离出单个钒色心，获得高纯度单光子发射与109 ns的Purcell增强寿命；905 nm重泵浦激光以95%效率恢复钒的电荷态（charge state）。

Conclusion: 硅-碳化硅（Si-on-SiC）被确立为可扩展、与晶圆厂工艺兼容（foundry-compatible）的平台，适用于电信波长的自旋-光子接口（spin-photon interfaces）。

Abstract: Vanadium color centers in silicon carbide (SiC) offer telecom O-band emission, sub-microsecond optical lifetimes, and second-scale spin relaxation times, rendering them promising candidates for quantum network nodes. However, efficient photon extraction from the high-refractive-index SiC host remains challenging. Here, we introduce a silicon-on-SiC heterogeneous photonic platform in which silicon nanocavities evanescently couple to shallow vanadium dopants in commercial 4H-SiC, enabling efficient zero-phonon line (ZPL) collection under resonant excitation. In as-grown ensembles, we observe Purcell-enhanced photoluminescence with transient spectral hole burning linewidths of 50 MHz on microsecond timescales. In dilute implanted samples, we isolate individual vanadium centers with high-purity single photon emission and a Purcell-enhanced lifetime of 109 ns. The entire optical interface operates through a single lensed fiber, including a 905 nm repump laser that recovers the vanadium charge state with 95% efficiency. </details>


### [36] [Catalogue of Collinear Magnetic Structures in Stoichiometric Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35712)
*Liangliang Huang,Houhao Wang,Yuanze Song,Ruixi Pu,Xiangang Wan,Feng Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用自旋空间群（spin space groups, SSGs）对非磁晶体结构预设共线磁结构（collinear magnetic structures），穷举枚举无机晶体结构数据库（Inorganic Crystal Structure Database）中72,075个结构的全部对称允许共线磁结构，得到365,639个共线磁结构，并对7,824个共线磁体进行第一性原理计算确定基态SSG对称性，进而结合SSG与磁空间群完成拓扑分类，输出数千种新磁性拓扑材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性材料是探索新奇量子涌现现象与推进信息技术的重要平台，而其磁结构（magnetic structures, MSs）的可靠确定通常依赖中子散射等实验手段。鉴于MAGNDATA中约一半磁结构为共线型，作者希望从对称性出发系统性地预设并枚举候选共线磁结构，以补充实验判定、指导表征并推动自旋电子学应用。

Method: 采用自旋空间群（SSGs）对给定非磁晶体结构预先限定共线候选磁结构；注意到MAGNDATA中超过80%的共线磁体对应于非磁群的最大子群即共线SSG，遂枚举230个非磁空间群的最大共线SSG子群在Wyckoff位置形成的全部共线磁结构。基于该穷举，对ICSD中72,075个非磁化学计量比结构进行分类，并对筛选出的21,315种材料中的7,824个共线磁体用第一性原理计算确定基态SSG对称性，最后进行结合SSG与磁空间群的拓扑分类。

Result: 获得365,639个对称允许的共线磁结构；确定7,824个共线磁体的基态SSG对称性；通过SSG与磁空间群结合的全面拓扑分类，输出数千种新的磁性拓扑材料。MAGNDATA中实验确定的磁结构仅是所构建共线磁体计算数据库的冰山一角。

Conclusion: 该计算数据库预期可指导实验人员的磁结构表征，并促进自旋电子学（spintronics）方向的进一步研究。

Abstract: Magnetic materials persistently serve as a fertile platform for exploring novel quantum emergent phenom ena and advancing information technologies. Reliably determining their magnetic structures (MSs), which is essential for understanding their emergent quantum properties, usually depends on experiments such as neutron scattering. Here, noting that around half of the MSs in MAGNDATA (a database of experimentally determined MSs) are collinear, we adopt the spin space groups (SSGs) to preset the candidate MSs for a given nonmagnetic crystal structure to be collinear from the outset. On the other hand, more than 80% of the collinear magnets in MAGNDATA are subject to maximal subgroups of nonmagnetic groups which are collinear SSGs, and we then enumerate all collinear MSs formed at Wyckoff positions by the maximal collinear SSG subgroups of the 230 nonmagnetic space groups. With such exhaustive enumeration, we categorize all symmetry-allowed collinear MSs for the 72,075 nonmagnetic stoichiometric structures in Inorganic Crystal Structure Database, obtaining 365,639 collinear MSs. We then determine the ground-state SSG symmetries of 7,824 collinear magnets among the 21,315 selected materials using first-principles calculations. These magnets then undergo a comprehensive topological classification combining SSGs and magnetic space groups, outputting thousands of new magnetic topological materials. The experimentally determined MSs in MAGNDATA represent the tip of the iceberg in our computational database of collinear magnets, expected to guide MS characterization by experimentalists and further investigation towards spintronics applications.

</details>

### [37] [Uniqueness of four-body convex central configurations for all masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35632)
*Tejasvi Singh Tomar*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该论文证明了四体问题中：对任意四个正质量与任意循环次序，恰存在一个（在相似意义下）满足该次序的严格凸平面中心构型（central configuration），从而解决 Albouy–Cabral–Santos 问题清单中的 Problem 10（即 Santoprete 所称的 Simó–Yoccoz 猜想）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决四体问题中凸中心构型（convex central configuration）的存在唯一性问题，即 Albouy、Cabral 与 Santos 问题清单中的 Problem 10，Santoprete 将其称为 Simó–Yoccoz 猜想。

Method: 核心技术步骤是对凸中心构型处的 Hessian 给出统一下界：其至少为径向部分（radial part）的四分之一，后者仅在平移与旋转方向上退化。借助 Dziobek 关系将 Hessian 的不定部分化为某平方的负倍数，并用 Cauchy–Schwarz 论证将其以径向部分乘以一个显式 2×2 矩阵之迹为上界，该矩阵不含质量参数。通过 Corbera–Cors–Roberts 坐标下的区间算术（interval arithmetic）在三维归一化凸中心构型集合上证明该迹小于 3/4；唯一性则由四等质量情形经覆盖论证（covering argument）推出。计算由第二个独立程序重复验证，且 Hessian 界与唯一性定理（含计算）已在 Lean 证明助手（proof assistant）中形式化并由其内核以标准公理检验。

Result: 证明了对任意四个正质量与任意循环次序，存在且仅存在一个（相差相似）满足该次序的严格凸平面中心构型；由此可得：任何退化四体中心构型必为凹的，凸中心构型解析依赖于质量，且关于风筝形（kites）、等腰梯形（isosceles trapezoids）与菱形（rhombi）的已知对称性定理可数行推出。解析依赖性与对称性定理亦已形式化。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了四体凸中心构型的存在唯一性，解决了 Simó–Yoccoz 猜想，并通过机器验证（Lean 形式化）与独立重复计算提升了结果可靠性，同时导出若干退化性、解析依赖与对称性的推论。

Abstract: We prove that for every choice of four positive masses and every cyclic ordering of the bodies there is exactly one strictly convex planar central configuration with that ordering, up to similarity. This answers Problem 10 in the list of Albouy, Cabral and Santos, which Santoprete calls the Simó-Yoccoz conjecture. The main step is a uniform lower bound for the Hessian at convex central configurations: it is at least one quarter of its radial part, which vanishes only on translations and rotations. Dziobek's relations turn the indefinite part of the Hessian into a negative multiple of a square, and a Cauchy-Schwarz argument bounds this term by the radial part times the trace of an explicit $2\times 2$ matrix in which the masses do not appear. We prove that this trace is less than 3/4 by interval arithmetic on the three-dimensional set of normalized convex central configurations, in the coordinates of Corbera, Cors and Roberts. Uniqueness then follows by a covering argument from the case of four equal masses. The computation has been repeated with a second, independently written program. The Hessian bound and the uniqueness theorem, including the computation, have been formalized in the Lean proof assistant and checked by its kernel, using only the standard axioms. As consequences, every degenerate four-body central configuration is concave, the convex central configuration depends analytically on the masses, and the known symmetry theorems for kites, isosceles trapezoids and rhombi follow in a few lines. The analytic dependence and the symmetry theorems are also formalized.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度杂散磁场（stray magnetic fields）的定量测量对理解与优化磁存储及逻辑器件具有重要意义，但现有探针难以兼顾高空间分辨率与微扰最小化，因此需要一种可转移的定量探测手段。

Result: 实验在条带边缘观测到正频率跳变，在条带外侧紧邻处观测到非预期的负跳变，在条带中心附近变化可忽略；三种对比信号可由微磁模拟的静磁边界场定量解释。全约束拟合流程使共振部分分辨时仍能稳健提取磁场。

Conclusion: NV-纳米金刚石磁测量（NV-ND magnetometry）可作为一种简单、可转移且定量的方法，用于表征薄膜与自旋电子器件（spintronic devices）中空间非均匀磁场。

Abstract: Quantitative measurement of nanoscale stray magnetic fields during domain reversal is important for understanding and optimizing magnetic memory and logic devices. Yet, achieving high spatial resolution with minimally invasive probes remains challenging. Here we demonstrate nanodiamonds (NDs) hosting an ensemble nitrogen vacancy (NV) centers as local quantum sensors of domain-reversal stray fields in a CoFeB strip. During magnetic-field-driven reversal, the ND sensors resolve three distinct local responses depending on their positions relative to the strip: a positive frequency jump at the edge, an unexpected negative jump just outside the strip, and a negligible change near the center. These contrasting signals are quantitatively explained by magnetostatic boundary fields from micromagnetic simulations. We further implement a fully constrained fitting procedure for optically detected magnetic resonance spectra, enabling robust field extraction even when resonances are partially resolved. Together, these results establish NV-ND magnetometry as a simple, transferable, and quantitative approach for characterizing spatially heterogeneous magnetic fields in thin-films and spintronic devices.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究准一维手性磁体（chiral magnet）中带电孤子（charged soliton）分支，对比相同静态能量泛函下反铁磁与铁磁两种动力学补全的差异。结果显示，DM相互作用与Zeeman场可关闭反铁磁Q-ball频率窗口，而铁磁Berry相位使旋转频率充当平移Zeeman场，从而选择不同极性的带电液滴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于澄清：当静态手性磁能相同但底层磁动力学不同（二阶时间导数 vs. Berry相位）时，带电孤子的存在条件与物理含义如何改变。该问题关涉手性磁体中拓扑/非拓扑孤子的可控生成机制。

Method: 在含Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya（DM）相互作用、易轴各向异性（easy-axis anisotropy）与Zeeman耦合的准一维手性磁系统中，于DM选定的螺旋分支上将问题约化为关于序参量极角的一维可解析系统。对反铁磁模型（二阶时间导数）与铁磁模型（Berry相位动力学）分别施加同一静态磁泛函，并由约化有效势曲率与非零转折点导出极性Q-ball（polar Q-ball）存在条件。

Result: 反铁磁情形允许的频率窗口对称，且可被DM耦合与Zeeman场联合效应完全关闭；零Zeeman场时同一约化还支持反铁磁Q-kink，并给出显式轮廓、电荷、能量与约化扇区裂变判据。铁磁情形中Berry相位使旋转频率表现为平移的Zeeman场，南/北极带电液滴由平移旋转量的相反符号选择；铁磁力学问题的形式化极到极解一般不对应有限能量磁孤子。

Conclusion: 带电孤子机制依赖于底层磁动力学，即使静态手性能量相同；Berry项并不重整化物理哈密顿量，故形式解未必构成有限能量孤子。

Abstract: We study charged soliton branches in quasi-one-dimensional chiral magnetic systems with Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interaction, easy-axis anisotropy, and Zeeman coupling. The same static magnetic functional is equipped with two different dynamical completions: an antiferromagnetic model with second-order time derivatives and a ferromagnetic model with Berry-phase dynamics. On the helical branch selected by the static DM interaction, the problem reduces to an analytically tractable one-dimensional system for the polar angle of the order parameter. We derive the existence conditions for polar Q-balls from the curvature of the reduced effective potential and the presence of a nonzero turning point. In the antiferromagnetic case, the allowed frequency window is symmetric and can be completely closed by the combined effect of the DM coupling and the Zeeman field. At zero Zeeman field, the same reduction also supports antiferromagnetic Q-kinks, for which we obtain explicit profiles, charges, energies, and reduced-sector fission criteria. In the ferromagnetic case, the Berry phase makes the rotation frequency act as a shifted Zeeman field. As a result, north- and south-pole charged droplets are selected by opposite signs of the shifted rotation. We also show that a formal pole-to-pole solution of the ferromagnetic mechanical problem does not generally correspond to a finite-energy magnetic soliton, because the Berry term does not renormalize the physical Hamiltonian. These results clarify how charged-soliton mechanisms depend on the underlying magnetic dynamics, even when the static chiral energy is the same.

</details>

*Katsuhiko Nishiguchi,Toshiaki Hayashi,Kensaku Chida,Takase Shimizu,Gento Yamahata,Seiya Kasai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种全硅独立双栅存储器（all-silicon independent-dual-gate memory），其泄漏电流低于 $10^{-22}$ A，实测保持时间超过 1000 s，并借此实现存内幅度调制（amplitude-modulation, AM）解调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低泄漏存储器（ultra-low-leakage memories）在存内传感与计算中受关注，但硅存储器因存取晶体管（access transistor）泄漏，难以获得足够长的保持时间以支持模拟信号处理。

Conclusion: 该存储器为超低功耗信号处理与存内传感提供了一种与 CMOS 工艺兼容（CMOS-compatible）的平台。

Abstract: Ultra-low-leakage memories are attracting increasing attention for in-memory sensing and computing. However, achieving sufficiently long retention in silicon memories for analog signal processing remains challenging because of leakage through the access transistor. In this work, we demonstrate an all-silicon independent-dual-gate memory whose leakage current, inferred from single-electron counting statistics, is below $10^{-22}$ A, giving a measured retention time exceeding 1000 s. Owing to the extremely low leakage, the subthreshold nonlinearity of the access transistor can be exploited without disturbing the stored charge, enabling in-memory amplitude-modulation (AM) demodulation. The proposed memory provides a CMOS-compatible platform for ultra-low-power signal processing and in-memory sensing.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑磁织构（topological magnetic textures）可作为自旋流驱动的信息载体，但其运动通常伴随霍尔效应（Hall effect），使其偏离电流方向，从而限制输运效率与可控性。因此需要一种可抑制并调控该横向偏转的机制。

Method: 构建统一的自旋空间变换框架，用于研究不同螺旋度的混合自旋织构（如 skyrmion、antiskyrmion、bimeron、antibimeron）；将解析理论与微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations）相结合，并求解广义 Thiele 方程（generalized Thiele equation）。

Result: 广义 Thiele 方程的解析解表明螺旋度可作为几何控制参数，并给出霍尔角的简洁表达式，实现霍尔角的连续调节、完全抑制以及沿任意面内方向的确定性转向；微磁模拟证实所生成的自旋织构在 Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert 动力学下保持稳定，并验证了解析预测。

Conclusion: 螺旋度被确立为拓扑磁织构可编程输运（programmable transport）的多功能控制参数，为输运效率与方向可控性提供调控途径。

Abstract: Topological magnetic textures can serve as information carriers driven by spin currents. However, their motion is generally accompanied by a Hall effect that deflects them from the current direction, limiting transport efficiency and controllability. We develop a unified spin-space transformation framework enabling a systematic study of hybrid spin textures with different helicities, such as skyrmions, antiskyrmions, bimerons, and antibimerons. Combining analytical theory with micromagnetic simulations, we establish the relation between helicity and current-driven transport. An analytical solution of the generalized Thiele equation identifies helicity as a geometric control parameter and yields a simple expression for the Hall angle, enabling its continuous tuning, complete suppression, and deterministic steering along arbitrary in-plane directions. Micromagnetic simulations confirm that the generated spin textures remain stable under Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert dynamics and validate the analytical predictions. These results establish helicity as a versatile control parameter for programmable transport of topological magnetic textures.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究动机在于系统比较三维节线半金属中无隙与有隙情形、以及不同倾斜方向下等离激元行为的差异，从而厘清能隙与倾斜对等离激元模式的独立与联合效应。现有摘要未进一步说明具体应用背景。

Conclusion: 研究提供了无隙情形与有隙情形之间、以及不同倾斜方向之间的系统性比较，刻画了三维节线半金属中等离激元模式对能隙与倾斜的响应。摘要未给出超出上述范围的具体结论或外推。

Abstract: We study plasmon modes in three-dimensional nodal-ring semimetals, considering both the gapless $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric nodal ring (PTNR) and its gapped counterpart (GNR). Working in the low-doping regime where the linearised Hamiltonian is valid, we compute the non-interacting density-density response function, the Drude weight, and the plasmon dispersion within the random-phase approximation. We treat the untilted configuration as well as in-plane tilt along $k_x$ and axial tilt along $k_z$. For the in-plane tilted GNR, a strong-enough tilt or a small enough chemical potential pushes part of the ring into a partially-gapped regime. Only part of the Fermi surface survives there. We obtain closed-form results for this regime in two limits: a small tilt and a small gap. The small-gap limit produces a fractional-power correction to the Drude weight and the plasmon frequency. For the axially-tilted GNR, the partially-gapped window has a double-valued local Fermi surface for which we obtain the density-of-states and the Drude weights in closed forms. We analyse how the tilt and the mass-gap affect the plasmon dispersion and the anisotropy between axial and in-plane directions. Our results provide a systematic comparison between the gapless and gapped cases and between different tilt directions.

</details>

*Li Ge*

TL;DR: 该文提出并证明手性边缘模式（chiral edge modes）可以不局限在带隙中，而存在于体能带（bulk band）内部，即连续谱中，称为连续谱手性边缘模式（CEMICs）。其由引入虚磁通（通过边缘非对称耦合实现）诱导产生，定域机制源于非对称耦合界面处系统与环境的能量交换所导致的 PT 对称性破缺，而非非厄米趋肤效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑物态中的手性边缘模式通常被认为位于带隙内，并随晶格动量发生形变与移动。本文意在探究这些模式能否存在于体能带内部（连续谱中），从而突破带隙这一常规限制。

Result: CEMICs 的定域并非来源于非厄米趋肤效应（non-Hermitian skin effect），而是源于非对称耦合界面处系统与环境之间的能量交换，该交换在布里渊区（Brillouin zone）中引发宇称-时间（parity-time, PT）相变。此外，在宏观极限下，CEMICs 的出现与系统尺寸无关，且由非对称耦合与其几何平均之间的黄金比例（golden ratio）决定。

Conclusion: 手性边缘模式可以存在于体能带内部的连续谱中而非仅限带隙；其定域由虚磁通诱导的 PT 相变机制主导，区别于趋肤效应，且其出现阈值由耦合的黄金比例给定并具尺寸无关性。

Abstract: Chiral edge modes in topological states of matter are routinely found inside band gaps, which may be deformed and shifted as a function of the lattice momentum. Here we show that they can exist inside a bulk band instead, i.e., in the continuum. These chiral edge modes in the continuum (CEMICs) are induced by introducing an imaginary magnetic flux to an otherwise topologically trivial two-dimensional system, realized by imposing asymmetric couplings along the edge. While the stronger couplings provide a preferred circulation direction, we find that the edge localization of CEMICs does \textit{not} originate from the non-Hermitian skin effect, which is typically associated with systems featuring asymmetric couplings and the open boundary condition. Instead, this edge localization is due to the energy exchange between the system and the environment at the asymmetric coupling junctions, which leads to a parity-time (PT) transition in the Brillouin zone. Intriguingly, the onset of CEMICs is independent of the system size in the macroscopic limit, which is given by the golden ratio between the asymmetric couplings and their geometric average.

</details>


### [44] [(Sub)nanoscale Visualization of Reconstruction-Driven Moiré Exciton Localization and Delocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34104)
*Medha Dandu,Sriram Sankar,Giovanny Espitia,Rwik Dutta,Patrick J. Hays,Daria Blach,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,James R. Chelikowsky,Seth Ariel Tongay,Peter Ercius,Jordan A. Hachtel,Mit H. Naik,Archana Raja,Sandhya Susarla*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Topological magnetization textures play a central role in modern magnetism. For many applications, spin textures with different topological charges $Q$ in the same system are particularly attractive. Although the coexistence of skyrmions and antiskyrmions ($|Q| = 1$) has been reported in inversion-symmetric magnets, extending this to high-$Q$ ($|Q| > 1$) textures with arbitrary charge remains elusive. We explore skyrmion and antiskyrmion rings as well as skyrmion bags with $|Q|$ up to 5, and characterize their nucleation mechanisms, thermal stability, and collapse pathways. We find that higher-order spin interactions (HOI), which extend the conventional bilinear-exchange Hamiltonian, are essential for stabilizing these high-$Q$ spin textures. The rings remain thermally stable at zero magnetic field. HOI substantially enhance their energy barriers while leaving their size nearly unchanged and stabilize them even in the absence of Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction. Skyrmion bags, in contrast, nucleate only in the presence of HOI. TL;DR: 通过莫尔工程（moiré engineering）在MnBi$_2$Te$_4$反铁磁薄膜中引入静电超晶格势，可在保持$PT$对称时实现量子化层霍尔电导$e^2/h$，并以弱电场驱动进入层极化量子反常霍尔相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶数层拓扑反铁磁薄膜（如MnBi$_2$Te$_4$）具有由层锁定贝里曲率（layer-locked Berry curvature）产生的非常规层霍尔效应（LHE），但其量子化版本（QLHE）在实验上仍难以实现，本文旨在提出可行方案。

Method: 提出莫尔工程方案：在MnBi$_2$Te$_4$薄膜表面附近放置莫尔控制层（moiré control layers），利用其静电超晶格势（electrostatic superlattice potential）调控体系，同时讨论单侧控制层与外加栅极电场两种情形。

Abstract: Even-layer topological antiferromagnetic thin films such as MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ exhibit an unconventional layer Hall effect (LHE) arising from a layer-locked Berry curvature. This effect manifests as a layer-polarized anomalous Hall effect that can be controlled by a vertical displacement field. However, achieving a quantized version of the LHE remains experimentally challenging. In this work, we propose that moiré engineering, using an electrostatic superlattice potential from control layers, can realize the quantum layer Hall effect (QLHE). Specifically, by placing moiré control layers in close proximity to the surfaces of a MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ thin film without breaking $PT$ symmetry, the resulting system exhibits a vanishing net Hall conductance but a quantized layer Hall conductance of $e^2/h$. A weak gate electric field then breaks $PT$ symmetry and drives the system into a layer-polarized quantum anomalous Hall (QAH) phase. Furthermore, when a single control layer is introduced on only one surface, the layer-polarized QAH emerges even in the absence of an external electric field. We identify the microscopic origin of the QLHE as an emergent layer-$U(1)$ gauge field generated by the scalar moiré potential, which in turn produces opposite periodic pseudomagnetic fields on the top and bottom layers. Our work provides a feasible pathway toward realizing the QLHE in antiferromagnetic thin films.

</details>

Conclusion: 栅极反射测量被确立为一种高度可扩展且有力的方法学（methodology），可用于探索范德华异质结（van der Waals heterostructures）中的拓扑相与内部电荷动力学；所提出的多路复用双谐振器方案可同时测量顶栅与底栅电容，以提取体系的层极化率（layer polarizability）。

Abstract: In the search for topological states in bilayer graphene, capacitance measurement methods are widely utilized in bridge type readouts or source-drain reflectometry setups. In this paper we demonstrate an alternative readout method based on gate reflectometry. We probe the capacitance of a BLG flake encapsulated between hBN and WSe$_2$ through a lumped-element LC resonator coupled to the top gate electrode of the heterostructure. The microwave readout reliably tracks the opening of a displacement field-induced band gap in the device. Compared to simultaneous DC transport measurements, the RF signal proves to be less affected by the percolating transport channels caused by sample disorder. Furthermore, we use the gate based readout to identify a small-angle alignment between the hBN and BLG flake. From the fixed frequency microwave readout, we extract the device capacitance change between the insulating and metallic state of the bilayer and utilize a comprehensive capacitance network model to explain its origin. Finally, we propose an advanced, multiplexed double-resonator readout scheme capable of simultaneously measuring top and bottom gate capacitances to extract the layer polarizability of the system. This work establishes gate reflectometry as a highly scalable and powerful methodology for exploring topological phases and internal charge dynamics in van der Waals heterostructures.

</details>

*Felix Puster,Matthias Thamm,Bernd Rosenow*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔态（fractional quantum Hall state）的元激发服从任意子统计（anyonic statistics），其交换相位（exchange phase）介于玻色子与费米子之间，是核心待测物理量。已有反点干涉仪（antidot interferometer）方案实现较复杂，需寻找更简洁、可稳定提取交换相位的实验构型。

Result: 干涉电流包含两准粒子交换的共隧穿项，对Laughlin态交换相位以θ=πν形式表现为两种电压配置下阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆振荡（Aharonov-Bohm oscillation）之间的相移。若电压引起封闭面积变化被充分屏蔽，可由稳健的相位平台差提取交换相位；若同时测量两个漏极的干涉电流，则可避开屏蔽假设，解析量化飞行时间相位（time-of-flight phase），并以受控外推获得交换相位，系统误差仅为百分之几。

Conclusion: 该三量子点接触干涉仪以简化构型复现反点干涉仪的共隧穿机制，提供了在有限温度与有限接触间距下提取任意子交换相位的可行方案，并给出一套可规避面积屏蔽假设的互补测量与外推流程，误差可控。

Abstract: The elementary excitations of fractional quantum Hall states obey anyonic statistics, described by an exchange phase $θ$ intermediate between the bosonic and fermionic values. We study an interferometer in which three chiral edge segments are pairwise connected by three quantum point contacts, so that, depending on the applied voltages, one of the segments acts as an open quantum dot: its spectrum is continuous, and tunneling through it is resonant without any gate tuning. This setup realizes the co-tunneling mechanism of a recently proposed antidot interferometer in a particularly simple setting. Using a non-equilibrium Keldysh calculation to third order in the tunneling amplitudes, we show that the interference current contains a co-tunneling contribution in which two quasiparticles are exchanged, so that for Laughlin states the exchange phase $θ=πν$ appears as the phase shift between the Aharonov-Bohm oscillations measured in two voltage configurations. We obtain a closed analytic expression for the interference current at finite temperature and finite separation between the quantum point contacts and show that, provided voltage-induced changes of the enclosed area are sufficiently screened, the exchange phase can be extracted from a robust phase-plateau difference. Alternatively, simultaneous measurements of the interference currents at two drains avoid this screening assumption and time-of-flight phases can be quantified analytically, allowing the exchange phase to be extracted by a controlled extrapolation with a few-percent systematic error.

</details>


### [51] [Dynamical transition in non-Hermitian Chern insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35492)

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了非厄米陈绝缘体（non-Hermitian Chern insulators）中波包传播的动力学相变：边缘拓扑波包的演化并非仅由拓扑陈数（Chern number）决定，而取决于常规拓扑局域化与非厄米趋肤效应（non-Hermitian skin effect）之间的竞争。
