  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在厄米陈绝缘体中，边缘波包始终沿系统边界传播；作者旨在探究非厄米性引入后该图像是否仍然成立，以及拓扑陈数能否单独决定边缘波包的动力学行为。

Method: 通过考察非厄米陈绝缘体中边缘初始化的波包演化，分析常规拓扑诱导的局域化与非厄米趋肤效应之间的竞争，并与广义布里渊区（generalized-Brillouin zone）框架下的拓扑相变预测进行比较。

Result: 与厄米情形不同，非厄米陈绝缘体中的边缘波包可能穿透进入体态（bulk）。当拓扑局域化占主导时，波包沿边界演化；反之则向体内扩散。此外，广义布里渊区框架虽能可靠预测拓扑相变，却无法单独确定动力学相变。

Conclusion: 该工作表明非厄米陈绝缘体中存在独特的动力学相变，边缘波包传播不由拓扑陈数唯一决定，且拓扑相变与动力学相变在广义布里渊区框架下并不等同。

Abstract: We unveil a peculiar dynamical transition for the propagation of wave packets in non-Hermitian Chern insulators, where the evolution of the topological wave packets at the edge is not solely determined by the topological Chern number. Unlike the Hermitian Chern insulator, where a wave packet initiated at the edge propagates along the system boundary, here it may instead penetrate into the bulk. This behavior is attributed to the competition between the localization induced by conventional topology and the non-Hermitian skin effect. Specifically, when the former dominant, the wave packet will evolve along the boundary; otherwise, it will spread into the bulk. These features demonstrate that while the generalized-Brillouin zone framework reliably predicts topological phase transitions, it does not on its own specify the dynamical transitions.

</details>


### [52] [Superfluid Spin Transport in the Van der Waals Antiferromagnet CrCl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35513)
*Peisen Yuan,Xiaomin Guo,Vincent Flynn,Benedetta Flebus,Fèlix Casanova,Luis E. Hueso*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究报告在易平面范德华反铁磁绝缘体 CrCl₃ 中通过非局域器件结构发现并调控了超流自旋输运（superfluid spin transport），在倾斜反铁磁（canted AFM）自旋构型下实现约 90 μm 的超长程弱衰减输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超流自旋（spin superfluidity）因具备相梯度驱动、超低损耗的自旋输运前景而成为自旋电子学（spintronics）前沿课题，但实验研究受限于合适材料体系的缺乏。

Method: 采用非局域器件结构，结合非局域磁振子输运（nonlocal magnon transport）测量与理论建模（theoretical modelling），对易平面反铁磁绝缘体 CrCl₃ 中的自旋输运行为进行研究。

Result: 在倾斜反铁磁自旋构型下，CrCl₃ 中呈现自旋超流，表现为约 90 μm 的超长程、弱衰减自旋输运；强磁场与升温会抑制该超流态，使非相干磁振子（incoherent magnons）恢复随距离的快速指数衰减。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了二维磁性绝缘体中自旋超流的潜力，并将 CrCl₃ 确立为下一代自旋电子学应用中高能效、长距离自旋输运的候选平台。

Abstract: Over the past decade, the quest for spin superfluidity has moved to the forefront of spintronics, driven by the promise of phase-gradient-driven, ultra-low-loss spin transport. However, experimental investigations remain limited, primarily due to the lack of suitable material systems. Here, we report on the discovery and control of a superfluid spin transport in the easy-plane van der Waals antiferromagnetic (AFM) insulator CrCl$_3$ by a nonlocal device structure. Combining nonlocal magnon transport measurements with theoretical modelling, we demonstrate that spin superfluidity emerges in CrCl$_3$ under canted AFM spin configurations, where it gives rise to ultra-long range (around 90 $μ$m), weakly decaying spin transport. We also provide direct evidence that strong magnetic fields and elevated temperatures suppress the superfluid state, restoring the rapid exponential decay with distance of incoherent magnons. These findings underscore the potential of spin superfluidity in two-dimensional magnetic insulators and establish CrCl$_3$ as a promising platform for energy-efficient, long-distance spin transport in next-generation spintronic applications.

</details>

### [53] [Hypothesis-awkward: Property-Based Testing Strategies for Awkward Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31820)
*Tai Sakuma,Ianna Osborne,Peter Elmer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Hypothesis-awkward 为 Awkward Array 提供基于性质测试（property-based testing）的策略集合，可生成覆盖广泛组合空间的嵌套变长混合类型数组，已在 Awkward Array 的持续集成中运行并发现 37 个缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理 Awkward Array 的众多工具普遍采用预定义输入样本进行单元测试，而这些样本仅能覆盖 Awkward Array 实例庞大组合空间中的一小部分，难以触发边界情况，因此需要自动化的测试数据生成手段。

Method: 基于 Python 性质测试库 Hypothesis，构建 Hypothesis-awkward 策略集合；其主策略可生成几乎全部可能的 Awkward Array，并提供对布局（layout）、数据类型（data types）、缺失值（missing values）、掩码（masks）及其他数组属性的控制选项。

Result: 使用该策略编写的性质测试已开始在 Awkward Array 的持续集成（continuous integration）中运行，目前仅覆盖 Awkward Array 的一小部分，但已发现 37 个 Awkward Array 缺陷以及 1 个 Hypothesis 自身缺陷。

Conclusion: 这些策略提升了 Awkward Array 及其使用工具测试的可靠性，并为后续扩大测试覆盖范围提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Hypothesis-awkward is a collection of Hypothesis strategies for Awkward Array. Awkward Array can represent a wide variety of nested, variable-length, mixed-type data. Many tools that process Awkward Arrays are widely used and actively developed. The unit test cases of many of these tools list predefined input samples. In practice, such samples can cover only a small portion of the vast combinatorial space of Awkward Array instances. Hypothesis, a Python property-based testing library, generates test data that can make test cases fail and automatically explores edge cases. The main strategy of Hypothesis-awkward generates nearly all possible Awkward Arrays, with options to control the layout, data types, missing values, masks, and other array attributes. Property-based tests written with these strategies have begun to run in the continuous integration of Awkward Array; to date, they cover a small part of Awkward Array and have found 37 bugs in it, as well as one in Hypothesis itself. These strategies make the tests of Awkward Array and of the tools that use it more reliable.

</details>

### [54] [DNA geometry around highly transcribed bacterial genes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34640)
*Marc Joyeux*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过布朗动力学（Brownian Dynamics）模拟与特制粗粒化（coarse-grained）模型，重现了高表达细菌DNA位点附近的拱形条纹（arched stripe）与束状结构域（bundled domain）Hi-C接触图谱，揭示其源于转录过程中扭转（twist）持续注入引起的强烈缠结DNA构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期高分辨率（500 bp）Hi-C实验在细菌高表达DNA位点附近观测到跨度近100 kbp的异常接触图谱模式（拱形条纹与束状结构域），其物理机制尚不明确，需要理论模型加以解释。

Method: 采用特制粗粒化模型开展布朗动力学模拟，模型核心特征是显式考虑转运中的聚合酶（polymerase）在其下游产生正超螺旋波（positive supercoiling）、上游产生负超螺旋波的效应。

Result: 当扭转注入速率（rate of twist injection）超过阈值时，模拟计算的接触图谱重现了拱形条纹与束状结构域模式；所得DNA构型显示其几何高度缠结，同时涉及plectonemic与toroidal两种超螺旋，部分DNA片段甚至同时具备两者。此外，聚合酶两侧的DNA片段系统性地缠绕到另一侧的片段上，该现象是持续注入扭转驱动的纯非平衡（out-of-equilibrium）效应。

Conclusion: 重复转录（repeated transcription）会系统性地使相隔数十kbp的DNA片段发生接触，这或有助于变构调节（allosteric modulation）与长程通讯（long-range communication）。

Abstract: Recent high-resolution (500 bp) Hi-C experiments reported contact maps with unusual patterns around highly expressed bacterial DNA loci. These patterns, described as arched stripes and bundled domains, extend over nearly 100 kbp. We performed Brownian Dynamics simulations with a specially designed coarse-grained model to rationalize these findings. The main feature of the model is that it takes explicitly into account the waves of positive (respectively, negative) supercoiling generated by the translocating polymerase downstream (respectively, upstream) of its position. Contact maps computed from the simulations also display the arched stripe and bundled domain patterns for above-threshold values of the rate of twist injection. Computed DNA conformations indicate that these patterns reflect an extraordinarily entangled DNA geometry, which involves both plectonemic and toroidal supercoiling, with some DNA segments experiencing both of them simultaneously. Moreover, DNA segments located on each side of the polymerase systematically wind around tracts located on the other side, which is a purely out-of-equilibrium effect driven by the continuous injection of twist. The present work therefore reveals that repeated transcription of a DNA locus systematically brings into contact DNA segments separated by several tens of kbp, which may eventually contribute to allosteric modulation and long-range communication.

</details>

### [55] [From a three-fluid junction to Young's law: Constitutive and rigid-support limits of wetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31672)
*Dieter Bothe*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文通过有序连续体（ordered continuum）构造，将刚性固体上的杨氏定律（Young's law）与三流体交汇处的诺伊曼平衡（Neumann balance）联系起来，阐明二者描述的是不同容许运动，并区分几何收敛与迁移动力学及反作用力的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杨氏定律与诺伊曼平衡传统上被视为描述不同容许运动的两个独立条件。作者希望建立统一框架，说明在可变形支撑上二者如何通过连续体构造相互关联，并厘清表面自由能与机械表面应力、位移与润湿-干燥分界迁移之间的区别。

Method: 采用有序连续体构造：仿射黏弹性（affine viscoelastic）第三相在固定模量、无松弛极限下获得永久变形记忆；独立指定材料表面定律以区分表面自由能与机械表面应力；在可变形支撑上分别给出固体材料位移与湿-干分区迁移的平衡条件。并以两类模型验证：带圆形液体界面的夹持有限应变（clamped finite-strain）模型，以及二次半空间（quadratic half-space）模型。

Result: 夹持有限应变模型显示约化能量的一致收敛，并在全局几乎极小化子（almost minimisers）处恢复空间杨氏角；二次半空间模型给出稳态迁移导数的收敛及非零极限牵引（traction）分布。锚定支撑的刚化在固定宏观观测距离处抑制位移，但保留可逆迁移，剩余变分给出杨氏定律，受约束力学则作为携带法向及一般切向毛细载荷的反作用保留。

Conclusion: 局部类诺伊曼脊（Neumann-like ridge）可与相对远壁测得的杨氏角共存。该框架将几何收敛与迁移及反作用力的控制区分开来，为杨氏定律与诺伊曼平衡提供了统一的连续体解释。

Abstract: Young's law on a rigid solid and the Neumann balance at a three-fluid junction describe different admissible motions. We connect them through an ordered continuum construction. An affine viscoelastic third phase first acquires permanent deformation memory in the no-relaxation limit at fixed modulus. Independently specified material-surface laws distinguish surface free energy from mechanical surface stress. On the deformable support, displacement of solid material and migration of the wet-dry partition give separate equilibrium conditions. Stiffening an anchored support suppresses displacement at a fixed macroscopic observation distance but leaves reversible migration possible. The surviving variation gives Young's law; the constrained mechanics remains as a reaction carrying normal and, in general, tangential capillary loads. Two complementary results substantiate this reduction. A clamped finite-strain model with circular liquid interfaces yields uniform convergence of reduced energies and recovery of the spatial Young angle for global almost minimisers. A separate quadratic half-space model gives convergence of stationary migration derivatives and a nonzero limiting traction distribution. The results explain how a local Neumann-like ridge can coexist with a Young angle measured against the distant wall, while distinguishing geometric convergence from the control of migration and reaction forces.

</details>


### [56] [Blockwise friction heterogeneity redistributes stretching in a 2D Gaussian polymer under Batchelor-Kraichnan random flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31707)
*Arpan Dey*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究以分为两个摩擦系数不同但弹性相同区块的二维高斯 Rouse 链为对象，考察摩擦异质性在静止条件和 Batchelor-Kraichnan 随机流场中对链构象与形变的影响；结果表明摩擦异质性本身即足以在力学上均匀的聚合物中重新分配形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多关注聚合物链在流场中的整体拉伸与弛豫，而链内局部摩擦不均匀性（friction heterogeneity）如何影响平衡构象与流动诱导形变的分布尚不清晰。本文旨在隔离该因素，检验在胡克弹性（Hookean elasticity）相同的前提下，仅靠摩擦差异能否改变形变的空间分配。

Method: 采用二维高斯 Rouse 链（Gaussian Rouse chain）模型，将其划分为两个相邻区块，二者局部摩擦系数不同但胡克弹性相同。先在静止条件下推导平衡构象、区块迁移率、广义弛豫谱（generalized relaxation spectrum）以及摩擦加权平动扩散系数；随后将链置于不可压 Batchelor-Kraichnan 随机流中，并在广义模坐标（generalized-mode coordinates）下分析随机应变对各模的作用，且在若干摩擦比下匹配链特定最长模 Weissenberg 数（chain-specific longest-mode Weissenberg number）进行对照。

Result: 静止条件下摩擦异质性不改变连续时间平衡高斯构象，但产生不同的区块迁移率并修正广义弛豫谱；摩擦加权平动扩散系数仍保持 \(D_\gamma\propto N^{-1}\) 的标度，仅前置因子依赖摩擦，该扩散律与广义模表述可推广至更一般的局部摩擦分布。在共同外流下，高摩擦区块比低摩擦区块更伸展，形变不对称性随摩擦对比增强，出现在弱至中等流动区间，并在最强有限时间条件下仍清晰可见；即使匹配最长模 Weissenberg 数，该效应依然存在。广义模坐标中，光滑随机应变不直接混合模指数，而优先放大最慢模。较大流速下伸展分布显著变宽并呈现明显时间漂移；由于所有模拟的最长模 Weissenberg 数均低于 1，该漂移被解释为向线圈-拉伸阈值（coil-stretch threshold）附近宽幂律尾平稳胡克区的缓慢收敛。

Conclusion: 摩擦异质性单独即可在力学上原本均匀的聚合物中重新分配形变，且该效应不能仅由全局弛豫时间尺度的移动来解释。该结论提示局部摩擦差异是调控聚合物流动形变的独立机制，所建立的摩擦加权扩散律与广义模框架可拓展至一般局部摩擦分布情形。

Abstract: We study a two-dimensional Gaussian Rouse chain divided into two contiguous blocks with different local friction coefficients but identical Hookean elasticity. In quiescent conditions, friction heterogeneity leaves the continuous-time equilibrium Gaussian conformation unchanged while producing distinct block mobilities and a modified generalized relaxation spectrum. The exact friction-weighted translational diffusion coefficient retains the scaling $D_γ\propto N^{-1}$, with a friction-dependent prefactor; the corresponding diffusion law and generalized-mode formulation extend beyond the diblock to more general local friction profiles. We then place the chain in an incompressible Batchelor-Kraichnan random flow. Under a common external flow, the high-friction block becomes more extended than the low-friction block. The deformation asymmetry strengthens with friction contrast and develops across the weak- to moderate-flow regime, remaining clearly visible at the strongest finite-time conditions. It also persists when the chain-specific longest-mode Weissenberg number is matched across friction ratios, showing that, within the same finite-time protocol, the effect is not accounted for solely by a shift of the global relaxation timescale. In generalized-mode coordinates, the smooth random strain does not directly mix mode indices and preferentially amplifies the slowest mode. At larger flow strengths, extension distributions broaden markedly and exhibit pronounced temporal drift over the simulated time window. Since all simulated chain-specific longest-mode Weissenberg numbers remain below unity, this drift is interpreted as slow convergence toward the broad, power-law-tailed stationary Hookean regime near the coil-stretch threshold. Overall, friction heterogeneity alone is sufficient to redistribute deformation along an otherwise mechanically uniform polymer.

</details>


### [57] [A Robust "Shrink-and-wrap" Piecewise Construction Transforms 3-Dimensional Mesh Structures into Mechanical Metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31761)
*Fnu Braminder,Issac Varghese,Noah Kim,Alyssa Thomas DeCruz,Eric A. Josephs*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出“shrink-and-wrap”变换方法：对任何可分解为三角形或四面体网格（triangular/tetrahedral meshes）的结构施加简单变换，在单纯形（simplexes）内部引入反向旋转多边形/多面体机制，从而保证负泊松比（negative Poisson's ratio）并实现局部可编程力学性能，可处理从凸多面体到手机视频重建的任意3D对象，且保持3D打印可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力学超材料（mechanical metamaterials）如负泊松比材料（auxetics）在自然界罕见，但其设计困难，尤其在三维尺度上；现有设计多局限于较简单的晶体、点阵或重复图案架构，且单元胞（unit cell）大多相同，难以推广到任意复杂结构。

Method: 针对任何可分解为三角形或四面体网格的结构，施加“shrink-and-wrap”变换，在三角形或四面体单纯形（simplexes）内部构造反向旋转的多边形/多面体机制（counter-rotating polygon/polyhedron mechanism），该机制与各单纯形自身几何无关，从而同时保证负泊松比并提供局部可编程的力学性能。方法依次应用于简单凸多面体、由构造实体几何（constructive solid geometry, CSG）与3D扫描设计的大量顶点结构，以及由手机摄像头视频生成（3D scanning / 视频重建）的3D对象。

Result: 所生成的单体式（monolithic）负泊松比结构可分解自简单凸多面体、CSG与3D扫描设计的任意多顶点结构，以及手机视频重建的3D对象，表明现实世界中任意设计或存在的物体与几何结构均可转化为力学超材料；这些结构仍可通过增材制造（additive manufacturing）技术方便地3D打印。

Conclusion: 该分片式（piecewise）设计方法可用于构建任意复杂的力学超材料结构，有望支持需要在其三维几何中实现可编程内部重配置（programmable internal reconfigurations）和/或力重定向（force redirection）的多种潜在应用。

Abstract: Mechanical metamaterials are materials that, by virtue of their microstructural architectures, exhibit mechanical properties not often found in nature: for example, auxetic mechanical metamaterials are materials that possess a negative Poisson's ratio in response to deforming forces. However, outside of relatively simple crystalline, lattice-based, or repeating-pattern architectures composed of mostly identical unit cells, mechanical metamaterials like auxetics are notoriously difficult to design, particularly across all three dimensions. Here we show that, for any structure that can be decomposed into triangular or tetrahedral meshes, those structures can be subjected to a simple transformation that that creates conditions within those triangular or tetrahedral simplexes that, regardless of their individual geometries, introduces a counter-rotating polygon/polyhedron mechanism that both guarantees a negative Poisson ratio and allows locally programmable mechanical properties. We apply this "shrink-and-wrap" construction to generate mechanical metamaterials from increasingly complex 3D structures, first from simple (convex) polyhedrons; then, to designs with arbitrarily large numbers of vertices and designed using constructive solid geometry (CSG) and 3D scanning; and, ultimately to 3D objects generated from videos captured by mobile phone camera -- making potentially any object or geometric structure designed or found in the real world transformable into a mechanical metamaterial. We show that these monolithic auxetic constructions remain readily 3D-printable via additive manufacturing techniques. We expect this piecewise approach to designing arbitrarily complex mechanical metamaterial structures can enable numerous potential applications where programmable internal reconfigurations and/or force redirection are required across and throughout their 3D geometries.

</details>


### [58] [Mechanical Clustering of Cells via the ExtraCellular Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31860)
*Ran Glinowiecki,Shahar Goren,Oren Tchaicheeyan,Bar Ergaz,Robin L. B. Selinger,Yair Shokef,Ayelet Lesman*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究结合实验与有限元模型（finite-element model）证明，收缩性细胞（contractile cells）通过重塑细胞外基质（extracellular matrix, ECM）形成长程定向致密纤维带，将细胞力学耦联为多细胞团簇；在仅约10%的收缩性颗粒体积分数下即出现由高浓度应力带介导的内部硬化（internal stiffening），其行为强依赖ECM的非线性力学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织（tissues）由活细胞与细胞外基质（ECM）复合而成，两者共同决定其独特力学行为，但细胞如何通过介质变形实现长程力学耦联并影响整体力学性质尚需量化。研究旨在揭示收缩性细胞重塑ECM所导致的宏观力学改变机制。

Method: 实验上展示收缩性细胞生成长程定向致密纤维带，将细胞力学耦联为多细胞团簇；随后采用有限元模型（finite-element model）量化不同细胞体积分数下由ECM带介导的集体力学相互作用。模型中引入模拟细胞主动力的收缩性颗粒（contractile particles），置于非线性生物凝胶（nonlinear biological gel）内，通过内部收缩引发硬化（stiffening）而非外部加载；并对带网络进行渗流分析（percolation analysis）。

Result: 即使收缩性颗粒体积分数很小（约10%），远低于球体接触渗流（percolation of sphere contacts）的预期阈值，仍可产生内部硬化，该效应由细胞间高浓度应力带（stress bands）介导，并强依赖ECM的非线性力学响应。带网络的渗流分析显示在临界颗粒体积分数处出现突变，且该转变与内部硬化的起始相吻合。

Conclusion: 收缩性细胞通过介质变形的长程相互作用，可全局性重塑块体凝胶（bulk gel）的结构与力学，形成具有改变性质的功能性结构。此类宏观改变可为组织图案化（tissue patterning）与形态发生（morphogenesis）提供组织原理。

Abstract: Tissues are composites of living cells and extracellular matrix (ECM), that jointly determine their unique mechanical behavior. Here, we experimentally demonstrate that contractile cells remodel the ECM by generating long-range bands of aligned and densified fibers that mechanically couple cells into multicellular clusters. Using a finite-element model, we quantify the collective mechanical interactions mediated by these ECM bands for various volume fractions of cells. The model incorporates contractile particles that mimic cell-active forces within a nonlinear biological gel, inducing stiffening internally rather than through external loading. We find that even a small volume fraction of contractile particles (approximately 10%), much below what is expected for percolation of sphere contacts, leads to internal stiffening, mediated by the high-concentrated stress bands between cells and is strongly dependent on the nonlinear mechanical response of the ECM. Percolation analysis of the band network reveals sharp transitions at a critical particle volume fraction, that aligns with the onset of internal stiffening. These results demonstrate that long-range interaction between contractile cells through the deformation of the medium has the potential to globally shape the structure and mechanics of the bulk gel, leading to a functional structure with modified properties. These modified macroscopic changes can define organizing principles in tissue patterning and morphogenesis.

</details>


### [59] [Domain growth in lipid membranes and the budding instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32951)
*Jean Wolff,Fabrice Thalmann,Carlos Marques,David Andelman,Haim Diamant,Shigeyuki Komura*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究用守恒的含时Ginzburg-Landau模型描述相分离脂质膜中圆畴的径向生长，发现畴尺寸从早期D∼t^{1/3}过渡到后期D∼t^{1/2}并饱和，同时线张力随时间上升；结合准静态失稳判据可得，出芽不稳定性时间随自发曲率增大而缩短、在临界点附近延长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清相分离脂质膜中圆畴生长动力学与出芽不稳定性（budding instability）之间的关系，特别是畴尺寸与线张力（line tension）的演化如何共同决定出芽所需时间。

Method: 在径向对称假设下，采用守恒的含时Ginzburg-Landau模型（time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau model）描述面内动力学，通过数值求解获得畴尺寸D(t)；由演化的浓度剖面计算含时线张力σ(t)，并将其与部分出芽畴失稳的准静态判据（quasistatic criterion）结合以估计出芽不稳定性。

Result: 畴尺寸D随时间由早期D∼t^{1/3}过渡至后期D∼t^{1/2}，随后因有限系统尺寸而饱和；σ(t)先快速上升后趋近平面界面值，从而区分出早期线张力主导区与后期畴尺寸主导区。出芽不稳定性时间随膜自发曲率（spontaneous curvature）增大而减小，在临界点附近则增大。

Conclusion: 畴尺寸与线张力的共同演化是确定出芽不稳定性所需时间的关键，二者缺一不可。

Abstract: We investigate the growth of a circular domain in a phase-separating lipid membrane and its relation to the budding instability. We describe the in-plane dynamics with a conserved, time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau model under radial symmetry. Numerical solutions show a crossover of the domain size $D$ as a function of time $t$ from $D\sim t^{1/3}$ at early times to $D\sim t^{1/2}$ at later times, followed by saturation due to finite system size. The time-dependent line tension $σ(t)$, evaluated from the evolving concentration profile, rises rapidly before approaching the planar-interface value. This behavior distinguishes an early line-tension-dominated regime from a later domain-size-dominated regime. To estimate the budding instability, we combine $D(t)$ and $σ(t)$ with a quasistatic criterion for the loss of stability of a partially budded domain. The budding-instability time decreases with increasing membrane spontaneous curvature and increases near the critical point. Thus, the evolution of both domain size and line tension is essential for determining the time required for the budding instability.

</details>


### [60] [On mechanistically accessible copolymer sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33784)
*Richard Golnik,Nicola Vassena,Alex Blokhuis*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出一种理论，将影响单体并入的最近邻数 k 与共聚物序列空间（sequence space）建立联系，发现反应机理可与度为 k 的正则 de Bruijn 图（regular de Bruijn graph）一一映射，并导出周期性序列数量的爆炸式增长规律及若干描述允许序列的简单法则（Simple Assembly Rules, SAR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物化学中的结构观察常被还原为单一机理层面的解释，而对机理族（families of mechanisms）及其伴随的唯象与定律式行为研究较少。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立机理特征（最近邻数 k）与可及共聚物序列空间之间的理论联系。

Method: 将反应机理与度为 k 的正则 de Bruijn 图进行一一映射，计算周期性共聚物序列随邻居指数 k 与单体数 s 的变化；推导周期性序列数量的下界 s!^{s^{k-1}}/s^k；并提出简单组装规则（SAR），刻画其可及的 k-聚合类型（k≤2），结合配位化学（coordination chemistry）与超分子化学（supramolecular chemistry）文献中的实例加以说明。

Result: 周期性序列数量随 s、k 呈爆炸式增长，其下界为 s!^{s^{k-1}}/s^k；周期性 k-聚合受若干简单法则支配，涉及允许序列、特定反应数目、重复单元长度，以及嵌段共聚物（block copolymers）中允许与不允许序列的共存。SAR 可捕捉文献体系中常见行为与设计，并刻画其可及的 k-聚合（k≤2）与对称性的关系。

Conclusion: 研究讨论了形成非平凡（k>1）序列的化学-结构贡献，强调空间位阻（steric hindrance）、长程相互作用（long-range interactions）与自由度（degrees of freedom）的重要性。

Abstract: Structural observations in polymer chemistry are often rationalized through underlying mechanistic insights. The study of families of mechanisms - and the phenomenology and lawlike behavior that can accompany them - is less common. Here we construct a theory that links a mechanistic feature - the number of nearest neighbors $k$ that influence monomer incorporation - with the accessible copolymer sequence space.
  We find that the reaction mechanisms can be mapped 1-to-1 to a regular de Bruijn graph of degree $k$. We calculate the periodic copolymer sequences as function of neighbor index $k$, and number of monomers $s$. We show that the number of periodic sequences grows explosively with $s,k$, bounded from below by $s!^{s^{k-1}}/s^k$. We find that periodic k-polymerization is governed by several simple laws describing allowed sequences, number of specific reactions, repeating unit length and the allowed and disallowed sequence coexistence in block copolymers.
  We formulate Simple Assembly Rules (SAR) that capture commonly observed behavior and design of literature systems, and characterize the possible k-polymerizations they are allowed to access ($k\leq2$) as function of symmetries and illustrate our findings with reported examples from the coordination chemistry and supramolecular chemistry literature.
  We discuss chemical-structural contributions by which nontrivial ($k>1$) sequences are formed, highlighting the importance steric hindrance, long-range interactions and degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [61] [Charge Regulation Mediated Interaction of Amphoteric Nanoparticle Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33793)
*Saurav Tyagi,Kamal Tripathi,Saikat Chakraborty,Sunita Kumari*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过显式离子分子动力学/电荷调控蒙特卡洛模拟，揭示电荷调控（charge regulation）会显著改变纳米颗粒间的静电力，甚至改变相互作用符号，而非仅是微小修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可电离生物分子及其对应物能响应pH、离子强度或邻近带电物种的静电相互作用而调整电离状态，但常用的恒电荷近似（constant charge approximation）是否足以描述纳米尺度静电力尚不明确。

Method: 采用显式离子分子动力学耦合电荷调控蒙特卡洛模拟（explicit-ion molecular dynamics / charge-regulation Monte Carlo simulations），计算两个球形电荷调控纳米颗粒间相互作用力随间距的变化，并对比对称（化学相同）与非对称（化学互补）表面构型在恒电荷近似下的结果。

Result: 对非对称纳米颗粒对，电荷调控在低电解质浓度下产生比恒电荷近似强至一个数量级的短程吸引；对对称化学相同颗粒，随离子强度降低，电荷调控驱动相互作用由净排斥转变为净弱吸引，而恒电荷假设下不存在该转变。

Conclusion: 电荷调控并非微小修正，可同时改变纳米尺度静电相互作用的强度与符号，对电荷调控胶体体系的组装与稳定性具有重要含义。

Abstract: Charge regulation refers to the ability of ionizable biomolecules and their counterparts to adjust their ionization state in response to external perturbations, including changes in pH, ionic strength, or electrostatic interactions with neighboring charged species. Here, we use explicit-ion molecular dynamics / charge-regulation Monte Carlo simulations to compute the interaction force between two spherical, charge-regulated nanoparticles as a function of separation, and compare symmetric (chemically identical) and asymmetric (chemically complementary) surface configurations against the constant charge approximation. We find that charge regulation produces up to an order-of-magnitude stronger short-range attraction than constant charge for asymmetric nanoparticle pairs, particularly at low electrolyte concentration, and that charge regulation qualitatively alters the interaction between symmetric, chemically identical nanoparticles, driving a crossover from net repulsion to net weak-attraction as ionic strength decreases. This transition is not observed under the constant charge assumption which shows that charge regulation is not a minor correction but can change both the strength and the sign of nanoscale electrostatic interactions, with implications for the assembly and stability of charge-regulated colloidal systems

</details>


### [62] [Mobility-network topology carries incremental, softness-orthogonal information about future aging dynamics in a glass-forming liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34022)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究探究玻璃态液体中粒子迁移率（mobility）的空间组织是否携带超出局部结构描述符的预测信息。在无泄漏（leakage-free）的老化 Kob–Andersen 液体模拟中，迁移率网络拓扑（mobility-network topology）在软度类别基线之上将折外 R²（out-of-fold R²）提升 ΔR²=0.0049–0.0122，且随淬火深度单调增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是检验粒子迁移率的空间组织能否在局部结构（local structure）已知信息之外预测未来运动，即识别仅靠单粒子描述符无法捕捉的额外预测通道。

Method: 采用无泄漏（leakage-free）的老化 Kob–Andersen 液体（Kob–Andersen liquid）模拟，规模为 N=2028、200 条轨迹、三个温度，并以软度类别（softness-class）基线作为对照，比较迁移率网络拓扑（mobility-network topology）的增量预测能力。

Result: 迁移率网络拓扑使折外 R²（out-of-fold R²）提升 ΔR²=0.0049–0.0122，且随淬火深度（quench depth）单调增加，在软度类别基线上仍保留 ≥98% 的增益。

Conclusion: 巨型可动组分（giant mobile components）是致密热点（dense hotspots），其成员随后趋于安静，构成协同运动的“耗尽区”（exhaustion zones）——一种对单粒子描述符不可见的空间反相关（spatial anticorrelation）通道。

Abstract: Does the spatial organization of particle mobility in a glass predict future motion beyond what local structure knows? In leakage-free simulations of an aging Kob--Andersen liquid ($N=2028$; 200 trajectories; three temperatures), mobility-network topology raises the out-of-fold $R^2$ by $\dRtwo=0.0049$--$0.0122$, monotonically with quench depth, retaining $\ge 98\%$ atop softness-class baselines. Giant mobile components are dense hotspots whose members subsequently quiet down: exhaustion zones of cooperative motion --- a spatial anticorrelation channel invisible to single-particle descriptors.

</details>


### [63] [A roadmap for polymer informatics super-intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34051)
*Akhlak Mahmood,Janhavi Nistane,Huan Tran,Chiho Kim,Rampi Ramprasad*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出通往聚合物超级智能设计系统的路线图，核心为模块化、智能体（agent）驱动的架构，通过"聚合物超级智能层"以自然语言解读设计问题并协调领域专用工具，覆盖从分子设计到产品性能的完整链条；生成式设计、合成可行性推理与实用性评估已构成自驱动聚合物实验室的决策核心，自主闭环实验为尚待突破的关键步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物信息学（polymer informatics）已从孤立的性质预测研究发展为整合数据、模型与决策的综合学科，但仍未达到真正的智能系统水平。现有系统欠缺按需逆向设计（inverse design）、跨化学—加工—性能的因果推理（causal reasoning）以及闭环自主实验（closed-loop autonomous experimentation）能力，本文旨在提出弥补这些差距的路线图。

Method: 基于开发两个互补的智能体与信息学平台（agentic and informatics platforms）的实践经验，提出模块化、智能体主导的架构。该架构由聚合物超级智能层（polymer super-intelligence layer）解析研究者的自然语言设计问题，并协调与可用数据相匹配的领域专用工具，覆盖纯聚合物（neat polymers）、复合材料与配方、溶剂以及合成与加工等方向。

Result: 系统可贯通从分子设计、加工到产品级性能与人类感知的完整链条。其生成式设计、合成可行性推理（synthesis-feasibility reasoning）与实用性评估经协同编排，已构成自驱动聚合物实验室（self-driving polymer laboratory）的决策核心，仅剩自主闭环实验为主要待完成步骤。文中还综述了该路线图上的新兴能力，包括文献性质数据自动抽取、化学感知表征（chemistry-aware representation）、膜与可持续塑料的性质预测、溶解度与绿色溶剂推荐，以及计算机引导的逆合成规划（retrosynthetic planning）。

Conclusion: 从当前的编排式工具生态（orchestrated tool ecosystem）迈向真正的超级智能聚合物设计伙伴，仍存在若干差距，需要相应的研究与基础设施投入。自主闭环实验是从现有系统走向完整智能系统的关键剩余步骤。

Abstract: Polymer informatics has matured from isolated property-prediction studies into an integrated discipline that couples data, models, and decision-making across the polymer design cycle. Yet it still falls short of a true intelligent system capable of inverse design on demand, causal reasoning across chemistry, processing, and performance, and closed-loop autonomous experimentation. This article traces a roadmap toward that goal, grounded in experience developing two complementary agentic and informatics platforms. Central to this vision is a modular, agent-directed architecture in which a polymer super-intelligence layer interprets a researcher's design question in natural language and coordinates domain-specialized tools, matched to the available data, for neat polymers, composites and formulations, solvents, and synthesis and processing. The resulting system spans the full chain from molecular design through processing to product-level performance and human perception. Orchestrated together, its generative design, synthesis-feasibility reasoning, and practicality assessment already form the decision-making core of a self-driving polymer laboratory, leaving autonomous, closed-loop experimentation as the principal step that remains. We survey emerging capabilities along this roadmap, including automated extraction of property data from the literature, chemistry-aware representation, property prediction for membranes and sustainable plastics, solubility and green-solvent recommendation, and computer-guided retrosynthetic planning, exposing the remaining gaps and the research and infrastructure investments needed to move from today's orchestrated tool ecosystem toward a genuinely super-intelligent polymer design partner.

</details>


### [64] [Cell division sets a universal flow geometry in cell layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34631)
*Tianxiang Ma,Lasse Bonn,Valeriia Grudtsyna,Nigar Abbasova,Martin Cramer Pedersen,Nuno A. M. Araujo,Amin Doostmohammadi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 细胞分裂（cell division）通过周期性的拓扑更新（topological renewal）调控上皮组织（epithelial tissues）集体流动中涡旋界面（vortical interfaces）的共形不变性（conformal invariance）与可重排能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上皮组织集体流动具有涡旋界面的几何骨架，其统计特征呈现临界渗流（critical percolation）与共形不变性（conformal invariance）的特征，但基本细胞过程如何导致这种对称性丰富的流动几何发生破坏尚不明确。

Method: 采用药理学扰动（pharmacological perturbations）在两条独立通路上抑制增殖，并阻断凋亡（apoptosis）以分离变量；同时构建具有可调分裂（tunable division）的顶点模型（vertex model）进行定量复现与验证。

Result: 增殖受抑时相干流动（coherent flows）仍持续，但邻居交换（neighbor exchanges）下降且共形不变几何消失；阻断凋亡不影响普适性，从而将分裂确定为关键控制因素。允许分裂的顶点模型可定量再现上述效应并恢复共形不变性。

Conclusion: 分裂作为间歇性的拓扑更新，松弛网络约束，维持细胞层的几何与力学柔性及跨尺度重排能力；细胞分裂因此不仅是生长的经典角色，更是集体自组织（collective self-organization）的结构与力学调控者，将基本生物过程与涌现物理对称性直接联系起来。

Abstract: Collective flows in epithelial tissues contain a geometric backbone of vortical interfaces whose statistics exhibit hallmarks of critical percolation and conformal invariance. Yet how fundamental cellular processes govern the breakdown of such symmetry-rich flow geometry remains unclear. Here we show that cell division plays a central physical role in regulating this universal flow organization by controlling both the geometric and mechanical flexibility of the cell-cell network. Using pharmacological perturbations, we find that when proliferation is suppressed through two independent interventions, coherent flows persist but neighbor exchanges decline and conformally invariant geometry is lost. Blocking apoptosis does not affect universality, isolating division as the key control. A vertex model with tunable division quantitatively reproduces these effects and restores conformal invariance when division is allowed. We further trace this effect to changes in both the geometric and mechanical organization of the cell layer: Divisions act as intermittent topological renewals that loosen constraints, preserving the network's flexibility and capacity to rearrange across scales. Thus, beyond its canonical role in growth, cell division acts as a structural and mechanical regulator of collective self-organization. These findings establish a direct connection between fundamental biological processes and emergent physical symmetries.

</details>


### [65] [Effects of chain stiffness on the breakdown of the Cox-Merz rule in linear and ring polymer melts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34723)
*Keishin Tsujino,Yuhi Sakamaki,Shota Goto,Kang Kim,Nobuyuki Matubayasi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过Kremer-Grest模型的粗粒化分子动力学模拟，系统考察了线性与环形聚合物熔体中Cox-Merz规则的有效性。结果表明该规则的适用性强烈依赖链长、链刚性与分子拓扑结构：短链及柔性链符合良好，长而刚的线形链偏离显著，而环形链偏离较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cox-Merz规则是联系线性黏弹性复数黏度与稳态剪切黏度的经验关系，但其在链长、链刚性及分子构型变化下的适用边界尚不明确。本研究旨在系统检验该规则在聚合物熔体中的有效范围。

Method: 采用Kremer-Grest模型对粗粒化聚合物熔体（coarse-grained polymer melts）进行分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations），针对线形（linear）与环形（ring）聚合物，系统改变链长与链刚性，比较复数黏度幅值|η*(ω)|与稳态剪切黏度η(γ̇)在对应角频率ω与剪切速率γ̇下的关系。

Result: 短链及柔性链中|η*(ω)|与η(γ̇)吻合良好；线形聚合物随链长与刚性增加偏差加剧，在高对应ω与γ̇下|η*(ω)|高于η(γ̇)，这与强稳态剪切下流动诱导的链伸展与取向改变了缠结约束有关。环形聚合物在所考察范围内吻合程度明显更好，Cox-Merz规则的失效更弱。

Conclusion: Cox-Merz规则的适用性取决于链长、链刚性与分子拓扑结构。当稳态剪切下的分子响应显著偏离近平衡线性黏弹性所表征的松弛行为时，复数黏度无法准确预测稳态剪切黏度，导致该规则出现明显失效。

Abstract: The Cox-Merz rule is an empirical relation between the magnitude of the complex viscosity, $\lvertη^*(ω)\rvert$, obtained from linear viscoelasticity, and the steady-shear viscosity, $η(\dotγ)$. For linear polymers, however, the discrepancy between the two viscosities becomes increasingly pronounced with increasing chain length and chain stiffness, with $\lvertη^*(ω)\rvert$ exceeding $η(\dotγ)$ at high corresponding values of $ω$ and $\dotγ$. In contrast, ring polymers retain substantially better agreement between $\lvertη^*(ω)\rvert$ and $η(\dotγ)$ with increasing chain length and stiffness within the range examined here, indicating a weaker breakdown of the Cox-Merz rule. These results demonstrate that the applicability of the Cox-Merz rule depends strongly on chain length, chain stiffness, and molecular architecture. When the molecular response under steady shear deviates substantially from the relaxation behavior characterized by linear viscoelasticity near equilibrium, the complex viscosity can no longer accurately predict the steady-shear viscosity, resulting in a pronounced breakdown of the Cox-Merz rule.

First, the characteristic pattern length scale is controlled by the sensing radius and remains approximately independent of the size ratio. Second, the total area occupied by the particles decreases with increasing size ratio. Third, the active fraction barely depends on the size ratio. These results demonstrate how both particle size and motility can couple microscopic regulation to large-scale pattern formation. I conclude by discussing possible extensions of the model.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 对 Landau–de Gennes 框架作渐近分析，其中 ε 表示向列相干长度与特征区域尺度之比。将 Q 张量系统分解为三个相互正交的子空间：单轴标量、几何倾斜矢量与横向各向异性张量，并逐阶求解。

Result: Oseen–Frank 导向场 n₀ 的领头 O(ε) 修正由非零倾斜场 p₁ 主导；p₁ 服从线性 Jacobi 方程 J_{n₀}(p₁)=0，并带非平凡、锚定驱动的 Dirichlet 边界条件。在 O(ε²) 阶，单轴标量与横向各向异性张量受到被动诱导的 off-shell 修正。直至 O(ε²)，倾斜仅通过表面项进入能量，而非体项。

Conclusion: 表面锚定能保持在物理标度时，边界会释放宏观变化的软模，为系统提供降低能量的途径。在刚性 Dirichlet 条件基准下该响应被完全抹除，表明建立在无限能量壁垒上的修正会掩盖锚定驱动倾斜模的底层物理。

Abstract: An asymptotic analysis of the Landau--de Gennes framework is performed to compute higher-order corrections to the Oseen--Frank limit in a bounded three-dimensional domain under appropriately scaled surface anchoring energy. A systematic decomposition of the $\mathbf{Q}$-tensor into three mutually orthogonal subspaces-the uniaxial scalar, geometric tilt vector, and transverse anisotropy tensor-reveals that the leading $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon)$ correction to the Oseen--Frank director field $\mathbf{n}_0(\mathbf{x})$ is dominated by a non-vanishing tilt field $\mathbf{p}_1(\mathbf{x})$, where $\varepsilon$ represents the ratio of the nematic coherence length to the characteristic domain size. This macroscopic variation constitutes a soft mode released from the boundary once the surface anchoring energy is retained at its physical scaling rather than driven to an infinite strength. We show that this tilt field is governed by the linear Jacobi equation, $\mathcal{J}_{\mathbf{n}_0}(\mathbf{p}_1)=\mathbf{0}$, subject to a non-trivial, anchoring-driven Dirichlet boundary condition, where $\mathcal{J}_{\mathbf{n}_0}$ is the on-shell Jacobi operator of the harmonic map $\mathbf{n}_0$ on $\mathbb{S}^2$. The two fields are accompanied at $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^2)$ by an off-shell correction to both the uniaxial scalar and the transverse anisotropy tensor, passively induced by the elastic non-uniformity $(\nabla\mathbf{n}_0\neq\mathbf{0})$ and the boundary-driven tilt $(\mathbf{p}_1\neq\mathbf{0})$. Through $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^2)$, the tilt enters the energy only through surface terms, not the bulk, providing a pathway for the system to lower its energy . Under the conventional benchmark of rigid Dirichlet conditions, this response is annihilated outright, demonstrating that corrections built upon infinite energy barriers obscure the underlying physics of anchoring-driven tilt modes.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于以最大无知（maximal-ignorance）处方，仅用 S-模不变性约束来构造随机矩阵理论，从而为在计算机上求解与三维量子引力（3D quantum gravity）对偶的矩阵/张量模型（matrix/tensor model）提供第一步。

Method: 方法为：对 S-模不变性约束在固定宽度能量窗口上进行粗粒化（coarse-graining）正则化，导出单随机矩阵势能，使其本征值扮演 2d CFT 原初场标度维数角色；并通过蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）检验行为，同时识别现象出现的标度极限（scaling limit）。

Result: 结果显示：该势能驱动粗粒化态密度在黑洞阈值（black hole threshold）能隙之上趋向 Cardy 剖面，模拟与之一致；邻近本征值则表现出 GUE（高斯酉系综，Gaussian Unitary Ensemble）统计。标度极限下呈现层级结构：宏观尺度上占据数冻结、自举约束（bootstrap constraint）刚性固定谱为 Cardy 剖面；介观尺度上 Vandermonde 斥力（Vandermonde repulsion）如随机矩阵理论般重排本征值；仅微观平均能级间距尺度可分辨单个能级。

Conclusion: 结论为：该随机矩阵模型表现出由粗粒化窗口能量宽度倒数设定的有效 Thouless 时间（Thouless time），并作为在计算机上求解三维量子引力对偶矩阵/张量模型的第一步。

Abstract: We construct and study a ``Cardy random matrix model'': a random matrix theory whose only input, in a maximal-ignorance prescription, is the constraint of $\mathbb{S}$-modular invariance. We regularize this constraint by coarse-graining over energy windows of fixed width, and obtain a potential for a single random matrix whose eigenvalues play the role of the scaling dimensions of primaries in a 2d CFT. The potential drives the coarse-grained density of states to the Cardy profile above a gap at the black hole threshold. We perform Monte Carlo simulations to confirm this behavior and find good agreement for the coarse-grained density of states, while at the same time revealing GUE statistics for nearby eigenvalues. We identify the scaling limit in which these phenomena emerge. In this limit, the model realizes a hierarchy of scales: at the macroscopic scale given by the window size, the occupations are frozen and the bootstrap constraint rigidly fixes the spectrum to the Cardy profile, while at the mesoscopic scale (inside a window) the Vandermonde repulsion rearranges the eigenvalues as in a random matrix theory. It is only at the microscopic scale of the mean level spacing that the individual levels are resolved. Accordingly, our random matrix model displays an effective Thouless time, set by the inverse energy width of the coarse-graining window. Our model provides a first step towards solving the matrix/tensor model dual to 3D quantum gravity on a computer.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a supersymmetric localization formula for the local perturbative contribution to the M-theory path integral, reducing it to an integral over equivariant flux data. This gives the first closed form expression for the complete perturbative ABJM partition function at general squashing and chemical potentials, and new analytic results for the ABJM topologically twisted index describing black holes. Applied to the non-toric $V^{5,2}$ theory, the same prescription yields a closed analytic Airy constant previously known only numerically. Our formulas reproduce all available independent field theory results, and provide analytic predictions at general parameters where no field theory answer is currently known. We derive an all-genus result for the Type IIA expansion which is an equivariant form of the usual topological string constant map result, and also extract integrated correlators.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In holography, analytically continued finite temperature correlation functions $G(t)$ exhibit signatures of the black hole singularity. Abstract: We present and study a family of exact Taub-NUT-AdS solutions with scalar hair in four-dimensional ${\cal N}=2$ gauged supergravity. Method: 基于二形式规范场 $C_2$ 与三形式规范场 $A_3$ 之间的 Stückelberg 耦合（Stückelberg coupling）构建高阶形式理论，要求弦世界面（string worldsheet）为壁世界体（wall worldvolume）的边界；随后推广至非线性、多分支（multi-branch）高阶形式理论。

Result: 在二次对偶有效理论（quadratic dual effective theory）中，有质量高阶形式模的交换产生平行壁之间的排斥 Yukawa 相互作用（Yukawa interaction），其系数由对偶性（duality）确定，所得到的 CSL 晶格间距（lattice spacing）与精确 Sine–Gordon 结果吻合良好且无需参数拟合；非线性多分支理论重现了精确的稀薄壁–壁相互作用系数、周期 CSL 及其能量极小化条件，以及无能隙 CSL 声子（phonon）。

Conclusion: CSL 声子被识别为周期性高阶形式背景的集体位移（collective displacement），标量绕卷（scalar winding）被重新解释为非线性高阶形式理论的分支流动（branch flow）。该框架为延展源与周期性孤子结构提供了统一的对偶描述。

Abstract: The chiral soliton lattice (CSL) is conventionally described as a periodic winding configuration of a compact scalar field. Higher-form gauge invariance requires the string worldsheet to be the boundary of the wall worldvolume. In the quadratic dual effective theory, exchange of the massive higher-form mode generates a repulsive Yukawa interaction between parallel walls. With its coefficient fixed by duality, the resulting CSL lattice spacing agrees well with the exact Sine--Gordon result without parameter fitting. We then extend the description to a nonlinear, multi-branch higher-form theory. It reproduces the exact dilute wall--wall interaction coefficient, the periodic CSL and its energy-minimization condition, as well as the gapless CSL phonon. The phonon is identified as a collective displacement of the periodic higher-form background, while scalar winding is reinterpreted as branch flow of the nonlinear higher-form theory.

</details>

TL;DR: 论文引入基于柯西核（Cauchy kernels）的有限平面诊断工具，利用Pick矩阵（Pick matrix）正性构建有限节点bootstrap，揭示正则多割（regular multicut）拓扑下低矩在矩空间中的嵌入方式，并重新导出四次与六次模型的已知平面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hermitian单矩阵模型的平面环方程（planar loop equations）会留下有限多个低矩未定，Hankel正性（Hankel positivity）虽可约束这些矩，却无法揭示固定的多割族如何在矩空间中嵌入。因此需要一种能刻画该嵌入结构的局部诊断方法。

Method: 以柯西核 f_n(x)=(z_n-x)^{-1} 作为谱探针（spectral probes），其Gram矩阵即半正定的Pick矩阵 P_{mn}，由此定义与Hankel bootstrap类似的有限节点bootstrap。对固定正则多割拓扑，将谱判别式的非分支双零点称为dressed saddles，它们对矩空间轨迹施加横向约束，其余方向对应填充分数（filling fractions）。以 z=E+iη 设定分辨率尺度，用 P(z,z) 作为有限平面谱响应，将 ∇_m P(z,z) 解释为矩空间磁化率（susceptibility）。

Result: 在正则实dressed saddle附近，该磁化率以 η^{-2} 增强并与填充分数流形的余法向（conormal）对齐，而沿切向变形时主阶增强相消。多个独立saddle的响应可重构余法向空间，并通过其公共核得到切空间。结合Pick正性与resolvent的实性、解析性，得到四次（quartic）与六次（sextic）模型中已知平面结果的替代解析推导：非对称四次模型显式呈现一维填充分数几何，六次模型展现独立的余法向方向。

Conclusion: 结果表明，局部一致性条件可在全局周期匹配（period matching）与平衡变分不等式（equilibrium variational inequality）选取平衡测度（equilibrium measure）之前，对低矩施加约束，从而澄清了矩空间嵌入的分层结构。

Abstract: The planar loop equations of a Hermitian one-matrix model leave finitely many low moments undetermined. Hankel positivity constrains these moments but does not reveal how a fixed multicut family is embedded in moment space. We introduce a finite-plane diagnostic using the Cauchy kernels $f_n(x)=(z_n-x)^{-1}$ which we use as spectral probes. Their Gram matrix is the positive-semidefinite Pick matrix $P_{mn}$, whose positivity defines a finite-node bootstrap analogous to the Hankel bootstrap.
  For a fixed regular multicut topology, the nonbranching double zeros of the spectral discriminant, which we call \emph{dressed saddles}, impose transverse constraints on the corresponding moment-space locus, while its remaining directions are filling fractions. Writing $z=E+iη$, $E$ selects a spectral region and $η>0$ sets a continuous resolution scale. We use $P(z,z)$ as a finite-plane spectral response and interpret $\nabla_{\mathbf m}P(z,z)$ as a moment-space susceptibility. Near a regular real dressed saddle, this susceptibility is enhanced as $η^{-2}$ and aligns with a conormal to the filling-fraction manifold, whereas the leading enhancement cancels along tangent deformations. Responses near several independent saddles can therefore reconstruct the conormal space and, through their common kernel, its tangent space.

The asymmetric quartic makes the one-dimensional filling-fraction geometry explicit, while sextic models exhibit independent conormal directions. These results clarify how local consistency conditions constrain the low moments before global period matching and the equilibrium variational inequality select the equilibrium measure.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate Hawking radiation of a quantum-corrected Schwarzschild black hole with vacuum polarization and conformal anomaly effects. In this case, the higher-spin modes become negligible, and the scalar $s=l=0$ mode dominates. The black hole lifetime is greatly extended as it asymptotically approaches a stable extremal remnant.

Nonmarginal $z=1$ and $z=3/2$ backgrounds instead realize mixed constitutive--relaxation weights, demonstrating that the topology is selected dynamically rather than imposed by the DBI square root. ### [86] [3d Lovelock gravity and the holographic c-theorem: Proof at all orders and resummation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32866)
*Gokhan Alkac,Luis Guajardo,Hikmet Ozsahin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明三维 Lovelock 引力（3d Lovelock gravity）在任意耦合值与所有阶数下均满足全息 c-定理（holographic c-theorem），并给出重整化后的拉氏量、c-函数与中心荷（central charge）的闭合形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维 Lovelock 不变量构成了 Horndeski 型理论，其全息对偶性质（是否存在 c-定理）此前未在任意阶与任意耦合下得到系统证明，因此需要建立该理论在全息框架下的单调性结构。

Method: 在畴壁解（domain wall solutions）的微超空间（minisuperspace）中，利用 Horndeski 标量方程在各阶均为全导数（total derivative）的性质，将标量导数与翘曲因子（warp factor）的导数锁定；据此对零能条件（null energy condition）积分得到单调 c-函数，并以球面上壳作用（on-shell action）对数发散系数定义中心荷。

Result: 真空的线性标量剖面破坏了特殊共形变换（special conformal transformations），故对偶理论是标度不变（scale-invariant）而非共形不变的。中心荷在 Gauss-Bonnet 情形精确计算，对完整谱系给出渐近结果，均与耦合线性相关并与 c-函数紫外值一致。所有结果仅通过两个生成函数依赖耦合，故对导致正则黑洞（regular black holes）的耦合选择给出了重整化拉氏量、c-函数与中心荷的闭合形式。

Conclusion: 该理论的全息 c-定理在所有阶与任意耦合下成立。基于真空上有效牛顿常数（effective Newton constant）的正性与非平凡标量真空的唯一性，可对重整化后的理论进行分类。

Abstract: We prove that 3d Lovelock gravity, the Horndeski theory obtained from the regularized Lovelock invariants in three dimensions, admits a holographic c-theorem at all orders and for arbitrary values of the couplings. In the minisuperspace of domain wall solutions, the equation of the Horndeski scalar is a total derivative at every order, which locks the derivative of the scalar to that of the warp factor along the flows we consider and allows the null energy condition to be integrated into a monotonic c-function. The linear scalar profile of the resulting vacuum breaks the special conformal transformations, so the dual theory is scale-invariant but not conformal, and the central charge is defined as the coefficient of the logarithmic divergence of the on-shell action on the sphere. We compute it exactly for the Gauss-Bonnet case and asymptotically for the full tower, and find that it is linear in the couplings and matches the UV value of the c-function. Since all results depend on the couplings only through two generating functions, we give the resummed Lagrangians, c-functions and central charges in closed form for the choices of couplings that lead to regular black holes, and classify the resummed theories by the positivity of the effective Newton constant on the vacuum and the uniqueness of the vacuum with a non-trivial scalar.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该理论源自正则化Gauss-Bonnet耦合，其Maxwell带电对应解在原点存在对数曲率奇点（logarithmic curvature singularity），成为需要修正的病理。作者旨在通过引入非线性电动力学消除该奇点，获得无奇点的正规时空。

Method: 在三维标量-矢量-张量理论框架下求解静态、圆对称的带电解，将作用量与非线性Born-Infeld电动力学（non-linear Born-Infeld electrodynamics）耦合，求出度规函数（metric function）并对核心区域的正规性进行显式验证。

Result: Maxwell带电情形下的对数曲率奇点被Born-Infeld耦合所消除，核心保持正规。所得解依参数选择呈现多种视界结构：单视界、双视界、极端黑洞（extremal black holes）以及无视界的正规几何（horizonless regular geometries）。

Conclusion: 该正规时空被视为在无奇点（singularity-free）设定下进行微观熵计算（microscopic entropy calculations）的候选检验平台，其意义有待进一步讨论。

Abstract: We construct exact, static, circularly symmetric charged regular AdS$_3$ black hole solutions in a three-dimensional scalar-vector-tensor theory obtained from regularized Gauss-Bonnet couplings. While the Maxwell-charged counterpart of this theory suffers from a logarithmic curvature singularity at the origin, we demonstrate that coupling the action to non-linear Born-Infeld electrodynamics cures this pathology. We obtain the metric function and show explicitly the regularity of the core. Depending on the choice of parameters, the solution exhibits various horizon structures, including single-horizon, double-horizon, extremal black holes, and horizonless regular geometries. Finally, we discuss the significance of this regular spacetime as a testbed for microscopic entropy calculations in singularity-free setups.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 对于一般洛伦兹破缺系数，响应是非平稳的，依赖于测量持续时间与时间位置；数值谱在无量纲系数约 10^{-7} 量级时，与通常的有质量热谱（thermal spectrum）呈现可辨差异。点粒子图像下亦给出了修正轨迹上的固有时 Fourier 响应。

Abstract: We investigate the Fulling-Davies-Unruh effect in a local Lorentz-violating effective field theory, focusing on the quantum-field response of accelerated detectors. We canonically quantize a real scalar field exactly in the coefficients for Lorentz violation. The dispersion relation, Hamiltonian, and massless and massive Wightman functions are obtained. We determine the conditions under which the scalar dynamics preserves boost symmetry and distinguish these from the weaker conditions required for stationarity along a single accelerated worldline. The Wightman functions are used to construct the finite-time Unruh-DeWitt response for a uniformly accelerated detector. For generic coefficients the response is nonstationary and depends on both the duration and temporal location of the measurement. The spectrum, which is the resulting excitation rate as a function of the energy level difference in the detector, is obtained numerically. It shows discernable differences with the usual massive thermal spectrum for dimensionless coefficients on the order of $10^{-7}$. Experimental implications of the result are discussed. As an alternative viewpoint, we place Lorentz violation in the point-particle sector, derive the exact trajectory generated by a constant electric field, and study the proper-time Fourier response of a conventional scalar wave along the modified trajectory.

</details>

Abstract: The renormalization of asymptotically AdS spacetimes in New Massive Gravity is studied using boundary terms constructed with the extrinsic curvature. This prescription applies to constant-curvature geometries and non-Einstein solutions with Brown-Henneaux asymptotic conditions. A suitable asymptotic expansion shows the agreement of the resulting holographic stress tensor with the one obtained from the auxiliary-field formulation.

A quadratic extrinsic counterterm removes the new divergences and leads naturally to a holographic picture with two independent sources. The corresponding responses obey a modified holographic Ward identity and determine finite conserved charges.

</details>


### [90] [Quantization of the electroweak Volkov-Proca theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33825)
*Tudor Pătuleanu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将电弱理论带电扇区在自发对称性破缺后置于外部电磁平面波背景中量子化，提出电弱 Volkov-Proca 理论，并系统建立其守恒荷、态的正交归一与完备性关系、传播子及谱表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

The residual rational term vanishes for the separation selected by the supersymmetry, under which the collective coordinates and the quantum fields form separate supermultiplets. We conjecture that the remaining integral over the collective coordinates contributes only to an overall normalization of the partition function, so it is 1-loop exact. For $k=1$ the quartic vertex contribution reduces to equation-of-motion terms as in the case of ${\rm AdS}_3 \subset { \rm AdS}_7 \times S^4$ in arXiv:2511.22306 related to $S^3 \subset {\rm AdS}_4 \times S^7$ case by a formal analytic continuation.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实射影平面上刘维尔理论的一点函数早由自举（bootstrap）方法精确导出，但从未与半经典路径积分计算进行对照验证。该检验具有特殊性：射影平面上所有常曲率度量均为正曲率，而经典刘维尔理论给出常负曲率度量，故不存在实经典解。

Abstract: The exact one-point function of Liouville theory on the real projective plane was derived long ago from the bootstrap, yet it has never been verified against a semiclassical path-integral computation. The check is less straightforward than one might expect. On the real projective plane every constant-curvature metric is positively curved, whereas classical Liouville theory produces metrics of constant negative curvature, so no real classical solution exists. Instead, as we show, the semiclassical path integral receives contributions from infinitely many complex saddles with a negative-definite metric. Once the integration contour is chosen so that the path integral converges, these saddles reproduce the exact one-point function. Complex saddles arise in the same way in timelike Liouville theory and in two-dimensional de~Sitter gravity, and the example treated here offers a rare opportunity to test their use against a known exact answer. \end{abstract

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清 D1–D5 CFT 中 BPS 态的循环结构与其偶然性之间的关系，并检验这些微观循环结构如何在可观测的探针响应中体现。

Abstract: We study BPS states in the D1--D5 CFT through their fortuity, cycle structure, and probe response. Explicit fortuitous states with both long- and short-string geometries show that the cycle partition does not determine fortuity state by state. At large $N$, fixed-charge counting nevertheless selects a long string. In the open uncondensed region, an entropy-carrying exact non-graviton subspace concentrates on one cycle of winding $N-O(1)$. Assuming subexponential monotone dimension, the same holds for the harmonic fortuitous complement. Beyond the critical line, the free ensemble develops a short-string condensate. To test how this cycle structure appears in observables, we study finite-winding probes. The long-cycle scalar correlator approaches the extremal-BTZ response at fixed time, while finite winding is resolved at \(t=O(\sqrt N)\), the exact circle recurs at \(t=O(N)\), and states with the same cycle geometry remain distinguishable by their spectral weights.

</details>


### [99] [Journey to the Center of a Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35705)
*Vyshnav Mohan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究三维 BTZ 黑洞中坠落两点函数（infalling two-point function）在奇点处发散的解释，指出引入 Hagedorn 增长的巨量粒子塔后，圈修正（loop corrections）会在奇点附近触发 Hagedorn 转变，使树级发散不再可靠，并对应边界理论中广义自由场描述的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维 BTZ 黑洞中坠落标量场两点函数在插入点到达奇点时发散，需澄清该发散在边界描述中的来源，并检验其在高维情形（由多应力张量部门编码曲率奇点）之外的机制。

Method: 在三维 BTZ 黑洞背景中计算含一个外部插入与一个沿类时测地线插入的标量场两点函数；在边界侧将其发散归因于双扭曲算符（double-twist operators）交换，并引入具有 Hagedorn 增长的巨量粒子塔以计算圈修正。

Result: 该发散完全来自双扭曲算符交换，而非高维中编码曲率奇点的多应力张量部门；Hagedorn 粒子塔带来的圈修正在奇点附近变得重要并触发 Hagedorn 转变，使树级发散不可信；边界上对应原初算符塔导致探针算符在黑洞态中的广义自由场描述（generalized free field）失效。

Conclusion: 在大黑洞中，这些修正仅在奇点附近重要，在视界处仍指数压低，故修正主导区域与视界呈参数化分离，与黑洞互补性原理（black hole complementarity）的预期一致。

Abstract: An infalling two-point function of a scalar field in a three-dimensional BTZ black hole, with one operator inserted in the exterior and the other inserted along a timelike geodesic, diverges when the infalling insertion point reaches the black hole singularity. In the boundary description, this divergence comes entirely from the exchange of double-twist operators, rather than from the multi-stress-tensor sector that encodes the curvature singularities in higher dimensions. We show that the inclusion of a tower of massive particles with Hagedorn growth gives rise to loop corrections that become important near the singularity, where they trigger a Hagedorn transition that renders the tree-level divergence untrustworthy. In the boundary theory, the corresponding tower of primary operators leads to a breakdown of the generalized free field description of the probe operator in the black hole state. In a large black hole, these corrections become important only near the singularity and remain exponentially suppressed at the horizon. The region where these corrections dominate is therefore parametrically separated from the horizon, as anticipated by the principle of black hole complementarity.

</details>
