### [100] [Emergent low-energy many-body Hilbert spaces in Chern bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31831)
*Chen-Xin Jiang,Zi-Xiang Hu,Bo Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究证明，在朗道能级（Landau levels）及更一般的陈能带（Chern bands）中，仅由双体相互作用筛选出的最相关多体子空间强烈依赖粒子密度，并可系统构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带宽与能隙趋于零的极限下，仅靠双体相互作用决定的多体希尔伯特子空间及其密度依赖性尚缺乏系统刻画，需要明确相互作用能标如何选取主导子空间。

Method: 利用共形希尔伯特空间（conformal Hilbert spaces, CHS）的层级结构，对费米子与玻色子系统分别计算多体量子度量（many-body quantum metric），以量化各子空间的相互作用能标，并针对一类短程库仑型相互作用进行分析。

Result: 对于一大类短程库仑型相互作用，粒子倾向于占据最小可容纳 CHS 中的多体态，从而可用相应的任意子（anyonic）自由度描述低能物理；同时解释了任意陈能带中能带混合（band mixing）即使强相互作用下也趋于被抑制的现象，且当能带更接近理想迹条件（trace condition）、双体相互作用更短程时抑制更强。

Conclusion: 最相关多体子空间可由 CHS 层级结构系统构造且依赖粒子密度，低能物理可由任意子自由度刻画；能带混合的抑制程度取决于能带偏离理想迹条件的程度与相互作用程长。

Abstract: We show that in Landau levels and more generic Chern bands, the most relevant many-body subspace selected by realistic two-body interactions alone (e.g., in the limit of vanishing bandwidth and band gap) depends strongly on the particle density and can be constructed systematically. Using the well-defined hierarchical structure of conformal Hilbert spaces (CHS), each with a highest-density vacuum state, we compute, for both fermionic and bosonic systems, the many-body quantum metric that quantifies the interaction energy scales of these subspaces. For a large family of short-range, Coulomb-based interactions, particles preferentially occupy the many-body states in the smallest accommodating CHS. This allows us to describe the low-energy physics of the interacting system in terms of the appropriate anyonic degrees of freedom. Our results also explain why, in any Chern band (in lattice or continuum systems), band mixing tends to be suppressed even in the limit of strong interaction; this suppression is stronger when the band deviates less from the ideal trace condition and when the two-body interaction is shorter-ranged.

</details>


### [101] [Zoology of chiral superconductors in Chern bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31839)
*André Grossi Fonseca,Aidan Reddy,Ahmed Abouelkomsan,Liang Fu,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将目标相优化（target-phase optimization）方法拓展至陈能带（Chern band）中强关联电子的手性超导（chiral superconductivity）搜索，在周期调制朗道能级（Landau level）的无自旋电子模型中揭示出一系列手性超导相，并首次发现手性超导体与复合费米液体（composite Fermi liquid）之间的直接相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在菱面多层石墨烯与扭角双层MoTe$_2$等范德华（van der Waals）体系中观测到手性超导证据，且超导出现在分数陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator）附近的载流子密度处。这引出核心问题：在具有强排斥相互作用（repulsive interactions）的拓扑能带中，电子可能形成何种超导体。

Method: 作者改造目标相优化方法，用于在陈能带最小模型中搜索手性超导体：基于配对结合能（pair-binding energy）的符号振荡构造可微损失函数（differentiable loss function），并将梯度优化（gradient-based optimization）与严格筛选流程相结合。框架在精确对角化（exact diagonalization）中应用于周期调制朗道能级中受屏蔽库仑相互作用（screened Coulomb interactions）的无自旋电子。

Result: 在填充因子（filling）$\nu=2/3$处复现了此前识别的$f-\mathrm{i} f$空穴超导体，并发现额外的$p\pm \mathrm{i} p$与$f+\mathrm{i} f$电子及空穴超导体，其出现位置跨越理想量子几何（ideal quantum geometry）极限附近与远离该极限的区域。在$\nu=1/2$处识别出$p- \mathrm{i} p$电子与$f-\mathrm{i} f$空穴超导体，并首次发现手性超导体与复合费米液体之间的直接相变。

Conclusion: 结果表明陈能带中的手性超导构成一个由多种竞争配对不稳定性（pairing instabilities）组成的丰富图景，同时确立了目标相优化作为在复杂相互作用体系中搜索量子相的通用策略。

Abstract: Evidence for chiral superconductivity has recently been observed in several van der Waals systems including rhombohedral multilayer graphene and twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$. In the latter, superconductivity emerges at carrier densities near a fractional Chern insulator. This raises the question of what kinds of superconductors may emerge in a system of electrons in a topological band with strong repulsive interactions. Here, we adapt the target-phase optimization method to search for chiral superconductors in a minimal model of interacting electrons in a Chern band. We construct a differentiable loss function for superconductors from the sign oscillation of the pair-binding energy and combine gradient-based optimization with a rigorous screening procedure to identify and characterize superconductors. Applying this framework within exact diagonalization to spinless electrons in periodically modulated Landau levels with screened Coulomb interactions, we uncover a broad family of chiral superconducting phases. At filling $ν=2/3$, we recover the previously identified $f-\mathrm{i} f$ hole superconductors and find additional $p\pm \mathrm{i} p$ and $f+\mathrm{i} f$ superconductors of both electrons and holes, occurring near and far from the limit of ideal quantum geometry. At $ν=1/2$, we identify $p- \mathrm{i} p$ electron and $f-\mathrm{i} f$ hole superconductors and find, for the first time, a direct transition between chiral superconductors and composite Fermi liquids. Our results reveal that chiral superconductivity in Chern bands comprises a diverse landscape of competing pairing instabilities and establish target-phase optimization as a general strategy for searching for quantum phases in complex interacting systems.

</details>


### [102] [Read-Rezayi fractional Chern insulators in modulated Bernal graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31846)
*Ruth Mora-Soto,André Grossi Fonseca,Raul Perea-Causin,Hui Liu,Emil J. Bergholtz,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在周期性调制（periodically modulated）的 Bernal 双层石墨烯（Bernal bilayer graphene）模型中，于填充因子（filling factor）ν=3/5 处识别出与 Read-Rezayi 分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator）一致基态的参数区域，提示零磁场下实现 Fibonacci 任意子（Fibonacci anyons）等非阿贝尔态（non-Abelian states）的可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fibonacci 任意子为拓扑量子计算（topological quantum computation）提供通用平台，并在分数量子霍尔效应（fractional quantum Hall effect）的 Z3 Read-Rezayi 相中以低能激发形式出现，但无磁场条件下该相的真实微观实现一直难以获得。

Method: 研究构建了带栅极屏蔽库仑相互作用（gate-screened Coulomb interactions）的周期性调制 Bernal 双层石墨烯模型，并采用近期发展的目标相优化方法（target-phase optimization method）结合能带投影精确对角化（band-projected exact diagonalization）。

Result: 在 ν=3/5 处找到一个参数区域，其基态与 Read-Rezayi 分数量子陈绝缘体相符；该基态源自一个双能带复合体中的部分填充能带，其几何特征模仿最低与第一朗道能级（Landau levels），且该复合体在 ν=1/2 处的基态与 Moore-Read 态一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明调制 Bernal 石墨烯可在零磁场下实现精细的非阿贝尔分数量子霍尔态，同时说明目标相优化是在高维真实微观模型中寻找此类相态的可行途径。

Abstract: Fibonacci anyons provide a universal platform for topological quantum computation, and emerge as low-energy excitations in the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ Read-Rezayi phase in the fractional quantum Hall effect. However, realistic microscopic realizations of this phase in the absence of a magnetic field have remained elusive. We study a model of periodically modulated Bernal bilayer graphene with gate-screened Coulomb interactions. Using the recently developed target-phase optimization method in conjunction with band-projected exact diagonalization, we identify at filling $ν=3/5$ a region of parameter space whose ground state is consistent with a Read-Rezayi fractional Chern insulator. The partially filled band from which it arises is a part of a two-band complex which mimics geometric aspects of the lowest and first Landau levels, with the ground state at $ν=1/2$ consistent with the Moore-Read state. Our results suggest that modulated Bernal graphene can realize delicate non-Abelian fractional quantum Hall states at zero magnetic field, while demonstrating target-phase optimization as a practical route to discovering such phases in realistic, high-dimensional microscopic models.

</details>


### [103] [Chiral Weyl-Kondo semimetallic state through enhanced correlation in CeGaGe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32012)
*Arushi,Kevin Allen,Yuan Fang,Kuan-Sen Lin,Mounica Mahankali,Hari Bhandari,Karthik Rao,Alberto Ruiz Biestro,Christopher Lane,Jian-Xin Zhu,Sanu Mishra,Geoffroy Hautier,Qimiao Si,Emilia Morosan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究报告了在CeGaGe中实现手性Weyl-Kondo半金属（chiral Weyl-Kondo semimetal, cWKSM）态的证据：其低温手性P4₃结构、Kondo相干与Berry曲率驱动的本征反常霍尔电导共同表明晶体手性、强电子关联与拓扑能带首次在同一材料中并存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性Weyl-Kondo半金属态理论上要求材料同时具备晶体手性、非简单空间群对称性与Kondo关联，但此前尚无已知材料满足全部条件。研究动机在于寻找并验证可承载该态的实际材料平台。

Method: 以RXY家族成员CeGaGe为对象，开展磁化测量以确定磁有序，通过结构表征追踪四方I4₁md（非手性）到P4₃（手性）的结构转变，并进行H || c构型下的霍尔电阻率测量，结合P4₃结构的第一性原理计算进行分析。

Result: 磁化测量确认T_N = 4.7 K以下存在长程反铁磁序及复杂的倾斜磁结构。霍尔测量显示反常霍尔电导（anomalous Hall conductivity, AHC）在Kondo温度T_K ≈ 7 K以下保持恒定、之上急剧下降，该交叉跟随Kondo相干而非磁有序。第一性原理计算支持AHC为Kondo杂化产生的本征Berry曲率驱动贡献，而非外禀散射或动态标量自旋手性所致。

Conclusion: CeGaGe被确立为晶体手性、强电子关联与非平凡能带拓扑共存的稀有实验平台，提供了Kondo驱动cWKSM态的首个材料实现。

Abstract: Strongly correlated chiral materials have been proposed to host a chiral Weyl-Kondo semimetal (cWKSM) state, in which Kondo hybridization pins chirality-induced Kramers-Weyl point crossings, together with additional symmetry-enforced band crossings, near the Fermi level. Realizing this state requires a material that combines crystal chirality, non-symmorphic symmetry, and Kondo correlations, and currently there are no known materials that combine all these requirements. Here we report evidence for a cWKSM state in CeGaGe, a member of the RXY (R = rare earth; X = Al, Si; Y = Ga, Ge) family. Magnetization measurements confirm long-range antiferromagnetic order below T$_N$ = 4.7 K with a complex, canted magnetic structure. Unique to CeGaGe among the known RXY systems is a structural transition from an achiral tetragonal I4$_{1}$md structure (at high temperatures) to the chiral tetragonal P4$_{3}$ structure (at low temperatures). The structural transition in CeGaGe allows us to highlight the role of chirality in stabilizing the Kramers cWKSM state. Hall resistivity measurements with $H || c$ reveal an anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) that is constant below the Kondo temperature $T_K \approx 7$ K and drops sharply above it. This crossover tracks the onset of Kondo coherence rather than the magnetic ordering, which occurs at T$_N$ = 4.7 K. Together with first-principles calculations for the $P4_3$ structure, this identifies the AHC as an intrinsic, Berry-curvature-driven contribution generated by Kondo hybridization, rather than a consequence of extrinsic scattering or dynamic scalar spin chirality. These results establish CeGaGe as a rare experimental platform in which crystal chirality, strong electronic correlations, and non-trivial band topology coexist, providing the first material realization of a Kondo-driven cWKSM state.

</details>


### [104] [Symmetry-permuting entanglers for non-invertible symmetry-protected topological phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32266)
*Minyoung You*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究非可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetry）下的1+1维对称保护拓扑相（SPT phases），指出三个 \(\mathrm{Rep}(D_8)\) SPT 相无法由对称纠缠器（symmetric entangler）连接，但可通过"对称置换"有限深度局域幺正电路相连，从而实现拓扑量子场论（TQFT）预言的 duality。作者显式构造了连接其中两个相的矩阵乘积幺正（matrix product unitary），并验证其作用在弦序参量（string order parameters）上再现了 TQFT duality 预言的任意子置换（anyon permutation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可逆对称性（invertible symmetry）下的1+1维 SPT 相可由对称纠缠器刻画，但该框架对非可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetry）失效：例如三个 \(\mathrm{Rep}(D_8)\) SPT 相之间不存在任何对称纠缠器连接。论文旨在寻找能沟通这些相的替代机制，并检验其是否对应 TQFT 所预言的 duality。

Method: 引入"对称置换"有限深度局域幺正电路（symmetry-permuting finite-depth local unitary circuits）作为连接手段，替代传统对称纠缠器。针对 \(\mathrm{Rep}(D_8)\) SPT 相，显式构造了连接其中两个相的矩阵乘积幺正（matrix product unitary），并考察该幺正对弦序参量（string order parameters）的作用。

Result: 三个 \(\mathrm{Rep}(D_8)\) SPT 相可由对称置换有限深度局域幺正电路相互连接，构成 TQFT 所预言的 duality 的实现。显式构造的矩阵乘积幺正作用在弦序参量上，所产生的任意子置换（anyon permutation）与 TQFT duality 的预言一致。

Conclusion: 对非可逆对称性，对称纠缠器不足以刻画 SPT 相之间的连通性，而对称置换幺正电路提供了更一般的连接方式，并可作为 TQFT duality 的格点实现。该结果将 SPT 相的刻画框架从可逆对称性推广至非可逆对称性情形。

Abstract: 1+1D symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases with invertible symmetry can be characterized by the symmetric entanglers: globally symmetric finite-depth local unitary circuits that generate representative ground states from a product state. For non-invertible symmetries, however, distinct SPT phases, such as the three $\mathrm{Rep}(D_8)$ SPT phases, may not be connected by any symmetric entangler. We show that the three $\mathrm{Rep}(D_8)$ SPT phases can nevertheless be connected by \emph{symmetry-permuting} finite-depth local unitary circuits, realizing dualities predicted by topological quantum field theory (TQFT). We construct an explicit matrix product unitary connecting two of the $\mathrm{Rep}(D_8)$ SPT phases, and show that its action on string order parameters reproduces the anyon permutation predicted by the TQFT duality.

</details>


### [105] [How Much Do We Understand Ce L3 XANES? Ab Initio Insights into Configuration-Specific Screening at the Multiplet--Continuum Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32968)
*Alessandro Mirone*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作以 XSpectruplet 方法将局域多重态动力学（multiplet dynamics）与连续态光电子传播在自洽 Kohn–Sham 屏蔽势中耦合，用以调和 Ce L₃ X 射线吸收近边谱（XANES）中的局域多重态特征、集体芯孔屏蔽与扩展光电子图像。结果显示，CeO₂ 中 Ce⁴⁺ 的两个屏蔽定态虽共享相同的有效 4f⁰ 局域组态，却可产生白线主峰与卫星区贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源自 Kvashnina（Chem. Eur. J. 30, e202400755, 2024）提出的问题：局域多重态信号、集体芯孔屏蔽（core-hole screening）与扩展光电子三者如何在同一 Ce L₃ XANES 谱中自洽共存。该问题涉及 Ce 体系中价态与屏蔽描述的长期争议。

Method: 采用 XSpectruplet 方法，将局域多重态动力学与连续态光电子传播耦合于自洽 Kohn–Sham 屏蔽势中；对 CeO₂ 中的 Ce⁴⁺ 进行启发式搜索以识别主、次定态屏蔽态，并计算白线峰位、总能量差及与未弛豫突变参考态的平方重叠。对独居石 CePO₄ 中的 Ce³⁺ 作为局域 4f¹ 旁观体（spectator）参与的对照情形进行分析，并将 CeO₂ 定义的净修正启发式迁移至 CePO₄。

Result: CeO₂ 中主、次屏蔽定态的白线峰位在计入各自独立计算的总能量差后相差约 7.3 eV，但二者保持相同的有效局域光谱 4f⁰ 区；其与未弛豫突变参考态的平方重叠给出约 63% 与 30% 的近似统计权重（独立通道描述）。两者的屏蔽响应差异源于占据轨道的集体再杂化（rehybridization），而非额外局域 4f 旁观体的占据，并在主白线与卫星区均有贡献。CePO₄ 中 Ce³⁺ 则为局域 4f¹ 旁观体显式参与多重态动力学的对照情形。独立弛豫中性 4f 与白线态给出 CeO₂ 的前边–白线间距为 10.51 eV，接近约 10.7 eV 的实验值；其相对于固定背景谱间距的超出量被定义为净修正并迁移至 CePO₄，估计其间距为 6.38 eV，实验值约为 8.1 eV。

Conclusion: 不同的谱学贡献可由集体屏蔽产生，而无需改变有效局域组态；CeO₂ 与 CePO₄ 的对照表明，4f 旁观体是否显式参与多重态动力学区分了两类屏蔽机制。所提净修正启发式迁移在 CePO₄ 上给出的估计值仍低于实验值，提示该方案存在改进空间。

Abstract: How can local multiplet signatures, collective core-hole screening, and an extended photoelectron be reconciled in Ce $L_3$ x-ray absorption near-edge spectra? Motivated by the question raised by Kvashnina [Chem. Eur. J. 30, e202400755 (2024)], we address this challenge using XSpectruplet, which couples local multiplet dynamics to continuum photoelectron propagation in self-consistent Kohn--Sham screening potentials. For Ce$^{4+}$ in CeO$_2$, our heuristic search identifies primary and secondary stationary screening states whose calculated white-line maxima lie about 7.3~eV apart after including their independently calculated total-energy difference, yet both retain the same effective local spectroscopic $4f^0$ sector. Their squared overlaps with the unrelaxed sudden reference give approximate statistical weights of 63\% and 30\%, respectively, within an independent-channel description. Their contrasting screening responses arise from collective rehybridization of occupied orbitals, rather than occupation of an additional localized $4f$ spectator, and produce contributions in both the main white-line and satellite regions. Ce$^{3+}$ in monazite CePO$_4$ provides the contrasting case of a localized $4f^1$ spectator participating explicitly in the multiplet dynamics. Independent relaxation of neutral $4f$ and white-line states gives a CeO$_2$ pre-edge--white-line gap of 10.51~eV, close to the experimental separation of approximately 10.7~eV. Its excess over the fixed-background spectral gap defines a net correction, which we transfer heuristically to CePO$_4$ without changing its local multiplet structure. This gives an estimated CePO$_4$ gap of 6.38~eV, compared with approximately 8.1~eV experimentally. Distinct spectral contributions can thus arise from collective screening without a change in the effective local configuration.

</details>


### [106] [Scaling Theory for Learning Low-Energy Quantum Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33777)
*Guijing Duan,Chen Mo,Di Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究指出，随参数变化可高效学习的对象是孤立的低能子空间（low-energy subspace）本身，而非单个标记本征态；在恰当采样模式下，能量密度误差由采样距离 \(d\) 与体系尺度 \(n\) 的标度律控制，并可据此探测临界相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相图与能量面需要对一族哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）的低能态进行求解，但逐点独立求解代价过高。核心问题在于：少量参考波函数（reference wavefunctions）中究竟包含多少低能区信息，以及这些信息能否被有效学习。

Method: 将低能子空间本身作为物理相关对象（其在简并（degeneracy）与能级交叉（level crossing）处仍良定义），并由邻近采样态学习。理论上证明：抵消前 \(q\) 阶非恒定响应项的采样模式使每个保留能级的能量密度误差以 \(d^{2(q+1)}\) 为界；对 \(n\) 格点的有能隙局域基态（gapped local ground state），局域性将该界收紧为 \(d^2(nd^2)^q\)。

Result: 误差界随采样距离按幂律衰减，并识别出 \(nd^2\) 为控制高阶采样波函数信息有效性的自然标度变量；在局域解析区域，固定参数维数 \(D\) 下采样代价为 \(K=\mathcal{O}([\log(1/\varepsilon)]^D)\)。对海森堡链（Heisenberg chain）与横场伊辛链（transverse-field Ising chain）的数值结果证实了距离标度律与 \(nd^2\) 塌缩。

Conclusion: 除刻画可学习性（learnability）外，该标度行为还提供了临界点附近相变（phase transition）的有限尺寸探针，将采样误差标度与物理相变诊断联系起来。

Abstract: Phase diagrams and energy surfaces require low-energy states across a family of Hamiltonians, but solving each parameter point independently is prohibitively expensive. An important question is not how a single labeled eigenstate behaves under parameter variation, but how much information about the low-energy sector is contained in a small set of reference wavefunctions. We show that the physically relevant object is the isolated low-energy subspace itself: it remains well defined through degeneracies and level crossings, and it can be learned efficiently from nearby sampled states. Working in this subspace, we prove that a sampling pattern cancelling the first $q$ nonconstant response orders yields an energy-density error bounded by $d^{2(q+1)}$ for every retained level, where $d$ is the parameter-space sampling distance. For a gapped local ground state on $n$ sites, locality sharpens this to $d^2(nd^2)^q$, identifying $nd^2$ as the natural scaling variable governing the effectiveness of higher-order information from sampled wavefunctions. In a local analytic regime this gives a sampling cost $K=\mathcal{O}([\log(1/\varepsilon)]^D)$ at fixed parameter dimension $D$. Numerical results on Heisenberg and transverse-field Ising chains confirm the distance scaling law and the $nd^2$ collapse. Beyond characterizing learnability, the scaling behavior further provides a finite-size probe of phase transitions near criticality.

</details>


### [107] [Bogoliubov coupled-cluster theory for su(2) Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34128)
*Swarnamoy Ghosh,Thomas M. Henderson,Gustavo E. Scuseria*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将 Bogoliubov 耦合簇理论（Bogoliubov coupled-cluster theory）应用于具有 su(2) 代数结构的哈密顿量，通过费米子配对算符与自旋-1/2 算符的对应关系，统一处理配对哈密顿量（pairing Hamiltonian）、自旋 XXZ 与 J1-J2 模型，并推导了响应密度矩阵与松弛密度矩阵以计算关联函数。基准计算表明该方法可为 su(2) 哈密顿量（包括几何阻挫自旋体系）提供系统性可改进的波函数方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合簇理论（coupled-cluster theory）适用于弱关联体系，但在强关联体系中因平均场参考态质量差而严重失效。使用对称破缺的平均场参考态（symmetry-broken mean-field references）可缓解这些失效，例如自旋对称破缺时采用非限制参考（unrestricted reference），粒子数对称破缺时采用 Bogoliubov 耦合簇理论。

Method: 针对具有 su(2) 代数结构的哈密顿量，利用费米子配对算符与自旋-1/2 算符的对应关系，将 Bogoliubov 耦合簇应用于配对哈密顿量及自旋 XXZ、J1-J2 模型。配对问题以 BCS（Bardeen--Cooper--Schrieffer）准粒子真空为参考，自旋体系则采用类似的 spin-BCS 参考，破坏 Sz 对称性；关联通过一系列耦合簇近似层级引入。此外计算响应密度矩阵以获得自旋-自旋关联函数、配对参数与粒子数方差，并推导包含轨道松弛效应的松弛密度矩阵（relaxed density matrices）。

Result: 在半填充配对哈密顿量与自旋模型的基准计算中，Bogoliubov 耦合簇为 su(2) 哈密顿量提供了系统性可改进的基于波函数的方法，其中包括需要复且非共线 BCS 参考（complex and noncollinear BCS references）的几何阻挫自旋体系。同时得到了 XXZ 与 J1-J2 海森堡模型的自旋-自旋关联函数，以及配对哈密顿量的配对参数与粒子数方差。

Conclusion: Bogoliubov 耦合簇方法可有效处理 su(2) 哈密顿量下的强关联问题，其零 Sz 对称破缺的 spin-BCS 参考为强关联区域提供了灵活的出发点，并能拓展至几何阻挫自旋体系。

Abstract: Coupled-cluster theory is the method of choice for weakly correlated systems, but in strongly correlated systems where the mean-field reference is qualitatively poor, it can break down badly. These failures can be ameliorated by using symmetry-broken mean-field references. For systems that spontaneously break spin symmetry, we might favor an unrestricted reference. In systems that instead break number symmetry, we turn to Bogoliubov coupled cluster theory. In this work, we apply Bogoliubov coupled-cluster to Hamiltonians with an underlying su(2) algebra to study the pairing Hamiltonian and the spin XXZ and J1-J2 models by exploiting the correspondence between fermionic-pair operators and spin-1/2 operators. For the pairing problem, the reference is the usual Bardeen--Cooper--Schrieffer (BCS) quasiparticle vacuum, while for spin systems we use the analogous spin-BCS reference, which breaks Sz symmetry and provides a flexible starting point for strongly correlated regimes. Correlation is incorporated through a hierarchy of coupled-cluster approximations. Beyond energies, we compute the response density matrices for the evaluation of spin--spin correlation functions for the XXZ and J1-J2 Heisenberg models, and the pairing parameter and particle-number variance for the pairing Hamiltonian. We also derive the corresponding relaxed density matrices, including the effects of orbital relaxation. Benchmark calculations for the half-filled pairing Hamiltonian and spin models demonstrate that Bogoliubov coupled cluster provides a systematically improvable wave-function-based approach for su(2) Hamiltonians, including geometrically frustrated spin systems requiring complex and noncollinear BCS references.

</details>


### [108] [Electronic and topological properties of Ce-based honeycomb ferromagnet Ce$_2$Zn$_6$Ge$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34477)
*Yanen Huang,Zihan Yang,Jiawen Zhang,Yuwei Zhou,Lubin Wang,Gang Li,Michael Smidman,Chao Cao,Yu Liu,Huiqiu Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: Ce$_2$Zn$_6$Ge$_3$ 作为罕见的 Ce 基蜂窝铁磁体（honeycomb ferromagnet），通过量子振荡（quantum oscillations）与 DFT 计算被研究其费米面（Fermi surface）与拓扑性质，结果支持 4$f$ 电子局域化及非平庸拓扑（nontrivial topology）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ce 基蜂窝铁磁体较为罕见，其磁性、拓扑与量子临界性（quantum criticality）之间的关联缺乏实验与理论结合的探索平台，Ce$_2$Zn$_6$Ge$_3$ 提供了研究这一交织关系的可能对象。

Method: 结合磁阻（magnetoresistance）与隧道二极管振荡器（tunnel diode oscillator, TDO）测量量子振荡，并配合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算，以获取费米面信息与电子结构。

Result: 观测到四个基本频率（fundamental frequencies），其角度依赖更符合 4$f$ 电子局域化的 DFT 计算结果；同时测得负纵向磁阻（negative longitudinal magnetoresistance）与非零贝里相位（Berry phase），计算显示费米能级附近存在两对 Weyl 点（Weyl points）。

Conclusion: 结果提示 Ce$_2$Zn$_6$Ge$_3$ 中铁磁性呈局域性质，并具有非平庸拓扑，可作为研究磁性、拓扑、量子临界性及其相互作用的独特平台。

Abstract: Ce$_2$Zn$_6$Ge$_3$ is a rare example of the Ce-based honeycomb ferromagnet. Here, we report its Fermi surface and topological properties by quantum oscillations via the magnetoresistance and tunnel diode oscillator (TDO) based measurements, in combination with the density functional theory (DFT) calculations. Four fundamental frequencies are observed in the quantum oscillations, and their angle dependence is more compatible with the DFT calculations assuming that the 4$f$-electrons are localized, suggesting a localized nature of ferromagnetism in Ce$_2$Zn$_6$Ge$_3$. Furthermore, the observations of negative longitudinal magnetoresistance and non-zero Berry phase, as well as the existence of two pairs of Weyl points near the Fermi level as revealed from the calculated electronic structures, provide strong evidence for nontrivial topology in Ce$_2$Zn$_6$Ge$_3$. These findings suggest that Ce$_2$Zn$_6$Ge$_3$ could provide a unique platform to study magnetism, topology, quantum criticality and their interplay.

</details>


### [109] [Enhanced sliding in the coexisting Charge Density Wave phase of strained TbTe3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34569)
*Antoine Gallo Frantz,Aleksander Sinchenko,Luc Ortega,Abdellali Hadj Azzem,Jose Emilio Lorenzo,Pierre Monceau,David Le Bolloch,Vincent Jacques*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过测量电流-电压特性及微分电阻，探究了TbTe₃中应力诱导的c取向与a取向电荷密度波（charge density wave, CDW）在纯相、共存相中的滑动动力学（sliding dynamics），证明应变诱导的CDWa同样具有非公度（incommensurate）性质与滑动行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来机械拉伸应力被认为可触发量子材料中的非常规相。TbTe₃在中等单轴拉伸应力下出现CDW取向转变（与面内a/c晶格各向异性相关），由c取向CDW（CDWc）消失并出现正交的a取向CDW（CDWa），当a=c时形成二者共存的相。作者希望探明这些不同相中的CDW滑动动力学，以理解其本质与微观结构。

Method: 通过施加拉伸应力调节体系进入纯CDWc、纯CDWa及共存相，测量各状态下的电流-电压特性（current-voltage characteristics）及相应的微分电阻（differential resistances），以表征CDW的滑动行为与阈值电流、阈值场随应变及温度的变化。

Result: 应变诱导的CDWa相表现出与CDWc相同的非线性动力学特征，证明其为非公度（incommensurate）性质；在共存相中CDWc与CDWa均发生滑动，且阈值电流降低；所有形变状态下阈值场保持线性关系，并在Tc处存在有限阈值场。

Conclusion: CDWa具有非公度性与滑动行为，共存相中正交CDW的实空间微观结构仍待澄清；阈值场的线性与Tc处有限阈值场可从公度性与钉扎势（commensurability and pinning potentials）角度讨论。该工作为准二维（quasi-2D）体系中滑动CDW的理论描述提出了新问题。

Abstract: In the last years, the application of mechanical tensile stress has been shown to trigger unconventional phases in quantum materials. Recently, an orientational transition of the charge density wave (CDW) of TbTe3 was reported under the application of moderate uniaxial tensile stress, associated to the in-plane a/c lattice anisotropy. This leads to the disappearance of the pristine c-oriented CDW (CDWc) and to the appearance of the orthogonal a-oriented one (CDWa). A coexistence phase with a superposition of CDWa and CDWc appears when a=c. Here, we probe the sliding dynamics of both CDWc and CDWa as a function of applied tensile stress, i.e. in the pure CDWc, pure CDWa and coexistence phases, by measuring current-voltage characteristics and associated differential resistances. If the pristine CDWc has been known to display non-linear dynamics and thus sliding for several years, we show here that the strain-induced CDWa phase also displays the same characteristics, proving its incommensurate nature. Moreover, we show that sliding also takes place both for CDWc and CDWa, with reduced threshold currents, in the coexistence phase, which questions the microstructure of these coexisting orthogonal CDWs in real space. Finally, the linearity of the threshold fields is kept for all deformation states, with finite threshold fields at Tc, which is discussed in terms of commensurability and pinning potentials. This work opens new questions for the theoretical description of sliding CDWs in quasi-2D systems.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum-Geometric Amplification of Nonperturbative Interaction Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34607)
*Stefano Bilamour,René Meyer,Johanna Erdmenger,Domenico Di Sante*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 相互作用诱导的QBT能隙属于超越全阶微扰的非微扰尺度，但其奇异量子几何可将其转化为代数增强的光学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维二次能带接触（QBT）处的弱相互作用可产生指数小的对称破缺能隙，该能隙无法被任何有限阶微扰论捕捉，因此需要寻找可观测此类非微扰尺度的物理量。

Method: 对有质量QBT进行解析分析，结合Souza–Wilkens–Martin逆频率光学求和规则；并在已知会因环流序在QBT处产生自发量子反常霍尔质量的相互作用kagome晶格模型中做微观验证。

Result: 量子度规（quantum metric）在动量空间形成热点（hot spot），其布里渊区积分对数发散，故当能隙由相互作用动力学产生时按代数方式增长；该放大同样体现在负一阶纵向光学矩中，尽管普通光电导仍为O(e²/ℏ)、吸收阈值指数小。

Conclusion: 量子几何（quantum geometry）可作为非微扰相互作用尺度的渐近放大器（asymptotic amplifier）。

Abstract: Weak interactions at a two-dimensional quadratic band touching (QBT) can generate an exponentially small symmetry-breaking gap, $m\sim e^{-A/V_1}$, that cannot be captured by every finite order of perturbation theory. We show that the singular quantum geometry of the gapped QBT converts this beyond-all-orders scale into an algebraically enhanced response. The quantum metric develops a momentum-space hot spot whose Brillouin-zone integral diverges logarithmically, and therefore grows algebraically when the gap is dynamically generated by interactions. Through the inverse-frequency Souza--Wilkens--Martin optical sum rule, the same amplification appears in the negative-first longitudinal optical moment, even though the ordinary optical conductivity remains $O(e^2/\hbar)$ and its absorption threshold is exponentially small. We establish these results analytically for a massive QBT and verify them microscopically in an interacting kagome lattice model known to develop a spontaneous quantum anomalous Hall mass at its QBT from loop currents order. Our results identify quantum geometry as an asymptotic amplifier of nonperturbative interaction scales.

</details>


### [111] [t-J model at 50](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35178)
*Józef Spałek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文简要回顾了t-J模型（t-J model）从Hubbard模型出发的原始构建，评述其优缺点，并强调磁性、非常规超导电性（unconventional superconductivity）与Mott-Hubbard局域化之间的关联，最后总结该模型向三轨道情形的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在梳理t-J模型的基本物理特征，并阐明其在讨论关联载流子在窄带中的物理性质时所具的优势与不足，同时将其与磁性和非常规超导电性等核心现象相联系。

Method: 采用综述性梳理的方式，首先从Hubbard模型出发回顾t-J模型的原始表述，随后对其向三轨道情形的推广进行总结归纳。

Result: 给出了t-J模型的原始构建脉络及其独特特征的概述，并特别刻画了窄带内关联载流子的磁性、非常规超导电性与Mott-Hubbard局域化之间的联系，同时列出了三轨道推广中的一些基本物理特征。

Conclusion: t-J模型可从Hubbard模型导出并具备独特的优势与不足；其物理内涵与磁性、非常规超导电性及Mott-Hubbard局域化密切相关，且可推广至三轨道情形。

Abstract: I briefly overview the original formulation of the t-J model starting from Hubbard model, as well as stress its unique features, i.e., advantages and shortcomings when discussing its physical properties. Particular emphasis is put on a brief characterization of connection between magnetism, unconventional superconductivity, and the Mott-Hubbard localization of the correlated carriers in a narrow band. In the second part I summarize the model generalization to the three--orbital situation. The main purpose is to list some basic features of the physics associated with t--J model.

</details>


### [112] [Chiral Weyl--Kondo semimetal and circular photogalvanic effect in a prototype Kondo lattice system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35602)
*Yuan Fang,Kuan-Sen Lin,Mounica Mahankali,Arushi,Kevin Allen,Sanu Mishra,Emilia Morosan,Qimiao Si*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个原型近藤晶格模型（Kondo lattice model），仅保留符合四方晶系对称性的核心耦合，从而在强关联重准粒子谱中稳健地展示对称性所强制的Kramers手性Weyl费米子及相关拓扑节点态，并进一步确定手性Weyl-近藤半金属（chiral Weyl-Kondo semimetal, cWKSM）的圆光电流效应（circular photogalvanic effect）特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性Weyl-近藤半金属为在费米能附近由近藤驱动重构产生手性Weyl准粒子提供了平台，CeGaGe实验被视为该效应的具体实现；本文旨在超越具体材料效应，从模型层面研究其普适特征。

Method: 构建仅包含符合相关四方晶体对称性（tetragonal crystalline symmetries）必要耦合的原型近藤晶格模型（Kondo lattice model），并进行解析与数值计算以求解非线性光学响应。

Result: 该简化模型稳健地展示了对称性强制的Kramers Weyl费米子（Kramers Weyl fermions）与相关拓扑节点态，并在频域中识别出尖锐峰值，作为近藤驱动手性Weyl节点的特征信号；谱的尖锐性反映了强关联电子激发的共振本质。

Conclusion: cWKSM为通过光谱学手段识别由强电子关联诱导的拓扑费米子提供了独特平台，有望为强关联无能隙拓扑物质（strongly correlated gapless topological matter）的理解带来新见解。

Abstract: Chiral Weyl--Kondo semimetals (cWKSM) provide a setting in which chiral Weyl quasiparticles emerge in the immediate vicinity of the Fermi energy from a Kondo-driven reconstruction of the strongly correlated electronic states in chiral heavy fermion systems (K.-S. Lin et al., arXiv:2602.22185). A defining characteristic of this strongly correlated topological state is the Kramers chiral Weyl fermions in the low-energy quasiparticle states. Recently, experiments in CeGaGe have emerged as a concrete realization of the proposed effect (Arushi et al., preprint). Motivated by these findings, here we go beyond the materials-specific effects by constructing a prototype Kondo lattice model; it incorporates only the essential couplings that respect the associated tetragonal crystalline symmetries. This simplification allows us to robustly demonstrate the symmetry-enforced Kramers Weyl fermions and related topological nodal states in the spectrum of heavy quasiparticles. Furthermore, the simplification provides a tractable setting to determine the salient features in the system's nonlinear optical response, the circular photogalvanic effect, in chiral Weyl--Kondo semimetals. Both analytical and numerical calculations identify sharp peaks in the frequency domain as signatures of the Kondo-driven chiral Weyl nodes; the sharpness of the spectrum reflects the resonant nature of the underlying strongly correlated electronic excitations. Thus, cWKSM provides a unique setting to spectroscopically identify topological fermions that are induced by strong electron correlations. As such, our results are expected to bring about much needed new insights into the understanding of strongly correlated gapless topological matter.

</details>


### [113] [Perfect Born Sampling of Symmetric Thermal Tensor Network for Quantum Lattice Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35754)
*Jianxin Gao,Qiaoyi Li,Yuan Gao,Chuanshu Xu,Guoliang Wu,Su Yi,Bin-Bin Chen,Wei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出“完美玻恩采样（perfect Born sampling）”，直接依据玻恩定则（Born rule）从纯化密度矩阵进行重要性采样，并通过采样对称性量子数同时纳入阿贝尔（Abelian）与非阿贝尔（non-Abelian）对称性，从而在热张量网络（thermal tensor networks）中兼具高采样效率与完整对称性加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温量子晶格模型的精确计算是多体物理（many-body physics）的重大挑战。张量网络态的随机采样（stochastic sampling）虽具前景，但既有方案长期面临采样效率与对称性加速无法兼得的根本困境。

Method: 提出面向热张量网络的完美玻恩采样方法，从纯化密度矩阵出发按玻恩定则进行重要性采样，并以采样对称性量子数的方式引入阿贝尔与非阿贝尔对称性；方法分别实现为随机矩阵乘积态（stochastic matrix product states, stoMPS）与随机投影纠缠对态（stochastic projected entangled pair states, stoPEPS）。

Result: 在大尺度量子晶格模型上进行基准测试：stoMPS 精确模拟宽度至 W=10 的柱面上方格点哈伯德模型（Hubbard model），并研究三角晶格哈伯德模型至 T/t = 1/64，揭示半填充时的标量手征序（scalar chiral order）与电子掺杂后的动能铁磁性（kinetic ferromagnetism）；进一步将 stoMPS 扩展至有限温度量子动力学，展示哈伯德模型的光导率（optical conductivity），并推广至 stoPEPS，在量子临界点的 20×20 方格点量子伊辛模型（quantum Ising model）上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该方法将高采样效率与完整对称性加速相结合，可作为研究超低温下平衡态与动力学性质的最先进框架（state-of-the-art framework）。

Abstract: Accurate calculations of quantum lattice models at low temperatures constitute a major challenge in many-body physics. Stochastic sampling of tensor-network states offers a promising route to tackle this problem; however, existing schemes have long faced a fundamental dilemma---sampling efficiency and symmetry acceleration \textit{cannot} be achieved simultaneously. Here we propose a perfect Born sampling approach for thermal tensor networks, which performs importance sampling directly from the purified density matrix via the Born rule and incorporates Abelian and non-Abelian symmetries by sampling symmetry quantum numbers. We benchmark the method, realized as both stochastic matrix product states (stoMPS) and stochastic projected entangled pair states (stoPEPS), on large-scale quantum lattice models. Using stoMPS, we accurately simulate the square-lattice Hubbard model on cylinders up to width $W=10$, and study the triangular-lattice Hubbard model down to $T/t = 1/64$, revealing scalar chiral order at half filling and kinetic ferromagnetism upon electron doping. We further extend the stoMPS method to compute finite-temperature quantum dynamics, as demonstrated by the optical conductivity of the Hubbard model, and generalize it to stoPEPS, as showcased on the $20\times20$ square-lattice quantum Ising model at its quantum critical point. Our method combines high sampling efficiency with full symmetry acceleration, and can be used as a state-of-the-art framework for studying both equilibrium and dynamical properties down to ultralow temperatures.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [CP-Agent: A Harness-Engineered Agent for Crystal Plasticity Simulation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31790)
*Samuel Onimpa Alfred,Abhishek Kumar,Veera Sundararaghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出 CP-Agent，一种基于大语言模型（large language model, LLM）的智能体，能够在自然语言任务驱动下自主完成晶体塑性（crystal plasticity, CP）建模的完整工作流，并在四个案例中验证其可靠性与物理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体塑性模拟虽可预测多晶金属的力学行为，但其常规应用受限于异构工具的手动配置、多步数据流程编排以及本构参数标定等瓶颈，阻碍了系统性参数研究的生产力，因而需要自动化工作流。

Method: 采用 ReAct 范式，智能体负责推理工具选择与调用顺序，并将数值搜索委托给既有优化器；其 harness 由极简系统提示、带类型的工具定义、调度器（dispatcher）及带安全边界的迭代循环组成，领域知识通过工具模式（tool schema）而非硬编码逻辑嵌入。

Result: 四个案例研究：对增材制造 316L 不锈钢的四个滑移参数进行拉伸数据标定；以已发表铜基准验证工作流，重现应力-应变与织构演化；恢复铜的初始晶体学织构并正确识别为弥散初始织构；重现 Mg-Zn-Ca 合金多道次轧制织构演化，串联五道次变形并复现实验观察到的弱化分裂基面织构。各案例均从任务陈述中推断出正确执行序列，多次重复运行稳健，结果具物理可解释性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立 harness engineering 为一种系统化途径，用于自动化晶体塑性建模工作流，并通过可见的推理轨迹（reasoning trace）保持物理可解释性与可审计性。

Abstract: Crystal plasticity (CP) simulations predict the mechanical behavior of polycrystalline metals, yet their routine use is hindered by the manual effort of configuring heterogeneous tools, orchestrating multi-step data pipelines, and calibrating constitutive parameters against experiments. These bottlenecks impede productivity in systematic parameter studies, motivating interest in automated workflows. This study presents CP-Agent, a harness-engineered LLM-based agent that autonomously executes complete CP modeling workflows from natural-language tasks. Operating under the ReAct paradigm, the agent reasons about tool selection and sequencing while delegating numerical search to established optimizers. The harness comprises a minimal system prompt, typed tool definitions, a dispatcher, and a safety-bounded iteration loop, encoding domain knowledge through tool schemas rather than hard-coded logic. CP-Agent is demonstrated on four case studies: calibrating four slip parameters of additively manufactured stainless steel 316L against tensile data; validating the workflow against published copper benchmarks, reproducing stress-strain and texture evolution; recovering the initial crystallographic texture of copper, where the agent correctly identifies a diffuse initial texture; and reproducing the multi-pass rolling texture evolution of a Mg-Zn-Ca alloy, where the agent chains five deformation passes and recovers the experimentally observed weakened, split basal texture. In all cases, the agent inferred the correct execution sequence from the task statement, robustly across repeated runs, and delivered physically interpretable results. This work establishes harness engineering as a systematic approach to automating CP modeling workflows while maintaining physical interpretability and auditability through visible reasoning traces.

</details>


### [115] [Escaping Alignment: A Physical Trap Model of Best-of-N Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32116)
*Marco Biroli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视 Best-of-$N$ 越狱攻击（jailbreaking）的标度律，指出此前观察到的幂律指数随 $N$ 漂移，属对抗数据集有限规模导致的伪影；作者提出一个由四个可解释参数构成的势垒模型（barrier model），可刻画完整的 $(N,M)$ 攻击面及其对生成温度 $T$ 的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作认为 BoN 攻击成功率（attack success rate, ASR）随 $N$ 呈幂律增长，但这一结论受到质疑：指数随 $N$ 变化并呈现指数型交叉，可能只是对抗数据集的有限尺寸效应。同时，完整的双预算 $(N,M)$ 攻击面及其对生成温度 $T$ 的依赖尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 提出一个简单的势垒模型（barrier model）：每个提示（prompt）具有基线安全水平（baseline safety level），每次增强（augmentation）对应一个随机热激活势垒（thermally activated barrier）。模型仅由四个数值参数决定，每个参数均对应一种可解释的安全机制。

Result: 该四参数模型能够确定整个 $(N,M)$ 攻击面；可将 $N \leq 100$ 的预测外推至 $N = 10^4$；能将五个不同模型坍缩（collapse）到同一标度函数（scaling function）上；并可由某一温度下拟合的参数预测其他温度下的 ASR。

Conclusion: BoN 攻击的标度行为可由具备可解释安全机制的势垒模型统一刻画，其幂律现象与指数漂移需重新评估；该模型在模型间、外推范围与温度依赖三方面均展现出良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Best-of-$N$ jailbreaking (BoN) bypasses safeguards of aligned models by drawing $N$ independent augmentations of an unsafe prompt and sampling $M$ completions of each. Previous works have shown that the attack success rate (ASR) seems to follow a power-law in $N$, which we challenge. The exponent drifts with $N$, with an exponential crossover which is a finite-size artifact of the adversarial dataset. Little work has been done to explore the entire two-budget ($N, M$) attack surface as well as its dependence on the generation temperature $T$. We introduce a simple barrier model where each prompt has a baseline safety level and each augmentation a random thermally activated barrier. Then four numbers, each backed by an interpretable safety mechanism, determine the entire ($N, M$) attack surface. They extrapolate predictions from $N \leq 100$ to $N = 10^4$, collapse five distinct models on the same scaling function and predict ASR at different temperatures from the one they were fitted at.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Universality classes of sharp gravitational production of light particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32202)
*Kanta Ito,Yusuke Mikura,Shuichiro Yokoyama*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文建立了光粒子引力产生（gravitational production）在标度因子（scale factor）不同渐近形式间急剧过渡（sharp transition）下的分类。依据过渡在急剧极限 Δη→0 下的正则性（regularity），划分为 C^0、C^1 与 C^2 及以上三个普适类，各自对应二次、对数与无增强的能量密度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清急剧过渡中粒子引力产生的增强机制由何种性质决定。论文指出标度因子在急剧极限下的正则性是控制该增强的关键属性，需要系统分类以区分不同过渡类型的效应。

Method: 方法上引入一族具有过渡持续时间 Δη 的光滑标度因子，以其在急剧过渡极限 Δη→0 下的正则性作为分类依据；并使用一个具体的 C^∞ 标度因子模型，解析推导能量密度，同时通过数值求解模方程（mode equation）进行验证。

Result: 结果给出三类普适行为：C^0 类能量密度呈二次增强 ρ∝Δη^{-2}；C^1 类呈对数增强 ρ∝ln(1/Δη)；C^2 或更光滑类不出现急剧过渡增强。对 C^∞ 模型，解析与数值结果一致，既确认了预测的二次标度关系，也确认了其系数。

Conclusion: 结论表明，标度因子在急剧极限下的正则性是决定急剧过渡增强的关键性质，并据此确立了光粒子引力产生在三类普适类中的分类框架。

Abstract: We establish a classification of the gravitational production of light particles across sharp transitions connecting different asymptotic forms of the scale factor. A family of smooth scale factors with a transition duration $Δη$ is characterized by its regularity in the sharp-transition limit $Δη\to 0$, leading to three qualitatively different universality classes: $C^0$, $C^1$, and $C^2$-or-smoother. We find that the $C^0$ class exhibits a quadratic enhancement in the energy density of produced particles, $ρ\proptoΔη^{-2}$, while the $C^1$ class shows a logarithmic enhancement, $ρ\propto\ln(1/Δη)$. No sharp-transition enhancement occurs for the $C^2$-or-smoother class. Using a specific $C^\infty$ model of the scale factor, we derive the energy density analytically and confirm both the predicted quadratic scaling and its coefficient by numerically solving the mode equation. These results identify the regularity of the scale factor in the sharp limit as the key property controlling the sharp-transition enhancement.

</details>


### [117] [Bimodal mass primordial black holes in three-field inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32572)
*M. M. Nasrolahzadeh,N. Khosravi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 研究非标准动能项的三场暴胀（three-field inflation）中曲率扰动与熵扰动的演化，通过运动学基（kinematic basis）刻画背景轨迹的转向率（turning rate）与非平面挠率（non-planar torsion），并数值构建含中间减速膨胀阶段的三阶段模型，得到双峰曲率功率谱（double-peaked curvature power spectrum），可同时解释暗物质与LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA双黑洞质量范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在刻画多场暴胀（multi-field inflation）中曲率扰动与熵扰动（entropy perturbations）的演化机制，特别是非标准动能项情形下背景轨迹几何量对扰动相互作用的影响，并探索其可观测后果。

Method: 采用场空间中的运动学基（kinematic basis），以转向率（turning rate）与非平面挠率（non-planar torsion）描述背景轨迹；强调扰动从该运动学系统继承的结构，从而分离转向率与挠率在扰动相互作用中的不同作用；并数值研究含中间减速膨胀阶段的三阶段模型。

Result: 数值模型中出现两个分离的瞬态时期（transient epochs），分别放大两类不同模式族（families of modes），生成双峰曲率功率谱（double-peaked curvature power spectrum）。该谱对应形成两种特征质量的原初黑洞（primordial black holes），即 M1∼2×10^{-12}M_⊙ 与 M2∼52M_⊙。

Conclusion: 双峰原初黑洞质量分布可同时解释暗物质（dark matter）与LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA双黑洞（binary black hole）质量范围，表明非标准动能三场暴胀中的运动学结构具有兼容多尺度观测的潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the evolution of curvature and entropy perturbations in three-field inflation with non-canonical kinetic terms. Using the kinematic basis in field space, we characterize the background trajectory through its turning rate and non-planar torsion. We also emphasize the inherited structure of perturbations from the kinematic system, which detaches the distinct roles of turning rate and torsion in the interactions among perturbations. We present and study numerically a three-phase model with an intermediate decelerated expansion stage in which two separated transient epochs amplify two distinct families of modes, generating a double-peaked curvature power spectrum. This double-peaked power spectrum leads to the formation of primordial black holes with two characteristic masses, $M_1\sim2\times10^{-12}M_\odot$ and $M_2\sim52M_\odot$ explaining, simultaneously, both dark matter and the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA binary black hole mass range.

</details>


### [118] [Constant-Roll Inflation as a Competitive Alternative to Slow-Roll Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32865)
*Sandip Biswas,Rajib Saha,Kaushik Bhattacharya*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文从常滚条件（constant-roll condition）出发导出标量功率谱与标量谱指数 $n_s$ 关于常滚参数 $\beta$ 和剩余 e 折数 $\tilde N$ 的解析表达式，并用 Planck 2018 及 Planck 2018 + ACT DR6 数据约束 $\beta$，发现其双峰结构与 $\beta=0$ 慢滚（slow-roll）极限的谱行为一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常滚暴胀（constant-roll inflation）模型中 $\beta$ 为基本常数，需检验其与最新宇宙微波背景（CMB）观测的相容性，并与标准慢滚暴胀范式作比较。

Method: 由常滚条件推导标量功率谱，得到 $n_s$ 关于 $\beta$ 与剩余 e 折数 $\tilde N$ 的解析关系；利用 Planck 2018 及 Planck 2018 + ACT DR6 数据对 $\beta$ 进行参数约束，并通过 pivot 尺度上的标量振幅归一化估计暴胀质量标度 $M$。

Result: $n_s$ 与 $\beta$ 的关系非单调，允许 $\beta<0$ 与 $\beta>0$ 两个分支给出近乎相同的标量倾斜；Planck 2018 下 $\beta$ 后验呈与两分支对应的双峰结构，Planck+ACT 联合分析收紧了 $\beta$ 的允许范围并保持与 $\beta=0$ 慢滚谱点的相容性，数据要求 $\beta$ 很小但不严格为零；两个分支均给出高能标归一化 $M\sim10^{-5}M_{\rm Pl}$。

Conclusion: $\beta\to0$ 极限仅在谱意义上类似慢滚，并不再现标准慢滚动力学；结果表明常滚暴胀在观测上与慢滚暴胀范式具有竞争力，且与最新 CMB 观测的支持程度相当或略优。

Abstract: Starting from the constant-roll condition, which involves the constant-roll parameter $β$, which is a fundamental constant in the constant-roll inflationary models, we derive the scalar power spectrum and obtain an analytic expression for the scalar spectral index $n_s$ in terms of $β$ and the number of remaining e-folds $\tilde N$. The resulting relation is non-monotonic in $β$, allowing two distinct constant-roll branches, one with $β<0$ and one with $β>0$, to reproduce nearly the same observed scalar tilt. We constrain $β$ using Planck 2018 CMB data, and further using the combined Planck 2018 + ACT DR6 data. For Planck 2018, the posterior distribution of $β$ exhibits a bimodal structure associated with the two analytical branches, while the combined Planck+ACT analysis tightens the allowed range of $β$ and shows continued consistency with the $β=0$ slow-roll-like spectral point. The data constrain $β$ to be small, but do not require it to vanish exactly. We also estimate the inflationary mass scale $M$ by using the observed scalar-amplitude normalization at the pivot scale. Both the negative and positive $β$ branches give a high-scale inflationary normalization of order $M\sim10^{-5}M_{\rm Pl}$. We show that the $β\to 0$ limit is only spectrally slow-roll like, but does not reproduce standard slow-roll dynamics. Our results show that constant-roll inflation is observationally competitive with the usual slow-roll inflationary paradigm. The constant roll inflation model is supported by the latest CMB observations equally well or marginally better when compared with the slow roll inflation model.

</details>


### [119] [The separate universe approach with non minimal coupling to gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34788)
*Hugo Holland,Julien Grain*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文将分离宇宙方法（separate universe approach）的有效性条件研究推广至非最小耦合于引力的多场暴胀模型，比较了Jordan框架下大尺度宇宙学微扰（cosmological perturbations）动力学与分离宇宙图像的推导结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离宇宙方法是大尺度宇宙学微扰、随机暴胀（stochastic inflation）与δN形式主义（δN formalism）的必要步骤，但其在非最小耦合多场情形下的有效性尚待检验，故需扩展对其有效条件的研究。

Method: 在允许场与引力存在非最小耦合的非线性σ模型（non linear sigma models）中，比较微扰理论在宇宙学大尺度上的动力学与分离宇宙图像所推导的动力学，并对Jordan框架模型与其经典等价的Einstein框架描述的界限进行对比。

Result: 发现只要所考虑模式的波长大于由背景演化导出的若干下界，分离宇宙方法即可充分复现大尺度微扰的动力学；这些下界进一步与经典等价的Einstein框架描述中导出的相应条件作了比较。

Conclusion: 分离宇宙方法在非最小耦合多场暴胀模型中仍然有效（在给定波长下界条件下），且这些条件在大场极限下的类Higgs玩具暴胀模型（Higgs-like toy model）中得到推导与对照。

Abstract: In this paper we extend a study of the validity conditions of the separate universe approach of cosmological perturbations to models of inflation with multiple fields that are non minimally coupled to gravity. The separate universe approach effectively describes the universe as a collection of homogeneous and isotropic patches, leading to an effective description of cosmological perturbations at large scales. This approximation is a necessary step in stochastic inflation and in the $δ\N$ formalism. We study and compare the dynamics of cosmological perturbation theory at large scales to the dynamics derived in the separate universe picture for non linear sigma models where we have allowed a non minimal coupling between the fields and gravity. We found that the separate universe approach adequately reproduces the dynamics of the large scale perturbations, provided the wavelength of the modes considered is greater than a list of lower bounds derived from the background evolution of the model we consider. These lower bounds are then compared to those derived in the classically equivalent Einstein frame description of the Jordan frame model at hand. In particular these conditions are derived and compared for a Higgs-like toy model of inflation in the large field limit.

</details>


### [120] [Gauge Backreaction in Standard Model Warm Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35756)
*Rafid H. Dejrah,Anish Ghoshal*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文聚焦标准模型温暴胀（Standard-Model warm inflation, SMWI）中QCD规范等离子体的实时响应，指出缓慢弛豫的规范螺旋度响应不能被简单吸收为有效摩擦系数，需作为动力学自由度演化。研究在完整扰动协方差系统中纳入夸克轴密度、硬螺旋度模式、QCD sphaleron（斯法勒龙）及有效 $2\leftrightarrow3$ 通道，发现反作用显著但以间接为主，并受局部高斯输运闭合限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMWI 使 QCD 成为暴胀耗散的微观起源，因此规范等离子体的实时响应（real-time response）直接进入宇宙学预言。既有处理可能将缓慢弛豫的规范螺旋度（gauge-helicity）响应简化为有效摩擦系数，本文质疑该近似的普适性并检验其动力学后果。

Method: 演化夸克轴密度（quark axial densities）与匹配的硬螺旋度模式（matched hard-helicity mode），在完整扰动协方差系统（full perturbation covariance system）中引入 QCD sphaleron 与有效 $2\leftrightarrow3$ 通道，两者同时产生电荷转移与相关随机源（correlated stochastic sources）。

Result: 反作用显著但主要间接：规范螺旋度屏蔽（gauge-helicity screening）可在强分支上将温耗散比降低量级为1的分数；$2\leftrightarrow3$ 通道对零密度 sphaleron 摩擦的直接贡献仅为 $0.2\%-0.7\%$。振幅归一化轨迹在 $n_s$ 与 $r_{\rm vac}$ 上均发生移动。

Conclusion: 相关轨迹缺乏参数上宽泛的硬-软层级（hard-soft hierarchy），并超出严格线性响应（linear response）范围，故预言须在所述局部高斯输运闭合（local Gaussian transport closure）框架内理解；无自由参数的SM结果需要有限亲和（finite-affinity）的实时 $SU(3)$ 漂移、磁化率与噪声核。

Abstract: Standard-Model warm inflation (SMWI) makes QCD the microscopic origin of inflationary dissipation, so the real-time response of the gauge plasma enters the cosmological prediction. We show that a slowly relaxing gauge-helicity response cannot, in general, be absorbed into an effective friction coefficient and must instead remain dynamical. We therefore evolve the quark axial densities and a matched hard-helicity mode, with QCD sphalerons and an effective $2\leftrightarrow3$ channel generating both charge transfer and correlated stochastic sources in the full perturbation covariance system. The resulting backreaction is substantial but mainly indirect: gauge-helicity screening can reduce the warm dissipation ratio by an order-one fraction on the strong branch, while the $2\leftrightarrow3$ channel contributes only $0.2\%-0.7\%$ of the zero-density sphaleron friction directly, shifting the amplitude-normalized trajectory in both $n_s$ and $r_{\rm vac}$. The relevant trajectories, however, lack a parametrically broad hard-soft hierarchy and extend beyond strict linear response. The predictions should therefore be understood within the stated local Gaussian transport closure; a parameter-free Standard-Model (SM) result requires finite-affinity real-time $SU(3)$ drift, susceptibility, and noise kernels.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Model-independent searches and anomaly detection at the CMS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31739)
*Tamas Almos Vami,for the CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该会议报告综述了CMS基于机器学习的异常检测（anomaly detection）策略，以模型无关（model-agnostic）方式搜寻超出标准模型（beyond the standard model）的新物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CERN LHC迄今未给出明确的新物理信号，这促使研究者采用不预设特定信号假设的搜寻策略。

Method: 利用基于机器学习的异常检测，对138 fb$^{-1}$、$\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV的质子-质子对撞数据中具有非典型子结构（substructure）的双喷注共振（dijet resonance）进行搜寻，采用五种互补的异常检测方法；并将AXOL1TL与CICADA两种无监督算法部署于CMS一级触发（level-1 trigger）的现场可编程门阵列（field-programmable gate arrays, FPGA）中实时运行。

Result: 该技术已通过在不使用标签的情况下恢复顶夸克（top quark）而在数据上得到直接验证；实时算法在2024年取数期间筛选出超过40亿个对撞事件。

Conclusion: 报告还概述了将共振异常检测扩展至事件级（event-level）观测量，表明该模型无关搜寻方向已从离线分析走向实时触发并持续拓展。

Abstract: The absence of a clear signal of physics beyond the standard model at the CERN LHC motivates search strategies that do not presuppose a specific signal hypothesis. This proceedings present machine-learning-based anomaly detection to search for new physics in a model-agnostic way. The first such search by CMS looks for dijet resonances whose jets have substructure atypical of jets initiated by light quarks or gluons, using five complementary anomaly detection methods applied to 138 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV. The technique has been validated directly on data by recovering the top quark without the use of labels. The program has also moved into real time: two unsupervised algorithms, AXOL1TL and CICADA, now run on field-programmable gate arrays inside the CMS level-1 trigger and selected more than four billion collision events during 2024 data taking. Finally, the extension of resonant anomaly detection to event-level observables is outlined.

</details>


### [122] [Recent CLFV searches at Belle and Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32531)
*Vindhyawasini Prasad*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该报告综述了Belle与Belle II实验在带电轻子味破坏（CLFV）衰变方面的最新结果，涵盖τ轻子、B介子与底夸克偶素（bottomonium）系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型（SM）中因中微子无质量且缺少右手中微子而禁止带电轻子味破坏，但中微子振荡的发现表明中微子有质量、轻子味并非严格对称，因此CLFV成为检验新物理的重要探针。

Method: 在标准模型中引入右手中微子的最小扩展可通过含中微子的圈图（loop-level diagrams）产生CLFV过程；Belle与Belle II利用在Υ(4S)共振附近采集的世界上最大的非对称e+e-对撞数据样本，搜寻τ轻子、B介子和底夸克偶素态的CLFV衰变。

Result: 最小扩展预言的CLFV过程极为稀有，例如μ→eγ的分支比（branching fraction）预计约为10^-54，远低于当前对撞机实验的灵敏度；而若干新物理模型预言的CLFV衰变率则处于Belle与Belle II等现有实验可探测范围之内。

Conclusion: 报告总结了Belle与Belle II在τ轻子、B介子及底夸克偶素CLFV衰变上的最新研究结果，表明此类大统计量数据可为新物理模型提供重要检验。

Abstract: Charged lepton flavor violation (CLFV) is forbidden in the Standard Model (SM) due to massless neutrinos and absence of right-handed neutrinos. The discovery of neutrino oscillations established that the neutrinos are massive and the lepton flavor is not an exact symmetry of nature. A minimal extension of the SM that incorporates right-handed neutrinos gives rise to CLFV through loop-level diagrams involving neutrinos. However, such processes are extremely rare, for instance, the branching fraction of $μ\to eγ$ is expected to be at the level of $10^{-54}$, far beyond the sensitivity of current collider experiments. In contrast, several new physics models predict CLFV decay rates within the sensitivity of current experiments, such as the Belle and Belle II experiments, which have collected the world's largest asymmetric $e^+e^-$ collision data near the $Υ(4S)$ resonance. The large data sample has utilized to explore the possibility of CLFV decays of $τ$ leptons, $B$ mesons, and bottomonium states. The report reviews some latest results on these CLFV decays at Belle and Belle II.

</details>

Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 BESIII 探测器在 3.773 GeV 采集的 20.3 fb⁻¹ 数据，改进了 ψ(3770) → γη_c(1S, 2S) 辐射跃迁（radiative transitions）的搜寻，未观测到显著信号，并给出分支比与部分衰变宽度的 90% 置信水平（confidence level）上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在利用更大统计量的数据改进对 ψ(3770) 到 η_c(1S, 2S) 辐射跃迁的搜寻灵敏度，此前的结果受限于积分亮度（integrated luminosity）较低。

Method: 使用 BESIII 探测器在 BEPCII 对撞机上于质心能量（center-of-mass energy）3.773 GeV 采集的 20.3 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻ 湮灭（annihilation）数据，通过强子衰变 η_c(1S, 2S) → K⁰_S K^± π^∓ 进行搜寻。

Result: 未观测到显著信号。分支比乘积的 90% 置信水平上限为：η_c(1S) 跃迁 5.0 × 10⁻⁶，η_c(2S) 跃迁 3.7 × 10⁻⁶；部分衰变宽度（partial decay widths）上限为 Γ(ψ(3770) → γη_c(1S)) < 5.5 keV 与 Γ(ψ(3770) → γη_c(2S)) < 29.4 keV。

Conclusion: 相比此前结果，积分亮度提高约七倍，使 η_c(1S) 与 η_c(2S) 跃迁的上限分别降低约三倍与两倍。

Abstract: Based on an integrated luminosity of $20.3~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of $e^{+}e^{-}$ annihilation data collected at a center-of-mass energy of $3.773~\rm{GeV}$ with the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, an improved search for the radiative transitions $ψ(3770) \to γη_{c}(1S, 2S)$ is performed using the hadronic decays $η_{c}(1S, 2S) \to K^{0}_{S} K^{\pm} π^{\mp}$. No significant signal is observed. The corresponding 90$\%$ confidence level upper limits on the product branching fractions are set to be $5.0 \times 10^{-6}$ for the $η_{c}(1S)$ transition and $3.7 \times 10^{-6}$ for the $η_{c}(2S)$ transition. The 90$\%$ confidence level upper limits on the partial decay widths are also reported to be $Γ(ψ(3770) \to γη_{c}(1S)) < 5.5$ keV and $Γ(ψ(3770) \to γη_{c}(2S)) < 29.4~\rm{keV}$. With about seven times larger integrated luminosity than used previously, these results lower the upper limits by approximately a factor of three and two for the $η_{c}(1S)$ and $η_{c}(2S)$ transitions, respectively.

</details>


### [124] [First measurement of the antihydrogen production cross section through the charge-exchange reaction of low-energy antiprotons with orthopositronium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33990)
*GBAR Collaboration,P. Adrich,I. Belosevic,M. Chung,P. Cladé,P. Comini,P. Crivelli,P. Debu,A. Douillet,S. Geffroy,S. Guellati-Khelifa,P. Guichard,P. -A. Hervieux,L. Hilico,P. Indelicato,S. Jonsell,J. -P. Karr,B. Kim,S. Kim,E. -S. Kim,N. Kuroda,B. Lee,L. Liszkay,D. Lunney,G. Manfredi,B. Mansoulié,V. Martimort,M. Matusiak,V. Nesvizhevsky,F. Nez,K. Park,N. Paul,E. Perez,P. Pérez,C. Regenfus,C. Roumegou,J. -Y. Roussé,F. Schmidt-Kaler,K. Szymczyk,T. A. Tanaka,B. Tuchming,D. P. van der Werf,D. Won,S. Wronka,P. Yzombard*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: GBAR 实验首次利用反质子束测量了反质子与正电子素云碰撞生成反氢的电荷交换截面（charge-exchange cross section），结果与近期三体理论计算一致，并为反氢引力加速度测量提供关键输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反氢（antihydrogen）形成速率及其截面数据是反物质实验的重要输入，尤其服务于 GBAR 合作组计划的反氢引力加速度（gravitational acceleration）测量。此前尚缺乏基于反质子束的电荷交换截面测量。

Method: 利用 CERN 的 AD-ELENA 设施提供的反质子束，在 4 与 6 keV 反质子动能下，测量反质子轰击正电子素（positronium）云生成反氢的形成速率，从而获得电荷交换截面。

Result: 测得截面为 6.2 keV 能量下 $(14.1 \pm 1.3 \mathrm{(stat)} ^{+2.2}_{-1.4} \mathrm{(sys)}) \times 10^{-16}$ cm$^2$，4.15 keV 能量下 $(8.7 \pm 2.4 \mathrm{(stat)} ^{+1.5}_{-0.09} \mathrm{(sys)}) \times 10^{-16}$ cm$^2$，与近期理论三体计算（three-body calculations）相符。

Conclusion: 该工作为首次使用反质子束完成的电荷交换截面测量，其结果为反物质实验（如 GBAR 计划的反氢引力加速度测量）提供了重要输入。

Abstract: The GBAR experiment has measured the formation rate of antihydrogen from antiproton impact on a positronium cloud for antiproton kinetic energies of 4 and 6 keV. This is the first charge-exchange cross section measurement performed using antiproton beams, which are provided by the AD-ELENA facility at CERN. The measured cross section values are $(14.1 \pm 1.3 \mathrm{(stat)} ^{+2.2}_{-1.4} \mathrm{(sys)}) \times 10^{-16}$~cm$^2$ at 6.2 keV energy and $(8.7 \pm 2.4 \mathrm{(stat)} ^{+1.5}_{-0.09} \mathrm{(sys)}) \times 10^{-16}$~cm$^2$ at 4.15 keV energy, and agree with recent theoretical three-body calculations for antihydrogen formation. These measurements are an important input for experiments with antimatter, e.g the planned measurements of antihydrogen gravitational acceleration in the GBAR collaboration.

</details>


### [125] [Using Graph Neural Networks for the segmentation of overlapping objects in high granularity calorimeters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34500)
*Matthieu Melennec,Frédéric Magniette*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究面向高亮度大型强子对撞机（High-Luminosity LHC）高 granularity 量能器（calorimeter）中粒子簇射（particle shower）重叠问题，提出一种优化的图神经网络（Graph Neural Network, GNN）分割模块，通过预测节点级能量分数来分离重叠的双光子簇射，并以降低算法复杂度实现具有竞争力的分离效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度大型强子对撞机（High-Luminosity LHC）下的高 granularity 量能器（calorimeter）面临粒子簇射（particle shower）相互重叠的挑战，现有方法难以有效解析，因此需要新的算法来分离重叠簇射。

Method: 提出一种优化的图神经网络（Graph Neural Network, GNN）分割模块（segmentation block），输出节点级（node-level）能量分数，用于解开重叠的双光子簇射（two-photon shower）；同时对图构建（graph construction）与卷积（convolution）操作进行加速。

Result: 该流程在显著降低算法复杂度（algorithmic complexity）的同时，达到了具有竞争力的分离效率（separation efficiency）。

Conclusion: 通过在建模与计算两端的优化，GNN 分割模块可在高 granularity 量能器重叠簇射分离任务中以更低复杂度实现有效性能，显示出作为高亮度 LHC 场景下新算法的潜力。

Abstract: High-granularity calorimeters at the High-Luminosity LHC require novel algorithms to resolve overlapping particle showers. We present an optimised Graph Neural Network (GNN) segmentation block that predicts node-level energy fractions to disentangle overlapping two-photon showers. By accelerating graph construction and convolution operations, our pipeline achieves competitive separation efficiency with significantly reduced algorithmic complexity.

</details>


### [126] [A study of CMS analysis pipelines through the Integration Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34698)
*Mohamed Aly,Peter Elmer,Peter Fackeldey,Oksana Shadura*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 为应对高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）带来的海量数据挑战，本文介绍了 CMS 正在开发的分析流程（analysis pipelines），其核心是由 IRIS-HEP 主导的集成挑战（Integration Challenge），作为端到端集成测试评估软件栈的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即将运行的 HL-LHC 将产生前所未有的高能物理（HEP）数据量，虽带来重大科学发现机遇，但其规模对传统分析工作流构成挑战，因此需要新的分析流程与配套软件栈。

Method: 所述流程基于更广泛的科学 Python 生态系统（scientific Python ecosystem），并辅以专为 HEP 设计的解决方案；以集成挑战作为端到端集成测试，通过实现完整的物理分析流程来评估工具互操作性（interoperability）与分析人员的整体使用体验。

Result: 当前流程涵盖列式数据处理（columnar data processing）、机器学习、统计推断（statistical inference）与可视化任务，覆盖多种 CMS 分析场景；同时探索了使用多样工具与数据格式高效交付 skimmed 数据的策略，并评估了 ServiceX 数据交付系统。

Conclusion: 测试阶段还调研了若干原型服务，如直方图即服务（histogram-as-a-service）及其他可能支持未来 HL-LHC 分析工作流的新兴服务；该工作旨在评估软件栈在真实物理分析中的就绪度并提升 HL-LHC 时代分析设施的准备水平。

Abstract: The upcoming High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC) at CERN will deliver an unprecedented volume of data for High Energy Physics (HEP). This wealth of information offers significant opportunities for scientific discovery, but its scale challenges traditional analysis workflows. In this paper, we present CMS analysis pipelines being developed to meet HL-LHC demands. These pipelines build on the broader scientific Python ecosystem, complemented by solutions specifically designed for HEP. A central focus of this work is the Integration Challenge, an IRIS-HEP led effort aimed at assessing the readiness of developed software stack to be used in real world physics analysis and improving the readiness of analysis facilities for the HL-LHC era. The Integration Challenge acts as an end-to-end integration test: by implementing a complete physics analysis pipeline, it evaluates tool interoperability and the overall user experience for analysts. The current pipeline includes columnar data processing, machine learning, statistical inference, and visualization tasks covering a variety of CMS analysis scenarios. In addition, the Integration Challenge explores efficient strategies for delivering skimmed data using diverse tools and data formats, as well as evaluating the ServiceX data-delivery system for HEP analyses. Throughout the testing phase, we also investigated several prototype services, such as histogram-as-a-service capabilities, along with other emerging services that may support future HL-LHC analysis workflows.

</details>


### [127] [Muon Detection and Direction Reconstruction with the Upgraded 1-ton Water Cherenkov Prototype Detector at CJPL-I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35135)
*Yapeng Wang,Yuzi Yang,Shaomin Chen,Wei Dou,Haoyang Fu,Guanghua Gong,Lei Guo,Ziyi Guo,XiangPan Ji,Jianmin Li,Jinjing Li,Bo Liang,Ye Liang,Ling Liu,Qian Liu,Zhiyi Liu,Wentai Luo,Ming Qi,Wenhui Shao,Haozhe Sun,Jian Tang,Yuyi Wang,Zhe Wang,Changxu Wei,Jun Weng,Yiyang Wu,Benda Xu,Chuang Xu,Tong Xu,Tao Xue,Haoyan Yang,Aiqiang Zhang,Bin Zhang,Xinshun Zhang,Yang Zhang,Zhicai Zhang,Lin Zhao,Yangheng Zheng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 锦屏中微子实验1吨原型探测器（JNE-1ton）完成硬件升级，在2400米岩石覆盖下的中国锦屏地下实验室（CJPL-I）以水模式和液闪模式成功运行；水模式数据表明缪子探测效率显著提升，方向重建精度大幅改善，并首次在CJPL实现低成本水探测器测量缪子通量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在通过硬件升级提升探测器性能，验证在极深地下实验室（CJPL）采用低成本水探测器开展缪子通量测量的可行性，并为中微子（neutrino）诱导事件研究开辟新途径。

Method: 升级内容包括重新设计机械支撑结构与光电倍增管（PMT）布局，将原滨松（Hamamatsu）PMT更换为北方夜视的8英寸MCP-PMT，PMT数量由30个增至60个。随后在水模式下分别运行85天（Water-I）与79天（Water-II）并采集数据。

Result: Water-II的缪子探测效率较升级前液闪探测器提高约59%；测得缪子通量φ_I+II = (3.55 ± 0.43_stat ± 0.28_syst) × 10^-10 cm^-2 s^-1，与先前测量一致；缪子方向重建不确定度约6°，降至原值的30%；并清晰识别出一个罕见的上行缪子事件。

Conclusion: 升级后探测器性能得到验证，方向重建精度显著提升；罕见上行缪子事件被排除于宇宙线缪子通量样本之外，为中微子诱导事件研究提供新窗口。该工作首次表明CJPL可利用成本有效的水探测器进行缪子通量测量。

Abstract: The 1-ton prototype detector for Jinping neutrino experiment (JNE-1ton) has completed its hardware upgrade and has been successfully operated in both water and liquid-scintillator modes at CJPL-I, which is situated under a 2400-m rock overburden. During the upgrade, the detector's mechanical support structure and PMT layout were redesigned. The original Hamamatsu PMTs were replaced with 8-inch MCP-PMTs from North Night Vision, increasing the PMT count from 30 to 60. The detector was then operated in water mode for 85 days (Water-I) and 79 days (Water-II). From the accumulated water-mode data, we derive a muon detection efficiency (Water-II) that is approximately 59% higher than that of the pre-upgrade liquid scintillator detector. The measured muon flux is $φ_{\text{I+II}} = (3.55 \pm 0.43_{\mathrm{stat}}\pm 0.28_{\mathrm{syst}}) \times 10^{-10}~\mathrm{cm}^{-2}\mathrm{s}^{-1}$, which is consistent with the previous measurement. Owing to the characteristic angular dependence of Cherenkov radiation and the increased PMT coverage, the uncertainty in muon direction reconstruction is approximately 6$^\circ$, which corresponds to a reduction to 30% of its previous value. Notably, one rare up-going muon event was clearly identified. It is excluded from the cosmic-ray muon flux sample and opens a new window for neutrino-induced event studies in the deepest underground laboratory in China. This work marks the first time that CJPL has employed a cost-effective water detector for muon flux measurement.

</details>

### [128] [Quantum undular bores, rainbows, and event horizons in superfluid dam breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32232)
*Liam M. Farrell,Wyatt Kirkby,Alex Harris,David Tyler,Maxim Olshanii,Duncan H. J. O'Dell*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文利用 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程研究玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate, BEC）势垒突然撤除后的准一维量子溃坝（quantum dam break）问题，涵盖微扰与非微扰两个区域。微扰区形成与潮涌（undular tidal bore）类比的外传色散波包，长时具有基于 Airy 函数的解析形式，并与双彩虹焦散现象共享射线结构；非微扰区在 Δn ≥ (8/9)n₁ 时出现自诱导声视界（sonic horizon）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 势垒的突然撤除会引发量子版流体力学溃坝，其波动力学与自旋系统畴壁等局域缺陷问题具有相似性。研究旨在借助 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程刻画该过程在微扰与非微扰密度差区域的波结构，并建立与已有焦散、声视界现象的类比。

Method: 以 Δn 表示水坝两侧上库 n₁ 与下库 n₀ 的初始密度差，采用 Gross-Pitaevskii 方程在准一维情形下分析两类区域：微扰区（Δn ≪ n₀）与非微扰区（Δn ∼ n₁）。此外讨论量子起伏放大的可能实验方案，以及利用盒式陷阱（box trap）中恒定流实现色散波产生的替代方案。

Result: 微扰区形成一对向外传播的色散波包，可视为量子版无旋潮汐涌，长时具有由 Airy 函数积分给出的解析形式，波长随 (ℏ²t)^{1/3} 增长。该问题具有与双彩虹自然焦散现象相同的射线结构，包括两弓之间的 Alexander 暗带；水坝破裂中的中间密度平台被识别为类似的“静默带”（silent band），其中仅存在倏逝声波。非微扰区在 Δn ≥ (8/9)n₁ 时出现自诱导声视界。

Conclusion: 量子溃坝问题在微扰区由 Airy 函数描述的色散波包主导，并与双彩虹焦散的射线结构及暗带现象形成类比，中间密度平台对应仅支持倏逝波的静默带；非微扰区则因足够大的密度差产生自诱导声视界。文中亦讨论量子起伏放大与盒式陷阱恒定流等实验实现途径。

Abstract: The sudden removal of a potential barrier from a Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) gives rise to a quantum version of a hydrodynamic dam break and leads to rich wave dynamics that have similarities to other localized defect problems such as domain wall dynamics in spin systems. Denoting $Δn$ as the initial difference in density between the upper $n_{1}$ and lower $n_{0}$ reservoirs on either side of the dam, we use the Gross-Pitaevskii equation to study the quasi-one dimensional case in both perturbative ($Δn \ll n_{0}$) and non-perturbative ($Δn \sim n_{1}$) regimes. In the perturbative regime a pair of outwardly propagating dispersive wavepackets forms which can be viewed as quantum versions of undular tidal bores that have analytic forms at long times in terms of the integrals of Airy functions with a wavelength that grows as $(\hbar^2 t)^{1/3}$. Airy functions are the universal wave functions that dress structurally stable fold caustics where pairs of rays coalesce, and, indeed, we show that the quantum dam break problem has the same ray structure as the naturally occurring caustic phenomenon of a double rainbow, including Alexander's dark band between the two bows where no light is scattered: we identify the intermediate density plateau in the dam break as an analogous `silent band' where only evanescent sound waves can exist. In the opposite regime of a non-perturbative dam break we show that a self-induced sonic horizon occurs when $Δn \geq (8/9)n_{1}$. A discussion of possible experimental schemes for amplification of quantum undulations is included as well as an alternative scheme for dispersive wave generation where a constant flow is imprinted on a BEC in box trap.

</details>


### [129] [Pancake-shaped vortex droplets in dipolar molecular BECs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32342)
*Zibin Zhao,Xinyi Tang,Tianmiao Zhang,Zhaopin Chen,Huanbo Luo,Guihua Chen,Bin Liu,Boris A. Malomed,Yongyao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作研究了强各向异性偶极-偶极相互作用支撑的扁平状量子液滴（quantum droplets, QDs）中嵌入涡旋的自囚禁模式，揭示了单电荷涡旋的稳定性、S=2 涡旋核分裂行为以及高电荷涡旋的稳定条件，并展示了碰撞与调控偶极相互作用所引发的不同动力学区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性相互作用对量子流体中的拓扑激发（topological excitations）有深刻影响；微波屏蔽极性分子（microwave-shielded polar molecules）研究的近期进展为在强各向异性偶极-偶极相互作用下探索自囚禁涡旋态提供了动机。

Method: 引入并分析扁平状（pancake-shaped）量子液滴中嵌入涡旋的自囚禁模式，该模式由强各向异性偶极-偶极相互作用维持；考察不同涡旋电荷数 S 的稳定性、核结构，以及涡旋量子液滴间的正碰（head-on collisions）与偶极相互作用柱对称分量的调控。

Result: 单电荷（S=1）涡旋的稳定区域与基态量子液滴（S=0）几乎重合，表明涡旋态的鲁棒性；强各向异性使 S=2 涡旋量子液滴的核心分裂为分离的单元核，其角动量与稳定性受单元核间距影响；S>2 的高电荷涡旋在粒子数与核间距足够大时同样稳定；S=±1 的束缚涡旋-反涡旋对（bound vortex-antivortex pairs）不稳定。正碰呈现回弹（rebound）、融合（merger）与碎裂（fragmentation）等不同区域；调控偶极相互作用的柱对称分量表现出显著符号依赖响应：正向调控保持自束缚涡旋，负向调控导致膨胀与碎裂。

Conclusion: 微波缀饰分子量子液滴（microwave-dressed molecular QDs）为自囚禁涡旋态的实现提供了鲁棒平台，显示强各向异性如何重塑其结构、稳定性与动力学。

Abstract: Anisotropic interactions profoundly affect topological excitations in quantum fluids. Motivated by recent advances in the studies of microwave-shielded polar molecules, we introduce self-trapped modes in the form of pancake-shaped quantum droplets (QDs) with embedded vorticity, which are maintained by strongly anisotropic dipole-dipole interactions. The stability region of singly charged ($S=1$) vortices nearly coincides with that of the ground-state QDs ($S=0$), demonstrating the robustness of the vortex states. The strong anisotropy of the system splits the core (pivot) of vortex QDs with $S=2$ into separated unitary ones. The angular momentum and stability of the state with $S=2$ are affected by the separation between the unitary cores. Higher-charge vortex QDs with $S>2$ are stable too, for sufficiently large particle numbers and inter-core separations. On the other hand, bound vortex-antivortex pairs with $S=\pm 1$ are unstable. Head-on collisions between the vortex QDs exhibit distinct regimes, including rebound, merger, and fragmentation. Tuning the cylindrically symmetric component of the dipolar interaction reveals a pronounced sign-dependent response: positive tuning preserves the self-bound vortex, whereas negative tuning drives expansion and fragmentation. The results demonstrate that the microwave-dressed molecular QDs offer a robust platform for the realization of self-trapped vortex states, demonstrating how the strong anisotropy reshapes their structure, stability, and dynamics.

</details>


### [130] [A projection operator approach for ultracold bosons coupled to a cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34908)
*Sarbarish Sen,Bimalendu Deb,K. Sengupta*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文采用投影算符技术（projection operator technique）研究二维Bose-Hubbard模型与高精细度腔耦合系统的平衡相与非平衡淬火/斜坡动力学。通过系统消去高能虚跳跃过程，在平均场处理腔诱导长程相互作用的同时纳入短程量子涨落，发现相边界被显著修正，并揭示淬火动力学的单频/多频特征及斜坡速率上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷玻色子（ultracold bosons）系统中局域短程相互作用与腔介导长程相互作用相互竞争，其平衡相图与淬火动力学亟需超越传统平均场的处理方法，以刻画短程量子涨落的作用。

Method: 采用投影算符技术，系统消去高能虚跳跃过程，从而纳入短程量子涨落对相图的影响，同时以平均场理论（mean-field theory）处理腔诱导的长程相互作用。

Result: 超流（SF）、超固态（SS）、电荷密度波（CDW）与莫特绝缘体（MI）相之间的相边界因量子涨落而被显著修正。淬火至临界点附近时动力学呈现单频振荡；深入SF相的淬火则涉及多个频率。与Gutzwiller平均场理论（Gutzwiller mean-field theory）的对比表明量子涨落对淬火后动力学具有重要作用。

Conclusion: 量子涨落对相图与后淬火动力学均具关键影响，该特征可通过实验检验；同时识别出斜坡动力学中保证方法准确的斜坡速率上限（ramp rate）。

Abstract: We employ a projection operator technique to study the equilibrium phases and the non-equilibrium quench and ramp dynamics of ultracold bosons described by a two-dimensional (2D) Bose-Hubbard model coupled to a high-finesse cavity with competing local short-range and cavity-mediated long-range interactions. Our analysis relies on a systematic elimination of high-energy virtual hopping processes; this enables us to capture the effects of short-range quantum fluctuations on the phase diagram of the model while treating the cavity-induced long-range interaction within mean-field theory. We find that the phase boundaries between the superfluid (SF), supersolid (SS), charge-density-wave (CDW) and Mott insulating (MI) phases are significantly modified by the presence of these fluctuations. We use this approach to study the dynamics of bosons following a quench/ramp from the CDW to the SS and SF phases; our analysis indicates the presence of oscillatory dynamics characterized by a single frequency due to a quench which takes the system near the critical point. In contrast, for a quench deep inside the SF phase, the dynamics involves multiple frequencies. An explicit comparison with Gutzwiller mean-field theory shows the importance of quantum fluctuations in shaping such post-quench dynamics; this feature may be tested experimentally as we discuss. We also study the ramp dynamics of these bosons and identify a bound on the ramp rate above which the present method is expected to produce accurate results.

</details>


### [131] [Spontaneous breaking of continuous scale invariance and Efimovian-like dynamics in a driven-dissipative harmonic oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34926)
*Zehui Wu,Jian Yu,Jian-Song Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究一个驱动-耗散谐振子（driven-dissipative harmonic oscillator）中连续标度对称性（continuous scale invariance）的自发破缺，证明无需多体相互作用即可出现类 Efimov 动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Efimov 效应通过三体束缚态的几何级数体现离散标度不变性（discrete scale invariance），类似对称性也见于标度不变强相互作用费米气体（Fermi gases）的 Efimovian 膨胀中；但强关联是否为必要条件尚不清楚。

Method: 构建一个驱动与耗散参数均按 1/t 标度的简单驱动-耗散谐振子，并通过变换到对数时间（logarithmic time），将动力学归结为一个非厄米超算符（non-Hermitian superoperator），分析其谱分解。

Result: 谱分解出现一对实部相反、虚部相等的本征模，标志连续标度不变性的自发破缺，并产生服从离散标度律的对数周期衰减振荡（log-periodic decay oscillations）；振荡的起始与对数时间中的 PT 对称破缺转变（PT-symmetry breaking transition）重合，且对弱非线性相互作用与微扰具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，连续时间标度对称性的自发破缺与类 Efimov 动力学的出现，甚至可在不含多体相互作用的简单驱动-耗散系统中被观测到，暗示强关联并非必要条件。

Abstract: The Efimov effect manifests discrete scale invariance through a geometric series of three-body bound states. Analogous discrete scale symmetry has been observed in the Efimovian expansion of scale-invariant strongly interacting Fermi gases. However, it is unclear whether strong correlation is necessary. Here we investigate the spontaneous breaking of continuous scale invariance in a simple driven-dissipative harmonic oscillator whose driving and dissipation parameters scale as $1/t$. By transforming to logarithmic time, we show that the dynamics is governed by a non-Hermitian superoperator whose spectral decomposition reveals the emergence of a pair of eigenmodes with opposite real parts and equal imaginary parts. This signals the spontaneous breaking of continuous scale invariance and gives rise to Efimovian-like oscillations: log-periodic decay oscillations obeying discrete scaling laws. The onset of oscillations coincides with a PT-symmetry breaking transition in logarithmic time. The Efimovian-like oscillations are robust against weak nonlinear interactions and perturbations. Our work implies that the spontaneous breaking of continuous temporal scale symmetry and the emergence of Efimovian-like dynamics can even be observed in a simple driven-dissipative system without many-body interactions.

</details>


### [132] [Structure and Dynamics of Bose Polarons across the Mott-Insulator to Superfluid Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35696)
*Ragheed Alhyder,Alessio Recati,Georg M. Bruun*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一波函数描述，用于刻画二维玻色-哈伯德模型（Bose-Hubbard model）中杂质从莫特绝缘体（Mott insulator）到超流（superfluid）相变过程中的行为，基于量子格点平均场（quantum Gutzwiller）方法的多模变分拟设，揭示了极化子（polaron）与分子态的形成、量子临界点处的非解析特征及非平衡多体动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浸没于量子简并气体中的可移动杂质粒子会形成准粒子，可作为探测其环境的传感器。作者希望建立统一框架，以跨越莫特绝缘体至超流相变，系统理解杂质在强关联玻色浴中的微观结构与谱学行为。

Method: 采用基于量子格点平均场（quantum Gutzwiller）方法的多模变分拟设（multi-mode variational ansatz），对二维玻色-哈伯德模型（Bose-Hubbard model）中的杂质进行统一波函数描述，并计算其谱学、实空间结构与动力学性质。

Result: 杂质可形成多种极化子（polaron）与分子态；波函数可直接给出这些态的微观结构。量子相变临界区（quantum phase transition）的强关联导致极化子性质出现若干非解析特征；增大杂质-玻色子相互作用会引发极化子与分子基态之间的转变，且在量子临界点处，随着浴模软化，杂质缀饰云（dressing cloud）中的玻色子数目迅速增长。此外，杂质注入不同相后展现出定性不同的非平衡多体动力学。

Conclusion: 该工作关于谱学、实空间与动力学的预测刻画了强关联玻色浴中极化子的互补性质，并可通过当前光晶格（optical lattice）中冷原子技术进行观测。

Abstract: A mobile impurity particle immersed in a quantum degenerate gas leads to the formation of a quasiparticle, which can
  serve as a sensor for its environment. Here, we develop a unified wave function description of an impurity in a two-dimensional Bose-Hubbard model across the Mott-insulator to superfluid transition. Using a multi-mode variational ansatz based on the quantum Gutzwiller approach, we show that
  the impurity can form several kinds of polaronic and molecular states. In addition to their spectral properties, the
  wave function provides direct access to the microscopic structure of these states, and we demonstrate that strong correlations in the critical regime of the quantum phase transition give rise to several non-analytic features in the polaron properties. Increasing the impurity-boson interaction leads to transitions between polaron and molecular ground states, and the number of bosons in the impurity dressing cloud grows rapidly at the quantum critical point as the bath modes soften. We furthermore reveal qualitatively distinct non-equilibrium many-body dynamics after the injection of the impurity in the different phases of the Bose-Hubbard model. Our spectral, real space, and dynamical predictions describe complementary properties of
  polarons in a strongly correlated bosonic bath, which can be observed using current techniques with atoms in optical lattices.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Reversibility and Multiplicity in Liquid-Vapor Phase Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31926)
*Luiz Eduardo Czelusniak,Alexander J. Wagner,Luben Cabezas Gómez*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 基于扩散界面理论（diffuse-interface theory），该研究揭示了广泛使用的蒸发与凝结模型所缺失的相变现象：无粘极限下相邻热力学态可存在等量反向质量通量的定常蒸发与凝结，粘性则破坏可逆性并导致多重定常凝结速率，且经格子玻尔兹曼模拟（lattice Boltzmann simulations）动力学验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有被广泛采用的蒸发与凝结模型未能涵盖某些相变行为，本文旨在通过扩散界面理论探索液-气界面（liquid--vapor interfaces）中尚未被研究的现象。

Method: 采用扩散界面理论（diffuse-interface theory）进行理论分析，并借助格子玻尔兹曼模拟（lattice Boltzmann simulations）进行动力学验证。

Result: 在无粘极限（inviscid limit）下，即使蒸气压低于饱和值，相同相邻热力学态也可支持质量通量等量反向的定常蒸发与凝结；粘性（viscosity）破坏该可逆性，但使同一蒸气态对应多重定常凝结速率。格子玻尔兹曼模拟在动力学上重现了这些状态。

Conclusion: 这些现象由扩散界面方程在动力学上实现，而非仅作为简化界面关系的数学解出现；结果揭示了这一成熟液-气界面理论中此前未被探索的行为。

Abstract: Using diffuse-interface theory, we uncover unexpected phase-change phenomena that are absent from widely used models of evaporation and condensation. In the inviscid limit, the same adjacent thermodynamic states admit stationary evaporation and condensation with equal and opposite mass fluxes, even when the vapor pressure lies below saturation. Viscosity breaks this reversibility but gives rise to multiple stationary condensation rates for the same vapor state. Lattice Boltzmann simulations dynamically recover these states, showing that these phenomena are dynamically realized by the diffuse-interface equations rather than arising solely as mathematical solutions of the reduced interfacial relations. Our results reveal previously unexplored behavior in a well-established theory of liquid--vapor interfaces.

</details>


### [134] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Depth-Averaged Avalanche Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34916)
*Pradyumn Singh Sikarwar,Vishal Sharma,Gaurav Bhutani*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究构建并评估了一个物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural network, PINN）框架，用于深度平均颗粒流（depth-averaged granular flow）的 Savage-Hutter 模型，从一维解析验证推进到二维实验验证，并在二维情形下采用物理-数据混合训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测雪崩（avalanche）运动对山区灾害评估至关重要。研究动机在于将物理信息神经网络框架与 Savage-Hutter 模型结合，以实现对颗粒流时空演化的可靠预测，并逐步验证其从一维解析问题到二维真实实验场景的有效性。
