Method: 方法上，首先构建三个复杂度递增的一维问题（给定速度预测高度、给定高度预测速度、以及基于守恒形式（conservative formulation）的双场耦合预测），并与解析解（analytical solution）对比；随后开展超参数敏感性研究（网络深度、宽度、配点密度（collocation density）、学习率与训练轮数）。框架进一步扩展至二维，针对倾斜平面上圆柱形颗粒堆（cylindrical granular pile）坍塌的实验室实验进行验证，并以 TITAN2D 作数值对比。

Result: 结果方面，解耦测试在给定另一场时能准确重建各场的时空演化；耦合形式无需预设数据即可学习两个场，无量纲高度与速度的平均 RMSE 分别为 0.043 和 0.079。纯物理训练在二维中收敛至平凡零解（trivial zero solution），加入最终堆积剖面（final deposit profiles）的 10 个稀疏训练点后形成物理信息-数据辅助混合框架。四个实验案例的全局高度 RMSE 为 2.7–6.7 mm，平均湿润面积 IoU（wetted-area IoU）为 69–81 %。

Conclusion: 结论上，研究表明该混合 PINN 框架在颗粒堆质量与坡度角（slope angle）变化下表现一致，兼具峰值流深（peak flow depth）与深度平均速度等局部及全局一致性，说明其在山地灾害评估中的可行性与稳健性。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of avalanche motion is essential for hazard assessment in mountainous terrain. This study develops and evaluates a physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework for the Savage-Hutter model of depth-averaged granular flow, progressing from 1D analytical verification to 2D experimental validation. First, three 1D problems of increasing complexity were verified against the analytical solution: height prediction with prescribed velocity, velocity prediction with prescribed height, and coupled prediction of both fields using the conservative formulation. The decoupled tests accurately reconstructed the spatio-temporal evolution of each field when the other was prescribed. The coupled formulation learned both fields without prescribed data, achieving mean height and velocity RMSEs of 0.043 and 0.079 in non-dimensional units. A hyperparameter sensitivity study evaluated the effects of network depth, width, collocation density, learning rate, and epochs. The framework was then extended to 2D and validated against laboratory experiments of a cylindrical granular pile collapsing on an inclined plane, with TITAN2D providing numerical comparisons. Purely physics-based training converged to the trivial zero solution; augmenting the loss with 10 sparse training points from final deposit profiles produced a physics-informed, data-assisted hybrid framework. Peak flow depth, depth-averaged velocity, RMSE, and wetted-area IoU evaluated global and local agreement. Global height RMSE ranged from 2.7 to 6.7 mm across four experimental cases, while mean wetted-area IoU ranged from 69 to 81 %, demonstrating consistent performance across variations in pile mass and slope angle.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [R-transforms for non-Hermitian block random matrices: a spherical integral approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31730)
*Pierre Bousseyroux*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文将球面积分方法（spherical-integral approach）扩展至具有固定 \(K \times K\) 分块结构的非厄米随机矩阵（non-Hermitian random matrices）的 \(\mathcal{R}\)-变换，并给出求和从属关系的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有球面积分方法主要用于研究非厄米随机矩阵的 \(\mathcal{R}\)-变换，但尚缺乏对具有固定分块结构矩阵的处理框架，而此类结构在实际谱分析中具有重要性。

Method: 引入定义在 \(2K \times 2K\) 重叠矩阵上的秩-\(K\) 球面积分，构造标量 \(H\)-变换及相应的矩阵值 \(\mathcal{R}\)-变换以编码联合分块结构；并采用复制方法（replica method）推导独立分块矩阵之和的从属关系（subordination relation）。

Result: 得到一个关于独立分块矩阵之和的从属关系猜想，该框架可用于确定具有固定分块结构的大型非厄米随机矩阵的谱边界（spectral boundaries）。

Conclusion: 该工作将球面积分与 \(\mathcal{R}\)-变换理论推广到固定分块结构的非厄米随机矩阵情形，为块结构矩阵的谱边界分析提供了方法基础。

Abstract: We extend the spherical-integral approach to $\mathcal{R}$-transforms of non-Hermitian random matrices to random matrices with a fixed $K \times K$ block structure. We introduce a rank-$K$ spherical integral defining a scalar $H$-transform on $2K \times 2K$ overlap matrices and an associated matrix-valued $\mathcal{R}$-transform encoding the joint block structure. Using the replica method, we derive a conjectural subordination relation for sums of independent block matrices. This framework provides a method for determining the spectral boundaries of large non-Hermitian random matrices with a fixed block structure.

</details>


### [136] [Connecting finite-size scaling and renormalization-group flows in Anderson localization on random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31872)
*Ignacio García-Mata,Weitao Chen,John Martin,Bertrand Georgeot,Gabriel Lemarié*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文通过对比小世界随机图上安德森转变（Anderson transition）的两种描述——本征函数矩（eigenfunction moments）的有限尺寸标度与多重分形维度（multifractal dimensions）$D_q$ 的重整化群（renormalization-group, RG）流——表明单一参数标度（single-parameter scaling）成立，$D_1$ 的RG轨迹族并不必然意味着双参数标度与Kosterlitz-Thouless型临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对小世界随机图上安德森转变，学界对信息维度（information dimension）$D_1$ 的RG流结构存在解释分歧：其轨迹族是否意味着双参数标度（two-parameter scaling）与Kosterlitz-Thouless型临界行为尚不明确，需借助其他可观测量加以检验。

Method: 工作比较两类互补描述：一是本征函数矩的有限尺寸标度，二是多重分形维度$D_q$的RG流，并将RG流扩展至更小矩阶（moment order）$q<1/2$；同时构造刻画本征函数矩流动的beta函数（beta function），检验其在不同无序强度下是否塌缩（collapse）为单一函数。

Result: RG流重现了此前对$D_1$报告的定性结构；扩展至$q<1/2$的$D_q$确认了局域相（localized phase）的强多重分形（strongly multifractal）特征，即强无序下多重分形行为与局域行为存在清晰分离。另一beta函数在不同无序强度下的塌缩证实了单参数标度性质。大$q>1/2$与小$q<1/2$的本征函数矩对应不同的临界行为，尤其是不同的临界指数（critical exponents）。

Conclusion: $D_1$所观察到的RG轨迹族并不必然意味着双参数标度与Kosterlitz-Thouless型临界行为；刻画该转变需选用不同可观测量，如大$q>1/2$与小$q<1/2$的本征函数矩，二者关联着不同的临界行为与临界指数。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between two complementary descriptions of the Anderson transition on small-world random graphs: finite-size scaling of eigenfunction moments and renormalization-group (RG) flows of multifractal dimensions $D_q$. The RG flows recover the qualitative structure previously reported for the information dimension $D_1$, while their extension to other $D_q$ with smaller moment orders $q<1/2$ confirms the strongly multifractal nature of the localized phase characterized by a clear separation between multifractal and localized behaviors at strong disorder. On the other hand, we show that we can construct another beta function characterizing the flow of eigenfunction moments, whose collapse onto a single function across different disorder strengths provides a clear confirmation of single-parameter scaling property. These results indicate that the family of RG trajectories observed for the information dimension $D_1$ need not imply two-parameter scaling and Kosterlitz-Thouless like critical behavior. To characterize these properties, one should consider different observables, such as large $q>1/2$ and small $q<1/2$ eigenfunction moments, which are associated with distinct critical behaviors, in particular different critical exponents.

</details>


### [137] [Trimers, pairs and localisation on disordered random graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32768)
*Alexei Vazquez*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文探讨高阶网络（higher-order networks）中的群组相互作用（group interaction）能否由图的成对动力学（pairwise dynamics）涌现，并以安德森局域化（Anderson localisation）作为决定其大尺度相关性的转变。结果表明，稀疏随机图上最简单的成对动力学即可产生三体束缚态（trimer）型涌现超边（emergent hyperedge），且该复合体在无序下比其组成部分更早局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶网络通常将群组相互作用视为给定（taken as given），缺乏对其微观来源的追问。该研究旨在考察当群组相互作用由图上的成对动力学产生时会发生什么，并追踪通过安德森局域化这一转变来判断其在大尺度上是否重要。

Method: 将涌现超边定义为哈密顿量的 $k$ 体束缚态，其项位于图的顶点与边上；当所有 $(k-1)$ 体面均未束缚时称为 Borromean。在有限图上以 $k$ 重符合（coincidence）检测，要求其在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下存活而 $(k-1)$ 重符合消失。在 Bethe 格子上求阈值，对配对给出闭式解，并区分扩展图（expander graph）具有而格子缺乏的两个扇区；对无序图分析局域化行为。

Result: 稀疏随机图由最简单的成对动力学产生一个涌现超边：三个可区分粒子在同位吸引 $U$ 与跳跃 $t$ 下，先于任何配对被束缚而结合成 trimer。阈值在 Bethe 格子上获得，配对为闭式，覆盖扩展图所具备的两个扇区（凝聚体所见与费米液体所见）。在无序图上，涌现超边在稀有深格点（deep sites）上成核并先于其组成部分局域化：紧凑 trimer 具有窄带及对格点能量的三倍敏感性，其安德森转变发生在无序 $1.6t$，而单粒子为 $17t$、配对为 $4t$；trimer 的费米液体在 $1.5t$ 至 $1.7t$ 之间的线以上为玻璃态，而同密度单粒子液体需 $15t$ 至 $16t$。

Conclusion: 该转变即为单粒子腔转变（single-particle cavity transition），其中使用复合体的跳跃与有效无序。由成对动力学产生的群组相互作用，对异质性（heterogeneity）的脆弱性比产生它的网络高一个数量级。

Abstract: Higher-order networks take group interactions as given. I ask what happens when a group interaction is made by pairwise dynamics on a graph, and follow it through the transition that decides whether it matters at large scale, Anderson localisation. An emergent hyperedge is a $k$-body bound state of a Hamiltonian whose terms live on the vertices and edges of the graph, Borromean when none of its $(k-1)$-body faces is bound, detected on a finite graph by a $k$-fold coincidence that survives the thermodynamic limit while the $(k-1)$-fold ones vanish. Sparse random graphs produce one from the simplest pairwise dynamics: three distinguishable particles with on-site attraction $U$ and hopping $t$ bind into a trimer before any pair binds. The thresholds are obtained on the Bethe lattice, in closed form for the pair, in both sectors that an expander graph has and a lattice lacks, the one a condensate sees and the one a Fermi liquid sees. On a disordered graph the emergent hyperedges nucleate on rare deep sites and localise before their parts: the compact trimer, with a narrow band and a threefold sensitivity to site energies, undergoes the Anderson transition at a disorder of $1.6t$, against $17t$ for a single particle and $4t$ for a pair, and the Fermi liquid of trimers is a glass above a line between $1.5t$ and $1.7t$, where a liquid of single particles at the same density needs $15t$ to $16t$. The transition is the single-particle cavity transition with the composite's hopping and effective disorder. A group interaction made by pairwise dynamics is an order of magnitude more fragile to heterogeneity than the network that makes it.

</details>


### [138] [Pre-registered tests of solid-state-physics-inspired LLM compression: a cluster-level negative result at small-language-model scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34292)
*Jun-qiang Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究以三个月自主研究代理（autonomous research-agent）项目，检验五种源自固态物理（solid-state physics）的压缩映射（compression mappings）在预训练语言模型（pretrained language models）上的适用性；采用预注册（pre-registration）加 3-sigma 门控，结果推翻核心预测，报告四项预注册阴性结果（negative results）与一次“反转”（inversion）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于检验“注意力头类似 Kohn 近视绝缘体（Kohn-nearsighted insulator）”这一预注册假设，即把单粒子密度矩阵的面积律／近近视衰减（area-law / Kohn-nearsighted decay）迁移到语言模型注意力与秩（rank）结构上是否成立。作者同时试图以预注册与 3-sigma 门控的纪律，为自主代理研究建立可核查的阴性结果（negative results）记录。

Method: 项目运行三个月，将预测在任何先导数据前提交至 git，并以 3-sigma 门控决定 PASS 或 SHELVE。共同锚点为单粒子密度矩阵的面积律／Kohn 近视衰减，分为距离面（P001 Wannier、P002 紧束缚 tight-binding）与秩面（P003 DMRG 截断 MLP、P005 Wilson-RG、P011 张量列车 tensor-train 嵌入）；评测在注意力面与秩面分别进行，并在 Pythia-160M、GPT-2-medium 及至 7–8B 规模上检验。方法还包含预注册＋3-sigma＋cluster-framing＋append-only-catalogue 的纪律设计，并说明自身执行门（enforcement gate）被设计但未部署的原因。

Result: P005 在 Phase 1 被抢先（pre-empted），Phase-3 四个先导中三个被证伪（falsified）。注意力面上，GPT-2-medium 的注意力—距离关系在排除 probe padding 后，16 个中位层头中有 12 个最优拟合为拉伸指数（stretched exponential）；紧束缚截断（P002）使困惑度（perplexity）增加 +96%；Pythia-160M 上 Wannier 稀疏度 0.054 ± 0.004 与 PCA、random-Haar、identity 基线无法区分（P001）。秩面上，逐 token 的张量列车键维（bond dimension）不追踪 surprisal（r = 0.016，预注册期望为 0.65），且该格式反而膨胀而非压缩（P011）。P003 结果混合：其标度主张被搁置（r = -0.434），MPO 前提在 stage-0 失败，其跨论文检验由初稿 r = 0.523 在同样修正下坍缩至 0.047，使两项跨论文检验均为空（null）。

Conclusion: 结果反转了“多数注意力头像 Kohn 近视绝缘体”的预注册预测，指向临界（critical）、玻璃态（glassy）或重尾（heavy-tailed）区域；该反转特定于所测试的 ≤ 350M 规模，而秩面无增益的结果在 7–8B 规模仍成立。贡献包括预注册＋3-sigma＋cluster-framing＋append-only-catalogue 的研究纪律（含执行门未部署的说明）、四项带完整数据发布的预注册阴性结果，以及该反转；目录（catalogue）现含十八项已结项研究，其中十七项为阴性。

Abstract: We report a three-month autonomous research-agent program testing five solid-state-physics-inspired compression mappings on pretrained language models, with predictions committed to git before any pilot data and a 3-sigma gate deciding PASS or SHELVE. The common anchor -- area-law / Kohn-nearsighted decay of the one-particle density matrix -- has a distance face (P001 Wannier, P002 tight-binding) and a rank face (P003 DMRG-truncated MLPs, P005 Wilson-RG, P011 tensor-train embeddings). P005 was pre-empted at Phase 1; three of four Phase-3 pilots were falsified. On the attention face, GPT-2-medium attention-versus-distance is best fit by a stretched exponential in 12 of 16 median-layer heads once probe padding is excluded, and a tight-binding cutoff costs +96% perplexity (P002); on Pythia-160M the Wannier sparsity 0.054 +/- 0.004 is indistinguishable from PCA, random-Haar and identity baselines (P001). On the rank face, per-token tensor-train bond dimension does not track surprisal (r = 0.016 vs a pre-registered 0.65) and the format inflates rather than compresses (P011). P003 is mixed: its scaling claim shelved (r = -0.434), its MPO premise died at stage-0, and its cross-paper check, r = 0.523 as first written, collapses to 0.047 under the same correction, leaving both cross-paper checks null. The results invert the pre-registered prediction that most attention heads behave like Kohn-nearsighted insulators, pointing instead to critical, glassy or heavy-tailed regimes; the inversion is specific to the <= 350M scale tested, while the rank-face no-gain result held to 7-8B. We contribute the pre-registration + 3-sigma + cluster-framing + append-only-catalogue discipline -- including why our own enforcement gate was designed but not deployed -- four pre-registered negative results with full data release, and the inversion. The catalogue holds eighteen concluded studies, seventeen negative.

</details>


### [139] [Localization in tight-binding models with power-law distributed couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34767)
*Maximilian Weigmann,Luca Schaefer,Barbara Drossel*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文比较幂律分布耦合（power-law distributed couplings）下紧束缚模型（tight-binding models）中拉普拉斯矩阵（Laplacian）与邻接矩阵（adjacency matrix）本征模的局域化性质，发现二者行为本质不同：邻接矩阵模在大尺度下全部局域化，而拉普拉斯矩阵因守恒律保留少量跨系统模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两类矩阵对应不同类型的动力学过程，因此有必要厘清其在同一无序耦合分布下的谱与局域化差异，以理解无标度耦合网络中的输运与弛豫行为。

Method: 对一维与二维紧束缚模型在不同系统尺寸与连通度下进行数值对角化（numerical diagonalization），比较拉普拉斯矩阵与邻接矩阵的本征谱及本征模局域化性质，并分析参与率（participation ratio）概率分布与其同本征值的关系。

Result: 邻接矩阵的所有本征模在系统足够大时均局域化；拉普拉斯矩阵因守恒律始终存在小比例跨系统模（system-spanning modes），导致参与率分布及其与本征值关系出现幂律尾部（power-law tails）。一维情形下幂律指数随耦合分布指数连续变化；二维情形下，当耦合分布指数大于 0.75 时，最大弛豫时间（relaxation times）对应的模由跨系统转为局域化。

Conclusion: 拉普拉斯与邻接矩阵的局域化行为存在系统性差异，且幂律耦合分布的指数控制局域化程度与跨系统模的出现条件；文中对上述发现给出了唯象学（phenomenological）解释。

Abstract: We study the localization properties of 1D and 2D tight-binding models with power-law distributed couplings by comparing the spectrum and the localization properties of the eigenmodes of the Laplacian and the adjacency matrix, using numerical diagonalization of these matrices for different system sizes and connectivities. These two matrices are relevant for different types of dynamical processes. While all eigenmodes of the adjacency matrix are localized for sufficiently large system sizes, the Laplacian matrix always leads to a small proportion of system-spanning modes due to a conservation law, and therefore to power-law tails in the probability distribution of the participation ratio and its relation to the eigenvalues. In one dimension, the exponent of these power laws change continuously with the exponent that characterizes the distribution of couplings. In two dimensions, the modes with the largest relaxation times change from system-spanning to localized when the exponent of the distribution of couplings becomes larger than 0.75. We provide phenomenological explanations for all these findings.

</details>


### [140] [Exact probability distributions of complex spacing ratios in non-Hermitian random matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35230)
*Kohei Kawabata*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 基于精确联合本征值概率密度，推导了非厄米（non-Hermitian）高斯系综类 AI$^†$ 与 AII$^†$ 的有限 $N$ 复谱间距比（complex spacing ratio）分布，刻画其谱关联与非可积性诊断特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复谱间距比（complex spacing ratio）的统计量可用于诊断开放量子系统（open quantum systems）中的谱关联与非可积性（nonintegrability）；但针对非厄米随机矩阵高斯系综的有限 $N$ 分布尚缺乏从精确联合概率密度出发的解析结果。

Method: 从精确的联合本征值概率密度（joint eigenvalue probability density）出发，对分别由复对称（complex symmetric）与复自对偶（complex self-dual）随机矩阵实现的类 AI$^†$ 与 AII$^†$ 做解析推导；类 AII$^†$ 给出任意 $N$ 的精确代数表达式，类 AI$^†$ 在 $N=3$ 时给出归一化一维积分表示并分析渐近行为；并以非厄米随机矩阵的直接数值对角化（numerical diagonalization）验证。

Result: 类 AII$^†$ 中获得了任意 $N$ 的精确代数表达式，并显式计算了 $N=3,4,5,6$ 的分布与代表性矩（moments）。类 AI$^†$ 中联合密度含非紧致（noncompact）的非酉本征矢自由度积分，但对 $N=3$ 解析导出了归一化一维积分表示，并确定渐近行为，包括三次能级排斥（cubic level repulsion）的对数修正与角密度（angular density）的非解析贡献。解析结果经数值对角化证实。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了非厄米高斯系综类 AI$^†$ 与 AII$^†$ 中复谱间距比分布的有限 $N$ 解析刻画，其中类 AII$^†$ 得到完整精确解，类 AI$^†$ 在 $N=3$ 获得积分类表示与渐近解析结构，且均与数值结果一致，为开放量子系统谱关联诊断提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The complex spacing ratio is the complex displacement from a reference eigenvalue to its nearest neighbor divided by the corresponding displacement to its next-to-nearest neighbor. Its statistics provide a useful diagnostic of spectral correlations and nonintegrability in open quantum systems. Here, starting from the exact joint eigenvalue probability densities, we derive finite-$N$ complex-spacing-ratio distributions for the Gaussian ensembles of non-Hermitian random matrices in classes AI$^†$ and AII$^†$, realized by complex symmetric and complex self-dual random matrices, respectively. In class AII$^†$, we obtain an exact algebraic expression for arbitrary $N$ and explicitly evaluate the distributions and representative moments for $N=3, 4, 5, 6$. In class AI$^†$, although the joint density retains a noncompact integral over nonunitary eigenvector degrees of freedom, we analytically derive a normalized one-dimensional integral representation for $N=3$ and determine the asymptotic behavior, including a logarithmic correction to the cubic level repulsion and a nonanalytic contribution to the angular density. We further confirm these analytical results through direct numerical diagonalization of non-Hermitian random matrices.

</details>


### [141] [Attraction-controlled torque organization and rotational states in frictional granular matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35270)
*Kiwamu Yoshii*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值模拟表明，在二维摩擦颗粒材料（frictional granular material）中，颗粒间吸引力（interparticle attraction）虽为中心力、不直接施加力矩（torque），却通过重组摩擦接触网络显著改变应力传递、力矩组织与颗粒旋转，进而调控宏观流变行为（rheology）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸引力是中心力，本不应对颗粒产生直接力矩，因而其如何影响应力传递与颗粒旋转这一问题具有探究价值。研究动机在于厘清吸引力对摩擦颗粒材料接触网络及宏观流变的影响机制。

Method: 采用数值方法（numerically）研究二维摩擦颗粒材料，考察颗粒间吸引力对应力传递、力矩组织与颗粒旋转的作用，并对剪切应力（shear stress）进行分解，区分吸引贡献与排斥贡献。

Result: 吸引力产生低速率应力状态，并在干系统剪切增稠（shear-thickening）区域附近选择高应力状态，而几乎不改变高速率增稠态。应力分解显示，直接吸引贡献仅占总应力一部分，排斥贡献被显著增强。吸引力提高配位数（coordination number）与力矩幅度，并可反转局部力矩符号相关性（torque-sign correlation）。在低堆积分数（packing fraction）和中等剪切速率下，出现应力塌缩（stress-collapse）状态，力矩与旋转被强烈抑制，尽管接触网络仍有有限连通。

Conclusion: 吸引力通过重组摩擦接触网络来控制宏观流变，说明接触网络的拓扑变化是吸引力影响颗粒材料力学响应的核心机制。

Abstract: In this study, we numerically investigate how interparticle attraction affects stress transmission, torque organization, and particle rotation in a two-dimensional frictional granular material. Although the attraction is a central force and exerts no direct torque on the particles, it strongly modifies the contact network and particle rotation. Attraction produces a low-rate stress state and selects the high-stress state near the shear-thickening regime of the dry system, while leaving the high-rate thickened state almost unchanged. By decomposing the shear stress, we find that the direct attractive contribution accounts for only a fraction of the total stress, while the repulsive contribution is strongly enhanced. Attraction increases the coordination number and torque amplitude and can reverse the local torque-sign correlation. At low packing fractions and intermediate shear rates, a stress-collapse state emerges with strongly suppressed torque and rotation despite a finite contact network. These results show that attraction controls macroscopic rheology through reorganization of the frictional contact network.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Hydrogen mon amour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32307)
*Christophe Grojean,Patrick Janot*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估未来环形对撞机（FCC）电力需求的脱碳路径，指出2050年前可再生能源可满足其大部分用电，从而将残余核电及其放射性废物需求降至五分之一，并探讨氢能可否填补风能与太阳能低谷时段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCC预计年耗电1.3 TWh，若全部由核电供应，将占用大型第三代核反应堆约10%的年发电量并产生相应放射性废物。论文动机在于寻找可降低核电依赖与废物负担的替代供能方案。

Method: 论文通过情景分析，评估在深度脱碳的欧洲电网下可再生能源配合长期购电协议（power-purchase agreements）与灵活运行可提供的电力份额；并系统考察氢能的制取、以氢或氨形式运输、储存与再发电各环节的效率损失与碳足迹，同时讨论天然氢（naturally occurring hydrogen）的不确定前景。

Result: 到2050年，可再生能源最多可提供FCC所需电力的80%，使残余核电需求及相关废物最多降低至原来的五分之一。氢能各条技术路线均无法提供现成解决方案，天然氢前景仍不确定。

Conclusion: FCC投运前的数十年时间为开发可行供能方案留出窗口；CERN可通过成为可信的长期客户来加速这一进程。

Abstract: The FCC's projected average electricity consumption is 1.3 TWh per year. If supplied entirely by nuclear power, this would represent about 10% of the annual output of a large third-generation nuclear reactor, together with a corresponding share of its radioactive waste. By 2050, however, renewable sources could provide up to 80% of this electricity on a substantially decarbonised European grid, supported by long-term power-purchase agreements and flexible operation. This could reduce the residual nuclear requirement, and its associated waste, by up to a factor of five.
  This essay asks whether hydrogen could help bridge the remaining periods of low wind and solar generation. It examines production from renewable electricity, transport as hydrogen or ammonia, storage and conversion back into electricity, together with their efficiency losses and carbon footprints. It then considers the still uncertain prospect of naturally occurring hydrogen. None of these routes offers a ready-made solution. But the decades before the FCC begins operation leave time to develop one--and CERN could help accelerate that development by becoming a credible long-term customer.

</details>


<div id='math.RA'></div>

# math.RA [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Algebras with Variable Operator-Valued Structural Constants and Their Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31628)
*Vitalii Shpakivskyi*

Main category: math.RA

TL;DR: 提出一类具有可变算子值结构常数的新代数，并在其上建立单演函数（monogenic functions）理论的基本要素；证明单演函数分量满足变系数二阶算子微分方程，并给出逆命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为处理含可变系数的微分、积分微分及函数微分方程提供更具一般性的代数框架，突破固定结构常数代数的限制。

Method: 引入带可变算子值结构常数（variable operator-valued structural constants）的代数，建立相容性条件（compatibility conditions），并发展该代数中的单演函数理论。

Result: 证明单演函数的分量满足变系数二阶算子微分方程（second-order operator-differential equations），且相应逆命题（converse results）成立；给出若干示例。

Conclusion: 该框架为求解微分、积分微分与函数微分方程提供构造性方法（constructive method），并辅以示例说明其有效性。

Abstract: We introduce a new class of algebras with variable operator-valued structural constants and develop elements of the theory of monogenic functions in such algebras. Compatibility conditions for the structural operators are established. It is shown that the components of monogenic functions satisfy second-order operator-differential equations with variable coefficients, and converse results are proved. The proposed approach provides a constructive method for obtaining solutions of various differential, integro-differential, and functional-differential equations. Several illustrative examples are presented.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Active 3D weaves for load-bearing and damage-resilient locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32099)
*Guowei Wayne Tu,Evgueni T. Filipov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出将活性材料（active materials）集成到三维（3D）编织壳体（woven shells）中的通用框架，构建兼具高轴向刚度、低弯曲刚度与系统级韧性的机器人结构，并展示五种可运动机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编织（weaving）工艺在软体机器人、假肢、可穿戴设备与外骨骼（exoskeletons）等领域潜力巨大，但纺织类系统固有柔顺性限制了承载能力，而承载是许多机器人功能的必要前提。

Method: 以"编织角（woven corners）"为3D编织结构基本单元进行模块化组装；采用特征值计算（eigenvalue calculations）识别结构的承载与驱动机制，并据此设计五种可运动机器人。

Result: 这些3D编织机器人可负载达自重70倍的载荷实现运动，且在经受极端压缩后仍能保持可重复的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来3D编织机器人系统的设计、制造与仿真提供了路径，适用于同时需要承载、高刚度、主动功能变形、运动与系统级韧性的场景。

Abstract: The craft of weaving, where different materials are interlaced, has tremendous potential for creating active and functional systems for use in soft robots, prosthetics, wearables, exoskeletons, and more. These textile-like systems are flexible and safe for human-machine interaction; however, this inherent flexibility limits their ability to carry loads, which is essential for many robotic functions. In this work, we introduce a general framework for integrating active materials into three-dimensional (3D) woven shells to create robotic structures that combine high axial stiffness for load bearing, low bending stiffness for efficient actuation, and system-level resilience for damage tolerance. These woven robots can be modularly assembled from 'woven corners', a fundamental unit of 3D woven structures. We use eigenvalue calculations to identify load bearing and actuation mechanisms of the 3D woven structures, and use that information to make five different robots capable of locomotion. We demonstrate that these 3D woven robots can locomote carrying loads 70 times their self-weight, and can maintain repeatable performance even after being subjected to extreme compression. This work is a pathway toward the design, manufacturing, and simulation of future 3D woven robotic systems where load bearing, high stiffness, active functional deformation, locomotion, and system-level resilience are all needed.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Odd stochastic density functional theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32080)
*Yael Avni,Michel Fruchart,David Martin,Tali Khain,Vincenzo Vitelli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作将随机密度泛函理论（SDFT）扩展为"odd SDFT"，用于描述具有手性过阻尼极限的体系（如磁化电解质与手性活性物质），并通过集体迁移率张量与霍尔电导率的计算验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 SDFT 以白噪声耦合朗之万方程（Langevin equations）的密度形式描述多体动力学，但未涵盖同时令弛豫时间尺度与振荡周期趋于零的手性过阻尼极限体系，故需扩展其理论框架。

Method: 从受洛伦兹力（Lorentz force）作用的欠阻尼粒子涨落流体力学描述出发，取过阻尼极限推导出"odd SDFT"；并分别用直接线性响应（direct linear response）与 Green-Kubo 关系计算相互作用粒子的集体迁移率张量（collective mobility tensor）。

Result: 所建立框架的经验证有效，可计算集体迁移率张量；将其应用于磁场中电解质的霍尔电导率（Hall conductivity），并借助两种不同的 Green-Kubo 关系导出完整电导率张量，将 Debye-Hückel-Onsager 理论的弛豫部分推广至有限磁场情形。

Conclusion: odd SDFT 为手性过阻尼体系提供了统一描述框架，并成功将电解质电导率理论从平均场（mean-field）层面扩展至有限磁场下的完整张量形式。

Abstract: Stochastic density functional theory (SDFT) provides a powerful framework for studying the dynamics of many-body systems both in and out of equilibrium. In its standard form, it can be viewed as a density-based formulation of coupled Langevin equations with white noise. Here, we extend SDFT to a class of systems, ranging from magnetized electrolytes to chiral active matter, that possess a chiral overdamped limit in which a relaxation timescale and an oscillation period are simultaneously taken to zero. We derive this formulation, which we dub ``odd SDFT", by taking the overdamped limit of a fluctuating hydrodynamic description of underdamped particles subject to a Lorentz force. We establish the validity and utility of the resulting framework by calculating the collective mobility tensor of interacting particles using both direct linear response and a Green-Kubo relation. We then apply the theory to the Hall conductivity of electrolytes in magnetic fields. Going beyond mean-field theory and using two distinct Green-Kubo relations, we derive the full conductivity tensor, extending the relaxation part of the Debye-Hückel-Onsager theory to the case of finite magnetic fields.

</details>


### [146] [Continuously varying exponents in the distribution of waiting times in the symmetric exclusion process on a percolation cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32314)
*Arpan Chatterjee,Kabir Ramola,Deepak Dhar*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究考察带侧枝（side branches）的一维与二维格子上硬核相互作用粒子（hard-core interacting particles）在对称排斥过程（symmetric exclusion process）稳态下的等待时间分布（waiting-time distribution）。结合数值模拟与侧枝捕获粒子的近似解析处理，揭示高密度下强动力学异质性（dynamical heterogeneity）及返回概率的幂律衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带侧枝的二维"地毯"结构可作为超临界渗流团簇（supercritical percolation cluster）中粒子捕获的简化模型。研究旨在理解此类几何受限环境中粒子等待时间的统计特征，尤其是稳态下侧枝对粒子动力学的捕获效应。

Method: 采用数值模拟（numerical simulations），并结合对稳态下被困于侧枝中粒子的近似解析处理（approximate analytical treatment）。研究对象为一维和二维格子，每个格点均附有侧枝，粒子间为硬核相互作用，遵循对称排斥过程。

Result: 高粒子密度下系统表现出强动力学异质性：等待时间对数的分布呈现彼此分离良好的峰，对应被困于距主干不同深度处的粒子。被标记粒子（tagged particle）在时刻 t_0+t 与 t_0 处于同一位置的概率按 t^{-ω} 幂律衰减，指数 ω 在粒子密度高于阈值 ρ^*<1 时随密度连续变化。此外还考察了同一被标记粒子轨迹上相继等待时间之间的相关性。

Conclusion: 侧枝结构在稳态下对粒子产生深度依赖的捕获效应，导致等待时间分布多峰化与返回概率的幂律衰减；指数 ω 对粒子密度的连续依赖性表明该行为受密度调控，且超临界渗流团簇的简化捕获模型可再现此类动力学异质性。

Abstract: We study the waiting-time distribution of hard-core interacting particles in the symmetric exclusion process on one- and two-dimensional lattices with side branches attached to each lattice site in the steady state. We use numerical simulations together with an approximate analytical treatment of particles trapped in side branches in the steady state. Such a two-dimensional carpet serves as a simplified model for trapping of a supercritical percolation cluster. At high particle densities, the system exhibits strong dynamical heterogeneity, with the distribution of logarithms of waiting times developing well-separated peaks corresponding to particles trapped at different depths from the backbone. We show that the probability that a tagged particle occupies the same position at time $t_0+t$ as at time $t_0$ decays algebraically as $t^{-ω}$, where the exponent $ω$ varies continuously with particle density for densities above a threshold value $ρ^*<1$. We also investigate the correlations between successive waiting times along the trajectory of a tagged particle.

</details>


### [147] [Criticality without Temperature in an Ising Spin System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33361)
*Christos Kakalis,Fotios K. Diakonos*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种自适应团簇动力学（adaptive cluster dynamics），证明即使动力学完全独立于 Ising 哈密顿量、且不含类温度参数，Ising 系统仍可自发涌现类临界行为（critical-like behavior）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Ising 模型临界性由哈密顿动力学（Hamiltonian dynamics）产生并由温度调控。本文旨在探索无需温度类参数、且动力学与能量无关时是否仍能产生集体临界行为。

Method: 引入自适应团簇动力学，其中自旋连接性（spin connectivity）由局部静默时间变量（quiet time，即自旋上次更新以来经过的时间）控制；Ising 哈密顿量仅作为表征构型的观测量，参数 α 用于调控连接强度。

Result: 随参数 α 变化，系统发生自发对称性破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking），伴随强烈集体涨落；团簇尺寸分布出现扩展的幂律区（power law regime），并在最大尺度处以小型有限尺寸峰终止，同时 Ising 能量对 α 变化表现出奇异响应。

Conclusion: 仅依赖记忆的连接性即可生成集体临界行为，揭示了不依赖能量动力学的非平衡临界性（nonequilibrium criticality）路径。

Abstract: Criticality in the Ising model is conventionally generated by Hamiltonian dynamics and controlled by temperature. Here we show that critical like behavior can emerge in an Ising system whose dynamics is completely independent of the Ising Hamiltonian and contains no temperature like parameter. We introduce an adaptive cluster dynamics in which spin connectivity is controlled by a local quiet time variable, the time elapsed since a spin was last updated. The Ising Hamiltonian enters only as an observable characterizing the resulting configurations. As the parameter $α$, controlling the connectivity strength, is varied, the system undergoes spontaneous symmetry breaking accompanied by strong collective fluctuations. The cluster size distribution develops an extended power law regime, terminated by a small finite size hump at the largest scales, while the Ising energy exhibits a singular response to the variation of $α$. These results show that memory dependent connectivity alone can generate collective critical behavior, revealing a route to nonequilibrium criticality without energy based dynamics.

</details>


### [148] [Backward Kolmogorov Transport: Sampling Invariant Laws of Reversible Diffusions from Trajectory Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32664)
*Yuanchao Xu,Isao Ishikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出后向 Kolmogorov 传输（Backward Kolmogorov Transport, BKT），一种无需估计密度、得分或漂移的可逆扩散不变律采样器。BKT 利用轨迹估计生成元特征对并沿 KL 散度的 Wasserstein 梯度流移动粒子，理论上证明精确特征对下可精确传输初始律的谱投影，估计特征对下给出传输恒等式与局部稳定性界，实验涵盖 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程、多势阱与丙氨酸二肽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从轨迹采样不变律时，基于密度或得分估计（score estimation）的方法会继承估计误差，尤其在亚稳态（metastable states）之间的势垒处误差显著。轨迹本身还包含动力学信息，可逆扩散的路径测度与不变律之比满足后向 Kolmogorov 方程（backward Kolmogorov equation），因此可利用生成元的特征对以闭式传播该比值，从而规避密度与得分估计带来的偏差。

Method: 方法基于可逆扩散生成元特征对，构造后向 Kolmogorov 传输（BKT）：用轨迹估计特征对，用初始粒子一次性计算系数，使粒子沿 Kullback-Leibler 散度的 Wasserstein 梯度流（Wasserstein gradient flow）移动。粒子之间无相互作用，BKT 定义一个传输映射（transport map），不估计密度、得分或漂移。

Result: 理论结果包括：在精确特征对下，当初始律的谱投影为正时 BKT 精确传输该投影；在估计特征对下，证明传输恒等式，其中截断仅通过初始律进入、估计仅通过特征对残差进入，并给出 2-Wasserstein 距离下的局部稳定性界。当系数由传输后粒子计算时，保留模态的采样误差相互抵消，中心极限定理（central limit theorem）给出渐近方差。实验在 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程、多势阱（multi-well potentials）、可分离乘积与丙氨酸二肽（alanine dipeptide）上进行，并与基于密度估计的粒子方法比较，结果与理论一致；在谱估计准确时，BKT 的误差达到或低于独立样本（independent samples）的误差。

Conclusion: BKT 提供了一种基于生成元特征对传输的可逆扩散不变律采样框架，回避了密度、得分与漂移估计，并在理论上刻画了截断与估计误差的影响。实验表明其误差在准确谱下可达到或低于独立采样的水平，为高维与亚稳态采样提供了新思路。

Abstract: We consider a reversible diffusion with unknown drift, observed through trajectories, and construct samplers of its invariant law. The trajectories contain samples of this law, but a sampler built from them by density or score estimation inherits the errors of these estimates, in particular on the barriers between metastable states. The trajectories also contain the dynamics, and for a reversible diffusion the ratio between the law of the process and its invariant law solves the backward Kolmogorov equation, so that the eigenpairs of the generator propagate it in closed form. Backward Kolmogorov transport (BKT) evaluates this ratio with eigenpairs estimated from the trajectories and coefficients computed once from the initial particles, and moves the particles along the Wasserstein gradient flow of the Kullback-Leibler divergence. The particles do not interact, BKT defines a transport map, and no density, score or drift is estimated. With exact eigenpairs BKT transports the spectral projection of the initial law exactly when this projection is positive. For estimated eigenpairs we prove a transport identity in which truncation enters only through the initial law and estimation only through the residual of the eigenpairs, and a local stability bound in the 2-Wasserstein distance. With coefficients computed from the transported particles, the sampling error of the retained modes cancels, and a central limit theorem identifies the asymptotic variance. Experiments on Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes, multi-well potentials, separable products and alanine dipeptide, including comparisons with particle methods based on density estimates, agree with these results, and with an accurate spectrum the errors of BKT reach or fall below those of independent samples.

</details>


### [149] [Periodically modulated traveling waves in integrate-and-fire networks: recursive speed law and propagation failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33006)
*Jie Nissel,Ricardo Erazo-Toscano,Rosahn Bhattarai,Marius Osan,Mandeep Chauhan,Remus Osan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究考察突触耦合（synaptic coupling）的空间非均匀性如何抑制神经组织中的行波活动。通过对积分-发放网络（integrate-and-fire network）建模，导出波速（wave speed）满足的标量方程，得到慢速不稳定与快速稳定的均匀波速，并揭示慢、快调制下折叠幅度（fold amplitude）的不同标度规律，结果经数值积分与脉冲发放映射模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组织中行波活动可被突触耦合的空间非均匀性终止，但其机制缺乏定量刻画。研究动机在于建立一个可解析处理的模型，明确非均匀耦合对波传播与终止的调控规律。

Method: 采用每个神经元仅发放一次、耦合呈指数衰减的积分-发放网络（integrate-and-fire network）。前沿发放映射（leading-edge firing map）精确约化为波速的标量方程，耦合的周期调制逐点进入该方程；通过微扰递推（perturbative recursion）显式求得速度剖面。

Result: 正周期波（positive periodic waves）终止于折叠。慢调制时折叠幅度趋近由调制谷值决定的高原，局部瓶颈速度为两均匀波速的几何平均；快调制时折叠幅度随频率线性增长，斜率由对积分调制剖面的全幅度平均给出，弱纹波截断会高估斜率约2.5倍。

Conclusion: 研究区分了周期波的丧失与特定发射（launch）的失败，并区分兴奋性区间与变号扩展情形。结果与约化方程的直接积分及网络首脉冲发放映射模拟一致，支持该标量方程对波终止机制的有效刻画。

Abstract: Traveling waves of activity in neural tissue can be halted by spatial inhomogeneity in synaptic coupling. We study an integrate-and-fire network in which each neuron fires once and the coupling decays exponentially. For this model the leading-edge firing map reduces exactly to a scalar equation for the wave speed in space, with a slow unstable and a fast stable homogeneous speed, $c_1$ and $c_2$, and a periodic modulation of the coupling enters this equation pointwise. Positive periodic waves terminate in folds. For slowly varying modulation the fold amplitude approaches a plateau set by the trough of the modulation, where the local bottleneck speed is $\sqrt{c_1 c_2}$; the approach to the plateau is set by the local geometry of the trough. For rapidly varying modulation the fold amplitude grows linearly with frequency, with a slope given by a full-amplitude average over the integrated modulation profile; a weak-ripple truncation of that average overestimates the slope about 2.5-fold at the default coupling. A perturbative recursion in the amplitude gives the speed profile explicitly. We distinguish loss of the periodic wave from failure of a particular launch, and the excitatory regime $ε\le 1$ from its sign-changing extension. The results are checked against direct integration of the reduced equation and first-spike firing-map simulations of the network.

</details>


### [150] [Quantum Dynamics of Full Counting Statistics in Fermionic Lattices with Localized Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33724)
*Bijay Kumar Agarwalla,Manas Kulkarni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维无相互作用晶格在边缘局域粒子注入下的全计数统计（full counting statistics, FCS）动力学，采用GKSL量子主方程与Schwinger-Keldysh路径积分方法，将多点计数问题约化为仅涉及注入点的局部问题，并给出任意时刻累积生成函数的解析表达式及长时极限下的Levitov-Lesovik型公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动开放费米子系统中的量子涨落与计数统计是重要问题，但针对局域粒子注入情形下的FCS完整动力学刻画仍缺乏统一框架；已有Levitov-Lesovik型结果主要针对稳态下的边界驱动设置，需推广到任意时刻与瞬态过程。

Method: 以GKSL（Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad）量子主方程建模系统动力学；将逐格点计数的计数问题重新表述为仅涉及局域注入点的等效问题；在GKSL框架内运用Schwinger-Keldysh路径积分形式，推导任意时刻累积生成函数的解析表达式，并取长时极限。

Result: 得到任意时刻累积生成函数的解析表达式，并在长时极限下获得Levitov-Lesovik型公式；对支持单粒子离域本征态的干净晶格（短程与长程跃迁均含），所有累积量在渐近区随时间线性增长；解析结果与直接数值模拟吻合良好。

Conclusion: 研究为具有局域粒子注入的驱动开放费米子系统提供了刻画FCS与量子涨落的通用框架，并将先前仅在稳态边界驱动设置下得到的Levitov-Lesovik型公式推广至瞬态与更一般的动力学情形。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of full counting statistics (FCS) of the growth of the total number of fermions in a one-dimensional non-interacting lattice subjected to a localized particle gain (source) at one edge. The dynamics of the setup is modeled by the Gorini--Kossakowski--Sudarshan--Lindblad (GKSL) quantum master equation. We recast the counting problem that involves counting particle number at every lattice site to a problem where only the local injected site is involved. We employ the Schwinger-Keldysh path integral formalism within the GKSL framework and derive an analytical expression for the cumulant generating function at arbitrary times and obtain a Levitov-Lesovik type formula in the long-time limit, earlier derived for boundary driven setups in the steady-state. For clean lattices with either short- or long-range hopping, supporting single-particle delocalized eigenstates, we show that all cumulants grow linearly with time in the asymptotic regime. Our analytical results are in excellent agreement with direct numerical simulations. These findings establish a general framework for characterizing FCS and quantum fluctuations in driven open fermionic systems with localized particle injection.

</details>


### [151] [Non-stationary Statistics and Energetics of Brownian Motion under Stochastic Harmonic Confinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33751)
*Deepak Gupta,Sabine H. L. Klapp*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究谐振势阱（harmonic trap）中被约束的布朗粒子（Brownian particle）的位置统计与热力学性质，其中粒子刚度（stiffness）随时间以布朗过程的平方形式涨落；结果表明该过程具有非平稳性（non-stationary）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于涨落刚度（fluctuating stiffness）模型的研究多描述平稳过程，而本文关注刚度按布朗过程平方涨落的情形，以刻画非平稳过程（non-stationary process）下的位置统计与热力学行为。

Method: 首先在无热浴（thermal bath）条件下推导粒子在时刻 t 位置的概率密度函数（probability density function），并给出计算第 n 阶位置矩（positional moment）的表达式；随后求解前四阶位置矩及其长时间极限的渐近行为，并推导对系统所做平均功（average work）与粒子与热浴交换平均热（average heat）的精确表达式。

Result: 获得了位置概率密度函数、第 n 阶位置矩表达式、前四阶位置矩的长时间渐近行为，以及平均功与平均热的精确表达式；这些量的长时间行为均反映该过程的非平稳特性，且理论预测得到数值模拟（numerical simulations）的支持。

Conclusion: 与既有涨落刚度模型相比，本文模型描述的是非平稳过程（non-stationary process），其位置统计与热力学量的长时间行为均体现非平稳性，理论结果由数值模拟验证。

Abstract: We investigate the positional statistics and thermodynamic properties of a Brownian particle confined in a Harmonic trap. The stiffness of the particle fluctuates in time as the square of the Brownian process. First, in the absence of a thermal bath, we investigate the probability density function of the position of the particle at time $t$. Then, we provide an expression to compute the $n$th positional moment. We evaluate the first four positional moments and discuss their asymptotic behavior in the long-time limit. In contrast to previously studied models of fluctuating stiffness, the current model describes a non-stationary process. Furthermore, we provide the exact expression for the average work performed on the system and the average heat exchanged by the particle with the bath. Their long time behavior again reflects the non-stationary nature of the process. Our theoretical predictions are supported by numerical simulations.

</details>


### [152] [Hall conductance of dilute electrolytes from odd stochastic density functional theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33797)
*Yael Avni,Michel Fruchart,David Martin,Tali Khain,Vincenzo Vitelli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出“odd SDFT”这一随机密度泛函框架，将随机密度泛函理论（stochastic density functional theory, SDFT）扩展至含横向迁移率（transverse mobilities）与手性时间关联噪声（chiral time-correlated noise）的体系，用于计算磁场下电解质的霍尔电导率（Hall conductivity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外磁场下电解质表现出类似金属与半导体的霍尔电导率，但现有理论无法定量解释实验测量值。为推进此类理论，需建立能够刻画磁场中电解质响应的微观统计框架。

Method: 构建 odd SDFT：在经典 SDFT 基础上引入横向迁移率与手性时间关联噪声，从而纳入破坏时间反演（time-reversal）与镜像对称（mirror symmetry）的效应。在不考虑流体力学溶剂效应（hydrodynamic solvent effects）的条件下，通过三种途径计算霍尔电导率：(i) 直接响应（direct response）、(ii) Green-Kubo 关系（Green-Kubo relation）、(iii) 混合涨落耗散关系（hybrid fluctuation-dissipation relation）。

Result: 该框架给出磁场下电解质的霍尔电导率结果，并将 Debye-Hückel-Onsager 理论的弛豫修正（relaxation correction）推广至磁化电解质情形；同时表明 odd SDFT 可更广泛地适用于具破缺时间反演与镜像对称的流体，如奇异扩散流体（odd-diffusive fluids）与手性活性物质（chiral active matter）。

Conclusion: odd SDFT 为描述磁场中电解质及更广泛的对称性破缺流体提供了统一的理论基础。作者指出，该框架可为纳入由溶剂介导的额外效应奠定基础，从而有望定量调和理论与实验之间的差异。

Abstract: Electrolytes under an external magnetic field exhibit a Hall conductivity, much like metals and semiconductors. However, existing theories fail to quantitatively account for their measured Hall conductivities. To make progress toward such a theory, we develop a stochastic density functional framework for electrolytes in a magnetic field. This framework, which we dub odd SDFT, extends the celebrated stochastic density functional theory (SDFT) to incorporate transverse mobilities and chiral time-correlated noise. Using this framework, we calculate the Hall conductivity in the absence of hydrodynamic solvent effects through three approaches: (i) direct response, (ii) Green-Kubo relation, and (iii) a hybrid fluctuation-dissipation relation. We further show that odd SDFT applies more broadly to fluids with broken time-reversal and mirror symmetry, including odd-diffusive fluids and chiral active matter. Our results generalize the relaxation correction of Debye-Hückel-Onsager theory to magnetized electrolytes and provide a foundation for incorporating additional effects mediated by the solvent that could quantitatively reconcile theory and experiments.

</details>


### [153] [Second largest eigenvalue does not bound stationary entropy production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34352)
*Naoto Shiraishi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文检验并证伪了 Oberreiter 等人提出的谱耗散—相干权衡不等式（spectral dissipation-coherence trade-off inequality），通过显式构造一个相干数有限但稳态熵产生为零的反例，说明第二大特征值并不能必然刻画相干振荡程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作（Oberreiter 等，Phys. Rev. E 106, 014106 (2022)）猜想：由第二大特征值定义的“相干数（coherent number）”为单位振荡的稳态熵产生（stationary entropy production per unit oscillation）提供下界。该文旨在检验此猜想是否成立。

Method: 采用显式构造反例（counterexample）的方式：构建一个模型，使其具有有限的相干数，同时其稳态熵产生（stationary entropy production）为零。

Result: 反例成立，原猜想被证伪（disprove）。该模型同时排除了一类基于跃迁速率矩阵（transition rate matrix）第二大特征值实部与虚部的热力学界（thermodynamic bound）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，第二大特征值并不必然刻画相干振荡（coherent oscillation）的程度。

Abstract: We examine the spectral dissipation-coherence trade-off inequality conjectured by Oberreiter {\it et al}. [Phys. Rev. E 106, 014106 (2022)], stating that the coherent number, defined from the second largest eigenvalue, provides a lower bound on the stationary entropy production per unit oscillation. We disprove this conjecture by explicitly constructing a counterexample, which accompanies finite coherent number with vanishing stationary entropy production. This model also excludes a wide class of thermodynamic bound with the real and imaginary parts of the second largest eigenvalue of the transition rate matrix. Our result implies that the second largest eigenvalue does not necessarily characterize the degree of coherent oscillation.

</details>


### [154] [Fluctuations and multifractality in stochastic models of interface growth and population dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34468)
*Maximilien Bernard*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本论文研究无序（disorder）与涨落（fluctuations）在三种相关情境下如何塑造大尺度增长现象：涨落界面（fluctuating interfaces）、随机弹簧链（random spring chains）与随机乘性增长（random multiplicative growth）。工作分别探讨反常标度（anomalous scaling）的起源、强无序下的 Anderson 局域化（Anderson localization），以及再分配与增长竞争产生的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在澄清无序与涨落在界面增长中反常标度的成因差异，并刻画强无序区中标准弱无序展开（weak-disorder expansions）失效时的谱性质。同时，针对随机乘性增长中被再分配（redistribution）调控的不平等与集中现象，探索其丰富的相图。

Method: 第一部分通过两个模型区分反常标度来源：非均匀弹性线（heterogeneous elastic line）与随机多孔介质方程（stochastic porous medium equation）。第二部分研究同时含随机质量与随机劲度系数的链，并提出新的组合方法（combinatorial method）探测强无序区。第三部分构建每个位点具有自身淬火增长率（quenched growth rate）与瞬态涨落的模型，考察再分配的竞争作用。

Result: 在非均匀弹性线中，表观反常被证明是纯统计效应；而在随机多孔介质方程这一强非线性模型中，反常为真实存在。针对随机质量与随机劲度的链，新组合方法可用于强无序谱性质分析。随机乘性增长模型中，局部淬火优势/劣势、瞬态涨落与再分配之间的竞争导致丰富的相图（phase diagrams）。

Conclusion: 论文表明反常标度可能源于统计假象或真实效应，取决于具体模型。强无序情形需要超越弱无序展开的新工具。随机乘性增长中再分配与增长机制竞争，产生解释人口动态、财富分布、城市增长与生态等问题的多样相图。

Abstract: This thesis studies how disorder and fluctuations shape large-scale growth phenomena in three related settings: fluctuating interfaces, random spring chains, and random multiplicative growth. The first part concerns interface growth and a phenomenon denoted anomalous scaling, where local and global exponents differ.To clarify its origin, we study two models in which this mismatch has different causes. The first is a heterogeneous elastic line, where we show that the apparent anomaly is a purely statistical effect. The second is the stochastic porous medium equation, a strongly nonlinear model in which the anomaly is genuine. The second part concerns Anderson localization, which appears here through the spectral properties of the heterogeneous elastic lines. We study chains with both random masses and random spring constants, with particular emphasis on the strong-disorder regime, where standard weak-disorder expansions break down. We develop a new combinatorial method to probe this regime. The last part concerns random multiplicative growth with redistribution. In such models, changes in wealth, population, or mass, for instance, are proportional to the amount already present, so that small differences are amplified over time. This naturally generates broad distributions and provides a simple mechanism for the emergence of inequality and concentration, with applications to population dynamics, wealth distribution, city growth, and ecology. We in particular study models in which each site has its own quenched growth rate, representing a persistent advantage or disadvantage, and is also subject to transient fluctuations. Redistribution competes with these mechanisms by homogenizing the system. This competition leads to rich phase diagrams.

</details>


### [155] [Out-of-equilibrium finite-time percolation transitions and spinodal-like behaviors after quenches across magnetic first-order transitions of Ising systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35485)
*Andrea Pelissetto,Davide Rossini,Ettore Vicari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究类伊辛（Ising-like）铁磁系统在外场突变或缓慢驱动下穿越低温磁一级相变（first-order transition, FOT）线的非平衡弛豫动力学，发现淬火后演化过程中出现动力学渗流转变（dynamic percolation transition），并揭示其临界行为与自旋odal-like磁化行为及渗流时间对场的指数依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注类伊辛铁磁系统穿越低温磁一级相变线时的非平衡弛豫动力学，尤其是外场突然与缓慢变化两种驱动方式下的物理行为，旨在刻画从亚稳负磁化相向稳定正磁化相转变过程中是否伴随普适的渗流现象。

Method: 以二维伊辛系统为范式，通过将均匀外场从 h_i<0 突然淬火至 h>0，驱动系统跨越低温一级相变线；考察淬火后演化中最大正自旋团簇的渗流与最大负自旋团簇的反渗流，并进行有限尺寸标度分析；同时采用缓慢变化磁场（类 Kibble-Zurek 协议）进行对照研究。

Result: 在有限（足够小）的 h 下，淬火后演化在有限临界时间 t_c(h) 处出现动力学渗流转变；该转变呈现与标准随机渗流相同的有限尺寸标度行为，渗流团簇分形维数与随机渗流值一致，但趋近临界性的指数不同且依赖 h；渗流转变标志系统从亚稳负磁化相进入稳定正磁化相，且 t_c(h) 在 h→0 时呈自旋odal-like指数依赖；缓慢驱动协议下观察到类似行为。

Conclusion: 类伊辛磁一级相变中渗流转变可能是普遍现象；这种非平衡渗流临界行为与磁化的自旋odal-like行为相关，并在缓慢（类 Kibble-Zurek）驱动协议中同样出现类似行为。

Abstract: We analyze the out-of-equilibrium relaxational dynamics of ferromagnetic Ising-like systems driven across their low-temperature magnetic first-order transition (FOT) line, by sudden and slow variations of an external homogenous magnetic field $h$. As a paradigmatic example, we consider a two-dimensional Ising system driven across its low-temperature FOT line by suddently quenching $h$ from $h_i<0$ to $h>0$, inducing a transition from the negatively to the positively magnetized phase. We show the emergence of a dynamic percolation transition at a finite critical time $t_c(h)$ along the post-quench evolution for finite (sufficiently small) values of $h$, which is marked by the percolation of the largest positive-spin cluster and the antipercolation of the largest negative-spin cluster. This out-of-equilibrium percolation transition displays a finite-size scaling behavior as in the standard random-percolation case. However, while the fractal dimension of the percolating clusters is consistent with the random-percolation value, the exponent controlling the approach to criticality differs and depends on $h$. We also show that the percolation transition marks the passage from the metastable negatively-magnetized phase to the stable positively-magnetized one. Therefore, in the small-$h$ limit, the percolation critical behavior is related to the spinodal-like behavior of the magnetization, implying that the percolation time $t_c(h)$ exhibits a spinodal-like exponential dependence on $h$. The existence of percolation transitions is likely a generic phenomenon at magnetic Ising-like FOTs. For example, we observe an analogous behavior in dynamic (Kibble-Zurek-like) protocols entailing slow variations of the magnetic field driving the crossing of the FOT line.

</details>


### [156] [Speed-Fisher Information: Chaos and Irreversibility in Classical and Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35687)
*Nachiket Karve,Nathan Rose,David Campbell,Anatoli Polkovnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出以系统定态对慢驱动（slow driving）的敏感性为统一视角，界定经典与量子混沌（classical and quantum chaos）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典混沌与量子混沌通常采用不同的判据，缺乏一个统一刻画框架，尤其缺少能将混沌与不可逆热力学量联系起来的普适表述。

Method: 引入对平均协议速度（average protocol speed）的敏感性度量，称为“速度-费舍尔信息（speed-Fisher information）”，并将其与系统不可逆熵产生（irreversible entropy production）相关联。

Result: 混沌动力学表现为速度-费舍尔信息随协议时间（protocol time）发散，该响应受微扰低频谱权重（low-frequency spectral weight）控制；该判据适用于经典与量子哈密顿系统（Hamiltonian systems），并可自然推广至非哈密顿经典流（non-Hamiltonian classical flows）；此外识别出短于普朗克时间尺度（Planckian timescale）的量子区制，其中该量呈现独特的量子标度行为（quantum scaling）。

Conclusion: 速度-费舍尔信息为经典与量子混沌提供了统一且与热力学相联系的判据，并通过简单的经典与量子示例加以说明。

Abstract: We propose a unified perspective on classical and quantum chaos based on the sensitivity of a system's stationary states to slow driving. We probe this sensitivity via the system's susceptibility to the average protocol speed, which we call the ``speed-Fisher information," and relate it to irreversible entropy production in the system. We show that chaotic dynamics manifests as a divergence of the speed-Fisher information with the protocol time, and that this response is controlled by the perturbation's low-frequency spectral weight. This approach to chaos applies to both classical and quantum Hamiltonian systems, and naturally extends to non-Hamiltonian classical flows. Additionally, we identify a quantum regime at times shorter than the Planckian timescale, in which the speed-Fisher information exhibits a uniquely quantum scaling. We illustrate this framework with simple classical and quantum examples

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Heavy Inert Doublet Reconciles LZ and IceCube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28819)
*Hitoshi Murayama,Bea Noether*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究指出惰性双重态暗物质（inert doublet dark matter）可同时解释 LZ 直接探测信号与 IceCube 约束，其优选质量区间为 11.3–20.3 TeV，比标准 higgsino 目标重约一个量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在调和（reconcile）LZ 直接探测所宣称的信号与 IceCube 的约束，并考察偏离标准 higgsino 目标质量的暗物质方案可行性。

Method: 基于惰性双重态暗物质框架，通过分析高质下较小的数密度（number density）对速度的指数敏感性来重现 LZ 事件，并利用更高的落入速度（infall velocity）抑制太阳中的俘获率（capture rate），同时要求惰性双重态与希格斯玻色子（Higgs boson）间的强耦合以给出正确的冻结（freeze-out）湮灭截面。

Result: 优选暗物质质量为 11.3–20.3 TeV；在朗道极点（Landau pole）以下存在约一个量级的有效范围，超出则需 UV 完备化（UV completion）；尽管耦合较大，电弱精度观测量（electroweak precision observables）仍低于实验上限。

Conclusion: 惰性双重态暗物质能够以重质量方案同时满足 LZ 信号与 IceCube 约束，并在朗道极点以下具有约一个量级的有效区间；电弱精度观测不构成限制。

Abstract: We show that the inert doublet dark matter can reconcile the purported LZ direct detection signal and IceCube constraints. The preferred dark matter mass is 11.3--20.3~TeV, an order of magnitude heavier than the standard higgsino target. The smaller number density at a high mass can reproduce the LZ event given the exponential sensitivity to the velocity, while suppressing the capture rate in the Sun because of the higher infall velocity. On the other hand, the high mass requires a large coupling between the inert doublet and Higgs boson to have the correct annihilation cross section for the freeze-out. There is about an order of magnitude of validity range below the Landau pole, beyond which a UV completion is needed. Electroweak precision observables are below the experimental limits despite the large coupling.

</details>


### [158] [Impact of Helicity-Isospin Convention Matching on the Extraction of $α$ from $B^0\to(ρπ)^0$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31828)
*Radek Žlebčík*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出 $B^0\to(\rho\pi)^0$ 含时 Dalitz 图测量中使用的 $\rho\to\pi\pi$ 螺旋度约定与后续同位旋分析所假设的约定不一致；修正后，直接测定与间接测定单位三角形角 $\alpha$ 的张力由 $2.9\sigma$ 降至 $2.1\sigma$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $B^0\to(\rho\pi)^0$ 衰变的含时 Dalitz 图测量被用于直接测定单位三角形角（unitarity-triangle angle）$\alpha$，其结果与全局拟合的间接测定存在张力，该不一致可能源于分析中隐含的约定不匹配问题。

Method: 对比含时 Dalitz 图测量中所用的 $\rho\to\pi\pi$ 螺旋度约定（helicity convention）与后续同位旋分析（isospin analysis）所假设的约定，识别并修正二者之间的不一致。

Result: 修正约定后，$\alpha$ 的直接测定与间接测定之间的偏差由 $2.9\sigma$ 减小至 $2.1\sigma$。结合 $B\to\pi\pi$ 与 $B\to\rho\rho$ 模式，直接测定结果为 $\alpha=(87.0^{+11.8}_{-4.7})^\circ$，原世界平均值为 $(84.1^{+3.7}_{-3.0})^\circ$。

Conclusion: 螺旋度约定不一致是导致 $\alpha$ 直接测定与间接测定出现显著张力的部分原因；修正后张力有所缓解，但并未完全消除，且结合其他衰变模式后直接测定的不确定度仍明显大于原世界平均值。

Abstract: We show that the $ρ\toππ$ helicity convention used in the time-dependent Dalitz-plot measurements of $B^0\to(ρπ)^0$ decays does not match the convention assumed in the subsequent isospin analysis. Once corrected, the tension between the direct determination of the unitarity-triangle angle $α$ from these decays and its indirect determination from global fits decreases from $2.9σ$ to $2.1σ$. Combined with the $B\toππ$ and $B\toρρ$ modes, the direct determination becomes $α= (87.0^{+11.8}_{-4.7})^\circ$, compared with the original world average $(84.1^{+3.7}_{-3.0})^\circ$.

</details>


### [159] [Prompting Particle Physics: Tokenized Multi-modal Foundation Models for Combinatorially Many Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31862)
*Nilotpal Kakati,Daniel Murnane,Baran Hashemi,Samuel Klein,Jeffrey Krupa,Eilam Gross,Lukas Heinrich,Michael Kagan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出将喷注（jet）中所有对象（径迹、量能器单元、簇、粒子、喷注）统一表示为共享的 token 词表（token vocabulary），并训练单一模型完成任意模态子集到任意模态的映射；粒子流（particle flow）、探测器模拟（detector simulation）与带电能量扣除（charged energy subtraction）因此成为同一组权重下的不同方向。模型在全部模态组合上训练，解码器支持自回归（autoregressive）与并行两种输出方式，且两者均训练稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对撞机实验中的重建与模拟（reconstruction and simulation）是由多个专用算法串联而成的长链条，每个算法仅针对单一环节调优，导致链条冗长且各步骤相互独立。作者希望探索单个模型能否同时服务其中多个环节，并仍输出可解释的中间对象（intermediate objects），以保持链条的可解释性。

Method: 将喷注中的每个对象表示在单一共享 token 词表中，训练一个模型实现任意模态子集之间的相互映射；任务被定义为“提供哪些模态、请求哪些模态”的选择。在全部模态组合上训练，解码器可自回归或并行地输出 token；tokenisation 使两种架构在少量调参下即可稳定训练。

Result: 在评估中，两种模型均能产生逼真的重建与模拟对象，其中自回归模型对 Geant4 输出的拟合格外忠实；该自回归模型在多项典型喷注重建指标（jet reconstruction metrics）上优于当前最优的粒子流算法（state-of-the-art particle flow algorithm）。

Conclusion: 单一共享 token 词表与多模态互映射的建模范式可将重建与模拟链条中的多个步骤统一到一个模型内，同时保留中间对象的可解释性；自回归解码在模拟保真度与喷注重建指标上表现突出，提示该方向具备替代或整合部分专用算法的潜力。

Abstract: Reconstruction and simulation at a collider experiment are long chains of specialised algorithms, each tuned to a single step. We explore how one model can serve many of those steps at once, while still producing the intermediate objects (tracks, calorimeter cells, clusters, particles and jets) that make the chain interpretable. To do so, we represent every object in a jet in a single shared token vocabulary and train one model to map any subset of these modalities to any other. A task is then only a choice of which modalities to provide and which to request: particle flow, detector simulation and charged energy subtraction are all directions through the same set of weights. We train over all modality combinations, with a decoder emitting tokens either autoregressively or in parallel. With tokenisation, both architectures train stably with little tuning. In evaluations, both models produce realistic reconstruction and simulation objects, with the autoregressive model particularly faithful to output from Geant4. The autoregressive model also outperforms a state-of-the-art particle flow algorithm on many typical jet reconstruction metrics.

</details>


### [160] [Escaping Solar Capture: Majoron mediated Elastic Dark Matter for the LZ 248~keV Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32435)
*Salvador Centelles Chuliá,Ayan Chattaraj,Rahul Srivastava,Surabhi Srivastava*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 将 LZ 报告的 248 keV_nr 核反冲候选事件解释为经由轻 Majoron 媒介的弹性暗物质散射，并在 scotogenic 紫外完备框架中给出具体实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ 实验报告的 248 keV_nr 核反冲候选事件需要物理机制解释。Majoron 作为轻赝标量玻色子，天然提供所需的赝标量相互作用，并将反冲信号与中微子质量产生机制直接关联。

Method: 先对轻 Majoron 媒介弹性暗物质散射进行模型无关分析，随后构建 scotogenic 紫外完备模型：最轻单态马约拉纳费米子构成暗物质，其质量与 Majoron 耦合均来自对称性破缺动力学；引入矢量型上夸克与夸克部门 type-I Dirac seesaw 将 Majoron 相互作用传递至上夸克。

Result: 低能有效理论产生非相对论算符 O6，其动量依赖可再现高反冲事件而不过度填充低能区间。该自旋结构消除主要重元素太阳捕获通道，氢核捕获则因运动学失配和动量转移小而被压低。该构造同时符合 LZ 反冲谱与观测到的热遗迹丰度，并强烈抑制太阳捕获。

Conclusion: Majoron 媒介方案可自洽解释 LZ 高反冲候选事件，兼顾遗迹丰度约束并抑制太阳捕获，为反冲信号与中微子质量起源提供统一联系。

Abstract: We interpret the $248~\mathrm{keV_{nr}}$ nuclear-recoil candidate reported by LZ through elastic dark matter scattering mediated by a light Majoron. The Majoron provides a natural origin for the required pseudoscalar interaction: it is the pseudo Nambu-Goldstone boson associated with spontaneous lepton number breaking, directly connecting the recoil signal to neutrino mass generation. After a model independent analysis of this idea, we present a concrete realization in a scotogenic ultraviolet completion in which the lightest singlet Majorana fermion is dark matter and acquires both its mass and Majoron coupling from the symmetry breaking dynamics. A vector like up type quark and a type-I Dirac seesaw in quark sector communicate the Majoron interaction to the up quark. At low energies, the resulting portal generates the nonrelativistic operator $\mathcal O_6$, whose momentum dependence reproduces the high-recoil event without overpopulating the lower energy bins. Its spin structure removes the dominant heavy element solar capture channels, while capture on hydrogen is suppressed by poor kinematic matching and small momentum transfer. This construction simultaneously reproduces the LZ recoil spectrum and the observed thermal relic abundance while strongly suppressing solar capture.

</details>


### [161] [Landscape of pentaquark bound states with neural-network Variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32624)
*Jing-Zhe Song,Wei-Lin Wu,Shi-Lin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究采用神经网络变分蒙特卡洛（neural network variational Monte Carlo）方法，在组分夸克势模型（constituent quark potential model）框架下系统研究五夸克（pentaquark）系统基态，扫描387种色、自旋与同位旋组态，识别出21个束缚态候选，全部为浅束缚的分子态（molecular state）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统检验显式奇异（manifestly exotic）负宇称五夸克系统是否存在深束缚态，因其无法通过轻或奇异夸克对的产生与湮灭与传统重子混合，可为五夸克结构提供干净的研究窗口。

Method: 在组分夸克势模型下使用神经网络变分蒙特卡洛方法，穷举387种色、自旋与同位旋组态，并以色–自旋–同位旋全空间搜索束缚态；同时以未束缚系统基态能量向重子–介子阈值（baryon–meson threshold）的自上收敛作为束缚判据的交叉验证。

Result: 识别出21个束缚态候选，束缚能（binding energy）介于2至15 MeV；所有束缚态均为浅束缚分子态，未发现深束缚五夸克态。QQqqQ̄、ssqqQ̄、QsqqQ̄与QqqqQ̄型束缚态由重矢量介子（heavy vector meson）与重子构成，QQqqs̄与Qqqqs̄型由K介子与重子构成；未束缚系统基态能量自上方收敛至重子–介子阈值，与S波散射态一致，并复现了实验观测隐藏粲五夸克共振（hidden-charm pentaquark resonance）所在的ηcN与ηcΛ最低散射态；全重五夸克态均未束缚。

Conclusion: 显式奇异五夸克系统中不存在深束缚态，束缚态均为接近阈值的浅分子态；该结果为LHCb、Belle II等实验未来的五夸克搜寻提供了有价值的指引。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of the ground states of pentaquark systems using the neural network variational Monte Carlo method within the constituent quark potential model. We investigate all manifestly exotic pentaquark systems with negative parity, which cannot mix with the conventional baryons through the creation and annihilation of light or strange quark pair. We exhaust 387 color, spin, and isospin configurations and identify 21 pentaquark bound state candidates with binding energies ranging from $2$ to $15$ MeV. A striking observation is that all of the bound states are shallowly bound molecular states, and a deeply bound pentaquark state does not exist. The $QQqq\bar{Q}$, $ssqq\bar{Q}$, $Qsqq\bar{Q}$ and $Qqqq\bar{Q}$ bound states are composed of a heavy vector meson and a baryon, while the $QQqq\bar{s}$ and $Qqqq\bar{s}$ bound states are composed of a $K$ meson and a heavy baryon. As a cross-validation, the ground-state energies of the unbound systems converge to the baryon--meson thresholds from above, which are consistent with the S-wave scattering states and provide a reliable baseline for the bound-state criterion. Especially, we reproduce the lowest scattering states $η_c N$ and $η_c Λ$ in the $qqqc\bar{c}$ and $qqsc\bar{c}$ channels respectively, where the hidden-charm pentaquark resonances were observed experimentally. We also find that all fully heavy pentaquark states are unbound. Our results provide valuable guidance for future pentaquark searches at LHCb, Belle II, and other experiments.

</details>


### [162] [Quantum tomography at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32800)
*Michael Fucilla,Yuxun Guo,Yoshitaka Hatta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出在未来的电子离子对撞机（EIC）上，通过方位角关联实现无质量与有质量夸克-反夸克对的自旋密度矩阵量子态层析，从而可在实验中研究纠缠与贝尔非局域性等量子信息性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在将量子态层析（quantum state tomography）拓展至电子离子对撞机（EIC）所生成的无质量与有质量夸克-反夸克对，以便在实验中检验高能物理中的量子信息特性。

Method: 利用柯林斯碎裂函数（Collins fragmentation function）与双强子碎裂函数（dihadron fragmentation function）所产生的强子-双强子对之间的方位角关联（azimuthal angular correlation），提取自旋密度矩阵（spin density matrix）的全部组分。

Result: 研究显示自旋密度矩阵所有组分均可通过上述方位角关联获取，并预测近期在重夸克（heavy-quark）领域发现的最大纠缠（maximal entanglement）会表现为通常柯林斯角依赖 cos(φ+φ') 的强烈修正。

Conclusion: 该方案使得在EIC上实验研究纠缠、贝尔非局域性（Bell-nonlocality）等多种量子信息性质成为可能。

Abstract: We initiate the study of quantum state tomography---the experimental reconstruction of the complete spin density matrix---of massless and massive quark-antiquark pairs produced at the future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC). We show that all components of the spin density matrix can be accessed through azimuthal angular correlations between hadron and dihadron pairs generated by the Collins and dihadron fragmentation functions. This enables the experimental investigation of a variety of quantum information properties at the EIC, such as entanglement and Bell-nonlocality. We predict that the recently discovered maximal entanglement in the heavy-quark sector is signaled by a strong modification of the usual Collins angular dependence $\cos(φ+φ')$

</details>


### [163] [Machine learning for the LHC physics program: a 2025-2026 stocktake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32874)
*Jesse Thaler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文以机器生成方式综述2025年5月至2026年5月高能物理机器学习（HEPML）进展，基于HEPML Living Review语料（分界前1,756篇、分界后569篇），由AI流程筛读103篇全文并综合出六项论断，核心结论是HEPML已从造工具的研究领域转变为LHC物理项目所依赖的基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于对LHC物理项目中的机器学习（machine learning, ML）进展进行阶段性盘点，并借由AI流程处理快速增长的文献。摘要指出该综述本身即由agentic AI系统生成，因而也附带讨论了这种工作方式引发的关切。

Method: 方法上，以HEPML Living Review为语料，在2025年5月切分为1,756篇早期论文与569篇后期论文；由AI流程调研569篇摘要，按引用量、时效性、主题与合作者参与度排序，全文阅读103篇（其中95篇为切分后、8篇为切分前基线），逐篇生成结构化笔记。

Result: 结果是将笔记综合为关于领域现状的六项论断，并经独立评审智能体（reviewer agents）核查、再与源论文复核。首要论断是HEPML已不再仅是构建工具的研究领域，而成为LHC物理项目依赖的基础设施——ATLAS与CMS已发表依赖神经网络的物理结果，归档的ALEPH数据重新进入生产。

Conclusion: 其余五项论断为：基于模拟的推断（simulation-based inference）与基础模型（foundation models）两场变革开始融合；AI智能体（AI agents）是真正的新前沿，十二个月内出现47篇论文但尚无被采用的测量；"我们是否信任它？"是增长最快的议程，近期每五篇论文中就有一篇涉及不确定性、校准或可解释性；减缓之处亦具信息量，等变性（equivariance）被吸收进工具，模型特定现象学让位于模型无关搜索；理论机器学习在多个研究方向跨过能力阈值。文末总结已定、将至与开放问题，并简述此种AI工作方式的隐忧。

Abstract: The first sentence of this abstract--and the introduction to these proceedings--was authored by a human, but the bulk of this document was generated by an agentic AI system. In this talk, I take stock of machine learning (ML) for the LHC physics program over the twelve months from May 2025 to May 2026. The corpus is the HEPML Living Review, split at May 2025 into 1,756 earlier papers and 569 later ones. An AI pipeline surveyed the 569 abstracts, ranked them by citations, recency, theme, and collaboration involvement, and read 103 papers in full (95 from after the split, plus 8 earlier baseline papers), producing a structured note for each. The notes were then synthesized into six claims about the state of the field, checked by independent reviewer agents, and re-verified against the source papers. The headline claim is that (1) ML for high-energy physics (HEPML) stopped being a research area that builds tools and became infrastructure that the LHC physics program depends on: ATLAS and CMS now publish physics results that depend on neural networks, and the archived ALEPH data have re-entered production. The other five claims are: (2) simulation-based inference and foundation models are two revolutions starting to merge; (3) AI agents are the genuinely new front, with 47 papers in twelve months and no adopted measurement yet; (4) "do we trust it?" is the fastest-growing agenda, with one recent paper in five about uncertainty, calibration, or interpretability; (5) what is slowing down is informative, since equivariance was absorbed into a tool and model-specific phenomenology ceded ground to model-agnostic searches; and (6) theory ML crossed a capability threshold in multiple research areas. I close with what is settled, what is incoming, and what is open, and briefly discuss the concerns raised by this way of working with AI.

</details>


### [164] [Constraining Ultra-Light Vector Bosons via Solar Neutrino Oscillations: The Gauged $L_μ- L_τ$ Model and Prospects for JUNO and XLZD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33877)
*Ilídio Lopes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在规范 \(U(1)_{L_\mu-L_\tau}\) 对称性框架下引入超轻矢量玻色子（ultra-light vector boson）\(Z^\prime\)，将其作为独立的太阳中微子现象学框架，利用 Borexino、SNO、Super-Kamiokande 数据与最新标准太阳模型限制其质量低于 \(5.7\times10^{-17}\,{\rm eV}\)，并给出 JUNO 与 XLZD 的未来投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该反常自由（anomaly-free）构造历史上源于对缪子反常磁矩（muon anomalous magnetic moment）的解释，本文则将其转而用作太阳中微子物理（solar neutrino physics）的独立现象学框架，探索其对新物理参数空间的约束能力。

Method: 通过 \(Z^\prime\) 与光子之间的动能混合（kinetic mixing），在太阳内部产生有效物质势（effective matter potential），借助 Mikheyev–Smirnov–Wolfenstein 机制修改中微子味振荡（neutrino flavour oscillations）；结合 Borexino、SNO、Super-Kamiokande 的近期太阳中微子测量与最新标准太阳模型进行拟合约束。

Result: 玻色子质量被限制在 \(5.7\times10^{-17}\,{\rm eV}\) 以下，可作为超轻暗物质候选者；最优模型取耦合参数 \(\eta_o \approx -2\) 时给出 \(\chi_\nu^2 = 2.7\)，优于标准三味情形的 \(\chi_\nu^2 = 3.1\)；最佳拟合对应有效四费米子耦合 \(g_{Z^\prime}^2\varepsilon/m_{Z^\prime}^2 \approx 6.6\times10^{-23}\,{\rm eV}^{-2}\)，约为费米常数（Fermi constant）的 \(5.7\) 倍；当 \(\varepsilon\approx1\) 且参考媒介质量 \(1\,{\rm eV}\) 时，规范耦合 \(g_{Z^\prime}\approx8\times10^{-12}\)。

Conclusion: JUNO（自 2025 年 8 月起运行，具世界领先的振荡参数精度）与 XLZD 合作组应能显著改进上述约束；此类超轻粒子或可缓解冷暗物质（cold dark matter）范式下星系模拟中的长期差异，而地面长基线中微子实验（long-baseline neutrino experiments）可提供互补的参数空间探测。

Abstract: We present an extension of the Standard Model based upon the gauged $U(1)_{L_μ- L_τ}$ symmetry, featuring an ultra-light vector boson that couples to the difference of muon and tau lepton numbers. This anomaly-free construction, historically motivated by the muon's anomalous magnetic moment, is employed here as an independent phenomenological framework for solar neutrino physics. Through kinetic mixing with the photon, the $Z^\prime$ boson generates an effective matter potential within the solar interior, thereby modifying neutrino flavour oscillations via the Mikheyev--Smirnov--Wolfenstein mechanism. Utilising recent solar neutrino measurements from Borexino, SNO, and Super-Kamiokande, together with an up-to-date standard solar model, we constrain the boson mass to be below $5.7\times 10^{-17}\,{\rm eV}$, a potential ultra-light dark matter candidate. The optimal model, with coupling parameter $η_o \approx -2$, yields $χ_ν^2 = 2.7$, compared with $χ_ν^2 = 3.1$ for the standard three-flavour scenario. This best fit corresponds to an effective four-fermion coupling $g_{Z^\prime}^2 \varepsilon / m_{Z^\prime}^2 \approx 6.6 \times 10^{-23}\,{\rm eV}^{-2}$, approximately $5.7$ times the Fermi constant, equivalent for $\varepsilon \approx 1$ and a reference mediator mass of $1\,{\rm eV}$ to a gauge coupling $g_{Z^\prime} \approx 8\times 10^{-12}$. We also present projections for forthcoming experiments: the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO), operating since August 2025 with world-leading precision on oscillation parameters, together with the XLZD consortium, should substantially refine these constraints. Such ultra-light particles may address long-standing discrepancies in galaxy simulations within the cold dark matter paradigm, whilst terrestrial long-baseline neutrino experiments provide complementary probes of this parameter space.

</details>


### [165] [Gottfried-Jackson polarization filters for holographic dilaton and transverse-traceless gluon responses in near-threshold $J/ψ$ photoproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33908)
*Arkadiy I. Syamtomov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在 Hatta-Yang 全息引力子-膨胀子振幅（holographic graviton-dilaton amplitude）框架下，研究识别了近阈 $J/\psi$ 光生过程在 Gottfried-Jackson 基中的精确膨胀子-零自旋投影通道，并提出以极化滤波区分胶子与膨胀子贡献的判据。与 GlueX、CLAS12 截面对比显示，原固定形状全息振幅无法以单一整体归一化描述全部近阈数据，故这些极化滤波更宜视为产生张量的检验而非已验证模型的预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近阈 $J/\psi$ 光生是探测核子内部胶子与可能膨胀子（dilaton）自由度的敏感窗口，但全息模型预言的可靠性与数据符合程度尚不明确。该工作旨在厘清膨胀子贡献在特定自旋投影通道中的分布，并检验原全息振幅对现代高精度数据的描述能力。

Method: 在 Gottfried-Jackson 基中分解 Hatta-Yang 全息引力子-膨胀子振幅，识别膨胀子不贡献的精确通道（如 $m=0$ 与 $m=-\lambda_\gamma$ 振幅）；对垂直于反应面的光子极化，$m=0$ 通道进一步孤立出 $A_g(t)+B_g(t)$ 并需核子自旋转移。利用标准束流极化自旋密度矩阵元构造仅含 TT 的控制量及含线性膨胀子-TT 干涉的双线性量。

Result: $m=0$ 与 $m=-\lambda_\gamma$ 振幅不含膨胀子贡献；当光子极化垂直于反应面时，$m=0$ 通道可进一步孤立 $A_g(t)+B_g(t)$ 且要求核子自旋转移。标准束流极化自旋密度矩阵元给出仅 TT 的控制项以及包含线性膨胀子-TT 干涉的双线性项。与 GlueX、CLAS12 近阈截面对比表明，原固定形状全息振幅无法用单一整体归一化拟合全部数据。

Conclusion: 所提出的极化滤波应被理解为对产生张量（production tensor）的检验，而非对已全局验证反应模型的预言；这意味着全息振幅的固定形状假设在近阈区受到数据挑战，需进一步修正方可作为可靠的全局模型。

Abstract: Within the Hatta-Yang holographic graviton-dilaton amplitude for near-threshold $J/ψ$ photoproduction, we identify exact dilaton-null spin-projection channels in the Gottfried-Jackson basis. The $m=0$ and $m=-λ_γ$ amplitudes receive no dilaton contribution; for photon polarization normal to the reaction plane the $m=0$ channel further isolates $A_g(t)+B_g(t)$ and requires nucleon spin transfer. Standard beam-polarization spin-density-matrix elements then furnish TT-only controls and a bilinear containing linear dilaton-TT interference. A comparison with modern GlueX and CLAS12 cross sections shows that the original fixed-shape holographic amplitude cannot describe the full near-threshold data with one overall normalization, making the polarization filters tests of the production tensor rather than predictions of a globally validated reaction model.

</details>


### [166] [Meson photoproduction at Jefferson Lab: from two-meson final states to meson-baryon spin-density matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35045)
*Vincent Mathieu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该综述总结了 JPAC 与 Jefferson Lab 的 GlueX、CLAS 实验合作在介子光致产生（meson photoproduction）方面的进展，涵盖 ηπ 系统的理解、混合介子（hybrid meson）寻找途径，以及 γp → ρ⁻Δ⁺⁺ 完整角分布的推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找最轻的混合介子 π₁(1600) 是核心动机，其预期主要衰变为 b₁π，因此涉及反冲 Δ⁺⁺ 的反应被视为奇异态（exotic）搜索的有前景通道。作为迈向 γp → b₁πΔ⁺⁺ 的第一步，需先建立相关反应的角分布描述框架。

Method: 基于 JPAC 与 GlueX、CLAS 实验的联合工作，对介子光致产生进行综述；推导了线偏振光子束下 γp → ρ⁻Δ⁺⁺ 的完整角分布，引入 56 个双指标自旋密度矩阵元（double-index spin-density matrix elements）以描述 ρ 与 Δ 衰变之间的关联，并考察产生振幅因子化情形。

Result: ηπ 系统光致产生已得到较好理解，从 a₂(1320) 共振区延伸至由双 Regge 交换（double Regge exchanges）主导的高质量区。γp → ρ⁻Δ⁺⁺ 的完整角分布由 56 个双指标自旋密度矩阵元刻画；当振幅因子化（如单 Regge 极点交换情形）时，强度简化为标准 ρ 与 Δ 衰变分布之积，仅依赖 14 个参数。

Conclusion: 该框架为通过 Δ⁺⁺ 反冲通道搜寻奇异介子提供了角分布层面的基础，因子化条件下参数数目由 56 减至 14，为后续 γp → b₁πΔ⁺⁺ 分析奠定第一步。

Abstract: Recent progress in the understanding of meson photoproduction, obtained from the joint effort of the Joint Physics Analysis Center (JPAC) and the GlueX and CLAS experiments at Jefferson Lab, is reviewed. The photoproduction of the $ηπ$ system is now well understood, from the $a_2(1320)$ resonance region to the high-mass region dominated by double Regge exchanges. Since the lightest hybrid meson, the $π_1(1600)$, is expected to decay predominantly into $b_1π$, reactions with a recoiling $Δ^{++}$ are promising channels for exotic searches. As a first step towards $γp \to b_1 πΔ^{++}$, the complete angular distribution of $γp \to ρ^-Δ^{++}$ with a linearly polarized photon beam is derived. It involves 56 double-index spin-density matrix elements, which describe the correlations between the $ρ$ and the $Δ$ decays. When the production amplitudes factorize, as is the case for the exchange of a single Regge pole, the intensity reduces to the product of the standard $ρ$ and $Δ$ decay distributions and depends on only 14 parameters.

</details>


### [167] [Perturbative QCD correction to the nonleptonic weak rate in dense quark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35165)
*Matti Heikinheimo,Hanna Lempiäinen,Risto Paatelainen,Tomi Ruosteoja,Kimmo Tuominen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次计算了稠密夸克物质中非轻子弱味平衡速率（nonleptonic weak flavor-equilibration rate）的领头微扰量子色动力学（perturbative QCD）修正，结果规范不变且完全有限，在低温高密区将树图速率（tree-level rate）提高约 30–40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前唯象计算所用的树图级速率已沿用三十余年，缺乏更高精度的微观输入，尤其在稠密夸克物质体黏滞（bulk viscosity）相关研究中需要更准确的速率为基础。

Method: 基于 Kadanoff-Baym 动力学方程（Kadanoff-Baym kinetic equation），采用实时热场论（real-time thermal field theory）进行计算，并联合实修正与虚修正。

Result: 所得 QCD 修正既规范不变又完全有限，所有软发散与共线发散（soft and collinear divergences）在实虚修正合并后相消，符合 Kinoshita-Lee-Nauenberg 定理；在适用于微扰 QCD 的高密度区间与致密星动力学相关的低温区，修正使树图速率提升 30–40%。

Conclusion: 该修正后的速率改进了沿用三十余年的树图结果，为稠密夸克物质体黏滞计算提供了更准确的微观输入。

Abstract: We present the first calculation of the leading perturbative quantum chromodynamics (QCD) correction to the nonleptonic weak flavor-equilibration rate in dense quark matter. The computation is based on the Kadanoff-Baym kinetic equation and performed using real-time thermal field theory. The resulting QCD correction is both gauge invariant and fully finite. In particular, all soft and collinear divergences cancel after combining real and virtual corrections, in accordance with the Kinoshita-Lee-Nauenberg theorem. In the low-temperature regime relevant to compact-star dynamics, the correction enhances the tree-level rate by $30 - 40\%$ over the high-density range where perturbative QCD is applicable. The corrected rate improves on the tree-level result used in phenomenological calculations for more than three decades, providing a more accurate microscopic input for bulk-viscosity calculations in dense quark matter.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Classical freeness of affine vertex algebras of $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ at boundary admissible levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33348)
*Tomoyuki Arakawa,Xuanzhong Dai*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文证明单仿射顶点代数（simple affine vertex algebra）$L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 在边界可允许水平（boundary admissible level）处是经典自由（classically free）的，从而验证了 Andrews、van Ekeren 与 Heluani 提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于确证 Andrews、van Ekeren 和 Heluani 关于 $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 在边界可允许水平下经典自由性的猜想，该问题涉及仿射顶点代数的结构性质。

Method: 摘要未说明具体技术路线，仅表明通过证明（prove）的方式处理该猜想，研究对象为 $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ 对应的单仿射顶点代数 $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 及其边界可允许水平参数。

Result: 证明了 $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 在边界可允许水平处具有经典自由性，即所涉猜想成立。

Conclusion: 该结果确认了 Andrews、van Ekeren 与 Heluani 的猜想，表明边界可允许水平是 $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ 经典自由性的一个成立情形。

Abstract: We prove that the simple affine vertex algebra $L_k(\mathfrak{sl}_2)$ is classically free at the boundary admissible level, which confirms a conjecture of Andrews, van Ekeren, and Heluani.

</details>


### [169] [Inequivalent modular tensor categories with identical $S$, $T$ and $W$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33231)
*Ran Luo,Jiahua Tian*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该论文否定了"模数据（modular data）$(S,T)$与Whitehead-link矩阵$W$共同决定模张量范畴（modular tensor category）至ribbon等价"这一猜想，通过对$(\mathbb Z/p\mathbb Z)^3$构造两个辫织不等价的Dijkgraaf--Witten模范畴，它们在单一单对象双射下具有相同的$(S,T,W)$乃至所有至多二分量的框定定向链环的Reshetikhin--Turaev不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：$(S,T)$与Whitehead-link矩阵$W$是否足以将模张量范畴确定到ribbon等价（ribbon equivalence）。该问题此前悬而未决，本文旨在给出否定回答，并揭示模张量范畴完全分类所需的额外不变量。

Method: 构造方法为：在固定二次上同调类（quadratic cohomology class）的前提下改变一个交错3-上循环（alternating 3-cocycle）；利用对秩至多为二的子群的限制来匹配链环不变量，并联合二次类与交错类来阻碍辫织等价（braided equivalence）。此外解析计算了一个闭定向三维流形上的无染色配分函数（uncolored partition function）。

Result: 对素数$p\ge5$，得到两个辫织不等价的Dijkgraaf--Witten模范畴，其在单一单对象双射下具有相同的$(S,T,W)$，并具有相同的所有至多二分量框定定向链环的Reshetikhin--Turaev不变量。所构造三维流形上的无染色配分函数可解析地分离该族中全部$(p-1)/2$个等价类。

Conclusion: 模张量范畴的完整分类（至ribbon等价）需要超出"至多二分量染色框定链环不变量"范围的额外不变量；即$(S,T,W)$不足以确定范畴，且仅靠这类链环不变量无法区分所构造的范畴族。

Abstract: It remains an open question whether the modular data $(S,T)$ together with the Whitehead-link matrix $W$ determine a modular tensor category up to ribbon equivalence. We answer negatively by constructing two braided-inequivalent Dijkgraaf--Witten modular categories for $(\mathbb Z/p\mathbb Z)^3$ for prime $p\ge5$ with identical $(S,T,W)$ and, more generally, identical Reshetikhin--Turaev invariants of all framed oriented links with at most two components under a single bijection of simple objects. The construction varies an alternating $3$-cocycle while fixing a quadratic cohomology class, using restriction to subgroups of rank at most two to match link invariants and the quadratic and alternating classes together to obstruct braided equivalence. A complete classification of modular tensor categories up to ribbon equivalence therefore requires invariants beyond those of colored framed links with at most two components. We also analytically evaluate an uncolored partition function on a closed oriented three-manifold that separates all $(p-1)/2$ equivalence classes in the family.

</details>
