### [170] [Physics-based logarithmic description of electrostatic field enhancement in hemisphere-on-cylindrical-post structures for high-field applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32974)
*Thiago A. de Assis,Fernando F. Dall-Agnol,Richard G. Forbes*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本论文针对半球-圆柱柱（HCP）模型的顶点场增强因子（AFEF）缺乏精确解析公式的问题，提出一种对数型紧凑近似公式，在ASR为1至1000范围内与有限元分析相比最大误差仅0.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尖锐导体结构处的静电（ES）场增强在防雷、电晕放电、真空电击穿（如粒子加速器）及场电子发射体等应用中具有核心地位，而HCP模型是研究该效应的典型几何，但其AFEF与顶点尖锐度系数（ASC）目前无已知精确解析公式（甚至可能不存在）。

Method: 论文针对HCP模型（结构置于大横向尺寸平面导体上、且与对电极间存在大间隙）发展出ASC的紧凑解析近似，从而给出AFEF的近似表达；该公式采用对数型（logarithmic-style）结构，而非以往近似所用的幂型（power-style）结构。

Result: 在计算可达的ASR范围1至1000内，将该对数型公式与HCP模型的精确有限元分析（finite-element analyses）结果对比，其最大误差幅度为0.15%，明显优于旧的近似方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的对数型紧凑公式为HCP模型的AFEF提供了高精度且形式简洁的解析近似，在精度上显著超越以往近似，可作为该经典静电场增强问题的实用计算工具。

Abstract: Electrostatic (ES) field enhancement at sharp conducting structures plays a central role in lightning protection, corona discharge, electrical breakdown in vacuum (for example in particle accelerators), and more generally in technological applications of field electron emitters. A canonical geometry for studying this ES effect is the hemisphere-on-cylindrical-post (HCP) model, in the regime where the structure stands on a planar conductor of large lateral extent, and a large gap exists between the structure and the counter-electrode. A parameter of major interest is the apex field enhancement factor (AFEF) (apex-ES-field/background-ES-field). For this (and other) structures, a formula for the AFEF can be written in the form AFEF = ASC x ASR, where the apex sharpness ratio (ASR) is given by the ratio (post-height/apex-radius-of-curvature), and the apex sharpness coefficient (ASC) depends on the post shape. For the HCP model, no exact analytical formulas for the ASC or the AFEF are currently known. (Quite possibly none exist.) This paper develops a compact analytical approximation for the ASC and hence for the AFEF. This compact formula has a logarithmic-style structure, rather than the power-style structure used in previous approximations. When compared with precise finite-element analyses of the HCP model, over the computationally accessible range 1 to 1000 for the ASR, this logarithmic-style formula has a maximum error-magnitude of 0.15 percent, which is significantly better than older approximations.

</details>

### [171] [Rare but stable: hidden states in the 1D swarmalator model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33929)
*Rommel Tchinda Djeudjo,Kevin P. O'Keeffe,Timoteo Carletti*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 该研究揭示一维 swarmalator 模型除已知的同步、相位波与异步三态外，还隐藏着大量稳定的 q-扭曲（q-twisted）与 m-聚簇（m-clustered）平衡态；这些态因淹没于退化中性流形（neutral manifold）中而极少被随机初值触及，但对高斯扰动具有代数衰减的局部稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有文献通常以一维 swarmalator 模型的三种宏观态（同步、相位波、异步）来理解其行为，可能遗漏了模型中更丰富的平衡结构。本文旨在检验同一模型是否还容纳其他稳定平衡态，并刻画其可达性与稳健性。

Method: 采用块循环 Jacobian（block-circulant Jacobian）以闭式形式确定稳定性；通过高斯扰动（per-coordinate amplitude ε）测量存活半径，并进行远踢实验（far-kick experiments）以探查吸引域（basin）的几何形态；与最近邻 Kuramoto 环上的扭曲态作对比分析。

Result: 模型包含大量隐藏的稳定平衡态族（q-扭曲与 m-聚簇），随机初始条件几乎无法到达，因其嵌入于全局吸引域趋近于零的退化中性流形。这些态在局部仍具稳健性：高斯扰动下存活半径仅以 1/q 与 1/m 的代数方式收缩，显著区别于最近邻 Kuramoto 环上扭曲态的高斯吸引域与缠绕上限（二者在平均场耦合下均被消除）。

Conclusion: 扩展 swarmalator 模型中报道的若干模式，实际上已在最小的一维模型中存在（或稳定、或中性稳定）；远踢实验表明这些吸引域是紧致核心（compact cores）而非触须状集合。

Abstract: The one-dimensional swarmalator model is usually understood through three macroscopic states: synchrony, the phase wave, and asynchrony. We show that the identical model also contains a much larger set of hidden stable equilibria - families of q-twisted and m-clustered states - which random initial conditions almost never reach, because many are embedded in a degenerate neutral manifold of vanishing global basin. They are nonetheless locally robust: under Gaussian perturbations of per-coordinate amplitude ε their survival radii shrink only algebraically, as 1/q and 1/m, in sharp contrast to the Gaussian basins and winding ceiling of twisted states on a nearest-neighbour Kuramoto ring, both removed by mean-field coupling. A block-circulant Jacobian fixes the stability in closed form, and far-kick experiments indicate the basins are compact cores rather than tentacled sets. Several patterns reported in extended swarmalator models thus already exist, stable or neutrally stable, in the minimal one.

</details>


### [172] [Global synchronization of topological signals with time-delayed interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34609)
*Wilfried Segnou,Thierry Njougouo,Diego Garlaschelli,Timoteo Carletti*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文将时滞相互作用引入高阶结构上的拓扑振子同步问题，基于Hodge-Laplace矩阵与Dirac算子分别推导主稳定函数，并用Lambert W函数给出可解析处理的谱条件；结果表明合理时滞既可促进也可抑制全局拓扑同步，而Dirac耦合情形下全局拓扑Dirac同步无法出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑信号（topological signals）是支撑于单纯复形（simplicial complexes）或胞腔复形（cell complexes）等高阶结构上的动力学变量，其同步机制尚待刻画，尤其时滞相互作用（time-delayed interactions）的影响缺乏解析性结论。因此有必要研究时滞如何影响拓扑振子的全局同步出现条件。

Method: 以Stuart-Landau系统为对象，分两种耦合情形：一是支撑在同一维度的单纯形或胞腔上的信号，经Hodge-Laplace矩阵耦合；二是支撑在不同维度单纯形或胞腔上的信号，经Dirac算子耦合。方法上推导主稳定函数（Master Stability Function），在合适的离散时滞取值下借助Lambert W函数求解稳定性，并配合数值仿真。

Result: 在Hodge-Laplace耦合情形下，得到全局拓扑同步（Global Topological Synchronization）出现的显式谱条件，且可行时滞可能促进或抑制同步；在Dirac算子耦合情形下，相应变分问题同样可简化，但可证明全局拓扑Dirac同步（Global Topological Dirac Synchronization）无法出现。单纯复形与胞腔复形上的数值仿真验证并补充了上述结论。

Conclusion: 时滞是调控高阶结构上拓扑同步的关键参数：其影响依赖耦合算子类型，在Hodge-Laplace耦合中可通过Lambert W函数解析判定并具双向调节作用，而Dirac耦合下同步不可实现，说明维度间耦合对同步具有本质限制。

Abstract: Topological signals are dynamical variables supported on higher-order structures such as simplicial or cell complexes. In this work, we investigate the impact of time-delayed interactions on the emergence of global synchronization of topological oscillators, with application to the Stuart-Landau system. We first examine the case where signals are supported on simplexes, or cells, of a given dimension and coupled through the Hodge-Laplace matrix. We derive the Master Stability Function and we prove that for suitable discrete delay values, the stability of the synchronous solution becomes analytically tractable and can be solved by using the Lambert W-function. This analysis yields explicit spectral conditions for Global Topological Synchronization to emerge and shows that admissible delays may either promote or suppress synchronization. We then study delayed interactions between signals supported on simplexes, or cells, of different dimensions coupled by the Dirac operator. Under analogous admissible time delays the variational problem, still related to the Master Stability Function, can again be simplified, however in this case we prove that Global Topological Dirac Synchronization cannot emerge. Numerical simulations on simplicial and cell complexes corroborate and complement these findings.

</details>

### [173] [Derived deformation theory of heterotic $G_2$ systems near the standard embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35071)
*Bram Brongers*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在自旋七维流形上固定弦 Courant 代数胚（Courant algebroid）上，研究异质 $G_2$ 结构在无挠标准嵌入附近、按 $\alpha'$ 一阶及形变的全形式阶展开的展开；通过小通量条件及其相容性恒等式构造两个解析，二者局部形式同构，并借助循环转移得到有效势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是研究异质 $G_2$ 结构（heterotic $G_2$ structures）在无挠标准嵌入附近的形变，并将异质超势（heterotic superpotential）置于广义 Dirac 泛函与形式量子场论框架下加以解析。为此需处理小通量扇区下的相容性问题，以获得一个良好定义的物理理论。

Method: 在自旋七维流形 $Y$ 上，容许的度规、旋量线与密度确定一个规范广义 Dirac 泛函（generalized Dirac functional），其在物理分裂下的表达式即异质超势。采用小通量扇区 $H^{[0]}=0$，将条件与相容性恒等式并入理论，构造解析物理理论与变分临界理论（variational critical theory）的相容性扩张，并用移位余切 Hamilton 构造、混合阶椭圆性（mixed-order ellipticity）与同伦转移（homotopy transfer）建立有限维极小模型。

Result: 两个解析作为实局部形式 $Q$-理论局部形式同构。当 $Y$ 闭且 gerbe 与反常扇区固定时，超势的临界场在每个集中于零次的局部实 Artin 代数上与 BPS 场重合；循环转移给出有效势 $W_{\mathrm{eff}}$，用于描述零次 Artin 代数上的普通形式物理解轨迹（经形式坐标变换后）。相对上同调的相容性比较在 dg-Artin 代数上被检测，并被变分临界理论遗忘，且标准嵌入处的带点相对纤维（pointed relative fibre）为同伦阿贝尔（homotopy abelian）。

Conclusion: 异质 $G_2$ 结构的形变可由广义 Dirac 泛函与超势统一刻画，且在小通量扇区下物理理论与变分临界理论通过相容性扩张局部形式同构；其相对上同调携带被变分理论遗忘的额外信息，标准嵌入处相对纤维呈同伦阿贝尔性。

Abstract: We study heterotic $G_2$ structures on a fixed string Courant algebroid on a spin seven-manifold $Y$, near a torsion-free standard embedding, to first order in $α'$ and to all formal orders in the deformations. The admissible metric, spinor line and density determine a canonical generalized Dirac functional whose expression in a physical splitting is the heterotic superpotential. We work in the small-flux sector $H^{[0]}=0$. We adjoin this condition and its compatibility identities to obtain a resolved physical theory, and construct a corresponding compatibility extension of the variational critical theory. The two resolutions are locally formally isomorphic as real local formal $Q$-theories.
  When $Y$ is closed and the gerbe and anomaly sector is fixed, the critical fields of the superpotential coincide with the BPS fields over every local real Artin algebra concentrated in degree zero. The shifted-cotangent Hamiltonian construction makes the variational critical theory cyclic. Mixed-order ellipticity and homotopy transfer give finite-dimensional minimal models, and cyclic transfer yields an effective potential $W_{\mathrm{eff}}$ for the ordinary formal physical solution locus over these degree-zero Artin algebras, after a formal coordinate change. We compute the relative cohomology of this compatibility comparison, which is detected on dg-Artin algebras and forgotten by the variational critical theory, and prove that the pointed relative fibre at the standard embedding is homotopy abelian.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Correlation-Driven Singlet--Triplet Inversion and Optical Spectra of N-Substituted Phenalenyls: Insights from PPP-FCI and TDDFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31738)
*Medha Rakshit,Alok Shukla*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用 Pariser-Parr-Pople（PPP）半经验模型结合全组态相互作用（FCI），系统考察十种氮取代 phenalenyl 体系中的单-三重态能隙（ΔE_ST），并借助 TDDFT 验证。结果表明准确描述负 ΔE_ST 需要包含相关驱动的多组态效应，常规绝热 TDDFT 无法重现该反转序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究报告了氮取代平面烃中最低单重态（S₁）与三重态（T₁）的反常反转，poly-aza phenalenyl 衍生物（如 cyclazine、heptazine）具有违反洪特规则（Hund's rule）的负单-三重态能隙。作者希望系统评估此类负能隙的可预测性，并厘清描述该现象所需的关键因素。

Method: 采用 Pariser-Parr-Pople（PPP）模型，即针对关联 π 电子的半经验波函数型哈密顿量，研究十种 N 取代 phenalenyl 结构。考察两套长程库仑相互作用方案，系统调节在位轨道能、Hubbard U 与跳跃积分等参数，并使用全组态相互作用（FCI）方法纳入电子相关效应。同时用不同交换-相关泛函进行第一性原理 TDDFT 计算作为验证。

Result: 通过优化 PPP 参数以最佳重现现有实验数据后，计算了基态与 T₁ 吸收光谱。常规绝热 TDDFT 无法重现反转的（S₁-T₁）排序，说明必须包含相关驱动的多组态效应；单重态 TDDFT 基态吸收光谱能重现 PPP-FCI 光谱的若干定性特征，但激发能系统性偏高。基态与 T₁ 光谱的详细分析揭示了显著组态混合。

Conclusion: 负单-三重态能隙（ΔE_ST）的可靠描述依赖于相关驱动的多组态效应，超出常规绝热 TDDFT 的适用范围。PPP-FCI 方法经参数标定后可估算此类体系的光学响应，并对 N 取代 phenalenyl 衍生物的光谱行为给出全面的定性刻画。

Abstract: Several researchers have reported an atypical inversion of the lowest singlet ($S_{1}$) and triplet ($T_{1}$) excited states in nitrogen-substituted planar hydrocarbons. Contrary to Hund's rule of maximum multiplicity, poly-aza derivatives of phenalenyl, such as cyclazine and heptazine, have long been known to possess a negative singlet-triplet gap, $ΔE_{ST}=E(S_{1})-E(T_{1})$. Using the well-known Pariser-Parr-Pople (PPP) model, a semi-empirical, wave-function-based Hamiltonian for correlated $π$-electrons, we investigate the singlet-triplet gap in ten N-substituted phenalenyl motifs. Two schemes of long-range Coulomb interactions are considered, and key parameters, including the on-site orbital energy, Hubbard U, and hopping integrals, are systematically tuned to benchmark the model for reliably estimating negative singlet-triplet gaps. Full configuration-interaction (FCI) method is employed to incorporate electron-correlation effects. Ground-state and $T_{1}$ absorption spectra are subsequently calculated using the optimized PPP parameters that best reproduce the available experimental data. For validation, first-principles TDDFT calculations using various exchange--correlation functionals are also performed. Conventional adiabatic TDDFT fails to reproduce the inverted ($S_{1}-T_{1}$) ordering, indicating that the inclusion of the correlation driven multiconfigurational effects are essential for describing it. We find that the singlet TDDFT ground-state absorption spectra reproduce several qualitative features of the PPP-FCI spectra, although the TDDFT excitation energies are systematically higher. Detailed analysis of the ground-state and $T_{1}$ absorption spectra reveals significant configuration mixing, providing a comprehensive characterization of the optical response of the N-substituted phenalenyl derivatives studied.

</details>


### [175] [ElectrolyteMD-Bench: How Well Do AI Agents Conduct Molecular Dynamics Studies across Electrolyte Solvation Regimes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31743)
*Shukai Wu,Shuo Niu,Zhaoming Xu,Yan Luo,Wentao Lin*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出 ElectrolyteMD-Bench，用于评估 AI 智能体（AI agents）能否自主完成科学可靠的锂电池电解质分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）研究。结果显示，24 次系统运行虽均产出有效轨迹，但八项研究中无一满足全部科学要求，揭示"工作流执行成功"与"研究完成可靠"之间存在显著鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体正推动分子动力学研究的自动化，有望加速锂电池电解质的发现与设计。然而，其能否自主完成科学上可靠的电解质 MD 研究尚缺乏充分评估，因此需要建立系统性评测基准。

Method: 构建 ElectrolyteMD-Bench，统一涵盖稀溶液、高浓度与局部高浓度电解质，在连续研究流程中串联局域配位（local coordination）、离子缔合（ionic association）、单粒子动力学与集体输运。智能体在冻结的科学模型下自主完成系统构建、平衡、产出采样、性质分析与解释，并自行决定纠错、继续采样或停止；每项研究从模拟有效性、分析有效性、证据完整性与结果校准四个维度进行审计。

Result: 在八种模型-框架配置下，全部 24 次系统运行均生成有效的产出轨迹，但八项研究无一满足全部科学要求。失败类型包括分析缺失、物理定义或数值实现错误、统计支撑不足，以及完成声明与所保留证据不一致。

Conclusion: 结果暴露了成功执行 MD 工作流与可靠完成科学研究之间的能力差距。ElectrolyteMD-Bench 提供了基础性测试，并界定了自主电解质模拟迈向"计算优先"材料探索（computation-first materials exploration）所必须跨越的关键能力阈值。

Abstract: AI agents are advancing the automation of molecular dynamics (MD) research, with the potential to accelerate discovery and design of lithium-battery electrolytes. However, whether they can autonomously complete scientifically reliable electrolyte MD studies remains insufficiently evaluated. We introduce ElectrolyteMD-Bench, which unifies dilute, high-concentration, and localized high-concentration electrolytes in a comparative study across distinct solvation regimes. The benchmark connects local coordination, ionic association, single-particle dynamics, and collective transport within one continuous research process. Agents autonomously perform system construction, equilibration, production sampling, property analysis, and interpretation under a frozen scientific model, while deciding whether to correct errors, continue sampling, or stop. We audit each study along four dimensions: simulation validity, analysis validity, evidence integrity, and outcome calibration. Across eight model--harness configurations, all 24 system runs produced valid production trajectories, yet none of the eight studies satisfied all scientific requirements. Failures included missing analyses, incorrect physical definitions or numerical implementations, insufficient statistical support, and completion claims inconsistent with retained evidence. These results expose a gap between successful MD workflow execution and reliable scientific study completion. ElectrolyteMD-Bench therefore provides a foundational test and defines a key capability threshold that autonomous electrolyte simulation must cross to advance toward computation-first materials exploration.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Observationanal and Thermodynamic Analysis of Power Law form of $f (Q)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31776)
*Agnidipto Bhattacharya,Shinjini Ghosh,Atreyee Biswas,Sujay Kr. Biswas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文结合统计分析与不可逆热力学，研究幂律形式 f(Q) 引力模型 $f(Q)=Q+mQ^n$，利用哈勃、超新星及 SH0ES 观测数据拟合参数，并与标准 ΛCDM 模型对比，探讨广义热力学第二定律与热力学平衡，最后研究宇宙学参数演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于借助当前观测数据与不可逆热力学，检验幂律形式 f(Q) 引力模型的可行性与物理合理性，并考察其相对标准 ΛCDM 模型的表现。

Method: 采用统计分析，使用 31 点哈勃数据（Hubble data）、潘泰翁型超新星（Pantheon type Supernova）数据及 1721 点 SH0ES 数据拟合模型特定参数；并基于标准 Eckart 非平衡热力学（non-equilibrium thermodynamics）理论，分别对一般形式与幂律形式的 f(Q) 引力进行热力学分析，利用统一热力学定律计算视界熵。

Result: 获得模型特定参数的最佳拟合值，并将 f(Q) 模型与标准 ΛCDM 模型进行对比研究；通过检验广义热力学第二定律（generalized second law of thermodynamics）与热力学平衡的有效性，确定了幂律模型参数的合适取值范围。

Conclusion: 在统计与热力学分析均兼容的 m、n 特定范围内，研究了宇宙学参数（cosmographic parameters）的演化特征，结论由统计与热力学两方面分析共同支持。

Abstract: In this study, the power law form of f(Q) gravity $(f(Q)=Q+mQ^n)$ has been studied in view of statistical analysis with current observed data and irreversible thermodynamics. The best fit values of model specific parameters are found with the use of of 31point Hubble data, Patntheon type Supernva and 1721 point SH0ES data and a comparative study has been performed between $f(Q)$ and standard $Λ$CDM model. The thermodynamic consequence using standard Eckart theory of non-equilibrium thermodynamics has been examined independently for general form of $f(Q)$ gravity followed by power law form of $f(Q)$ where the horizon entropy is calculated using unified law of thermodynamics. Suitable range of power law model specific parameters has been explored through investigating validity of generalized second law of thermodynamics and thermodynamic equilibrium. Finally evolution nature of cosmographic parameters are studied within the specific range of $m,n$ which is compatible with the results obtained from both statistical and thermodynamical analysis.

</details>


### [177] [Chaos bound for spinning particles in a hairy black hole with two photon spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31791)
*Chuanhong Gao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在有双光子球（double photon sphere）结构的毛发黑洞（hairy black hole）时空中，研究自旋测试粒子（spinning test particles）的不稳定圆轨道及其经典轨道混沌界（classical orbital chaos bound）。内支轨道始终满足混沌界，外支轨道可违反该界，且违反程度受粒子总角动量、背景形变参数与自旋影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多关注视界附近（near-horizon）对混沌界（chaos bound）的违反，本文则将考察对象扩展至具有双光子球结构的黑洞时空中的不稳定圆轨道，以探讨混沌界是否依赖轨道分支。

Method: 在容许双光子球结构的毛发黑洞背景中，分析自旋测试粒子的不稳定圆轨道（unstable circular orbits），将其分为内支与外支两支，并计算轨道李雅普诺夫指数（Lyapunov exponent）与黑洞表面引力（surface gravity）以检验混沌界。

Result: 在所考察参数范围内，内支（inner branch）始终满足混沌界，而外支（outer branch）可出现违反。粒子总角动量对局域轨道不稳定性影响显著，并能扩大外支的违反区域；背景几何的形变参数（deformation parameter）通过改变轨道李雅普诺夫指数与黑洞表面引力来调控混沌界的有效性；粒子自旋影响相对较弱，主要经自旋-曲率耦合（spin-curvature coupling）移位既有不稳定圆轨道及相应临界边界。

Conclusion: 混沌界的满足与否明确依赖于轨道分支：内支不违反，外支可能违反，且违反区域与粒子的总角动量和背景形变参数密切相关。该结果揭示了混沌界对轨道分支的依赖性，并区别于以往主要关注视界附近违反现象的研究。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the unstable circular orbits of spinning test particles and the associated classical orbital chaos bound in a hairy black hole spacetime admitting a double photon sphere structure. This background supports two distinct branches of unstable circular orbits, referred to as the inner and outer branches. We find that, within the parameter ranges considered, the inner branch always satisfies the chaos bound, whereas violations can occur on the outer branch. This behavior contrasts with many previous studies focusing primarily on near-horizon violations of the chaos bound and reveals a clear dependence of the bound on the orbital branch. Our analysis further shows that the total angular momentum of the particle has a pronounced effect on the local orbital instability and can enlarge the bound-violating region on the outer branch. Meanwhile, the deformation parameter of the background geometry regulates the validity of the bound by modifying both the orbital Lyapunov exponent and the black hole surface gravity. By contrast, the effect of the particle spin is comparatively weak and mainly shifts the existing unstable circular orbits and the corresponding critical boundaries through the spin-curvature coupling.

</details>


### [178] [Cosmological Dynamics and Observational Constraints of a Logarithmic Herglotz $f(R,T)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31800)
*Vishal M C,Sankarsan Tarai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究对数型 Herglotz 型 f(R,T)=R+βln(-T/T_0) 引力模型（logarithmic Herglotz-type f(R,T) gravity）的宇宙学动力学与观测可行性。通过数值求解并结合 CC、DESI DR2 BAO 与 Union3 数据，模型能一致描述背景膨胀历史，偏离标准 ΛCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验一种对数型 Herglotz 型 f(R,T) 引力（f(R,T) gravity）模型能否在背景层面重现晚期宇宙膨胀，并评估其相对标准 ΛCDM 模型的观测可行性。

Method: 在空间平坦 FLRW 背景与无压物质（pressureless matter）假设下，将修正场方程表述为含 Herglotz 场与归一化物质密度的耦合动力学系统（coupled dynamical system），并对有效 Herglotz 部分采用唯象闭合关系（phenomenological closure relation）进行数值求解。利用宇宙钟（Cosmic Chronometer）、DESI DR2 重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillation, BAO）与 Union3 超新星样本，在贝叶斯马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Bayesian Markov-chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）框架下进行单独与联合约束。

Result: 联合数据分析给出了统计上自洽的背景膨胀描述；重构的膨胀历史给出当前有效状态方程 w_eff,0≈-0.702，并呈现由晚期加速膨胀向更高红移处减速膨胀的预期转变。Om(z) 诊断与 statefinder 参数进一步支持该模型的可行性。

Conclusion: 对数型 Herglotz 模型为均匀且各向同性的晚期膨胀历史提供了自洽的唯象描述，同时允许偏离标准 ΛCDM 背景。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological dynamics and observational viability of a logarithmic Herglotz-type $f(R,T)$ gravity model described by $f(R,T)=R+β\ln(-T/T_0)$. Assuming a spatially flat Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker background and pressureless matter, the modified field equations are formulated as a coupled dynamical system involving the Herglotz field and the normalized matter density. A phenomenological closure relation is adopted for the effective Herglotz sector, allowing the background evolution to be determined numerically. The model is constrained using Cosmic Chronometer measurements, the DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation compilation, and the Union3 supernova sample, considering both individual and combined datasets within a Bayesian Markov-chain Monte Carlo framework. The combined analysis provides a statistically consistent description of the background expansion. The reconstructed expansion history yields a present-day effective equation of state $w_{\rm eff,0}\simeq-0.702$ and exhibits the expected transition from accelerated expansion at late times toward decelerated expansion at higher redshifts. We further investigate the $Om(z)$ diagnostic and statefinder parameter. These results indicate that the logarithmic Herglotz model provides a consistent phenomenological description of the homogeneous and isotropic late-time expansion history, while allowing departures from the standard $Λ$CDM background.

</details>


### [179] [Identifiability Limits of Gravitational Wave Phase Deviations: Multiclass Classification with a Multihead Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31993)
*Lavinia Heisenberg,Shayan Hemmatyar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究评估神经网络（neural network）对引力波（gravitational-wave）相位偏差的识别能力，通过分类头区分广义相对论（general relativity, GR）与六类参数化后爱因斯坦相位族（parametrized post-Einsteinian phase families），回归头预测耦合强度的对数 log₁₀|β|。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是检验引力波波形相位偏差能否被有效识别，并区分偏差类型与幅度，从而为广义相对论之外的引力理论检验提供工具性评估。研究还旨在理解网络识别能力的根本限制来源。

Method: 网络输入为响应函数（response function），量化波形失配对波形形变的敏感度；采用含分类与回归双头的神经网络。使用 Advanced LIGO 设计谱的平稳高斯噪声（262 个源）与 O3 实测 Hanford 谱（132 个源），并测试注入实测 Hanford 应变数据；同时构建基于预测波形残差的近似分类器作为对照，并测试十三种网络变体。

Result: 在两个高斯数据集上识别相位指数（exponent）的准确率分别为 0.427 与 0.329，明显高于噪声水平；网络与近似分类器时常混淆相同的相位族。在部分实测 Hanford 应变上，基于模拟或实测噪声训练的网络分类准确率接近模拟 Hanford 噪声上的结果。在 Advanced LIGO 设计数据集上，循环网络（recurrent network）对强偏差的平均分类准确率最高，但所有变体均低于近似分类器。

Conclusion: 网络准确率接近近似分类器，表明在已知内禀源参数（intrinsic source parameters）条件下，识别能力受限的主要原因在于相邻相位族之间的相似性，而非网络本身的能力不足。

Abstract: We study how well gravitational-wave phase deviations can be identified using a neural network with classification and regression heads. The classification head distinguishes general relativity (GR) from six parametrized post-Einsteinian phase families with exponents $b\in\{-7,-5,-3,-1,+1,+2\}$; the regression head predicts the logarithm of the coupling magnitude, $\log_{10}|β|$. The network input is a response function quantifying the sensitivity of waveform mismatch to waveform deformations. We use stationary Gaussian noise with the Advanced LIGO design spectrum for 262 sources and a measured Hanford spectrum from the third observing run (O3) for 132 sources, and also test injections into recorded Hanford strain. On the two Gaussian datasets, the network identifies the exponent of deviations clearly above the noise with accuracies of $0.427$ and $0.329$. To interpret these results, we compare the network with an approximate classifier built from predicted waveform residuals for the six phase families and same sources. The two classifiers often confuse the same families. Examining residuals left by different phase corrections, we link these errors to similarities between the underlying deviations. On selected recorded Hanford strain, networks trained on simulated or recorded noise achieve overall classification accuracies close to those on simulated Hanford noise. Of thirteen neural-network variants tested on the Advanced LIGO design dataset, the recurrent network has the highest mean classification accuracy for loud deviations, although all variants remain below the approximate classifier. The network nearly matches that classifier's accuracy, indicating that identification is limited by similarities between neighboring phase families rather than by the network, for known intrinsic source parameters.

</details>


### [180] [Relational Frame Equivalence and Exact Cosmology in Brans--Dicke Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32047)
*Yaser Tavakoli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将正则时钟扇区框架应用于Brans–Dicke理论的平坦FLRW宇宙学，得到可积的关系型动力学及闭式宇宙学观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为框架适配的关系型动力学（frame-adapted relational dynamics）提供显式的宇宙学实现，并给出闭式形式的宇宙学观测量，以检验标量—张量宇宙学中的参照系等价性。

Method: 从既有完整ADM理论出发限制到均匀扇区，得到正则时钟动量 P_Φ=p_φ−ap_a/2φ，使Jordan与Einstein约化系统共享同一正则相空间；选取Einstein框架下为常数的二次Jordan框架势，求解精确可积的关系型FLRW系统，并以数值积分对照验证。

Result: 轨迹可由单一参数 u 表示，给出标度因子、宇宙时哈勃率、哈勃慢滚参数、e折数与标量状态方程参数的闭式表达式；存在从 u=arctanh(1/√3) 开始的暴胀阶段并趋于渐近de Sitter；数值结果重现精确轨迹。

Conclusion: 该分析为标量—张量宇宙学中的关系型参照系等价性提供了精确可解实验室，并给出可用于推广至更一般势与标量—张量模型的显式宇宙学观测量。

Abstract: We apply the canonical clock-sector framework developed in Ref.~\cite{Tavakoli:2026coc} to spatially flat FLRW cosmology in Brans--Dicke theory. The aim is to obtain an explicit cosmological realization of the frame-adapted relational dynamics and to determine the corresponding cosmological observables in closed form. Starting from the previously developed full ADM theory and restricting it to the homogeneous sector, we obtain the canonical clock momentum as $P_Φ=p_φ-ap_a/2φ$. This identification allows the Jordan and Einstein-frame reduced systems to be represented on a common canonical phase space. We then consider a quadratic Jordan-frame potential for which the Einstein-frame potential is constant. The resulting relational FLRW system is exactly integrable. The complete trajectory can be expressed in terms of a single parameter $u$, yielding closed-form expressions for the scale factor, the cosmic-time Hubble rate, the Hubble slow-roll parameter, the number of e-folds, and the scalar equation-of-state parameter. In particular, the exact solution provides an explicit inflationary regime that begins at $u=\operatorname{arctanh}(1/\sqrt{3})$ and transitions toward an asymptotic de Sitter regime. We finally compare the exact solution with direct numerical integration in the adapted Jordan and Einstein descriptions, and with the naively reduced Jordan system based on the unshifted scalar momentum. The numerical results reproduce the exact trajectory and illustrate how the different reduced canonical representations are related by the clock-dependent transformation implied by the canonical clock-sector construction. The analysis provides an exactly solvable laboratory for relational frame equivalence in scalar--tensor cosmology and gives explicit cosmological observables that can be used in extensions to more general potentials and scalar--tensor models.

</details>


### [181] [Inhomogeneous dust collapse in five-dimensional AdS-Chern-Simons gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32200)
*Luis Avilés,José Díaz,Cristian Quinzacara,Patricio Salgado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在五维 AdS Chern–Simons 引力（Chern–Simons gravity）中研究球对称非均匀尘埃云（dust cloud）的引力坍缩，在无挠（torsion-free）情形下导出约化场方程与精确坍缩内部解，并讨论奇点、能量条件、表观视界（apparent horizon）及与黑洞外解的匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究五维 AdS Chern–Simons 引力背景下非均匀尘埃引力坍缩的解析行为，以厘清初始速度与密度剖面对壳层聚焦（shell focusing）与俘获（trapping）的控制作用，并检验高维修正引力中坍缩几何与奇点结构。

Method: 采用共动 Lemaître–Tolman–Bondi 坐标（comoving Lemaître–Tolman–Bondi coordinates），限制于无挠扇区，推导约化场方程并求得精确坍缩内部解；进一步给出中心正则性与避免壳层穿越（shell crossing）的条件，分析 Kretschmann 标量（Kretschmann scalar）、能量条件及零膨胀（null expansions），并将坍缩解与静态 AdS Chern–Simons 外部的黑洞分支匹配。

Result: 面积半径（areal radius）随时间周期演化，但物理坍缩分支仅延伸至壳层聚焦或更早的正则性丧失；初始密度决定一个径向函数，其平方正比于守恒准局域质量（quasilocal mass）。壳层聚焦对应真实曲率奇点，弱、强能量条件在正则坍缩阶段保持。仅当准局域质量超过临界阈值时形成有限半径表观视界；高于该阈值且初始未俘获的壳层在壳层聚焦前被俘获，而低于总质量阈值时正则内部无有限半径的未来俘获对称球。

Conclusion: 五维 AdS Chern–Simons 引力中的非均匀尘埃坍缩可给出精确内部解并平滑匹配静态黑洞外部；初始速度与密度剖面分别控制壳层聚焦与俘获，表观视界的出现由准局域质量阈值决定。均匀极限与五维 FLRW（FLRW）内部解一致。

Abstract: We study the gravitational collapse of a spherically symmetric, inhomogeneous dust cloud in five-dimensional AdS Chern--Simons gravity. Working in the torsion-free sector and using comoving Lemaître--Tolman--Bondi coordinates, we derive the reduced field equations and obtain an exact collapsing interior solution. The areal radius evolves periodically in time, but the physical collapsing branch extends only up to shell focusing or an earlier loss of regularity, while the initial density determines a radial function whose square is proportional to the conserved quasilocal mass. This separates the roles of the initial velocity and density profiles in controlling shell focusing and trapping.
  We derive conditions for central regularity and for avoiding shell crossing, analyze the Kretschmann scalar, and show that shell focusing corresponds to a genuine curvature singularity, the weak and strong energy conditions are preserved throughout the regular collapsing phase. The homogeneous limit reduces consistently to a five-dimensional FLRW interior.
  The collapsing cloud is matched smoothly to the black-hole branch of the static AdS Chern--Simons exterior. The null expansions show that a finite-radius apparent horizon forms only when the quasilocal mass exceeds a critical threshold. Initially untrapped shells above this threshold become trapped before shell focusing, whereas below the total-mass threshold no finite-radius future-trapped symmetry sphere forms in the regular interior.

</details>


### [182] [Stability Analysis of Canonical Acoustic Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32277)
*HengZhong Fang,LiQiang Miao,ChangSen Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究了正则声学黑洞（canonical acoustic black hole）在非最小耦合标量场、电磁场与狄拉克场扰动下的稳定性，采用三阶WKB近似方法计算准正规模（quasi-normal modes, QNMs），结果表明该类黑洞在上述三种扰动下均为稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在考察正则声学黑洞在多种场扰动下的稳定性，通过非最小耦合标量场、电磁场和狄拉克场的扰动分析来检验其动力学行为。

Method: 采用三阶WKB近似方法（third-order WKB approximation method）计算准正规模（QNMs），并分别针对非最小耦合标量场、电磁场和狄拉克场三种扰动情形进行分析。

Result: 三种扰动下有效势（effective potential）均为正值，QNMs虚部均为负值，表明黑洞稳定。标量场扰动中，有效势与QNMs依赖角量子数l、泛音数n及耦合参数ζ，ζ越大扰动衰减越快；电磁场扰动中依赖l与n；狄拉克场扰动中依赖n与总角量子数|k|。

Conclusion: 正则声学黑洞在非最小耦合标量场、电磁场和狄拉克场扰动下均表现为稳定，且不同扰动类型下QNMs对量子数与耦合参数的依赖关系各异。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the stability of canonical acoustic black hole under the perturbation of non-minimally coupling scalar field, electromagnetic field and Dirac field. The quasi-normal modes(QNMs) are computed using the three-order WKB approximation method. The results show that, for all these three kinds of perturbation, the values of the effective potential are positive, while the imaginary parts of QNMs are negative, the canonical acoustic black hole is a table. In details respectively, for scalar field perturbation, the effective potential and the QNMs depend on the angular quantum number $l$, the overtone number $n$, and the coupling parameter ζ, the perturbation decays more rapidly for bigger ζ. For electromagnetic field perturbation, the effective potential and QNMs depend on $l$ and $n$. For Dirac field perturbation, the effective potential and QNMs depend on $n$ and the total angular quantum number | k |.

</details>


### [183] [Memory effect from neutron star merger counterparts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32412)
*T. Brabant,M. Pillas,H. Inchauspé,Z. Lin,F. Foucart,M. Bulla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文量化了双中子星并合（binary neutron star mergers, BNSs）过程中产生的非线性记忆（nonlinear memory）与线性记忆（linear memory）两种引力波记忆（gravitational-wave memory）贡献，覆盖引力波、伽马射线暴（GRBs）、余辉、中微子、动力学与盘风抛射物及千新星辐射，并评估其在爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）与LISA下的可探测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波记忆效应（gravitational-wave memory effect）是广义相对论的预言，表现为时空几何的永久改变，但至今尚未被观测到。下一代探测器（如ET与LISA）预期具备首次探测所需灵敏度，因此需要定量评估各类记忆成分的可探测性。多信使事件为检验该效应提供了现实场景。

Method: 工作量化了双中子星并合中非线性记忆（源于引力波自相互作用）与线性记忆（源于物质与辐射的各向异性发射）两类贡献，涵盖引力波、伽马射线暴、余辉、中微子、动力学与盘风抛射物及千新星辐射。基于此评估ET与LISA的可探测性，并应用于两个场景：多信使事件GW170817–GRB 170817A–AT2017gfo，以及合成伽马射线暴族群。

Result: 对GW170817–GRB 170817A–AT2017gfo，其组合记忆信号在ET中的信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio, SNR）为10.7，且以非线性记忆为主导。在合成伽马射线暴族群的评估中，仅极高能量且参数协同有利的构型才可能在ET中产生可探测信号。

Conclusion: 结果表明，下一代探测器将能够探测非线性记忆，而从双中子星并合对应体中观测线性记忆仍将十分困难，除非处于最有利的情形。

Abstract: The gravitational-wave (GW) memory effect is a prediction of general relativity, characterized by a permanent change in spacetime geometry. Although it has not yet been observed, next-generation detectors such as the Einstein Telescope (ET) and the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) are expected to provide the sensitivity required for its first detection. GW memory comprises two distinct contributions: the nonlinear memory, generated by the self-interaction of GWs, and the linear memory, originating from the anisotropic emission of matter and radiation associated with astrophysical transients. In this work, we quantify both nonlinear and linear memory contributions produced during binary neutron star mergers (BNSs), including those associated with GWs, gamma-ray bursts (GRBs), afterglows, neutrinos, dynamical and disk-wind ejecta, and kilonova emission. We evaluate the detectability of the resulting memory signals with ET and LISA through two applications. First, we apply our analysis to the multi-messenger event GW170817 - GRB 170817A - AT2017gfo. We find that the combined memory signal from this event would have been detectable by ET with a signal-to-noise ratio of 10.7 dominated by the nonlinear memory. We also assess the detectability of the GRB memory across a synthetic population of GRBs, and find that only extreme-energy and jointly favorable configurations could produce a detectable signal in ET. Together, these results demonstrate that while next-generation detectors will probe nonlinear memory, observing linear memory from BNS counterparts will remain elusive except in the most favorable scenarios.

</details>


### [184] [Energy extraction from Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson black holes: polar region collapse and the capture of negative energy protons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32494)
*Ke Wang,Xiao-Xiong Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞（渐近均匀磁场中的旋转黑洞精确解）中带电粒子的加速度与能量抽取，发现弱磁场下黑洞增质、强磁场下可抽取能量，且无需Wald解所需的各向异性密度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson解描述的磁力线不再是柱状的，且\(\vec{E}\cdot\vec{B}=0\)界面随半径收缩并最终消失；这些差异可能改变带电粒子吸积与黑洞能量收支，因而值得重新考察粒子加速与能量抽取过程。

Method: 逐点计算带电粒子加速度，区分极区与赤道区中质子与电子的运动方向；以规范不变通量判据（gauge-invariant flux criterion）确定俘获域，并在\((r,\theta)\)平面上积分求解黑洞质量变化。

Result: 极区中质子被推向外、电子被推入内，赤道区相反，且极区随磁场增强而收缩；被俘获质子携带负能量与负角动量，被俘获电子携带正能量与正角动量。积分结果显示，弱磁场下黑洞增质，强磁场下发生能量抽取。

Conclusion: 在Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞中，能量抽取可在通常的球对称密度下实现，无需像Kerr黑洞的Wald解那样引入各向异性密度分布。

Abstract: The Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson black hole is an exact Einstein-Maxwell solution describing a rotating black hole in an asymptotically uniform magnetic field. It differs from the Kerr black hole in the Wald solution: the magnetic field lines are no longer cylindrical, with the constant-\(A_φ\) lines saturating at large distances, and the interface \(\vec{E}\cdot\vec{B}=0\) is no longer a cone but contracts with radius and eventually disappears. Determining the acceleration of charged particles pointwise, we find that protons are driven outward and electrons inward in the polar region, and vice versa in the equatorial region; the polar region shrinks as the magnetic field grows. Captured protons carry negative energy and angular momentum, whereas captured electrons carry positive energy and angular momentum. Fixing the capture domain by the gauge-invariant flux criterion and integrating over the \((r,θ)\) plane, we find that in weak magnetic fields the black hole gains mass, while in strong magnetic fields energy extraction occurs. Energy extraction can be achieved under the usual spherically symmetric density, without introducing the anisotropic density distribution required in the Wald solution for a Kerr black hole.

</details>


### [185] [Exact Solutions for Chiral Gravitational Waves from Spin-2 Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32571)
*Mohammad Ali Gorji,Yuhang Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究在暴胀背景下精确求解了引力子与旁观者自旋-2场经宇称破坏混合的耦合张量系统，发现一个螺旋度（helicity）被指数增强，可产生强手性原初引力波谱，其圆偏振度上限为 tanh(πθ)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旁观者自旋-2场可与度规张量扰动发生线性混合，此前分析多依赖微扰展开，难以刻画强混合情形。本文旨在不展开混合项、非微扰地处理混合强度，以探究宇称破坏对原初引力波谱的影响。

Method: 在暴胀背景下求解耦合张量系统，引入由θ参数化的宇称破坏效应（parity violating effect）；对任意质量精确求解，非微扰处理混合强度。两个螺旋度的模函数（mode functions）通过算符值高斯超几何函数（Gauss hypergeometric function）${}_2F_1$作用于Whittaker模构造，晚期功率谱以广义超几何函数（generalised hypergeometric function）${}_3F_2$闭式表达。

Result: 一个螺旋度呈指数增强，说明与旁观者自旋-2场的强混合可生成大且高度手性的原初引力波谱。圆偏振度（degree of circular polarisation）由 tanh(πθ) 界定，且与自旋-2质量和混合强度无关。同一解还给出旁观者场的晚期功率谱及其与引力子的交叉谱（cross spectrum）。弱混合极限下，精确结果回到微扰Schwinger-Keldysh结果。

Conclusion: 所获精确解为研究由额外张量模式（tensor modes）产生的原初引力波提供了非微扰框架。

Abstract: A spectator spin-2 field can mix linearly with the metric tensor perturbations. We study the coupled tensor system in an inflationary background, including a parity violating effect parametrised by $θ$. Without expanding in the linear mixing between the two fields, we solve the system exactly for arbitrary mass while treating the mixing strength nonperturbatively. The mode functions of both helicities are constructed by acting with an operator-valued Gauss hypergeometric function ${}_2F_1$ on Whittaker modes, and the late-time power spectrum of each graviton helicity is expressed in closed form in terms of the generalised hypergeometric function ${}_3F_2$. We find that one helicity is exponentially enhanced, showing that strong mixing with a spectator spin-2 field can generate a large and highly chiral primordial gravitational wave spectrum. The degree of circular polarisation is bounded by $\tanh(πθ)$, independently of the spin-2 mass and the mixing strength. The same solution also gives the late-time power spectrum of the spectator field and its cross spectrum with the graviton. In the weak mixing limit, our exact result reproduces the perturbative Schwinger-Keldysh result. These solutions provide a nonperturbative framework for studying primordial gravitational waves sourced by additional tensor modes.

</details>


### [186] [Thermal equilibrium in general relativity and the special nature of the cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32608)
*M. L. Rodrigues,J. B. Formiga*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文讨论广义相对论（general relativity）中的热平衡问题，强调宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）所关联流体的独特地位，并借助广义相对论的远平行等价理论（teleparallel equivalent of general relativity）计算若干黑洞的准局域能量（quasi-local energy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清广义相对论中热平衡的本质，特别是宇宙学常数相关流体在其中的特殊角色，并探讨空间体积是否具有内禀能量这一与量子引力（quantum gravity）相关的问题。

Method: 采用广义相对论的远平行等价理论（teleparallel equivalent of general relativity）分析能量问题；将宇宙学常数流体的能量表述为 $E_\Lambda=\rho_\Lambda V$（$V$ 为空间体积）；并计算 Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter 黑洞与一类正则黑洞（regular black hole）的准局域能量（quasi-local energy），同时结合引力能量问题讨论零点能（zero-point energy）困难。

Result: 分析表明，宇宙学常数流体是唯一始终处于热平衡的流体，其能量可视为由 $E_\Lambda=\rho_\Lambda V$ 给出的普适量（universal quantity）；若宇宙学常数为普适常数，则任意空间体积 $V$ 具有内禀能量的结论自然成立；在远平行理论下计算得到的黑洞准局域能量与预期一致。

Conclusion: 宇宙学常数流体在广义相对论热平衡中具有独特且普适的地位，其能量表达式支持空间体积具有内禀能量的观点，该结论可望应用于假定存在最小长度尺度（minimal length scale）的量子引力理论；远平行理论给出的黑洞准局域能量结果与理论预期相符。

Abstract: The problem of thermal equilibrium in general relativity is discussed and the unique role played by the fluid associated with the cosmological constant is emphasized. In addition to being the only fluid that is always in thermal equilibrium, it is shown here that its energy can also be seen as a universal quantity given by $E_Λ=ρ_ΛV$, where $V$ is a spatial volume. Furthermore, if the cosmological constant turns out to be a universal constant, then the conclusion that any spatial volume $V$ has an intrinsic energy follows naturally from the approach considered here. A possible application to quantum theories of gravity that postulate the existence of a minimal length scale is discussed. In studying the energies, the so-called teleparallel equivalent of general relativity is used, and the difficulty with the zero-point energy is discussed in light of the gravitational energy problem. The quasi-local energies of the Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter black hole and of a regular black hole are calculated. The results obtained here with the teleparallel theory are consistent with our expectations.

</details>


### [187] [One nat per cell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32879)
*Ira Wolfson*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该摘要提出一种不依赖场方程、微观理论或预设希尔伯特空间的黑洞熵推导：仅以“熵为不可约不确定性”与“视界为完全扰乱器”两项输入，导出每个普朗克格（Planck cell）恰含 1 nat，从而 $S=N=A/\ell_P^2$，再乘运动学因子 $1/4$ 得 $S_{BH}=A/4\ell_P^2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在不指明黑洞由何种物质构成、不引入场方程或微观理论的前提下，解释黑洞视界每个普朗克格承载恰好 1 nat 信息的结论。其出发点是两个概念性前设：熵代表不可约不确定性（irreducible uncertainty），而非观测者的无知；视界则是完全扰乱器（complete scrambler）。

Method: 论证采用相空间（phase space）视角：因视界为完全扰乱器，到厄伦费斯特时间（Ehrenfest time）其维格纳函数（Wigner function）已折叠至普朗克下限之下，任何格都无法被证明为空。单格因而包含未解析的丝状交叉，被交叉 $n$ 次的格对应 $n!$ 种被扰乱抹除的排序，故基数 $n$ 的权重为 $1/n!$，其计数 $\Omega_1=\sum_n 1/n!=e$。$N=A/\ell_P^2$ 个独立格给出 $\Omega=e^N$。

Result: 由 $\Omega=e^N$ 得熵 $S=N$，即每格 1 nat；再乘以运动学因子（kinematic factor）$1/4$——即零测地相空间（null phase space）中因果可及的比例，该因子另行建立——即恢复贝肯斯坦-霍金熵（Bekenstein–Hawking entropy）$S_{BH}=A/4\ell_P^2$。

Conclusion: 结论为黑洞视界的熵密度可由纯信息论式的格计数直接给出，形式上无需场方程、微观理论或预设的希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space），从而将 $S_{BH}=A/4\ell_P^2$ 归结为两项物理输入与一个独立的运动学因子。

Abstract: A black hole horizon carries exactly one nat of information per Planck cell, without naming what the black hole is made of. Two inputs suffice: entropy is irreducible uncertainty (the configurations no observation can resolve at the Planck floor, an objective bound, not ignorance), and the horizon is a complete scrambler, so by the Ehrenfest time its Wigner function has folded below the floor and no cell can be certified empty. A single cell then holds unresolved filament crossings; a cell crossed $n$ times admits $n!$ orderings that scrambling erases, so cardinality $n$ carries weight $1/n!$ and its count is $Ω_1=\sum_n 1/n!=e$, one nat. The $N=A/\ell_P^2$ independent cells give $Ω=e^N$, hence $S=N$. Multiplied by the kinematic factor $\tfrac{1}{4}$ (the causally accessible fraction of the null phase space, established separately), this returns $S_{BH}=A/4\ell_P^2$. The derivation invokes no field equations, no microscopic theory, and no posited Hilbert space.

</details>


### [188] [Gravitational-Wave Modulation of Atomic Spontaneous Emission: Electrodynamical Theory, Quadrupolar Redistribution, and Rydberg Cavity Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32905)
*Sohrab Rahvar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在量子电动力学（quantum electrodynamics, QED）框架下研究弱经典引力波（gravitational wave, GW）对自发辐射的影响，指出引力波对真空电磁模进行动态相位调制，形成频率间隔为GW频率的Floquet边带结构，并提出基于腔QED（cavity QED）的探测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波为研究量子物质与时空动力学之间的相互作用提供了独特探针，但缺乏将自发辐射置于引力波背景下的QED理论框架及可行的实验观测方案。

Method: 在线性化引力背景下量子化电磁场，构建弱经典引力波存在时的自发辐射QED框架；进一步建立腔QED主方程（master equation）描述，并提出采用里德堡系综（Rydberg ensembles）或高电荷态离子（highly charged ions）的差分跨腔探测协议。

Result: 经过的引力波将真空电磁模动态相位调制，使光子态缀饰为间隔等于GW频率的Floquet边带阶梯；在一阶引力波应变下总自发衰减率不变，但发射图案出现随GW相位调制的自旋-2四极角向重分布；有限相互作用时间下，载波与Floquet边带干涉产生瞬态发射谱中相位依赖的光谱不对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了腔QED作为探索动态时空中的量子电动力学以及高频引力波探测的有前景平台。

Abstract: Studying Gravitational Waves (GWs) provide a unique probe of the interplay between quantum matter and the dynamics of spacetime. We develop a QED framework for spontaneous emission in the presence of a weak classical GW by quantizing the electromagnetic field in a linearized gravitational background. We show that a passing GW dynamically phase-modulates vacuum electromagnetic modes, dressing photon states into a Floquet ladder of sidebands separated by the GW frequency. While the total spontaneous decay rate remains unchanged to first order in the GW strain, the emission pattern develops a characteristic spin-2 quadrupolar angular redistribution modulated with the GW phase. For finite interaction times, interference between the carrier and Floquet sidebands generates phase-dependent spectral asymmetries in the transient emission spectrum. To explore experimental observability, we formulate a cavity-QED master equation description and propose a differential cross-cavity detection protocol employing Rydberg ensembles or highly charged ions. These results establish cavity QED as a promising platform for exploring quantum electrodynamics in dynamical spacetime and for high-frequency gravitational-wave detection.

</details>


### [189] [Hybrid-star asteroseismology across sharp phase interfaces: Slow and rapid conversion in the $f$- and $p_1$-mode spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33002)
*Zoey Zhiyuan Dong,Hao-Jui Kuan,Nicolas Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在完整广义相对论（general relativity）框架下计算冷、无转动混合星（hybrid star）的四极 $f$ 模与 $p_1$ 模复频谱，比较尖锐强子—夸克界面处慢速与快速相变两种极限。结果显示 $p_1$ 模实部对界面响应敏感，而 $f$ 模仅受弱影响，前者可探测局部界面响应，后者反映混合星序对强子参照的整体偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星内部的一阶相变（first-order phase transition）可能在极向准正规模（polar quasi-normal-mode）谱中留下特征信号。研究旨在厘清尖锐强子—夸克界面的相变动力学如何影响星体振荡模式，从而借助引力波相关观测约束星体内部状态方程（equation of state）。

Method: 将强子 SLy4 相与常声速（constant-sound-speed）夸克相匹配构建状态方程，并采用尖锐强子—夸克界面。在完整广义相对论下计算冷、无转动混合星的四极 $f$ 模与 $p_1$ 模复频谱，对比慢速相变（流体元保持相态、界面随流体运动）与快速相变（物质跨越边界时改变相态、界面遵循相平衡而非流体）两种物理极限，并对二者作代数插值。

Result: 在同一平衡背景上，两种极限在 $p_1$ 模频率实部产生可观分离，且当本征函数（eigenfunction）的局部切向位移占比在界面处快速变化时该分离最大。在所考察的状态方程参数空间中，快速相变相对慢速相变普遍降低 $p_1$ 模频率，紧凑度平均（compactness-averaged）分离在断开的孪生星（twin-star）支上可达 $\sim3.5\,{\rm kHz}$；最大分离出现在较低相变压力与较大密度间断处。相比之下，单颗星体模型的 $f$ 模仅受界面条件弱影响。在常声速参数空间中，超出强子 SLy4 参照残差的偏离主要出现在低相变压力、大密度间断或两者兼有时，且实部与虚部拟合变量的边界不同。

Conclusion: $p_1$ 模可探测界面的局部响应，而所追踪的 $f$ 模反映混合星序相对强子标定的整体偏离。二者结合可为区分离散界面相变动力学与星体整体结构提供互补的诊断手段。

Abstract: First-order phase transitions in neutron-star interiors can leave signatures in the polar quasi-normal-mode spectrum. We compute the complex quadrupolar $f$- and $p_1$-mode spectra in full general relativity for cold, nonrotating hybrid stars with a sharp hadron--quark interface. We model the equation of state by matching a hadronic SLy4 phase to a constant-sound-speed quark-phase. We compare slow phase conversion, in which fluid elements retain their phase and the interface moves with the fluid, with rapid phase conversion, in which matter changes phase as it crosses the boundary and the interface follows phase equilibrium rather than the fluid, together with an algebraic interpolation between these limits. On identical equilibrium backgrounds, the two physical limits produce a sizable separation in the real part of the $p_1$-mode frequency. This $p_1$ frequency separation is largest when the local tangential-displacement fraction of the eigenfunction changes rapidly across the interface. Across the surveyed EOS parameter space, rapid conversion generally lowers the $p_1$-mode frequency relative to slow conversion, with compactness-averaged separations reaching $\sim3.5\,{\rm kHz}$ on disconnected twin-star branches. The largest separations occur at relatively low transition pressures and large density discontinuities. By contrast, the $f$-mode is only weakly affected by the interface condition for individual stellar models. Across the sampled constant-sound-speed parameter space, departures exceeding the hadronic SLy4 reference residual occur mainly at low transition pressure, large density discontinuity, or both, with different boundaries for the real- and imaginary-part fitting variables. Thus, the $p_1$-mode probes the local interface response, whereas the tracked $f$-mode reflects the global departure of the hybrid sequence from the hadronic calibration.

</details>


### [190] [Two-horizon sector thermodynamics and NUT bi-hair in Kerr-Newman-NUT spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33032)
*Di Wu,Robert B. Mann,Shuang-Qing Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本工作针对电中性 Taub-NUT 与带电 Kerr-Newman-NUT 黑洞族，构建“双视界分区诊断（two-horizon sector diagnostic）”，将内外视界熵与逆温度组织为和（Σ）与差（Δ）两个扇区。结果表明 Σ 扇区仅由类电荷变量 Q、N 闭合，Δ 扇区还需类转动变量 J、J_N=MN，从而支持 NUT “双毛（bi-hair）”组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭热力学第一定律（first law）的一致性无法唯一确定 NUT 带电时空的黑洞状态空间，因为多种不等价的宏观表述都能给出自洽热力学。作者因而需要一个更强的判别工具，并检验带电转动情形是否较中性 Taub-NUT 结果更具约束力。

Method: 通过将内、外视界熵与逆温度重组为和（Σ）与差（Δ）扇区，发展双视界扇区诊断；在物理锚定的最小齐次状态空间（minimal homogeneous state spaces）内，考察由 M、Q、J、N 以及 NUT 次级毛 J_N=MN 共同参与的两视界热力学，并检验去除 J_N 或 N 的自然约化是否仍能重现由扇区温度固定的第一定律系数与 Bekenstein-Smarr 关系。

Result: 对定义为并检验的最小齐次状态空间，出现清晰的扇区分离：Σ 扇区以类电荷变量 Q、N 闭合，Δ 扇区则额外需要类转动变量 J 与 J_N。消除 J_N 或 N 的自然约化无法重现扇区温度所固定的第一定律系数，其 Bekenstein-Smarr 关系也不能按指定标度权重闭合。

Conclusion: 在该类中，电荷与常规转动不能吸收 NUT 的次级响应，带电转动族支持 NUT 双毛组织：N 承担类电荷的扇区角色，而 J_N=MN 是类转动的热力学次级毛。后者在齐次状态方程中离线（off shell）定义，而非新的度规参数或渐近守恒荷。

Abstract: First law consistency alone does not determine the black hole state space of NUT-charged spacetimes, because several inequivalent macroscopic formulations can yield consistent thermodynamics. We develop a two-horizon sector diagnostic for the electrically charged Kerr-Newman-NUT family by organizing the inner- and outer-horizon entropies and inverse temperatures into sum ($Σ$) and difference ($Δ$) sectors. The charged rotating geometry gives a more stringent test of the uncharged Taub-NUT sector result, since the mass $M$, ordinary electric charge $Q$, ordinary angular momentum $J$, NUT charge $N$, and the NUT secondary hair $J_N=MN$ all enter the same two-horizon thermodynamics. For the physically anchored minimal homogeneous state spaces defined and tested here, we find a clear sector separation: the $Σ$ sector closes with the charge-like variables $Q$ and $N$, whereas the $Δ$ sector additionally requires the rotation-like variables $J$ and $J_N$. Natural reductions that eliminate either $J_N$ or $N$ fail to reproduce the first law coefficient fixed by the sector temperature, and their associated Bekenstein-Smarr relations do not close with the assigned scaling weights. Within this class, electric charge and ordinary rotation therefore do not absorb the NUT secondary response, and the charged rotating family supports the NUT bi-hair organization: $N$ has a charge-like sector role, while $J_N=MN$ is a rotation-like thermodynamic secondary hair. The latter is defined off shell in the homogeneous equation of state, not as a new metric parameter or asymptotic conserved charge.

</details>


### [191] [A construction of charged naked singularities for the Einstein--Maxwell-charged scalar field equations in 3+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33168)
*Weihao Zheng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在 (3+1) 维球对称 Einstein–Maxwell-带电标量场方程中构造了一类全局裸奇点（global naked singularity）解，其源于渐近平坦的单端特征初值，并发展出构建裸奇点外部区域的通用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探讨不事件视界包裹的曲率奇点（curvature singularity）是否存在，即裸奇点的形成问题，并将其推广至含非零 Maxwell 场的真正带电情形。

Method: 采用球对称 Einstein–Maxwell-带电标量场方程组，从 C^{1,α}（0<α≪1）渐近平坦单端特征初值（characteristic initial data）出发进行构造，并建立构建裸奇点外部区域（奇点发出的过去光锥的因果未来区域）的通用框架。

Result: 所得时空为真正带电，Maxwell 场非零且直至奇点满足定量正下界，并具有显式渐近剖面；框架可容纳广泛类型的奇异特征数据，尤其允许 Maxwell 场在奇点附近表现出奇异行为。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了含电荷的全局裸奇点解的存在性，并提供了一套可推广的裸奇点外部区域构造框架，拓展了对奇异特征数据与 Maxwell 场奇异行为的处理范围。

Abstract: We construct a class of global naked singularity solutions for the $(3+1)$-dimensional spherically symmetric Einstein--Maxwell-charged scalar field equations. The solutions arise from $C^{1,α}$ asymptotically flat one-ended characteristic initial data with $0<α\ll1$ and develop a curvature singularity that is not enclosed by an event horizon. The resulting spacetimes are genuinely charged, with a nonzero Maxwell field that satisfies a quantitative positive lower bound up to the singularity and admits an explicit asymptotic profile. As part of the construction, we develop a general framework for constructing naked singularity exteriors, namely, the causal future region of the past light cone emanating from the singularity. The framework accommodates a broad range of singular characteristic data and, in particular, allows for singular behavior of the Maxwell field near the singularity.

</details>


### [192] [Quasinormal modes of Hayward black holes from Schwarzschild to extremality: a comparative spectral analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33267)
*Davide Batic,Denys Dutykh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文以多精度切比雪夫配点法（multiprecision Chebyshev collocation）计算 Hayward 黑洞的标量与电磁准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs），并考察特定 Regge–Wheeler 型有效模型的轴向谱，覆盖从 Schwarzschild 极限到精确极端（extremality）区间；同时对纯虚本征值序列作谨慎的物理分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于系统刻画 Hayward 黑洞在自旋 0、1 及自旋 2 情形下的准正规模谱，并厘清自旋 2 方程因模型选择差异所带来的频率分歧。摘要亦强调对纯虚本征值是否构成真实 QNM 需加以甄别。

Method: 采用多精度切比雪夫配点法进行离散化，复现现有低阶振荡基准并给出高阶泛音（overtone）候选；对自旋 2 方程采用指定模型，并与近期 Hayward 研究中使用的有效源势（effective-source potential）作对比。

Result: 方法复现了多数可得的低阶振荡基准并提供更多泛音候选；离散化还产生纯虚本征值序列，其近 Mω 间距约为四分之一，与喉半径（throat radius）的极端乘积接近 1/3。自旋 2 方程具模型依赖性，与近期研究所用有效源势不同，并量化了代表性频率差异。

Conclusion: 纯虚轴条目被保留为未分类的谱候选（unclassified spectral candidates），而非已确立的 QNM，因高度可复现的虚轴阶梯未必是连续格林函数（continued Green function）的极点。文中量化了有限表间隙色散（finite-table gap dispersion）及其对间隙数目的敏感性，并指出相关间距与乘积性质仅属所列表候选，不足以确立普适 QNM 间距或受喉半径控制的阻尼。

Abstract: We study scalar and electromagnetic quasinormal modes of the Hayward black hole and the axial spectrum of a specified Regge--Wheeler-type effective model, from the Schwarzschild limit to exact extremality. Multiprecision Chebyshev collocation reproduces most available low-lying oscillatory benchmarks and supplies further overtone candidates. The spin-2 equation is model dependent. It differs from the effective-source potential used in recent Hayward studies, and we quantify representative frequency differences. The discretisation also produces purely imaginary eigenvalue sequences, whose physical classification requires special care because highly reproducible imaginary-axis ladders need not be poles of the continued Green function. Accordingly, the imaginary-axis entries reported here are retained as unclassified spectral candidates, not established QNMs. We quantify their finite-table gap dispersion and sensitivity to the number of included gaps. Their spacings near one quarter in $Mω$, and the extremal product near $1/3$ with the throat radius, remain properties of the tabulated candidates, and they do not establish universal QNM spacing or throat-controlled damping.

</details>


### [193] [Dimensional (In)dependence of Lorentzian Quantum Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33452)
*Masazumi Honda,Hiroki Matsui,Kota Numajiri,Kazumasa Okabayashi,Takahiro Terada*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦引力框架下，用洛伦兹路径积分与 Picard-Lefschetz 理论研究 D 维德西特迷你超空间中的量子宇宙跃迁振幅，并借助基于 Borel-Padé 重求和的 resurgence 分析处理残余 Stokes 模糊性，发现隧穿方案在各维度普遍实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是在 D 维时空的德西特迷你超空间近似中，为“宇宙从无到有”的创生过程给出良定义的跃迁振幅，并检验其在不同维度下的普遍性。

Method: 采用洛伦兹路径积分（Lorentzian path integral）结合 Picard-Lefschetz 理论识别鞍点，在 Dirichlet 边界条件下推导跃迁振幅；并使用基于 Borel-Padé 重求和（Borel-Padé resummation）的 resurgence 分析处理某些情形下的残余 Stokes 模糊性。

Result: 数值上表明 Borel-Padé 重求和的 resurgence 分析能有效解决某些情形中出现的残余 Stokes 模糊性；通过分析三维、四维、五维情形以及大 D 极限，发现宇宙从无到有的创生普遍实现隧穿方案（tunneling proposal）。

Conclusion: 该结果意味着基于爱因斯坦引力的量子宇宙学具有跨维度的普遍特征。

Abstract: We study transition amplitudes of the quantum universe in $D$-dimensional spacetime within the de Sitter minisuperspace approximation in Einstein gravity. Using the Lorentzian path integral combined with Picard-Lefschetz theory, we identify the saddle points that contribute to the integral and derive a well-defined transition amplitude under Dirichlet boundary conditions. We further show numerically that a resurgence analysis based on Borel-Padé resummation is effective in resolving the residual Stokes ambiguities that arise in certain cases. Analyzing three-, four-, and five-dimensional cases, as well as the large-$D$ limit, we find that the tunneling proposal is commonly realized for the creation of the universe from nothing. This result implies a universal feature of quantum cosmology based on Einstein gravity across dimensions.

</details>


### [194] [Polar-metric/axial-gauge perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33511)
*Abhishake Sadhukhan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究 Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov（MMP）可穿越虫洞的线性微扰，将极性度规扰动与规范场轴向扰动约化为两个耦合的 Zerilli-Moncrief 型主方程，并给出虫洞喉部在费米子反作用一阶的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMP 虫洞的喉部由带电费米子最低朗道能级的 Casimir 能量维持张开，因而其稳定性取决于费米子对扰动的响应；MMP 原有处理忽略了应力张量的迹部分，需检验自洽性（Bianchi 恒等式）并确定线性稳定性。

Method: 在磁荷背景上处理极性度规扰动与规范场轴向扰动的耦合扇区，约化为两个耦合的 Zerilli-Moncrief 型主方程；用二维共形反常（conformal anomaly）精确计算费米子响应及诱导 Hall 电流，并检验线性化 Bianchi 恒等式。

Result: 喉部方程仅在费米子响应（含迹部分）与诱导 Hall 电流同时计入时自洽；Hall 电流由费米子有效作用量导出需要系数为 c/48π 的 Euler 项，类似 Wen-Zee 项。α=0 时喉部为精确 AdS₂×S²，模式退耦为两个 Pöschl-Teller 问题，质量为 l(l-1) 与 (l+1)(l+2)，频率间隔 1/ℓ；给出 O(α) 闭式修正，其混合两模式且依赖频率，并在重叠区与口部匹配。势的对称部分在整个虫洞内为正，排除纯指数增长；修正降低正常频率并劈裂两模式共享的能级，一阶为实位移。

Conclusion: 在该扇区、一阶 α 下，对 l≥2 不存在增长模式；喉部稳定性要求费米子响应与诱导 Hall 电流一并纳入，且需引入 c/48π 的 Euler 项。

Abstract: We study linear perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov (MMP) traversable wormhole. On a magnetically charged background, polar metric perturbations couple to axial perturbations of the gauge field. We treat this sector and reduce it to two coupled master equations of Zerilli-Moncrief type. The wormhole has two regions. In the mouths the geometry is a nearly extremal magnetic Reissner-Nordstrom black hole with no quantum source. In the throat the Casimir energy of the lowest Landau level of charged fermions holds the wormhole open. We show that the throat equations are consistent only if the fermions respond to the perturbation, computed exactly from the two-dimensional conformal anomaly with the full stress tensor including the trace part that MMP discard. This response must be accompanied by an induced Hall current of the fermions. With both effects included, the linearised Bianchi identities hold at first order in the fermionic backreaction $α$. Deriving the Hall current from the fermion effective action instead requires an Euler term with coefficient $c/48π$, which becomes a coupling between magnetic flux and curvature similar to a Wen-Zee term. At $α=0$ the throat is exact AdS$_2\times S^2$ and the modes decouple into two Poschl-Teller problems with masses $l(l-1)$ and $(l+1)(l+2)$, with levels spaced by $1/\ell$ in frequency. We give the $O(α)$ corrections in closed form. They mix the two modes and depend on frequency, and match the mouths in the overlap region. The symmetric part of the potential is positive throughout the wormhole, excluding purely exponential growth. The corrections lower the normal frequencies and split the levels shared by the two modes, with real shifts at first order in $α$. This sector has no growing mode for $l\ge2$ at this order.

</details>


### [195] [Stochastic gravitational-wave background from self-interacting superradiant clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33596)
*Yin-Da Guo,Richard Brito,Chen Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了黑洞-玻色子云（black hole–boson cloud）系统产生的随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational-wave background, SGWB），并在前人工作基础上引入标量自相互作用；结果表明自相互作用可压低 SGWB，从而放松现有 LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA 的约束，同时该背景仍可被下一代探测器探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波（gravitational-wave, GW）观测是探测新基本场的有力手段，而黑洞-玻色子云系统通过超辐射（superradiance）形成超轻标量场云并辐射长寿命准单色引力波，是动机明确的波源。既有工作未纳入标量自相互作用，本文旨在补足这一环节并评估探测前景。

Method: 在先前计算的基础上，纳入标量自相互作用（scalar self-interactions）来计算此类系统产生的随机引力波背景，并据此讨论其被下一代地基引力波探测器探测的可能性。

Result: 自相互作用会压低 SGWB，从而放松由零结果搜索得出的 LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA 约束：设计灵敏度下的 LIGO 对衰减常数（decay constant）f_s ≲ 3×10^17 GeV 的标量场所产生的 SGWB 不敏感，且与标量场质量无关。在保守假设下，对 f_s = 10^17 GeV，爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope, ET）可敏感的质量区间约为 [10^-13.0, 10^-11.8] eV，宇宙探索者（Cosmic Explorer, CE）约为 [10^-13.2, 10^-11.7] eV。ET 与 CE 可探测 SGWB 所需的最小衰减常数分别约为 6×10^16 GeV 与 3×10^16 GeV。

Conclusion: 自相互作用显著影响黑洞-玻色子云系统的 SGWB 幅度及现有约束强度；即便存在中等强度的自相互作用，下一代探测器仍有望探测到该类 SGWB，为超轻标量场参数空间提供新的探测窗口。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) observations offer a powerful probe of new fundamental fields. One well-motivated source is black hole (BH)--boson cloud systems, in which an ultralight scalar field forms a cloud around a rotating BH via superradiance and emits long-lived nearly monochromatic GWs. In this work, we compute the stochastic GW background (SGWB) from such systems, extending previous work by including scalar self-interactions, and discuss its detectability with next-generation ground-based GW detectors. We find that self-interactions can suppress the SGWB and thereby relax existing LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA constraints inferred from null searches. Namely, the LIGO detectors at design sensitivity are insensitive to a SGWB produced by scalar fields with a decay constant $f_\mathrm{s} \lesssim 3\times 10^{17}$ GeV, independently of the scalar field mass. Looking ahead, we show that a moderately self-interacting cloud can still produce a detectable SGWB with next-generation detectors. Under conservative assumptions, for a decay constant $f_\mathrm{s} = 10^{17}$ GeV, the Einstein Telescope (ET) will be sensitive to scalar masses in the range $\sim[10^{-13.0},10^{-11.8}]$ eV, while Cosmic Explorer (CE) will be sensitive to scalar masses in the range $\sim[10^{-13.2},10^{-11.7}]$ eV. We also find that the minimum decay constants that still yield a detectable SGWB for ET and CE are $f_\mathrm{s}\sim 6\times10^{16}\,$GeV and $\sim 3\times10^{16}\,$GeV, respectively.

</details>


### [196] [Relic Neutrinos Probing Small Scale Primordial Non-Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34324)
*Suraj Gavhale,Shin'ichi Nojiri,Sergei D. Odintsov,Oem Trivedi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用原初遗迹中微子（relic neutrinos）记录中微子解耦后的电磁熵产生，探测被原初CMB抹除的原初模式；推导了由声学阻尼产生的立方光子数源及其领先的折叠敏感双谱窗口，并给出激发初态下的透射条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子解耦后的电磁熵产生信息留存于遗迹中微子中，可探测从主CMB中已被抹除的原初模式，弥补主CMB观测的盲区。研究动机在于建立小尺度原初非高斯性与遗迹中微子俘获率之间的直接联系。

Method: 将中微子解耦（neutrino decoupling）、热化（thermalization）与声学相干性（acoustic coherence）合并到单一响应核（response kernel）中，并由此推导声学阻尼产生的立方光子数源（cubic photon number source）及其领先的折叠敏感双谱窗口（folded sensitive bispectrum window）。

Result: 对激发初态（excited initial states），该响应核过滤出物理折叠剖面（folded profile），并给出一个依赖锐度（sharpness dependent）的透射条件（transmission condition）。

Conclusion: 该透射条件将小尺度原初非高斯性（small scale primordial non-Gaussianity）与遗迹中微子俘获率（relic neutrino capture rates）直接联系起来，为通过中微子观测探测原初非高斯性提供了途径。

Abstract: Relic neutrinos retain a record of electromagnetic entropy production after neutrino decoupling, probing primordial modes erased from the primary CMB. We derive the cubic photon number source from acoustic damping and its leading folded sensitive bispectrum window, with neutrino decoupling, thermalization, and acoustic coherence combined in a single response kernel. For excited initial states, this kernel filters the physical folded profile and yields a sharpness dependent transmission condition, directly connecting small scale primordial non-Gaussianity to relic neutrino capture rates.

</details>


### [197] [Avoided crossings and resonances in black hole ringdown with coupled fields: A case study of the Einstein-Maxwell-axion system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34401)
*Takuya Takahashi,Hayato Motohashi,Kazufumi Takahashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将黑洞准正规模（QNM）谱中避让交叉（avoided crossing）的研究扩展到多耦合场扰动系统，以 Einstein-Maxwell-axion 带电黑洞为例，揭示了共振对铃宕（ringdown）波形的具体印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避让交叉在黑洞 QNM 谱中表现出共振特性（如激发因子增强），但此前研究多限于单场情形；多耦合场系统中该现象及其对铃宕波形的影响尚待考察。

Method: 考虑 Einstein-Maxwell-axion 系统中的带电黑洞，依据解耦极限下连续连接的引力（gravitational）、电磁（electromagnetic）与轴子（axion）模式对 QNM 分支分类，聚焦引力主导与电磁主导分支之间的避让交叉；通过 QNM 重构与直接演化的时域波形对比，并进行 QNM 分解分析。

Result: QNM 重构能准确再现直接演化时域波形的铃宕部分；在避让交叉附近，特定初始扰动下出现有效更慢的衰减，而两个 QNM 的共振增强贡献在物理波形中大幅抵消；QNM 分解显示两分支在共振两侧发生物理性质的交换。

Conclusion: 上述结果刻画了多耦合场系统中共振在黑洞铃宕上的印记，表明共振效应不仅体现在谱的激发因子增强上，也通过分支性质交换与非单调衰减行为反映在可观测波形中。

Abstract: Avoided crossings in black hole quasinormal-mode (QNM) spectra exhibit characteristic resonant properties, including enhanced excitation factors. We extend the study of this phenomenon to perturbation systems with multiple coupled fields and investigate its consequences for ringdown waveforms. As a concrete example, we consider a charged black hole in the Einstein-Maxwell-axion system, whose QNM branches can be classified according to the gravitational, electromagnetic, and axion modes to which they continuously connect in the decoupling limit. We focus on an avoided crossing between the gravity- and electromagnetic-led branches thus defined. We verify that the QNM reconstruction accurately reproduces the ringdown portion of the directly evolved time-domain waveform. Near the avoided crossing, an effectively slower decay emerges for particular initial perturbations, while the resonantly enhanced contributions from the two QNMs largely cancel in the physical waveform. The QNM decomposition further reveals an exchange of the physical characters of the two branches across the resonance. These results characterize the imprint of resonances on black hole ringdown in systems with multiple coupled fields.

</details>


### [198] [Charged Simpson-Visser-AdS spacetime: QPOs and observational constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34443)
*Saeed Ullah Khan,Javlon Rayimbaev,Muhammad Zahid,Tolaniddin Nurmukhamedov,Abat Muratov,Weiwei Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究带电 Simpson-Visser-AdS 时空中的粒子动力学与准周期振荡（quasi-periodic oscillations, QPOs），揭示正则化参数 b 与 AdS 半径 l 对 ISCO 及双峰 QPO 的显著调制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需检验正则化参数（regularization parameter）b、电荷 Q 与 AdS 半径 l 共同作用下，Simpson-Visser-AdS 时空的度规结构、视界拓扑及可观测 QPO 信号如何偏离标准广义相对论预言。

Method: 分析度规函数根确定视界与拓扑分类；计算有效势（effective potential）以定位最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）；推导 Kepler 频率、径向与垂向频率用于建模 QPOs。

Result: 度规函数根呈现三类拓扑：非极值双视界、极值退化视界、以及无视界虫洞几何；b 与 l 的相互作用显著上调 QPO 基频，并改变 ISCO、角动量与能量；两个黑洞双星的高频双峰 QPO 数据以约 4σ 排除 Schwarzschild 极限，但仅约束 Q 与 AdS 曲率的退化组合，b 完全不受数据约束。

Conclusion: 正则化参数 b 显著改变 QPO 频率并对抗广义相对论基准；然而现有双峰 QPO 数据尚不能独立约束 b，只能限定电荷与 AdS 曲率的组合，故该时空参数空间仍存在较大观测盲区。

Abstract: In this article, we examine particle dynamics and Quasi-periodic oscillations within a charged Simpson-Visser-Anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime. We first analyze the behavior of the metric and black hole horizons under the competing impact of electric charge $Q$, regularization parameter $b$, and AdS radius $l$. The roots of the metric function reveal transitions between three distinct topological classes: non-extremal configurations featuring distinct inner and outer horizons, extremal limits with a single degenerate horizon, and strictly positive, horizonless wormhole geometries. By evaluating the effective potential, we determine how this modified geometry shifts the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO) and alters the angular momentum and energy of test particles. Moreover, we derive the Keplerian, radial, and vertical frequencies to model quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs). We show that the interplay between the regularization parameter $b$ and the AdS radius $l$ significantly modifies twin-peak QPOs. In particular, $b$ drives fundamental QPO frequencies higher than those predicted by standard general relativistic backgrounds. Twin-peak high-frequency QPO data from two black-hole binaries disfavor the Schwarzschild limit at $\sim\!4σ$, but constrain only a degenerate combination of charge and AdS curvature, leaving the regularization parameter entirely unconstrained by the data.

</details>


### [199] [Dynamical dark energy from quantum gravity condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34917)
*Daniele Granata,Daniele Oriti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在群场论（group field theory, GFT）凝聚态宇宙学框架下，研究量子引力诱导的动态暗能量，通过拓展参数空间并纳入额外标量物质场与多凝聚模贡献，推导有效宇宙动力学并分析其晚期行为，发现系统趋近渐近德西特（de Sitter）状态且可产生偏离w=-1的动力学演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在推广此前量子引力诱导宇宙加速的模型，通过考虑更广的参数空间、在关系时钟之外引入额外标量物质场，并同时计入相位动力学与多凝聚模贡献，以检验GFT暗能量机制的稳健性与普适性。

Method: 在平均场近似（mean-field approximation）下采用相干峰值态（coherent peaked states），并限制于厄米GFT相互作用（Hermitian GFT interactions），推导相应的有效宇宙动力学方程并分析其晚期行为。

Result: 系统趋近渐近德西特（de Sitter）状态，由单一凝聚模主导；趋近过程中可呈现出非平凡的动态暗能量演化。凝聚相位与次主导模均可导致对渐近状态方程w=-1的偏离，包括幽灵行为（phantom behaviour），并在适当条件下跨越幽灵分界（phantom divide）。额外标量物质场直接进入这些条件，将物质组分与量子引力诱导的暗能量动力学联系起来。

Conclusion: 结果拓展了GFT动态暗能量机制的稳健性与普适性，并为将该量子引力动力学与宇宙学观测相对照提供了基础。

Abstract: We investigate dynamical dark energy emerging from quantum gravity within the framework of group field theory (GFT) condensate cosmology. We generalise previous models of quantum gravity induced cosmic acceleration by considering a broader parameter space, including an additional scalar matter field besides the relational clock, and accounting simultaneously for the phase dynamics and the contribution of multiple condensate modes. Working in a mean-field approximation with coherent peaked states and restricting to Hermitian GFT interactions, we derive the corresponding effective cosmological dynamics and analyse its late-time behaviour. We show that the system approaches an asymptotic de Sitter regime, dynamically dominated by a single condensate mode, while the approach to this regime can exhibit a non-trivial dynamical dark-energy evolution. In particular, both the condensate phase and subdominant modes can generate deviations from the asymptotic equation of state $w=-1$, including phantom behaviour and, under suitable conditions, a crossing of the phantom divide. The additional scalar matter field enters directly into these conditions, providing a link between the matter content and the quantum gravity induced dark-energy dynamics. These results extend the robustness and generality of the GFT mechanism for dynamical dark energy and provide a basis for confronting the underlying quantum gravity dynamics with cosmological observations.

</details>


### [200] [Horizon, Null-Orbit, and Photon-Sphere Phase Structures in Logarithmic Nonlinear Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34957)
*Peshwaz Abdulkareem Abdoul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在 $(Q,\epsilon)$ 参数空间中考察了LNED理论中静态带电渐近平直黑洞的相结构，区分视界几何、背景圆轨道与有效光学几何三类相，并揭示它们共享零点边界但具有不同的临界结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清LNED黑洞在电荷与量子修正参数共同作用下，视界几何、背景时空与光学几何所对应相结构的异同，特别是分叉点与黑洞点行为。

Method: 方法上在 $(Q,\epsilon)$ 参数空间内，分别计算视界几何、背景圆零测地线（null orbits）与有效光学几何的圆光子轨道三类相结构，并分析其临界曲线、有效势与测地线行为。

Result: 三类相结构共享零点边界 $Q_{\rm nc}(\epsilon;M)=\gamma M^{2/3}\epsilon^{1/6}$，但极值曲线 $Q_{\rm ext}$ 与合并曲线 $Q_{\rm m}$ 终止于分叉点 $(Q_{\rm b},\epsilon_{\rm b})$，而有效光子轨道合并曲线 $Q_{\rm m}^{\rm ph}$ 不经过该点。当 $\epsilon\ge\epsilon_{\rm b}$ 趋近 $Q_{\rm nc}$ 时视界收缩为零并形成黑洞点，熵 $S_\eta(r_{\rm h}\to0)=\eta$，温度在分叉点消失、在 $\epsilon>\epsilon_{\rm b}$（除 $\eta\delta=1$ 外）发散。背景势在 $r=0$ 处形成无限势垒而无局部极值，呈完全反射体；光子势在有限半径处有最大值，呈部分反射体，故黑洞点保留有限半径的光子球。非径向测地线受阻于奇点并可形成亚稳束缚态，径向测地线或达 $r=0$ 或逃逸至无穷远；临界碰撞参数同时编码引力与光学几何效应。

Conclusion: 结论表明LNED黑洞的视界几何、背景轨道与光学几何具有共享边界但各异的临界结构，黑洞点仍保留光子球，且临界碰撞参数可同时探测多种相结构。

Abstract: We investigate static, charged, asymptotically flat black holes in LNED in the $(Q,ε)$ parameter space. We distinguish three phase structures: the horizon geometry, the background circular null orbits, and the circular photon orbits of the effective optical geometry. All share a zero-radius boundary $Q_{\rm nc}(ε;M)=γM^{2/3}ε^{1/6}$, but have distinct critical structures: the extremal curve $Q_{\rm ext}(ε;M)$ and the merger curve $Q_{\rm m}(ε;M)$ both terminate at the bifurcation point $(Q_{\rm b},ε_{\rm b})$, with $Q_{\rm b}=9M/[2Γ^2(1/4)]$ and $ε_{\rm b}=128π^2Q_{\rm b}^2$, whereas the effective photon-orbit merger curve $Q_{\rm m}^{\rm ph}(ε;M)$ does not pass through this point.
  For $ε\geε_{\rm b}$, approaching $Q_{\rm nc}$ shrinks the horizon to zero and produces black points with quantum-corrected entropy $S_η(r_{\rm h}\to0)=η$. Their temperature vanishes at the bifurcation point and diverges for $ε>ε_{\rm b}$, except at $ηδ=1$. The background potential $V_{\rm eff}^{\rm bg}(r)$ develops an infinite barrier at $r=0$ without local extrema, acting as a perfect repeller. The photon potential $V_{\rm eff}^{\rm ph}(r)$ is regularized with a maximum at finite radius, acting as a partial reflector. Thus the black points retain a photon sphere at finite radius.
  Away from $Q_{\rm nc}$, for $ε<ε_{\rm b}$ both geometries support horizonless structures above $Q_{\rm ext}$; for $ε\geε_{\rm b}$ only the optical geometry does so in $Q_{\rm nc}<Q<Q_{\rm m}^{\rm ph}$. Non-radial geodesics are blocked from the singularity and can form metastable bound states, while radial geodesics either reach $r=0$ or escape to infinity. The critical impact parameter encodes both gravitational and optical-geometry effects, probing the distinct phase structures.

</details>


### [201] [Analytic detector responses and entanglement in constant-curvature spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29992)
*Dyuman Bhattacharya,Jiayue Yang,Ming Zhang,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了与实、无质量、共形耦合标量场耦合的 Unruh–DeWitt 探测器（Unruh–DeWitt detector）的解析响应函数，用以分离场谱与整体几何效应同相互作用协议（interaction protocol）的贡献。工作覆盖 Minkowski、反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）与德西特（de Sitter, dS）时空中的高斯开关静态对，以及三维 BTZ 黑洞中的径向自由下落探测器，并给出可检验的纠缠判据与对数增长系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于区分探测器响应中来自场谱（field spectrum）与整体几何（global geometry）的效应和来自相互作用协议（如开关方式、轨迹）的效应。为此需要针对可解析处理的共形耦合标量场场景，给出领先阶（leading-order）激发概率与双探测器相干（coherence）的解析表达。

Method: 在时空维数 D=d+1≥3 下，对 Minkowski、AdS 与 dS 中高斯开关（Gaussian-switched）静态对计算领先阶激发概率与双探测器相干；对等红移（equal-redshift）对讨论时间有序相干（time-ordered coherence）的能隙依赖。dS 中利用 KMS 细致平衡（KMS detailed balance）确定使非局域相干与局域激发之比最大的能隙。三维 BTZ 黑洞用积分勒让德级数（integrated Legendre series）处理锐开关径向自由下落探测器的响应，并用均匀镜像求和方法（uniform image-sum estimate）估计奇点附近的响应。并以独立数值计算校验解析结果。

Result: 时间有序相干要求两条空间世界线不同，而局域响应与单激发相干可容许世界线重合。对等红移对，高斯开关可分离时间有序相干的能隙依赖。在 dS 中，KMS 细致平衡确定了使非局域相干与局域激发比值最大的能隙，从而给出领先阶纠缠（entanglement）存在性的单能隙判据，并将纠缠能隙限制在一个有界区间内；当纠缠存在时，共生度（concurrence）峰值出现在更小的正能隙处。BTZ 情形中，镜像求和估计给出奇点附近的对数增长并确定其系数；足够平滑的单调开启消除有限时间开启故障但保留正的对数系数，累积领先阶响应保持有限。

Conclusion: 解析响应函数可有效区分场谱与几何效应同相互作用协议的贡献，并在 dS 中提供关于领先阶纠缠存在的单能隙检验；BTZ 中奇点附近响应呈对数增长且累积领先阶响应有限。独立数值计算验证了解析表达式、其收敛性质及上述物理区分。

Abstract: We derive analytic response functions for Unruh--DeWitt detectors coupled to real, massless, conformally coupled scalar fields, to distinguish the effects of the field spectrum and global geometry from those of the interaction protocol. For Gaussian-switched static pairs in Minkowski, anti-de Sitter, and de Sitter spacetimes, we obtain the leading-order excitation probabilities and two-detector coherences in spacetime dimension $\mathcal D=d+1\ge3$. Time-ordered coherence requires distinct spatial worldlines; the local response and single-excitation coherence also admit coincidence. For equal-redshift pairs, Gaussian switching separates the gap dependence of the time-ordered coherence. In de Sitter space, KMS detailed balance fixes the gap that maximizes the ratio of nonlocal coherence to local excitation, giving a single-gap test for the existence of leading-order entanglement and restricting any entangling gaps to one bounded interval. When entanglement is present, the concurrence itself peaks at a smaller positive gap. In the three-dimensional BTZ black hole, an integrated Legendre series gives the sharply switched response of a radially infalling detector. A uniform image-sum estimate establishes logarithmic growth near the singularity and determines its coefficient from the covering-AdS response. A sufficiently smooth monotone onset removes the finite-time onset glitches but leaves a positive logarithmic coefficient; the accumulated leading-order response remains finite. Independent numerical calculations test the analytic expressions, their convergence properties, and these physical distinctions.

</details>


### [202] [A Cuscuton Representation of the Loop Quantum Cosmology Bounce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35222)
*Niayesh Afshordi,Kristina Giesel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明 Loop Quantum Cosmology（LQC）的反弹背景动力学可由局部 cuscuton 有效理论实现，首次给出满足零能量条件物质下精确 flat-FLRW LQC 背景的闭式、局部、一般协变表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LQC 以修正 Friedmann 方程将均匀宇宙的大爆炸奇点替换为反弹，但缺乏一个局部、协变的有效理论实现。该文旨在为这一背景动力学寻找不引入额外局部动力学自由度的协变表述。

Method: 将 cuscuton 用角坐标 θ 表示，并与 LQC 的聚合角（polymerization angle）2λb 等同；其约束给出 H∝sinθ，Einstein 约束给出 ρ=ρ_c sin²(θ/2)。另通过分支式 Legendre 变换建立 clock-gauge-fixed 均匀 mimetic 系统与 cuscuton 系统的正则等价，并给出分支 f(K) 表示（K 为空间超曲面的平均外曲率）。

Result: cuscuton 的标量方程是约束而非波动方程，可精确复现 LQC 反弹；Legendre 变换选出常张力 cuscuton 坐标 Θ=sinθ−θ，而 θ 仍保留 LQC 聚合角含义；角作用量可写成显式分支 f(K) 表示。

Conclusion: 该构造是 LQC holonomy correction 的有效协变表示，而非从 full loop quantum gravity 的推导；其等价性不适用于非均匀理论及其扰动。

Abstract: Loop Quantum Cosmology (LQC) replaces the big bang singularity of the homogeneous universe by a bounce, usually described by the modified Friedmann equation $H^2=ρ(1-ρ/ρ_c)/(3M_p^2)$. We show that this background dynamics follows from a local cuscuton effective theory, whose scalar equation is a constraint rather than a wave equation. One way to establish this is to write the cuscuton in terms of an angular coordinate $θ$, identified with the LQC polymerization angle $2λb$. Its constraint gives $H\propto\sinθ$, while the Einstein constraint gives $ρ=ρ_c\sin^2(θ/2)$, exactly reproducing the LQC bounce. To our knowledge, this is the first closed-form, local, generally covariant realization of the exact standard flat-FLRW LQC background dynamics for minimally coupled matter satisfying null energy condition, without introducing additional local dynamical degrees of freedom. A branchwise Legendre transformation establishes a canonical equivalence between the clock-gauge-fixed homogeneous mimetic system in the case of vanishing mimetic dust energy density and the cuscuton systems. It selects the constant-tension cuscuton coordinate $Θ=\sinθ-θ$, while $θ$ retains its interpretation as the LQC polymerization angle. This equivalence does not establish agreement of the inhomogeneous theories or their perturbations. The angular action also admits an explicit branched $f(K)$ representation, where $K$ is the mean extrinsic curvature of spatial hypersurfaces. The construction is therefore an effective covariant representation of the LQC holonomy correction, not a derivation from full loop quantum gravity.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Discrete Mixed Quantization for Cat Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32113)
*Elena Kim,Qiaochu Ma*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文为图向量丛（graph vector bundles）发展了一种混合量子化（mixed quantization）技术，将半经典分析（semiclassical analysis）与量子猫映射（quantum cat maps）相结合，并应用于 Kesten–McKay 律（Kesten–McKay law）与量子遍历性（quantum ergodicity）的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确说明研究动机，仅指出所发展的混合量子化技术被用于研究 Kesten–McKay 律与量子遍历性，可推断其目标在于为图向量丛上的相关谱与遍历性质提供统一分析框架。

Method: 方法上提出面向图向量丛的混合量子化技术，融合半经典分析与量子猫映射两类工具。

Result: 摘要未给出具体结果，仅说明该技术被应用于 Kesten–McKay 律与量子遍历性的研究。可确认的应用对象为上述两个主题。

Conclusion: 结论层面摘要未展开，仅隐含该混合量子化技术对图向量丛上 Kesten–McKay 律与量子遍历性问题的研究具有适用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a mixed quantization technique for graph vector bundles that combines semiclassical analysis and quantum cat maps. We apply it to study the Kesten--McKay law and quantum ergodicity.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Locality of KMS states for infinite fermionic lattice systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31858)
