*Lennart Becker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究无限费米子晶格热平衡态在哈密顿量微扰下的响应，证明在扩展微扰（extensive perturbation）下，指数衰减相关性与指数局部不可区分性可推出微扰态的相关性呈拉伸指数衰减（stretched-exponential decay）。方法基于在底层 \(C^*\)-代数上直接建立量子信念传播（Quantum Belief Propagation, QBP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无穷费米子晶格在扩展微扰下的稳定性，且不要求初始态或微扰态唯一，从而可在相共存（phase coexistence）情形下讨论稳定性。

Method: 在底层 \(C^*\)-代数（\(C^*\)-algebra）上直接建立量子信念传播（Quantum Belief Propagation, QBP），并结合 Lieb--Robinson 界限（Lieb--Robinson bounds）进行分析；对迹态（tracial state）微扰在衰减、支撑与一致性假设下构建推论循环。

Result: 指数衰减相关性与指数局部不可区分性在扩展微扰下推出微扰态的拉伸指数衰减相关性；在迹态微扰中，得到相关性衰减、局部微扰局部影响原则与局部不可区分性之间的相互蕴含；在一致性聚集条件下获得局部平衡期望的可微路径，其变化以微扰相互作用范数线性有界。

Conclusion: 量子信念传播（QBP）与 Lieb--Robinson 界限结合，为扩展微扰下热平衡态稳定性提供统一框架，允许相共存情形并刻画局部平衡期望的线性响应。

Abstract: We study how thermal equilibrium states of infinite fermionic lattices respond to perturbations of the Hamiltonian. Our main result concerns extensive perturbations of a chosen KMS state. We show that exponential decay of correlations and exponential local indistinguishability under the extensive perturbation yield stretched-exponential decay of correlations for the perturbed state. Neither the initial nor the perturbed state is required to be unique, allowing stability to be studied in the presence of phase coexistence. Our approach uses Quantum Belief Propagation (QBP), which we establish directly on the underlying $C^*$-algebra. Combining QBP with Lieb--Robinson bounds, we show implications between decay of correlations, the principle that local perturbations perturb locally, and local indistinguishability, closing the cycle for perturbations of the tracial state under the stated decay, support and uniformity assumptions. For extensive perturbations under uniform clustering conditions, we also obtain a differentiable path of local equilibrium expectations and bound their change linearly in the interaction norm of the perturbation.

</details>


### [205] [Local Determinant Defects and Low-Energy Subspace Stability in Semifinite von Neumann Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32095)
*Seyed Mahmoud Manjegani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出低能谱子空间稳定性的有限温度判据：通过两个吉布斯态（Gibbs states）的 α-z Renyi 核（Renyi kernel）局部行列式缺陷，界定其低能投影间的迹 L2 距离（tracial L2 distance）。核心工具为半有限 von Neumann 代数（semifinite von Neumann algebra）中有界正单射算子的谱平坦化原理（spectral-flattening principle），并给出矩阵、量子比特及无界哈密顿量的应用与行列式不等式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种不依赖哈密顿量在算子范数下接近（close in operator norm）的判据，来刻画低能谱子空间的稳定性。传统比较方法对谱信息与温度效应的耦合处理不足，促使引入有限温度下的局部行列式判据。

Method: 构造两个吉布斯态 α-z Renyi 核的局部行列式缺陷（local determinant defect），并借助半有限 von Neumann 代数（semifinite von Neumann algebra）中关于有界正单射算子的谱平坦化原理（spectral-flattening principle），将局部乘积缺陷约化为精确的双投影公式（two-projection formula）。该方法适用于具有迹类吉布斯算子（trace-class Gibbs operators）的下有界从属哈密顿量（lower-bounded affiliated Hamiltonians）。

Result: 热学结果给出两低能投影的迹 L2 距离上界，且无需哈密顿量算子范数接近；对矩阵情形可化为主角余项（principal-angle remainders）之和；对量子比特（qubits），缺陷与热隙（thermal gaps）精确决定基态重叠（ground-state overlap）；并给出有效低能模型中上有界可观测量的一致误差界。此外证明了矩阵、有限与半有限情形下的行列式不等式、等号情形及 z-单调性（z-monotonicity）。

Conclusion: 所提出的有限温度判据在无界哈密顿量与多层级示例中有效，统一了矩阵主角与量子比特基态重叠的刻画，并为有效低能模型中的可观测误差提供估计。行列式不等式及其等号、单调性结果扩展至半有限框架。

Abstract: We give a finite-temperature test for the stability of low-energy spectral subspaces. The test uses a local determinant defect for the alpha-z Renyi kernel of two Gibbs states and bounds the tracial L2 distance between their low-energy projections. The main tool is a spectral-flattening principle for bounded positive injective operators in a semifinite von Neumann algebra. It reduces the local product defect to an exact two-projection formula. The thermal result applies to lower-bounded affiliated Hamiltonians with trace-class Gibbs operators and does not require the Hamiltonians to be close in operator norm. We give an infinite-level example with unbounded Hamiltonians. For matrices, the result gives a sum of principal-angle remainders. For qubits, the defect and the thermal gaps determine the ground-state overlap exactly. The same estimate gives a uniform error bound for bounded observables in an effective low-energy model. We also prove the determinant inequalities, their equality cases, and z-monotonicity in matrix, finite, and semifinite settings.

</details>


### [206] [The Hamilton--Jacobi and Symplectic Analysis for Extended Hořava Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32218)
*Diana Vanessa Castro-Luna,Alberto Escalante*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 对线性化非投影 $\\lambda R$ 模型（含 Blas-Pujolàs-Sibiryakov 最低阶项与 Cotton 张量平方高阶项）开展 Hamilton-Jacobi 分析与 Faddeev-Jackiw 量子化，分 $\\lambda\\neq1/3$ 与 $\\lambda=1/3$ 两种情形讨论，并获得一致的括号结构与路径积分测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在对扩展后的线性化非投影 $\\lambda R$ 模型进行系统的约束 Hamiltonian 分析，考察 $\\lambda\\neq1/3$ 与 $\\lambda=1/3$ 两种情形在经典与量子层面的差异。

Method: 采用 Hamilton-Jacobi 分析（Hamilton-Jacobi analysis）与 Faddeev-Jackiw 量子化（Faddeev-Jackiw quantization）两种方法，分别处理 $\\lambda\\neq1/3$ 与 $\\lambda=1/3$ 两个情形，并构造辛张量（symplectic tensor）。

Result: 在 HJ 框架下得到完整的 Hamiltonian 集合、广义括号（generalized brackets）、基本微分、特征方程与规范变换；在 FJ 框架下由辛张量识别出基本括号，且 HJ 括号与 FJ 括号一致，并由辛张量计算量子路径积分（quantum path integral）的测度。

Conclusion: 两种方法给出的括号结构相互吻合，说明分析自洽；论文进一步讨论了 $\\lambda\\neq1/3$ 与 $\\lambda=1/3$ 两情形在量子路径积分层面的差异。

Abstract: The Hamilton-Jacobi [HJ] analysis and the Faddeev-Jackiw [FJ] quantization for the linearized, non-projectable $λR$ model, extended by the lowest-order term of Blas, Pujolàs, and Sibiryakov and the higher-order term involving the square of the Cotton tensor, were carried out. The cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ are analyzed. Within the Hamilton-Jacobi framework, we obtain the complete set of Hamiltonians, generalized brackets, fundamental differential, characteristic equations, and gauge transformations of the theory. In the Faddeev-Jackiw framework, a symplectic tensor is constructed from which the fundamental brackets are identified. The HJ and FJ brackets coincide, and from the symplectic tensor, the measure for the quantum path integral is calculated. The differences between the cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ at the quantum path integral are discussed.

</details>


### [207] [Absolute and relative invariants for low-dimensional topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32442)
*Federico Manzoni,Domenico Monaco,Gabriele Peluso*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文受拓扑绝缘体（topological insulators）启发，比较了二维及以下环面上、满足 AZC 对称性的投影值映射的三种等价关系：Murray-von Neumann、酉（unitary）与同伦（homotopy）等价，并统一给出绝对与相对拓扑不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受拓扑绝缘体研究推动，需厘清低维环面上投影值映射在不同等价关系下的分类差异。核心问题在于比较 Murray-von Neumann、酉与同伦三类等价性，明确其各自的拓扑不变量来源。

Method: 在伪周期性（pseudo-periodicity）约束下，为投影值域的取值构造对称基（symmetric bases），即部分基向量绕环面一周时获得相位。以此为基础分析三种等价关系在各 AZC 类中的表现。

Result: 当 d=1 时，所有 AZC 类均存在完全周期的对称基；当 d=2 时出现拓扑障碍，表现为由 ℤ（Chern 数）或 ℤ₂（Fu-Kane-Mele）不变量刻画的非平凡相位。这些不变量是绝对不变量，同时分类 Murray-von Neumann 与酉等价。同伦等价则依赖环境 Hilbert 空间：非最小维数时退化为酉等价，最小维数时出现额外相对同伦不变量，其值在酉共轭下改变，仅相对拓扑相位良定义。

Conclusion: 研究为低维有能隙量子系统（gapped quantum systems）提供了统一的几何框架，揭示了不同等价关系如何分别产生特定的绝对拓扑不变量与相对拓扑不变量。

Abstract: [An extended abstract is provided in-text]
  Motivated by topological insulators, this paper analyzes classification schemes for projection-valued maps on $d$-dimensional tori, $d \le 2$, subject to Altland-Zirnbauer-Cartan (AZC) symmetries (time-reversal, particle-hole, and chiral). The main objective is to compare three notions of equivalence for these maps: Murray-von Neumann, unitary, and homotopy equivalence. The analysis relies on constructing symmetric bases for the projection ranges under a pseudo-periodicity constraint, where select basis vectors pick up a phase when looped around the torus. If $d=1$, fully periodic symmetric bases exist across all AZC classes. If $d=2$, topological obstructions appear as non-trivial phases characterized by $\mathbb{Z}$ (Chern number) or $\mathbb{Z}_2$ (Fu-Kane-Mele) invariants. These serve as absolute invariants that classify both Murray-von Neumann and unitary equivalences. For homotopy equivalence, the behavior depends on the ambient Hilbert space: if this has non-minimal dimension, homotopy equivalence reduces directly to unitary equivalence, while in minimal ambient spaces additional relative homotopy invariants emerge. Their precise values change under unitary conjugation, leaving only relative topological phases between maps well-defined. Ultimately, this provides a unified geometric framework for gapped quantum systems in low dimensions, demonstrating how distinct equivalence relations generate specific absolute and relative topological invariants.

</details>


### [208] [Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization for fractional and long-range Anderson models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33084)
*Peter D. Hislop,Rodrigo Matos,Constanza Rojas-Molina*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究长程 Anderson 模型（long-range Anderson models）在强无序（strong disorder）下的局域本征值统计与动力学（去）局域化。结果表明，当 β>d/2 时，本征值统计服从 Poisson 点过程（Poisson point process），且不存在动力学局域化（dynamical localization），从而验证了 Disertori 等人的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明强无序长程 Anderson 模型具有密集纯点谱（dense pure point spectrum）与多项式衰减本征向量，但其本征值统计类型及动力学局域化行为尚未明确，尤其与仅含最近邻跃迁（nearest-neighbor hopping）的经典 Anderson 模型形成对比。

Method: 作者针对 d 维格点上具有长程跳跃项 |T(n,m)| ∼ ‖n−m‖^{−(d+2β)}（β>0）的随机 Anderson 型扰动模型，在强无序区域建立了 Green 函数分数矩（fractional moments）的显式、精确下界。

Result: 在强无序且 β>d/2 时，中心位于确定性谱（deterministic spectrum）中任意 E 的局域本征值统计为强度由态密度函数（density of states）n(E) 给出的 Poisson 点过程；同时位置算符（position operator）的高阶矩发散，导致动力学去局域化。特别地，在 d=1 且 1/2<α<1 时，分数 Anderson 模型亦呈现 Poisson 本征值统计与动力学去局域化。

Conclusion: 长程跃迁显著改变无序系统的谱统计与动力学性质：与经典最近邻 Anderson 模型不同，强无序分数 Anderson 模型不出现动力学局域化，且本征值统计为 Poisson 型，从而验证了 Disertori 等人的猜想。

Abstract: We study local eigenvalue statistics and dynamical (de)-localization properties for long-range Anderson models, including the fractional Anderson model. These systems are random Anderson-type perturbations of operators with long-range (non random) hopping terms of the form $|T(n,m)| \sim \|n-m\|^{-(d+2β)}$ for $β>0$, on the $d$-dimensional lattice. In the presence of a strong enough random potential, these models exhibit dense pure point spectrum with polynomially decaying eigenvectors, almost surely. We show that in this strong disorder regime, and for $β>d/2$, the local eigenvalue statistics centered at any $E$ in the deterministic spectrum is a Poisson point process with intensity given by the density of states function $n(E)$. Moreover, we prove that, at strong disorder, there is no dynamical localization for these models, verifying a conjecture of Disertori et al. We achieve this by establishing explicit, sharp lower bounds on the Green's functions fractional moments which imply that large moments of the position operator are infinite. Hence, these models exhibit Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization, that is, the absence of dynamical localization. In particular, this shows that in dimension $d=1$, the fractional Anderson model, a random perturbation of the fractional Laplacian $(-Δ)^α$ with $α\in (0,1)$, exhibits Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization if the disorder is strong and the exponent $\frac{1}{2}<α<1$. This is in stark contrast with what is known for the usual Anderson model that has only nearest-neighbor hopping.

</details>


### [209] [Minimal output entropy for the channels that add or remove a box of a Young diagram, and a Pauli principle for every permutation symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33498)
*Robin Reuvers*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究 $U(d)$ 两个不可约表示（由相差一个方格的杨图给出）之间由四个共变量子信道（covariant quantum channels）连接的情形。作者证明相干态（coherent state）的输出在所有输出中占优（majorize），从而最小化输出熵，并给出以钩长（hook length）表示的显式最优输出。应用上得到了单粒子约化密度矩阵谱的尖锐界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杨图相差一个方格的不可约表示之间存在自然的加/减方格操作，对应四个共变量子信道；理解这些信道的输出熵最小值及其实现态，是量子信息与表示论交叉的基本问题。此前对哪种输入态最优化熵、以及最优输出谱的精确形式缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 作者对四个信道（保留新杨图或保留该方格，后者对应 $\mathbb{C}^d$ 或其对偶）逐一证明相干态的输出占优（majorization）其他所有输出，并借助钩长（hook length）公式显式计算最优输出。对其中“保留方格”的两个信道还刻画了全部最小化态。

Result: 对每个信道，相干态输出均占优其余输出，故相干态最小化输出熵；最优输出可由钩长显式给出。对保留方格的两个信道，全部最小化态被确定，且这些态不必是相干态，即使其为纯态时亦然。

Conclusion: 作为应用，考虑具有给定置换对称性的 $N$ 粒子体系，得到单粒子约化密度矩阵谱的尖锐界，并精确刻画混合态可达到的谱集合；对费米子情形这些结果分别对应泡利不相容原理（Pauli principle）与 Coleman 定理。

Abstract: Two irreducible representations of $U(d)$ whose Young diagrams differ by a single box are connected by four covariant quantum channels: the box can be removed or added, and one keeps either the new diagram or the box ($\mathbb{C}^d$ or its dual). We prove that for each of these channels the output of a coherent state majorizes every other output, so that coherent states minimize the output entropy, and we compute the optimal output explicitly in terms of hook lengths. For the two channels that keep the box we also determine all minimizers; these need not be coherent, even when they are pure. As an application we consider $N$ particles with a given permutation symmetry and find sharp bounds on the spectrum of the reduced density matrix of a single particle, as well as the exact set of spectra that mixed states can reach (for fermions these are the Pauli principle and Coleman's theorem).

</details>


### [210] [Effective atom-dimer bands of three-body lattice systems at strong coupling: exact critical mass ratios and a statistics-quasimomentum duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33576)
*Mikhail V. Dolgopolov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在正方格子上研究三粒子无穷大接触相互作用（contact interaction）问题，将原子-二聚体（atom-dimer）扇区推广至整个布里渊区（Brillouin zone），给出有效哈密顿量、原子-二聚体区域及临界质量比的闭式结果，并揭示其重整化群（renormalization-group）意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅处理三聚体（trimer）与零准动量（zero quasimomentum）情形，缺乏对整个布里渊区原子-二聚体扇区的系统分析，也缺少对临界质量比（critical mass ratio）及阈值行为（onset law）的严格刻画。

Method: 采用 Feshbach-Schur 约化（Feshbach-Schur reduction），将问题投影到配对接触算符（pair-contact operator）的无穷简并本征值 1 上，得到带一阶误差估计的显式有效哈密顿量；阈值问题化为由方格两点电阻（two-point resistance）构造的 4×4 Hermitian 矩阵判据，进而化为四次多项式（quartic polynomial）。

Result: 给出闭式的原子-二聚体区域与临界质量比，零准动量处精确值 pi/(pi-2)，界介于 1 与 pi/(pi-2) 之间；证明每个准动量与质量比至多两个原子-二聚体态；给出阈值律（onset law），包括区边界本质奇异与纯 p 波线性除以对数的阈值行为；给出两个精确对偶性（duality），即统计改变等价于准动量平移 pi；并得到第二费米子阈值的全局范围。所有常数由格点求积、计算机代数、精确对角化与无限格点求解器独立验证。

Conclusion: 阈值律具有重整化群解读：逆格点格林函数（inverse lattice Green function）为边缘 s 波耦合（marginal s-wave coupling），束缚能经量纲嬗变（dimensional transmutation）产生，格点标度为 16，区边界属边缘（BCS/Kondo）型而非 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 型。

Abstract: We study three particles on the square lattice with contact interactions of infinite strength, for all total quasimomenta, for three statistics: three identical bosons, two identical fermions plus a third particle, and two identical bosons plus a third particle. Previous work treated only the trimer and zero quasimomentum. Here the atom-dimer sector is analyzed on the whole Brillouin zone. A Feshbach-Schur reduction onto the infinitely degenerate eigenvalue 1 of the pair-contact operator gives explicit effective Hamiltonians with proved first-order error. The threshold problem reduces to a 4x4 Hermitian matrix criterion built from the two-point resistances of the square lattice, and hence to a quartic polynomial. This yields the complete atom-dimer zone and the critical mass ratio on the Brillouin zone, with bound between 1 and pi/(pi-2), and closed forms at all symmetry points. We prove: the zones in closed form; the critical mass ratio with exact value pi/(pi-2) at zero quasimomentum, reproducing a numerical value; at most two atom-dimer states for every quasimomentum and mass ratio; the onset laws, an essential singularity on the zone boundary and a linear-over-logarithm onset in the pure p-wave; two exact dualities showing that a change of statistics is equivalent to a shift of quasimomentum by pi; and the global range of the second fermionic threshold. The onset laws admit a renormalization-group reading: the inverse lattice Green function is a marginal s-wave coupling, binding energies arise by dimensional transmutation with lattice scale 16, and the zone boundary is of marginal (BCS/Kondo) type rather than of Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless type. All constants are verified independently by lattice quadrature, computer algebra, exact diagonalisation, and infinite-lattice solvers.

</details>


### [211] [$p$-adic spectral zeta functions via the inverse Stieltjes transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33660)
*Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 $p$-adic 谱 zeta 函数框架从离散谱推广到连续谱，借助逆 Stieltjes 变换（inverse Stieltjes transform）构造 $p$-adic 谱分布，并定义玻色型与费米型 zeta 函数及泛函行列式，证明其解析性质并给出应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱 zeta 函数（spectral zeta function）是量子物理中正则化微分算子行列式的标准工具；此前工作仅针对离散谱，需将其扩展到连续谱情形以覆盖更一般的算子。

Method: 以有界算子的预解式（resolvent）为出发点，通过逆 Stieltjes 变换（inverse Stieltjes transform）构造广义分布（称为 $p$-adic 谱分布），据此定义玻色型与费米型 zeta 函数及泛函行列式（functional determinant）。

Result: 建立了所定义 zeta 函数的解析性质（analytic properties）、特殊值公式（special value formulas）与 Stirling 展开（Stirling expansions）；证明离散情形下旧框架嵌入新框架；玻色型与费米型情形呈现对称性；并将结果应用于 $\mathbb{C}_{p}$ 任意紧子集上 $p$-adic 量子力学中的位置算子（position operator）。

Conclusion: 该工作将 $p$-adic 谱 zeta 函数框架成功推广至连续谱，统一了离散与连续情形，并揭示了玻色型与费米型之间的对称结构，为 $p$-adic 量子力学中的算子行列式正则化提供了工具。

Abstract: Spectral zeta functions provide a standard tool for regularizing determinants of differential operators in quantum physics. In a previous paper (J. Math. Phys. 66: 083505, 2025), we introduced a $p$-adic spectral zeta function for discrete spectra via a locally analytic interpolation function. In this paper we extend the framework to continuous spectra using the inverse Stieltjes transform. Starting from the resolvent of a bounded operator, we construct a generalized distribution, called the $p$-adic spectral distribution, and define bosonic and fermionic zeta functions and functional determinants. We establish their analytic properties, special value formulas, and Stirling expansions, and show that our previous framework is embedded into the present one in the discrete case. In this approach, the bosonic and fermionic cases exhibit an interesting symmetry. As an application, we consider the position operator in $p$-adic quantum mechanics on an arbitrary compact subset of $\mathbb{C}_{p}$.

</details>


### [212] [Homogenized graphs with spectrally embedded defect states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33907)
*Kirill D. Cherednichenko,Stephen P. Shipman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 通过均质化高对比度周期度量图（periodic metric graphs），构造出在连续谱（continuous spectrum）内部嵌入束缚态（bound states）的连续介质，其机制为两套耦合格图的混合态能带与能隙重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是在连续介质中实现连续谱内的束缚态（bound states in the continuum），利用局部缺陷（local defects）在嵌入连续谱的能量处产生束缚态；为此需将高对比度周期图结构均质化为可分析的连续介质模型。

Method: 将两拷贝格图（lattice graph）同时嵌入空间并以连接边耦合，通过 Fourier 分析插值将图上的常微分方程（ODE）算子与空间中的偏微分方程（PDE）算子表示于同一函数空间以实现均质化；两图耦合后形成具有内部自由度的有效介质，可分解为两个正交的混合态（hybrid states）空间，并借助临界高对比度保留微观共振。

Result: 在临界高对比度下，微观共振（microscopic resonances）在有效介质中得以保留，使一个混合态空间的谱带与另一混合态空间的谱隙发生重叠；这一机制在有效介质连续谱内嵌入频率处生成束缚态，并因缺陷区域约化预解式（reduced resolvents）的 O(ε) 算子范数估计，使这些连续谱内束缚态在 ε 尺度度量图上持续存在。

Conclusion: 高对比度周期度量图的均质化可构建带内部自由度的有效介质，通过混合态谱带与谱隙的重叠实现连续谱内束缚态，并给出其稳定性的算子范数估计。

Abstract: By homogenizing high-contrast periodic metric graphs, we create continuous media that admit bound states produced by local defects at energies embedded in the continuous spectrum. This is achieved by fitting two copies of a lattice graph simultaneously into space and coupling them by connecting edges. The homogenization of an ODE on a graph to a PDE in space is achieved by representing the associated operators in a common functional space through interpolation by Fourier analysis. The two coupled graphs result in an effective medium with internal degrees of freedom that allow a decomposition into two orthogonal spaces of hybrid states. Critical high contrast retains microscopic resonances in the effective medium and allows a spectral band of one hybrid state space to overlap a spectral gap of the other. This serves as the mechanism for creating bound states at frequencies embedded in the continuous spectrum of the effective medium. These bound states in the continuum persist for $\varepsilon$-scale metric graphs due to $O(\varepsilon)$ operator-norm estimates for reduced resolvents on the defect region.

</details>


### [213] [The equivalence of topological indices for $\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34119)
*Hal Tasaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：在整数自旋链、在位 \(\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2\) 对称与对称局部唯一有隙基态（symmetric locally-unique gapped ground state）的共同设定下，Ogata 的算子代数指标与 Tasaki 的基本扭转指标（elementary twist index）等价，且不依赖平移不变性或矩阵乘积表示（matrix product representation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋链中的对称保护拓扑相（symmetry-protected topological phases, SPT phases）需借助拓扑指标加以区分，但既有指标适用范围不一：Ogata 的算子代数指标适用于较广的对称类而不要求连续对称性，Tasaki 的基本扭转指标则要求 \(\mathrm{U}(1)\) 对称。二者关系不明，限制了指标的可计算性与互证。

Method: 在整数自旋链与在位 \(\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2\) 对称、对称局部唯一有隙基态的共同框架中展开证明：先通过电荷涨落估计（charge-fluctuation estimate）构造相位固定的半链旋转之强极限（strong-limit construction），再用精确的群对易子恒等式（group commutator identity）将 Ogata 指标等同于局部扭转期望的极限，并给出显式误差界。

Result: 证明了两类指标在上述共同设定下的等价性，并给出显式误差界，从而在已知能隙下界与相关基态期望时提供可计算公式。作为应用，先前的扭转指标计算可确定反铁磁海森堡链（antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chain）的 Ogata 指标为 \((-1)^S\)，其条件为显式的唯一性假设与边界场能隙假设。

Conclusion: Ogata 指标与 Tasaki 扭转指标在整数自旋、\(\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2\) 对称情形下等价，该结果不依赖平移不变性或矩阵乘积表示，并赋予扭转指标计算以算子代数意义；据此可由已有扭转指标结果读出反铁磁海森堡链的 Ogata 指标。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases in quantum spin chains are distinguished by topological indices. Ogata's operator-algebraic indices apply to a broad range of symmetry classes without requiring continuous symmetry, whereas the elementary twist index of Tasaki requires $\mathrm{U}(1)$ symmetry. We prove their equivalence in the common setting of integer-spin chains with on-site $\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry and a symmetric locally-unique gapped ground state, without assuming translation invariance or a matrix product representation. A charge-fluctuation estimate yields a strong-limit construction of half-chain rotations with their phases fixed. An exact group commutator identity then identifies the Ogata index with the limit of local twist expectations and gives an explicit error bound. This provides a computable formula when a lower bound on the gap and the relevant ground-state expectations are available. As an application, a previous twist-index calculation determines the Ogata index to be $(-1)^S$ for the antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chain under explicit uniqueness and boundary-field gap assumptions.
  The author formulated the problem and proposed the basic strategy, while ChatGPT made substantial contributions to the development of the proof.

</details>


### [214] [Discontinuity of Continuum Fermion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34402)
*Oliver Siebert*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究连续费米子（continuum fermions）在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中经对势（pair potential）相互作用的 Heisenberg 动力学，证明在较广泛条件下该动力学即使在规范不变 CAR 代数（gauge-invariant CAR algebra）上也不是逐点范数连续（pointwise-norm continuous）的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注连续费米子系统的 Heisenberg 动力学性质，特别是其连续性及代数不变性问题。研究动机在于对一大类非常数对势，在色散关系（dispersion relation）与外势（external potential）的适当假设下，考察动力学是否保持代数结构。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中通过哈密顿量描述连续费米子间的对势相互作用，并在色散关系与外势的适当假设下进行数学分析；进一步使用自动连续性论证（automatic-continuity argument）推导代数不变性结果。

Result: 证明对一大类非常数对势，动力学在规范不变 CAR 代数上不具有逐点范数连续性；自动连续性论证表明，CAR 代数（CAR algebra）及其规范不变子代数（gauge-invariant subalgebra）均不在动力学下保持不变。

Conclusion: 非不变性（non-invariance）在几乎所有时刻（almost all times）都会发生，说明此类连续费米子动力学无法保持相应代数结构。

Abstract: We study the Heisenberg dynamics of continuum fermions in $\IR^d$ interacting through a pair potential. For a large class of nonconstant pair potentials and under suitable assumptions on the dispersion relation and external potential, we prove that the dynamics fails to be pointwise-norm continuous even on the gauge-invariant CAR algebra. An automatic-continuity argument then shows that neither the CAR algebra nor its gauge-invariant subalgebra is invariant under the dynamics, with non-invariance occurring for almost all times.

</details>


### [215] [Classical BV Pushforward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34533)
*Xin Geng,Andrey Losev,Vyacheslav Lysov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文定义了经典 BV 前推（classical BV pushforward），并证明其两条主要性质：将全空间上经典主方程（classical master equation, CME）的解映为较小空间上 CME 的解；在孤立临界点（isolated critical points）情形下，将 BV 规范等价（BV canonically equivalent）解映为 BV 规范等价解。文中以示例说明该构造并讨论其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指出该构造可通过示例说明并具有应用价值，未提供背景性动因信息。

Method: 核心方法为定义经典 BV 前推（classical BV pushforward），并对其性质进行定理式证明，辅以示例说明该构造。

Result: 证明了两个主要性质：其一，该前推将全空间（total space）上的 CME 解映为较小空间（smaller space）上的 CME 解；其二，对孤立临界点，该前推保持 BV 规范等价性。

Conclusion: 该前推为经典 BV 框架下解的约化提供了保持 CME 结构与规范等价性的工具；文中讨论了其应用，但摘要未列出具体应用内容。

Abstract: We define the classical BV pushforward and establish its two main properties. First, we prove that it maps solutions of the classical master equation (CME) on the total space to solutions of the CME on the smaller space. Second, we prove that, for isolated critical points, it maps BV canonically equivalent solutions to BV canonically equivalent solutions. We illustrate the construction with examples and discuss its applications.

</details>


### [216] [Scattering Measures and Information Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34721)
*Tatsuro Oda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将散射实验表述为结果空间上的参数化有限测度，并构建由Fisher度量定义的信息几何，从而把散射实验、测量过程与最优实验设计统一到信息论框架中，并推广至不完备探测的量子测量情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射实验的传统分析缺乏统一的信息论框架，难以在同一几何语言下比较不同实验操作的统计效能。该工作旨在借助信息几何（information geometry）刻画散射测量中的信息结构，并将其与最优实验设计理论相联系。

Method: 将散射实验形式化为结果空间（outcome spaces）上的参数化有限测度（parameterized finite measures），并以Fisher度量（Fisher metric）定义相应信息几何。通过将散射测度分解为总质量与归一化形状，分离出两类Fisher信息贡献；同时用概率核或次概率核（probability or sub-probability kernels）表示测量过程。

Result: 测量核作用于散射测度时会使Fisher信息收缩（contract），从而将广泛类别的实验操作纳入共同的信息论框架。该表述还通过散射测度的Fisher几何，将散射实验与最优实验设计（optimal experimental design）理论相连接。

Conclusion: 相同的有限测度结构自然出现在不完备探测（incomplete detection）的量子测量中，明确了其从量子测量到经典结果统计这一层级结构中的位置。

Abstract: We formulate scattering experiments in terms of parameterized finite measures on outcome spaces and develop the associated information geometry defined by the Fisher metric. The decomposition of a scattering measure into total mass and normalized shape separates Fisher information into contributions from changes in overall scattering strength and redistribution among outcomes. Measurement processes are represented by probability or sub-probability kernels acting on the scattering measure. Such kernels contract Fisher information, placing a broad class of experimental operations within a common information-theoretic framework. This formulation connects scattering experiments to the theory of optimal experimental design through the Fisher geometry of scattering measures. The same finite-measure structure arises naturally in quantum measurements with incomplete detection, clarifying its place in the hierarchy from quantum measurement to classical outcome statistics.

</details>


### [217] [Notes on Magnetostatic Interactions in $\mathbb R^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34766)
*Vladimir Gol'dshtein,Wolfgang H. Müller,Reuven Segev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种静磁学（magnetostatics）的紧凑表述，在不使用本构关系（constitutive relations）的前提下，从偶极分布与矢量势（vector potential）出发构造势能，并导出机械力泛函（force functional），其中除洛伦兹力与开尔文力密度外还包含非对称主动应力项与压强项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静磁学的传统表述通常依赖本构关系（constitutive relations），其中包括以太关系（aether relations）。作者希望在不引入这类关系的情况下给出偶极分布受场作用的能量与力表述，以获得更基本的力学图景。

Method: 以电流密度势场（current density potential field）描述磁偶极分布，并据此在矢量势场（vector potential field）下提出承载磁偶极分布区域的势能表达式。假设偶极分布作虚运动（virtual motion）时场消耗的虚功等于势能对时间的负导数，从而导出机械力泛函。

Result: 所得的力泛函除包含洛伦兹（Lorentz）与开尔文（Kelvin）力密度外，还包含一个非对称的主动应力场（asymmetric active stress field）；该应力场的反对称部分对应机械力偶密度（mechanical couple density），并另含一个压强项（pressure term）。

Conclusion: 这表明在不采用本构关系与以太关系的紧凑表述下，静磁学中的机械力可自然地分解为洛伦兹力、开尔文力、非对称主动应力（含力偶密度）与压强项的组合。

Abstract: A compact formulation of magnetostatics is presented. Without using constitutive relations, including the aether relations, an expression is proposed for the potential energy of a region carrying a distribution of magnetic dipoles under a vector potential field, where the dipole distribution is specified by a current density potential field. Assuming that during a virtual motion of the dipole distribution the virtual work expended by the field is equal to minus the time derivative of the potential energy, an expression for the mechanical force functional is derived. In addition to the Lorentz and Kelvin force densities, the force functional contains an asymmetric active stress field, the skew-symmetric part of which is the mechanical couple density, and a pressure term.

</details>


### [218] [A renormalized Lee Model: resolvent and spectral analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34980)
*Claudio Cacciapuoti,Federica Muscolino,Diego Noja,Andrea Posilicano*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建并分析重整化三维有质量 Lee 模型，通过抽象方法定义其重整化哈密顿量并给出算子域、作用及 Kreın 型预解公式，借助激发数守恒将谱分析化为逐扇区的算子铅笔研究，得到 HVZ 型谱公式及亚阈值谱缺失结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是对重整化三维有质量 Lee 模型进行严格数学构造与谱分析，该模型重整化哈密顿量需要通过抽象构造内在定义，涉及算子域、作用及预解公式的明确给出。

Method: 利用总激发数守恒将全哈密顿量的谱分析约化为对显式算子铅笔（operator pencil）的逐扇区谱轮廓研究，该铅笔充当算子值 Weyl 函数（operator-valued Weyl function）的角色；并借助有界自伴非负 Birman–Schwinger 算子描述点谱。

Result: 在下激发扇区生成的扇区中构造本质谱（essential spectrum）成分，得到预期逐扇区 HVZ 型公式的包含关系，并在前两个扇区及（在显式条件下）每个扇区证明完整公式；在自由或单激发阈值以下，每个更高扇区的点谱由有界自伴非负 Birman–Schwinger 算子描述。对至少含两个激发的每个扇区，当重整化间隙不小于玻色子质量时对任意耦合证明无亚阈值谱，当间隙更小时在显式弱耦合条件下同样证明无亚阈值谱，并给出双激发扇区存在单束缚态的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为重整化三维有质量 Lee 模型建立了严格的谱分析框架，逐扇区 HVZ 型公式与亚阈值谱缺失结果刻画了模型谱结构，亚阈值点谱与束缚态存在性可通过 Birman–Schwinger 算子及弱耦合条件判别。

Abstract: We construct and analyze a renormalized three-dimensional massive Lee model; its renormalized Hamiltonian is defined intrinsically through an abstract construction which explicitly provides the operator domain, action and a Kre\uın-like resolvent formula. Conservation of the total excitation number reduces the spectral analysis of the full Hamiltonian to a sector-wise study of the spectral profile of an explicit operator pencil, playing the role of an operator-valued Weyl function. We construct essential spectrum components in a sector generated by lower excitation sectors and obtain the inclusion in the expected sector-wise HVZ type formula; the full formula is proved in the first two sectors and, under explicit conditions, in every sector. Below the free or one-excitation threshold, the point spectrum in every higher sector is described by a bounded self-adjoint nonnegative Birman--Schwinger operator. In every sector with at least two excitations we prove absence of sub-threshold spectrum for arbitrary coupling when the renormalized gap is not smaller than the boson mass, and under an explicit weak-coupling condition when it is smaller. In the latter case we also derive a sufficient condition for the existence of a simple bound state in the two-excitation sector.

</details>


### [219] [GR-FM: Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching for SDF-Based Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35006)
*Yuxin Ai,Zhichang Guo,Fanghui Song,Dazhi Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 GR-FM（Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching）框架，将医学图像分割建模为从初始分布到目标隐式表示的连续概率输运过程，用符号距离场（SDF）替代二值掩膜作为目标结构表示，并引入双调和正则项与 Eikonal 约束以增强空间平滑性与距离场特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割在边界定位与复杂结构保持方面存在困难，目标区域常具有弱边界、细粒度结构与不规则形状，且高质量图像与精确标注通常有限。现有基于 Flow Matching 的生成式分割方法主要学习状态空间中的速度场，缺乏对恢复表示的空间规律性与距离场性质的显式约束，可能导致空间振荡、边界位移与细结构不连续。

Method: GR-FM 以图像为条件，构建从初始分布到目标隐式表示分布的连续概率输运过程；不直接建模二值掩膜，而采用符号距离场（signed distance field, SDF）描述目标结构，每像素以到目标边界的符号距离表示，形成连续几何场；随后利用 Flow Matching 与常微分方程（ordinary differential equations, ODE）实现确定性且高效的分布输运；训练目标中进一步引入双调和正则项（biharmonic regularization term）与 Eikonal 约束（Eikonal constraint），以增强恢复表示的空间平滑性、结构一致性与距离场特性。此外，作者分析了几何约束下的连续输运过程，并研究零水平集（zero level set）的演化。

Result: 在 MoNuSeg、GlaS 与 DRIVE 数据集上的实验表明，GR-FM 在区域重叠（region overlap）与边界精度（boundary accuracy）两方面均取得有竞争力的性能，降低了性能波动，并且在仅需少量积分步数（integration steps）时仍保持稳定的分割结果。

Conclusion: 通过以 SDF 为隐式表示并结合几何正则化与 Eikonal 约束，GR-FM 缓解了 Flow Matching 分割中缺乏空间规律性约束的问题，在边界精度、稳定性与推理效率（少量积分步）上表现出优势。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation remains challenging in terms of accurate boundary localization and complex-structure preservation, as target regions often exhibit weak boundaries, fine-grained structures, and irregular shapes, while high-quality images and precise annotations are usually limited. Existing generative segmentation approaches based on Flow Matching mainly learn velocity fields in the state space but lack explicit constraints on the spatial regularity and distance-field properties of the recovered representation. This limitation may lead to spatial oscillations, boundary displacement, and discontinuities in fine structures. To address these issues, we propose an image-conditioned Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching framework, termed GR-FM, which formulates segmentation as a continuous probability transport process from an initial distribution to a target implicit representation distribution. Instead of directly modeling binary masks, GR-FM adopts the SDF to describe the target structure, where each pixel is represented by its signed distance to the object boundary, resulting in a continuous geometric field. Flow Matching and ordinary differential equations are then employed to achieve deterministic and efficient distribution transport. The training objective further incorporates a biharmonic regularization term and an Eikonal constraint to enhance the spatial smoothness, structural consistency, and distance-field characteristics of the recovered representation. Moreover, we analyze the continuous transport process under geometric constraints and investigate the evolution of the zero level set. Experiments conducted on the MoNuSeg, GlaS, and DRIVE datasets demonstrate that GR-FM achieves competitive performance in both region overlap and boundary accuracy, reduces performance variation, and maintains stable segmentation results with only a small number of integration steps.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [From Ehrenfest to Heisenberg scales: a hierarchy of mixing times for a phase-randomized quantum baker walk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32067)
*Amir Sepehri*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于 Balazs–Voros 量子化的强噪声量子面包师映射（quantum baker map）模型，其随机传播子（random propagator）构成 $\U(N)$ 上的结构化随机游走。工作从系综平均、双拷贝统计（two-copy statistics）与全测度三个层级刻画随机性扩散速度，给出精确 $1$-设计（unitary $1$-design）时刻、$2$-设计混合时间、Wasserstein 混合上界与下界，并证明在 $t<N$ 时全测度对 Haar 测度保持奇异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子面包师映射是量子混沌（quantum chaos）的标准模型，但其如何在随机扰动下扩散随机性缺乏严格分析。作者沿用 Schack 与 Caves 的“面包师加刷新对角扰动”架构，构造可处理的强噪声模型以定量研究该扩散过程。

Method: 在每一步幺正传播子后接一个对角幺正算子，其元素独立且均匀分布于单位圆上，得到 $\U(N)$（$N=2^k$）上的结构化随机游走。分析工具包括与 $B_N$ 关联的 unistochastic 马尔可夫核（unistochastic Markov kernel）——一个仿射二进链，其非平凡 Fourier 模在 $k$ 步后精确消失，以及相位自适应路径耦合（phase-adapted path coupling）、度量熵（metric-entropy）与 Haar 小球（Haar small-ball）估计。

Result: 该游走在时刻 $k+1$ 成为精确幺正 $1$-设计，其固定精度 $2$-设计混合时间为 $\Theta(\log N)$，因此诱导态系综在 Ehrenfest 尺度上重现 Haar 均值与双拷贝统计。全测度混合显著更慢：相位自适应路径耦合给出归一化 Wasserstein 混合的 $O_\varepsilon(N)$ 上界，度量熵与 Haar 小球估计给出 $\Omega_\varepsilon(N/\log N)$ 下界，且在每个 $t=o(N/\log N)$ 处距离渐近最大。当 $t<N$ 时，该测度对 Haar 测度始终奇异。

Conclusion: 随机性扩散呈现层级结构：态系综的 Ehrenfest 尺度随机化与全幺正群近 Heisenberg 尺度的探索相互分离。矩结果与 Wasserstein 上界由与 $B_N$ 关联的 unistochastic 马尔可夫核驱动，其非平凡 Fourier 模的有限步消失是核心机制。

Abstract: The quantum baker map is a standard model of quantum chaos, but rigorous analysis of how it spreads random perturbations remains difficult. Following the repeated ``baker plus refreshed diagonal perturbation'' architecture of Schack and Caves, we study a tractable strong-noise model based on the Balazs--Voros quantization: at each time step, its unitary propagator is followed by a diagonal unitary whose entries are independent and uniform on the unit circle. The resulting random propagator is a structured random walk on $\U(N)$, $N=2^k$. We quantify how quickly the baker dynamics spreads this basis-local randomness at three levels: ensemble means, two-copy statistics, and the full law of the accumulated propagator. The walk becomes an exact unitary $1$-design at time $k+1$, while its fixed-accuracy $2$-design mixing time is $Θ(\log N)$. Thus the induced state ensembles reproduce Haar means and two-copy statistics on the Ehrenfest scale. The full law mixes much more slowly. A phase-adapted path coupling gives normalized Wasserstein mixing in $O_\varepsilon(N)$ steps, whereas metric-entropy and Haar small-ball estimates give an $Ω_\varepsilon(N/\log N)$ lower bound and asymptotically maximal distance at every $t=o(N/\log N)$. The law remains singular with respect to Haar measure for every $t<N$. The moment results and the Wasserstein upper bound are driven by the unistochastic Markov kernel associated with $B_N$, an affine dyadic chain whose nonconstant Fourier modes vanish exactly after $k$ steps. The resulting hierarchy separates Ehrenfest-scale randomization of state ensembles from near-Heisenberg-scale exploration of the full unitary group.

</details>


### [221] [Power variations of critical Gaussian multiplicative chaos and their spectral applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35681)
*Mo Dick Wong*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文引入任意维临界高斯乘法混沌（Gaussian multiplicative chaos, GMC）沿细化剖分的幂变差，在适当重整化下证明一致分数阶矩界与向超临界GMC的稳定收敛，并据此统一处理临界混沌的正则性与谱问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界GMC的正则性与谱性质（如连续性模、Liouville量子引力表面的谱结构、傅里叶衰减）此前缺乏统一处理框架。作者希望通过研究沿细化剖分的幂变差，为这些问题提供统一的路径。

Method: 在任意维度上定义临界GMC沿细化划分（refining partitions）的幂变差（power variations）；在适当重整化（renormalisation）下，利用拉普拉斯变换估计（Laplace transform estimates）证明一致分数阶矩界（uniform fractional moment bounds）以及向超临界GMC（supercritical GMC）的稳定收敛（stable convergence）。

Result: 上述结果给出三项应用：(i) 对数连续性模（logarithmic modulus of continuity）的尖锐指数；(ii) 临界Liouville量子引力（Liouville quantum gravity）曲面存在非空本质谱（essential spectrum）；(iii) 几乎必然的定量傅里叶衰减（almost-sure quantitative Fourier decay）。

Conclusion: 幂变差结合重整化、矩界与稳定收敛构成研究临界乘法混沌的统一方法，可同时导出正则性尖锐指数与谱性质，表明临界混沌在连续性与谱层面具有可控且非平凡的结构。

Abstract: We introduce power variations of critical Gaussian multiplicative chaos (GMC) along refining partitions in arbitrary dimension. Under suitable renormalisation, we prove uniform fractional moment bounds via Laplace transform estimates as well as stable convergence to supercritical GMCs. Together, these results provide a unified approach to regularity and spectral questions for critical chaos, yielding: (i) the sharp exponent for the logarithmic modulus of continuity, (ii) the existence of non-empty essential spectrum for critical Liouville quantum gravity surfaces; and (iii) an almost-sure quantitative Fourier decay.

</details>


### [222] [On eigenvectors of the SYK model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35024)
*Yukun He*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究具有 $N$ 个马约拉纳费米子（Majorana fermions）与相互作用阶数 $q$ 的 Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev 模型（SYK 模型），对偶数 $q\in\{2,4,\dots,N-2\}$ 给出初等证明，表明其特征向量在所有确定性方向上完全离域（completely delocalized）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK 模型的量子混沌与随机矩阵性质是凝聚态与高能物理交叉领域的重要议题，其中特征向量的离域程度刻画了模型的遍历性与热化行为。摘要未进一步说明研究缺口，仅以给出更简明的证明与更强结论为指向。

Method: 采用初等证明（elementary proof），针对相互作用阶数取偶数 $q\in\{2,4,\dots,N-2\}$ 的 SYK 模型展开论证；摘要未披露具体技术步骤或所用工具。

Result: 证明了该模型的特征向量在所有确定性方向（deterministic directions）上完全离域，且该结论对上述整段偶数 $q$ 取值统一成立。

Conclusion: 在偶数相互作用阶数范围内，SYK 模型的特征向量具有完全的离域性；所采用的初等证明路径或可为同类随机哈密顿量的谱性质研究提供更简洁的论证方式。

Abstract: We consider the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model with $N$ Majorana fermions and interaction order $q$. For even $q\in \{2,4,...,N-2\}$, we give an elementary proof and show that the eigenvectors of the model are completely delocalized in all deterministic directions.

</details>

### [223] [Modulation spaces: from Feichtinger's original definition to modern Gabor and symplectic characterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33894)
*Antonio Caputo,Elena Cordero,Gianluca Giachi,Luigi Rodino*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文对调制空间（modulation spaces）进行综述性梳理，从Feichtinger在局部紧阿贝尔群上的原始构造出发，延伸至近期辛（symplectic）表述，并以metaplectic Wigner分布为核心统一其理论脉络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在追溯调制空间理论中一以贯之的局部—全局原理（local-global principle），并厘清各类等价描述与加权、拟Banach、Gelfand-Shilov等推广之间的关系，进而将近期辛理论纳入Feichtinger框架。

Method: 采用阐述性（expository）梳理路径，沿有界一致单位分解（bounded uniform partitions of unity）、短时傅里叶变换（short-time Fourier transform）、coorbit方法与Gabor系数展开讨论，并重点分析metaplectic Wigner分布 $W_{\mathcal A}$ 的结构与离散化。

Result: 在shift-invertible情形下，metaplectic Wigner分布本质上是经过chirp与相空间变量线性变换的、重新标度的短时傅里叶变换；该结构事实同时解释了它们对调制空间与Wiener amalgam空间的刻画，以及通过metaplectic Gabor框架实现的离散化。

Conclusion: 该结构关系将近期辛理论自然地安置于Feichtinger的调制空间框架之内，并统一了Gabor矩阵、卷积与嵌入估计以及非线性色散方程应用等多方面的结果。

Abstract: We present an expository excursus on modulation spaces, from Feichtinger's original construction on locally compact abelian groups to recent symplectic formulations. We trace the same local-global principle through bounded uniform partitions of unity, the short-time Fourier transform, coorbit methods, and Gabor coefficients, while distinguishing equivalent descriptions from the weighted, quasi-Banach, and Gelfand-Shilov extensions of the underlying functional setting. We also discuss Gabor matrices, convolution and embedding estimates, and an application to a nonlinear dispersive equation. The final part is devoted to metaplectic Wigner distributions $W_{\mathcal A}$. In the shift-invertible case these representations are, up to chirps and a linear change of phase-space variables, rescaled short-time Fourier transforms. This structural fact explains both their characterization of modulation and Wiener amalgam spaces and their discretization by metaplectic Gabor frames, and places the recent symplectic theory naturally within Feichtinger's modulation-space framework.

</details>

### [224] [Geometry-Controlled Polarization Photocurrents in Scalable PtSe2 Infrared Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32136)
*Eunice Y. Paik,Owen A. Vail,Madaline R. Marland,William A. Beck,Antonio Llopis-Jepsen,Wendy L. Sarney,Jeffery H. Leach,Stefan Heiserer,Nikolas Dominik,Cormac Ó Coileáin,Paul B. Seifert,Georg S. Duesberg,Blair C. Connelly,George J. de Coster*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作利用可规模化生长的宽面积 PtSe2 薄膜，在室温下实现近红外至中波红外的偏振光响应，并证明器件几何结构会重塑偏振响应空间分布；结合有限元电流流动模拟，将横向偏振响应局域化于像素中心，从而分离出对称性允许的光电流与邻近接触区的二色性介导光热贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强度型探测器（intensity-based detectors）无法提供多维光学信息，而贵金属二硫属化物（noble metal dichalcogenide）PtSe2 因强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）、材料稳定性、可定制宽带偏振响应及与后端制程（back-end-of-line）硅集成的兼容性，具备可调偏振探测的独特潜力。该研究旨在实现室温红外波段的偏振光响应并厘清其物理来源。

Method: 采用可规模化、直接生长的宽面积 PtSe2 薄膜制备器件，进行室温近红外至中波红外偏振光响应测量；通过有限元电流流动模拟（finite-element current-flow simulations）复现偏振敏感响应的空间再分布，并借助波长依赖的激光光斑扫描（laser spot scans）开展诊断。

Result: 模拟复现了偏振敏感响应的空间再分布，并将横向偏振响应（transverse polarization response）局域化于像素中心附近。近红外波长下观测到与线性二色性光热电电流（linear-dichroic photothermoelectric currents）一致的空间响应；中波红外测量则揭示斜入射（oblique illumination）下依赖螺旋性（helicity-dependent）的光热贡献。

Conclusion: 像素边界工程（pixel boundary engineering）既是可规模化 PtSe2 偏振敏感红外像素的设计手段，也是有效的诊断工具；几何结构驱动的响应分离可用于区分对称性允许的光电流与接触邻近的二色性介导光热贡献。

Abstract: Polarization-sensitive photodetectors provide multi-dimensional optical information beyond the capabilities of traditional intensity-based detectors. The noble metal dichalcogenide PtSe2 presents unique opportunities for tunable polarization detection due to its strong spin-orbit coupling, material stability, customizable broadband polarization responses, and direct compatibility with a wide range of substrates for back-end-of-line silicon integration. In this work, we demonstrate room-temperature near-infrared to mid-wavelength infrared polarization photoresponses using scalable, as-grown, wide-area PtSe2 films, and we show that the measured polarization response is reshaped by device geometry. Finite-element current-flow simulations reproduce the observed spatial redistribution of the polarization-sensitive response and localize the transverse polarization response near the pixel center. This geometry-enabled separation allows wavelength-dependent laser spot scans to distinguish symmetry-allowed photocurrents from contact-proximate, dichroism-mediated photothermal contributions. At near-infrared wavelengths, we observe spatial response that is consistent with linear-dichroic photothermoelectric currents, whereas mid-wavelength infrared measurements reveal a helicity-dependent photothermal contribution under oblique illumination. These results identify pixel boundary engineering as both a design lever and a diagnostic tool for scalable PtSe2 polarization-sensitive infrared pixels.

</details>


### [225] [Plasmon-Enhanced Second-Harmonic Generation in Atomically Thin Crystalline Silver Nanostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35477)
*Saad Abdullah,Philipp K. Jenke,Andrew P. Weber,Álvaro Rodríguez Echarri,Vahagn Mkhitaryan,Fadil Iyikanat,Frederik Schiller,Philip Walther,J. Enrique Ortega,Lee A. Rozema,F. Javier García de Abajo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作利用原子级厚度外延银薄膜，通过垂直电子量子限域与横向等离激元增强的协同作用，突破中心对称介质中二阶非线性被对称性禁戒的限制，并在纳米条纹/纳米三角阵列中实现可调谐的二次谐波产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料的非线性光学响应本征较弱，且中心对称介质（centrosymmetric media）中的二阶过程受对称性禁戒，严重制约了深亚波长、超薄体积内的高效频率转换（frequency conversion），这成为非线性纳米光子学（nonlinear nanophotonics）发展的关键瓶颈。

Method: 制备原子级厚度的外延生长（epitaxially grown）晶体银薄膜，利用垂直电子量子限域（electronic quantum confinement）与横向等离激元增强的相互作用；随后将薄膜图形化为周期性纳米条纹（nanoribbon）与纳米三角（nanotriangle）阵列，以支撑红外局域表面等离激元共振（localized surface plasmon resonances）；并通过对纳米结构几何形貌的精确控制实现共振及谐波频率的光谱调谐。

Result: 原子级厚度薄膜表现出与电子量子阱（electronic quantum wells）相关的增强非线性响应；图形化阵列中的强近场限域（near-field confinement）相较未图形化薄膜进一步提升了二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation）；纳米结构几何的精确调控可实现等离激元共振乃至增强谐波频率的光谱调谐。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了在量子限域金属（quantum-confined metals）中激活稳健二阶非线性（second-order nonlinearities）的方法，其中本征尺寸效应与等离激元共振协同作用。高质量外延生长与微芯片制造技术的兼容性，为片上频率转换、传感与量子光子学应用的超紧凑非线性光学元件提供了可规模化路径。

Abstract: The intrinsically weak nonlinear optical response of existing materials, further constrained by symmetry-forbidden second-order processes in centrosymmetric media, severely limits efficient frequency conversion in deeply subwavelength, ultrathin volumes. Addressing this challenge is crucial for the development of nonlinear nanophotonics. Here, we show that atomically thin, epitaxially grown crystalline silver films circumvent these restrictions through the interplay of vertical electronic quantum confinement and lateral plasmonic enhancement. We fabricate atomically thin films that exhibit an enhanced nonlinear response associated with electronic quantum wells, and subsequently pattern them into periodic nanoribbon and nanotriangle arrays sustaining infrared localized surface plasmon resonances. Strong near-field confinement in these structures further boosts second-harmonic generation compared to unpatterned films. Precise control over nanostructure geometry enables spectral tuning of the plasmonic resonance, and consequently, the enhanced harmonic frequency. Our findings establish an approach for activating robust second-order nonlinearities in quantum-confined metals, where intrinsic size effects and plasmonic resonances act synergistically. The compatibility of high-quality epitaxial growth with microchip fabrication technology offers a scalable route toward ultracompact nonlinear optical components for on-chip frequency conversion, sensing, and quantum photonic applications.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [Not all spins of black-hole binaries formed in clusters are isotropic and not all spins of black-hole binaries formed in isolation are aligned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31879)
*Sofia Dossena,Davide Gerosa,Tristan Bruel*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文挑战了“动力学形成双黑洞自旋各向同性、孤立形成双黑洞自旋与轨道角动量对齐”的标准预期，提出解析几何框架描述两类形成通道下并合双黑洞的自旋方向分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋方向被视作区分恒星质量双黑洞并合通道（形成通道，formation channels）的重要可观测量，但传统假设（动力学通道自旋各向同性、孤立通道自旋对齐）可能过于简化，需重新审视。

Method: 对动力学形成双黑洞，考虑星团中恒星自旋可能具有偏好对齐的观测结果，构建几何描述，得到第一代双黑洞极角的解析联合分布，其核心特征是两自旋-轨道倾角（spin-orbit tilt）之间的相关性；对孤立双黑洞，引入依赖首次超新星（supernova）爆炸期间natal kick的自旋演化模型，并考虑潮汐相互作用（tidal interactions）与质量比反转（mass-ratio reversal）。同时考察相对论自旋进动（relativistic spin precession）和星团有限逃逸速度（escape speed）的影响。

Result: 动力学形成情形下，两自旋-轨道倾角存在相关性；在合理假设且不依赖初始自旋构型时，单次黑洞并合后自旋完全随机化，意味着经由层级并合（hierarchical mergers）形成的双黑洞自旋方向呈各向同性。孤立双黑洞的自旋方向分布同样可完全解析表达。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个简洁的解析框架，用于在引力波（gravitational-wave）族群拟合（population fits）中建模并合黑洞的自旋方向。

Abstract: Spin directions are promising observables for distinguishing between the formation channels of merging stellar-mass binary black holes with gravitational-wave observations. In this work, we challenge the standard expectation that binaries formed via dynamical interactions in stellar clusters have isotropically distributed spins and that binaries formed in isolation have spins aligned with the orbital angular momentum. For dynamically formed binaries, we account for observational findings suggesting that the spins of stars in clusters may exhibit a preferred degree of alignment and construct a simple geometrical description of the resulting black-hole spin directions. This results in an analytical joint distribution for the polar angles of first-generation black-hole binaries, whose key feature is a correlation between the two spin-orbit tilts. We show that, under reasonable assumptions and independently of the initial spin configuration, spins are fully randomized after a single black-hole merger, implying that binaries formed through hierarchical mergers have isotropically distributed spin orientations. For isolated binaries, we introduce a simple model for spin evolution as a function of the natal kick imparted during the first supernova explosion, while accounting for tidal interactions and mass-ratio reversal. The resulting distribution is also fully analytic. We investigate the impact of relativistic spin precession, as well as that of the finite escape speed of clusters, on our geometrically derived distributions. Our results provide a simple analytical framework for modeling the spin directions of merging black holes in gravitational-wave population fits.

</details>


### [227] [Polarization Signatures of Sgr A* with Tilted Hybrid Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32127)
*Yushu Feng,Hao Yin,Songbai Chen,Jiliang Jing*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究利用半解析辐射低效吸积流（RIAF）模型与广义相对论偏振辐射转移模拟，探究倾斜外部磁场对Sgr A*偏振信号的影响，发现外部磁场倾角显著改变电场矢量位置角（EVPA）图样与亮度分布，并引入依赖观测方位角的非轴对称偏振形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞近视界区域的磁场几何对黑洞图像偏振特征具有重要塑造作用，因此需要厘清外部磁场倾角如何影响Sgr A*的偏振信号。

Method: 采用由轴对称分裂单极磁场（split-monopole field）与倾斜均匀外部磁场组成的混合磁场，结合稳态半解析辐射低效吸积流（RIAF）模型及广义相对论偏振辐射转移模拟展开研究。

Result: 外部磁场倾角显著改变电场矢量位置角（EVPA）图样与亮度分布；倾斜磁场分量引入的非轴对称性使偏振形态依赖观测方位角，此为纯轴对称磁场构型中不存在的特性。结合事件视界望远镜（EHT）数据，分析了Sgr A*周围存在此类混合磁场的可能性。

Conclusion: 倾斜外部磁场能够产生依赖观测方位角的偏振形态，代表一种新的行为特征；结合EHT数据可探讨Sgr A*周围混合磁场的可行性。

Abstract: The magnetic field geometry in the near-horizon region is very important in shaping polarimetric signatures of black hole images. With a hybrid magnetic field consisting of an axisymmetric split-monopole field and a tilted uniform external field, we investigate the effects of external magnetic field inclination on the polarimetric signatures of Sgr A* by employing a stationary semi-analytical radiatively inefficient accretion flow (RIAF) model and general relativistic polarized radiative transfer simulations. Our results show that the external magnetic field tilt significantly modifies the electric vector position angle (EVPA) pattern and brightness distribution. The induced non-axisymmetry from the tilted magnetic filed component renders polarization morphology dependent on the observer's azimuthal angle, which is a novel behavior absent in purely axisymmetric magnetic field configurations. In combination with Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) data, we analyse the possibility of such hybrid magnetic fields around Sgr A*.

</details>


### [228] [Spin wandering of an accreting neutron star near torque balance: implications for gravitational wave searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32164)
*Andrew Melatos*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于理想化 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 模型（Ornstein-Uhlenbeck model）从理论上推导了吸积中子星（accreting neutron star）引力波频率（gravitational wave frequency）$f_{\rm gw}$ 的涨落标准差与波应变幅、X 射线通量等可观测量的标度关系，并据此讨论该涨落对引力波搜索相干时间上限与半相干算法设计的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸积中子星因引力辐射反作用与磁离心吸积之间的力矩平衡而转速慢于离心解体极限，但吸积盘内的随机过程会使自转频率与引力波频率围绕平衡值随机游走。该涨落既可用于检验引力波探测候选体的物理自洽性，也直接影响搜索策略，因此需要定量刻画其统计规律。

Method: 采用理想化的 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 模型（Ornstein-Uhlenbeck model）对随机力矩驱动的频率游走进行理论分析，引入随机力矩的分数振幅（fractional amplitude）$\beta$ 与自相关时间尺度（autocorrelation time-scale）$T$，推导 $f_{\rm gw}$ 涨落标准差 $\sigma_{f_{\rm gw}}$ 的解析标度律。

Result: 理论结果显示 $\sigma_{f_{\rm gw}}(h_0) \propto f_{\rm gw} h_0 D \beta T^{1/2}$，等价形式为 $\sigma_{f_{\rm gw}}(F_{\rm X}) \propto f_{\rm gw}^{1/2} F_{\rm X}^{1/2} D \beta T^{1/2}$，其中 $h_0$ 为特征波应变（characteristic wave strain），$F_{\rm X}$ 为 X 射线通量，$D$ 为源距离。针对瑞利-泰勒不稳定性（Rayleigh-Taylor instability）与闪烁噪声（flicker noise），模型预测相干时间上限满足 $10^{-1} \lesssim \max(T_{\rm coh})/(1\,{\rm day}) \lesssim 10^3$，对应 $3\times10^{-2} \lesssim \beta T^{1/2}/(1\,{\rm s^{1/2}}) \lesssim 4$。

Conclusion: $\sigma_{f_{\rm gw}}(h_0)$ 与 $\sigma_{f_{\rm gw}}(F_{\rm X})$ 可用于将观测到的 $\sigma_{f_{\rm gw}}$ 与理论预测比对，从而检验引力波搜索中探测候选体的物理自洽性。由于预测的 $\max(T_{\rm coh})$ 通常短于现今音频段干涉仪（audio-band interferometer）长达一年的观测周期，故强调半相干搜索算法（semi-coherent search algorithm）的必要性与实用性，即将数据切分为若干时长为 $\max(T_{\rm coh})$ 的相干段。

Abstract: Torque balance between gravitational radiation reaction and magnetocentrifugal accretion is one reason why accreting neutron stars rotate slower than centrifugal break-up. Random processes in the accretion disk, such as Rayleigh-Taylor instabilities and flicker noise, drive the spin frequency $f_\ast$ and gravitational wave frequency $f_{\rm gw}\propto f_\ast$ to wander stochastically around their torque balance values. Here it is shown theoretically, in terms of an idealized Ornstein-Uhlenbeck model, that the standard deviation of $f_{\rm gw}$ is given by $σ_{f_{\rm gw}}(h_0) \propto f_{\rm gw} h_0 D βT^{1/2}$, or equivalently $σ_{f_{\rm gw}}(F_{\rm X}) \propto f_{\rm gw}^{1/2} F_{\rm X}^{1/2} D βT^{1/2}$, where $h_0$ is the characteristic wave strain, $F_{\rm X}$ is the X-ray flux, $D$ is the source distance, and $β$ and $T$ are the fractional amplitude and autocorrelation time-scale of the stochastic torque. One can use $σ_{f_{\rm gw}}(h_0)$ and $σ_{f_{\rm gw}}(F_{\rm X})$ to check the physical consistency of a detection candidate in a gravitational wave search by comparing with $σ_{f_{\rm gw}}$ observed. Spin wandering impacts search design, because it sets the maximum time interval $\max(T_{\rm coh})$, during which the signal may be treated as coherent. The Ornstein-Uhlenbeck calculation predicts $10^{-1} \lesssim \max(T_{\rm coh}) / (1\, {\rm day}) \lesssim 10^3$, with $3\times 10^{-2} \lesssim βT^{1/2} / (1 \, {\rm s^{1/2}} ) \lesssim 4$, for Rayleigh-Taylor instabilities and flicker noise. The predicted $\max(T_{\rm coh})$ is shorter typically than the year-long observation runs of audio-band interferometers operating today, emphasizing the utility of semi-coherent search algorithms, which divide the data into multiple coherent segments of duration $\max(T_{\rm coh})$.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Multilevel Plaquette-Space Sampling for Lattice Gauge Theories with Local Constraint Solves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35722)
*Ankur Singha,Jacob Finkenrath,Karl Jansen,Vipul Arora,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该工作提出多层级归一流（multilevel normalizing flow）构造，直接在格点规范理论的等边方块空间（plaquette space）采样，并精确满足 Bianchi 约束；在 U(1) 与 SU(2) 上验证其性能显著优于链空间（link space）基线，且优势在弱耦合区增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点规范理论（lattice gauge theory）的玻尔兹曼分布（Boltzmann distribution）采样依赖传统蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）方法，面临临界慢化（critical slowing down）与拓扑冻结（topological freezing）等限制。现有生成式采样器多在链空间构建，而链空间关联在弱耦合下变得长程，学习困难；等边方块空间作用量局部，表征更自然，但精确 Bianchi 约束将其限制在低维流形上，难以直接生成建模。

Method: 提出多层级归一流构造，直接在等边方块空间采样并精确满足 Bianchi 约束。核心是粗到细（coarse-to-fine）因子分解，将全局耦合的约束问题转化为一系列局部求解：每次细化时，每个被确定的等边方块至多依赖四个新生成变量，且约束问题规模与格点尺寸无关。

Result: 在二维与四维 U(1) 及二维 SU(2) 上验证了该构造。多层级等边方块空间采样器（multilevel Plaquette-Space Sampler, PSS）大幅优于链空间基线，且优势随耦合变弱而增大；该弱耦合区对生成式采样尤为困难，在渐近自由（asymptotically free）规范理论中与连续极限研究相关。

Conclusion: 直接在等边方块空间并精确满足 Bianchi 约束的多层级归一流构造，可有效缓解生成式采样在弱耦合长程关联下的困难，为格点规范理论的采样提供了优于链空间基线的方案，并具有推广至渐近自由理论连续极限研究的潜力。

Abstract: Lattice gauge theories are an important class of physical models with high-dimensional structured distributions that underpin first-principles calculations in particle, nuclear, and condensed-matter physics. Recent advances in generative modeling have opened new avenues for sampling Boltzmann distributions in lattice gauge theories, offering the potential to alleviate limitations of traditional Monte Carlo methods, including critical slowing down and topological freezing. However, generative samplers for lattice gauge theories are typically constructed in link space, where correlations become increasingly long-ranged toward weak coupling, posing a challenge for learning. Plaquettes provide a more natural representation, as the action is local in these variables. However, exact Bianchi constraints restrict them to a lower-dimensional manifold, complicating direct generative modeling. We introduce a multilevel normalizing-flow construction that samples directly in plaquette space while satisfying these constraints exactly. The key idea is a coarse-to fine factorization that transforms a globally coupled constraint problem into a sequence of local solves: at each refinement, every determined plaquette depends on at most four newly generated variables, while the size of the constraint problem remains independent of the lattice size. We validate the construction for $U(1)$ in two and four dimensions and for $SU(2)$ in two dimensions. Our multilevel Plaquette-Space Sampler (PSS) substantially outperforms link-space baselines, with the advantage increasing toward weak coupling, where the gauge coupling becomes small. This regime is particularly challenging for generative sampling and, in asymptotically free gauge theories, is relevant to continuum studies.

</details>


### [230] [Accurate Sampling from Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29902)
*Dénes Sexty*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 提出 DM-SMC（扩散模型-序贯蒙特卡洛，Diffusion Model - Sequential Monte Carlo）方案，用基于集合样本训练的扩散模型来采样由作用量定义的集合，并借助序贯蒙特卡洛保证采样精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是对以作用量（action）定义的集合（ensemble）进行采样，并克服扩散模型近似性及随机过程数值求解中有限步长（finite stepsize）带来的采样误差。

Method: 将扩散模型与序贯蒙特卡洛（Sequential Monte Carlo, SMC）结合，扩散模型在集合样本上训练，SMC 框架用于在扩散模型近似和有限步长条件下实现精确采样；同时研究了改进的更新策略（update strategies）。

Result: 在二维 $Z_2$ 对称标量场论（$Z_2$ symmetric scalar field theory）中、接近其二阶相变（2nd order phase transition）处给出了结果。

Conclusion: DM-SMC 能够在扩散模型近似和有限步长存在的情况下实现精确采样，并通过改进的更新策略进一步提升表现；具体验证限于二维 $Z_2$ 对称标量场论近二阶相变区域。

Abstract: A new proposal called DM-SMC (Diffusion Model - Sequential Monte Carlo) is investigated, which samples ensembles defined in terms of an action, using diffusion models trained on samples from the ensemble. The SMC setup allows for accurate sampling in spite of an approximate diffusion model and the finite stepsize used in the numerical solution of the stochastic process. Improved update strategies are also investigated. Results are presented for a $Z_2$ symmetric scalar field theory in 2 dimensions near its 2nd order phase transition.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Toroidal-Core Certificates for PPT-Squared Diagonal Orthogonal Covariant Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32979)
*Samuel Marquez Gonzalez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对正部分转置平方（PPT-squared）猜想在任意有限维对角正交共变（DOC）映射类的判定问题，本文给出一个充分条件：将量子相干数据分离为两个关联矩阵，混合至秩一关联矩阵凸包并转化为三分完全正（TCP）核，从而得到逐元素混合不等式，保证复合信道为纠缠破坏（EB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPT-squared 猜想追问两个正部分转置（PPT）量子信道之复合是否必为纠缠破坏（EB），该问题一般情形仍开放。在 DOC 类中，首个未解决的确定性维度为四维，因此需要针对任意有限维 DOC 映射建立可判定的充分条件。

Method: 证明将量子相干数据分离为两个关联矩阵（correlation matrices），分别与单位阵混合直至落入秩一关联矩阵的凸包（convex hull），再把所得对象转换为显式的三分完全正（triplewise completely positive, TCP）核。剩余部分是纯经典问题，只要满足一个简单的逐元素混合不等式即为 TCP。据此对 M_d 上的 PPT DOC 映射 Φ_{A,B,C} 与 Φ_{D,E,F} 给出逐元素界。

Result: 逐元素界 (AD)_{ij} ≥ 2⌊√d⌋√(A_{ii}A_{jj}D_{ii}D_{jj}) 对所有 i,j 成立时，可保证 Φ_{A,B,C}∘Φ_{D,E,F} 为 EB；在四维时普适系数变为四。进一步构造出一族连续的双随机 PPT 信道，其中凡 a≠1 的成员均非 EB，而族内任意两两复合均被证明为 EB。还给出秩敏感（rank-sensitive）的细化，表明低相干秩可改进普适系数。

Conclusion: 所得证书是充分而非必要的，与因子宽度（factor-width）及半定层级（semidefinite-hierarchy）方法互补；该结果在 DOC 类中为 PPT-squared 复合的纠缠破坏性提供了新的可验证判据。

Abstract: The positive-partial-transpose-squared (PPT-squared) conjecture asks whether the composition of two positive-partial-transpose (PPT) quantum channels must be entanglement breaking (EB). The problem remains open in general and, within the diagonal orthogonal covariant (DOC) class, the first unresolved deterministic dimension is four. A sufficient condition for PPT-squared composition is derived for DOC maps in arbitrary finite dimension. The proof isolates the quantum coherence data into two correlation matrices, mixes each with the identity until it lies in the convex hull of rank-one correlation matrices, and converts the resulting objects into an explicit triplewise completely positive (TCP) core. The remaining contribution is purely classical and is TCP whenever a simple entrywise mixing inequality is satisfied. For PPT DOC maps $Φ_{A,B,C}$ and $Φ_{D,E,F}$ on $M_d$, the entrywise bound $(AD)_{ij}\ge 2\lfloor\sqrt{d}\rfloor\sqrt{A_{ii}A_{jj}D_{ii}D_{jj}}$ for all $i,j$ guarantees that $Φ_{A,B,C}\circΦ_{D,E,F}$ is EB. In dimension four the universal coefficient becomes four. A continuous family of bistochastic PPT channels is then constructed; every member with $a\ne1$ is not EB, while every pairwise composition within the family is proved to be EB. A rank-sensitive refinement shows explicitly how low coherence rank can improve the universal coefficient. The resulting certificate is sufficient rather than necessary and is complementary to factor-width and semidefinite-hierarchy approaches.

</details>


### [232] [Nakayama's reduction of quantum topos and Bayesian quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34200)
*Atsuhide Mori*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要指出 Nakayama 通过拓扑选择统一了 Döring-Isham 拓扑量子理论（topos quantum theory）中的语境限制与概率定义，并将此统一解释为支持贝叶斯视角（Bayesian perspective）下概率随数据更新而变化，进而将这种变化刻画为预测理论适用性的局部移动，并应用于量子计算（quantum computing）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于处理 Döring-Isham 拓扑量子理论中语境限制（limit the contexts）与概率定义相分离的问题，寻求二者的统一表述。该统一有望为概率随数据获取而更新的贝叶斯立场提供理论支撑，并拓展至量子计算场景。

Method: 方法上，采用 Nakayama 提出的方案，将语境限制与概率定义归结为对拓扑（topology）的单一选择。随后把概率随数据获得而变化的过程，描述为预测理论（predictive theory）适用性的局部移动（local shift）。

Result: 结果表明，该统一表述支持贝叶斯视角，即概率随数据获取而更新；这种更新可被解释为预测理论适用范围的局部移动。该框架进一步被应用于量子计算。

Conclusion: 结论是，通过单一拓扑选择统一语境限制与概率定义，可为贝叶斯式的概率更新提供拓扑量子理论层面的解释，并具备在量子计算中应用的潜力。

Abstract: Nakayama found the way to limit the contexts in the Döring-Isham topos quantum theory and unified such limitation and the definition of probability as a single choice of topology. We explain how this unification supports the Bayesian perspective where the probability changes as data is obtained. We describe this change as a local shift of the applicability of a predictive theory and apply it to quantum computing.

</details>


### [233] [Dilation theorem for continuum quantum stochastic processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35403)
*Jonáš Fuksa,Clara Wassner,Jens Eisert,Gregory A. L. White*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在连续过程张量（continuous process tensor, cPT）框架下提出对应的膨胀（dilation）定理，证明所有 cPT 均可由系统与环境经有界哈密顿量（bounded Hamiltonian）耦合的显式闭合描述以任意精度逼近，并由此导出适用于连续区间的 Choi 对偶（Choi duality）与连续控制的严格处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将开放量子系统（open quantum systems）的数学形式与其底层物理联系起来需要膨胀（dilation）概念，用以在更大空间上桥接随机动力学与确定性薛定谔演化（Schrödinger evolution）。现有文献已对态、信道与量子梳（quantum combs）给出膨胀结果，但在连续区间上非马尔可夫动力学（non-Markovian dynamics）这一完全一般情形仍存在明显空白。

Method: 在配套工作中引入连续过程张量（cPT）框架，将此类过程表示为 L² 函数的多组分玻色福克空间（multi-species bosonic Fock spaces）中的向量，以容纳量子系统在区间上的全部多时统计量。本文据此构造相应的膨胀定理，给出系统与环境经有界哈密顿量耦合的显式闭合描述。

Result: 所有 cPT 均可被上述显式闭合描述以任意高的精度逼近。此类正则表示（regular representations）实际上构成框架的基础，定义了所有 cPT 中一个稠密且易于处理的子空间。

Conclusion: 该膨胀定理彰显了 cPT 框架的物理吸引力并证明其完备性；正则表示进一步导出适用于连续区间的 Choi 对偶（Choi duality），并使连续控制（continuum controls）获得严格处理。

Abstract: Connecting mathematical formalism in open quantum systems to its underlying physics necessitates the notion of a dilation, a way to bridge stochastic dynamics with deterministic Schrödinger evolution on a larger space. Although dilations are well known in the literature for states, channels, and quantum combs, there is a substantial gap when it comes to the fully general setting of non-Markovian dynamics on a continuous interval. In an accompanying work, we introduce a continuous process tensor (cPT) framework wherein these processes are represented by vectors in multi-species bosonic Fock spaces of $L^2$ functions. This accommodates all multi-time statistics of a quantum system on an interval. Here, we present a corresponding dilation theorem and show that all cPTs can be approximated arbitrarily well by an explicit closed description with system and environment coupled by a bounded Hamiltonian. As well as its physical appeal and demonstrating completeness of the framework, we show that such regular representations actually underpin the formalism, defining a dense subspace of all cPTs that is easy to handle. This leads to a continuum-suitable version of Choi duality, and permits the rigorous treatment of continuum controls.

</details>


### [234] [Nash Equilibrium from Quantum-Field Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33188)
*Hao Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将纠缠采集（entanglement harvesting）嵌入量子输入博弈（quantum-input game），证明真空场关联可改变稳定的策略结构。在微扰最低阶，平均收益正比于带符号纠缠余量，不同场关联区间对应不同的颤抖手完美均衡（trembling-hand perfect equilibria）数目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠采集可将量子场关联转移至局域探针，但此类关联能否改变稳定的策略行为此前尚不清楚。作者因此构建博弈框架以检验真空纠缠是否、以及如何影响均衡结构。

Method: 两名 Unruh-DeWitt 探测器在 (3+1) 维 Minkowski 时空中从自由无质量标量场采集真空纠缠；裁判用独立问题量子比特（question qubits）与相位校准的局域 Bell 测量将该态转换为收益。分析在最低微扰阶进行，并允许玩家选择退出或早/晚相互作用。

Result: 最低微扰阶下，平均收益正比于带符号纠缠余量（signed entanglement margin）√(ℋ²+𝒬²)−𝒫，其中 ℋ 为 Hadamard 关联贡献、𝒬 为 Pauli-Jordan 因果贡献、𝒫 为局域激发噪声。在收益非简并情形，均衡数为 1+2n₊，n₊ 为正收益计时均衡数；对某一固定协议，五个可解析验证的设定实现序列 7→3→1→7→1，分别对应类空间区域、第一对易子瓣、中心载波相消、第二瓣与波前之后的惠更斯（Huygens）区。

Conclusion: 在该固定量子输入验证协议内，不同的场关联区间会产生不同的稳定策略结构，表明纠缠采集所转移的真空关联足以重塑博弈均衡。

Abstract: Entanglement harvesting transfers quantum field correlations to localized probes, yet whether these correlations can alter stable strategic behavior has remained unclear. We formulate a quantum-input game in which two Unruh-DeWitt detectors harvest vacuum entanglement from a free massless scalar field in $(3+1)$-dimensional Minkowski spacetime. A referee converts this state into payoffs using independent question qubits and phase-calibrated local Bell measurements. To leading perturbative order, the mean reward is proportional to the signed entanglement margin $\sqrt{\mathcal{H}^2+\mathcal{Q}^2}-\mathcal{P}$, where $\mathcal{H}$ is the Hadamard correlation contribution, $\mathcal{Q}$ is the Pauli-Jordan causal contribution, and $\mathcal{P}$ is the local excitation noise. Away from payoff degeneracies, allowing each player to exit or to choose an early or late interaction yields $1+2n_+$ trembling-hand perfect equilibria, where $n_+$ counts the positive-reward timing equilibria. For one fixed protocol, five analytically certified settings realize the sequence $7\to3\to1\to7\to1$. They represent the spacelike region, the first commutator lobe, central carrier cancellation, the second lobe, and the post-wavefront Huygens regime. Thus, within this fixed quantum-input verification protocol, distinct field-correlation regimes generate different stable strategic structures.

</details>


### [235] [Local Many Worlds in Spacetime: Deutsch-Hayden Descriptors and Local Wavefunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33453)
*Mordecai Waegell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并论证一种“局域多世界”（local many worlds）量子力学形式，它在时空（spacetime）中是可分离的物理理论，且具有洛伦兹协变（Lorentz covariant）因果结构；文章核心目的是证明任意 N 量子比特（N-qubit）系统的局域波函数可始终由 Deutsch-Hayden（DH）描述符直接构造，从而结合局域薛定谔绘景与局域海森堡绘景的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔定理（Bell's theorem）表明，一个局域且空间可分离、不含超决定论（superdeterminism）或逆因果（retrocausality）的物理理论必然是多局域世界的理论。Everett 多世界诠释依赖构型空间（configuration space）中不可分离的通用波函数，与此不同，局域多世界形式需要一种透明的可分离描述。然而，局域海森堡绘景中的 DH 描述符虽紧凑却晦涩，导致该绘景难以理解，因此有必要打通两种绘景之间的构造关系。

Method: 采用局域薛定谔绘景（local Schrödinger picture）与局域海森堡绘景（local Heisenberg picture）的对照分析。前者为时空中每个事件赋予自身局域波函数，仅由过去光锥（past light cone）内的局域波函数与局域幺正演化算符（local unitary evolution operators）构建，与类空间隔的局域波函数完全断开；后者为每个个体系统赋予 Deutsch-Hayden（DH）描述符，其在局域幺正操作下演化。文章通过构造性论证，说明可由 DH 描述符直接构建任意 N 量子比特系统的局域波函数。

Result: 两种绘景中的局域波函数与 DH 描述符均记录了过去相互作用的信息：局域波函数提供清晰直观的物理叙述，而描述符以更紧凑的形式承载相同信息。文章证明，任意 N 量子比特系统的局域波函数总可由 DH 描述符直接构造。

Conclusion: 通过由 DH 描述符直接构造局域波函数，可同时利用局域薛定谔绘景的直观性与局域海森堡绘景的紧凑性，从而缓解后者因形式晦涩而难以使用的问题，为局域多世界形式提供更易操作的统一描述。

Abstract: Bell's theorem establishes that a local and spatially separable physical theory with no superdeterminism or retrocausality must be a theory of many local worlds. The local many worlds formalism of quantum mechanics is a transparent separable physical theory in spacetime with a Lorentz covariant causal structure. Unlike the Everett many worlds interpretation, there is no nonseparable universal wavefunction in configuration space, but like Everett, there is only local unitary evolution which conserves probability (fluid) current, and no wavefunction collapse. Each event in spacetime has its own local wavefunction which is built up using only the local wavefunctions and local unitary evolution operators within its past light cone, and which is entirely disconnected from spacelike separated local wavefunctions. We call this the local Schrödinger picture. Local many worlds can also be described in the Heisenberg picture by ascribing Deutsch-Hayden (DH) descriptors to each individual system, each of which evolves under local unitary operations. Both the local wavefunctions and DH descriptors contain records of past interactions, and while the local wavefunctions provide a clear and intuitive physical narrative, the descriptors contain all of the same information in a much more compact form. Unfortunately, the compact form is also somewhat cryptic, which has made the local Heisenberg picture relatively inaccessible. The purpose of this article is to show that the local wavefunctions for any $N$-qubit system can always be constructed directly from the DH descriptors, allowing us to combine the benefits of both pictures.

</details>


### [236] [Probing the classical complexity of quantum dynamics experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31830)
*Thomas Schuster,Andreas Elben*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一种新的复杂度度量“反应性（reactivity）”，用于刻画量子实验整体的经典可模拟性，并给出可高效测量反应性的实验协议。低反应性量子实验在模拟、学习与快速推进上均表现简单；高反应性实验或可规避这些经典攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，即使纠缠（entanglement）与魔力（magic）等传统复杂度度量很高，许多量子线路与动力学仍可被追踪局部信息的经典算法高效模拟。这揭示了既有复杂度度量未能捕捉新的经典攻击方式，需要引入新的度量来刻画这一现象。

Method: 引入“反应性（reactivity）”作为复杂度度量，其不同于传统度量，不刻画孤立量子态或算符的性质，而是刻画量子实验（quantum experiment）整体。同时提出名为“泡利路径谱（Pauli path spectroscopy）”的可便捷实现实验协议，用于高效测量任意目标量子实验的反应性。

Result: 数值与严格证据表明，低反应性的量子实验在多种度量意义上均是简单的：可被高效经典模拟、学习和快速推进（fast-forward）。此外，所提出的泡利路径谱协议在实验本身已超出经典模拟能力时仍适用。

Conclusion: 反应性为理解量子实验的经典可模拟性提供了新视角，其低值对应一系列简单特征，进而激励寻找可规避这些特征的高反应性量子实验。

Abstract: A confluence of recent works has shown that many quantum circuits and dynamics are efficiently simulable by classical algorithms that track local information, even when conventional complexity measures such as the entanglement and magic are high. Here, we introduce a novel measure of complexity, the reactivity, to capture this new method of classical attack. Unlike conventional complexity measures, the reactivity does not capture a property of a quantum state or operator in isolation, but rather a quantum experiment as a whole. We provide numerical and rigorous evidence that quantum experiments with low reactivity are simple by a host of measures: they are efficient to classically simulate, learn, and fast-forward. This motivates the search for quantum experiments with high reactivity, which may evade these simplistic features. To this end, we introduce easily implementable experimental protocols---dubbed Pauli path spectroscopy---that allow one to efficiently measure the reactivity of any quantum experiment of interest. Our protocols are applicable even when the experiment itself is beyond the reach of classical simulation.

</details>


### [237] [Quantum error mitigation from information dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31934)
*Philippe Suchsland,Thomas Schuster,Manuel S. Rudolph,Nicholas Noll,Thomas E. O'Brien,Zlatko K. Minev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出以“反应性函数（reactivity function）”这一紧凑表示来刻画量子电路的信息动力学，并据此发展两种误差缓解方法，在保持高精度的同时显著降低采样成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子科学中实验误差的克服是核心挑战，在大型复杂量子电路中尤为严重：量子信息扩散至指数多的路径，使直接理解误差影响变得不可行。因此需要新的表示与分析框架来理解并缓解误差。

Method: 基于量子电路信息动力学的紧凑表示——反应性函数，其形状决定电路的噪声响应及对各类误差缓解方法的适用性。由此提出两种互补方法：泡利路径零噪声外推（Pauli-path zero-noise extrapolation），对反应性函数拟合参数化模型以实现向零噪声的精确外推；以及可调误差消除（tunable error cancellation），对反应性函数进行滤波以消除阈值局域性（locality）以内全部信息的噪声。两法均含可调参数，可系统性提升精度直至收敛。

Result: 在来自已发表实验的三个电路族上，方法克服了传统零噪声外推（zero-noise extrapolation）高达30%的偏差；在约10^{-2}的均方根误差（root-mean-square error）下，相较概率误差消除（probabilistic error cancellation），采样成本最多降低10^3倍。

Conclusion: 反应性函数为理解电路噪声响应与误差缓解方法适用性提供了统一视角，据此构建的两种方法具备可收敛的调参机制，并在真实实验电路上实现了精度与采样成本的显著改进。

Abstract: Overcoming experimental errors is a central challenge in quantum science. This challenge is particularly acute in large and complex quantum circuits, where quantum information spreads into exponentially many paths, foiling any direct attempt to understand errors' impact. In this work, we present a novel approach to understanding and mitigating experimental errors, based on a surprisingly compact representation of a quantum circuit's information dynamics, the reactivity function. The shape of a circuit's reactivity function governs its noise response, and its amenability to different error mitigation methods. From this viewpoint, we introduce two complementary such methods. The first, Pauli-path zero-noise extrapolation, fits a parameterized model to a circuit's reactivity function and uses it to accurately extrapolate to zero noise. The second, tunable error cancellation, filters a circuit's reactivity function to cancel noise on all information up to a threshold locality. Crucially, both methods feature a tuning parameter that allows one to systematically improve their accuracy until convergence. Across three circuit families from published experiments, our methods overcome conventional zero-noise extrapolation biases as large as 30%; at $\sim$$10^{-2}$ root-mean-square error, they reduce sampling cost relative to probabilistic error cancellation by up to a factor of $10^3$.

</details>


### [238] [Correlation measure for statistical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34859)
*V. I. Yukalov,E. P. Yukalova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种用于统计系统的关联度量，称为关联指数（correlation index），适用于量子与经典、平衡与非平衡统计系统，并通过密度算符与约化密度矩阵的实例展示其计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计系统需要一个同时适用于量子与经典、平衡与非平衡情形的通用关联度量。该文旨在提出此类具普适性的关联指数，以刻画任意迹类算符（trace-class operators）的关联程度。

Method: 首先对任意迹类算符给出关联指数的一般定义，随后以若干密度算符与约化密度矩阵为例进行计算说明。平衡系统的计算例示于含玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensate）的玻色子系统、超导系统与自旋系统；非平衡系统的关联指数则针对自旋与赝自旋系统的非线性动力学进行考察。

Result: 关联指数的一般形式得以建立，并成功应用于多类密度算符与约化密度矩阵。计算示例覆盖平衡的玻色子系统（含玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚）、超导系统、自旋系统，以及非平衡的自旋与赝自旋非线性动力学系统。

Conclusion: 所提出的关联指数是一般性关联度量，可统一适用于量子与经典、平衡与非平衡统计系统，并在多种物理系统中得到具体计算与应用。

Abstract: A measure of correlations for statistical systems is advanced, called correlation index. The measure is general, being applicable to quantum and classical, equilibrium and nonequilibrium statistical systems. After formulating the general notion of correlation indices for arbitrary trace-class operators, the calculation of the correlation indices are illustrated by several examples of density operators and reduced density matrices. The computation of the correlation indices for equilibrium systems is exemplified for bosonic systems with the Bose-Einstein condensate, superconducting systems, and for spin systems. Correlation indices for nonequilibrium systems are considered for nonlinear dynamics of spin and pseudospin systems.

</details>


### [239] [Improving the Stability of the Hierarchical Equations of Motion for Open Quantum Systems with Strong Coupling to Structured Bosonic Baths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35484)
*Salvatore Gatto,Samuel L. Rudge,Bokang Hou,Eran Rabani,Michael Thoss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作针对玻色环境中层级运动方程（HEOM）因有限截断导致的数值不稳定性，借助辅助Fock空间图像识别失衡的升层项，并通过非幺正相似变换将其转换为升层与降层的平衡组合，从而显著扩展了可模拟的系统-浴耦合强度范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HEOM是模拟开放量子系统动力学的有力方法，且在诸多参数区间与物理情形中表现稳定高效；但玻色环境下对层级不可避免的有限截断（finite truncation）会引发根本性的数值不稳定性，这一问题尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 采用层级（hierarchy）的辅助Fock空间（auxiliary Fock-space）图像，首先识别导致强瞬态放大（transient amplification）的失衡升层项（hierarchy-raising terms），并指出其与非正规代数结构（non-normal algebraic structure）的关联；随后施加非幺正相似变换（non-unitary similarity transformation），将该类项改写为升层与降层项的平衡组合以抑制其数值影响。

Result: 该变换后的HEOM方法首先在具有简单布朗振子谱密度（Brownian oscillator spectral density）的自旋-玻色模型（spin-boson model）中得到验证，随后推广至结构化谱密度（structured spectral density）；两种情形均显示，相比未变换方法，可模拟显著更大的系统-浴耦合强度。

Conclusion: 通过辅助Fock空间分析与非幺正相似变换，可有效抑制玻色HEOM中由失衡升层项引起的截断不稳定性，从而拓展HEOM方法在强耦合区间的适用性。

Abstract: The hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) approach is one of the most powerful methods avail- able to simulate the dynamics of open quantum systems. Although the HEOM approach has been shown to be stable and efficient in many parameter regimes and physical scenarios, it is also well known that the unavoidable finite truncation of the hierarchy can lead to fundamental numeri- cal instabilities for bosonic environments. In this work, we apply recently developed techniques to analyze these issues. We use an auxiliary Fock-space picture of the hierarchy to first identify the unbalanced hierarchy-raising terms responsible for the strong transient amplification associated with the non-normal algebraic structure of the HEOM generator. We then apply a non-unitary similarity transformation that converts these terms into balanced combinations of hierarchy-raising and hierarchy-lowering terms, thereby suppressing their numerical impact. We first demonstrate the efficacy of this approach via a spin-boson model with a simple Brownian oscillator spectral den- sity and subsequently extend the analysis to a structured spectral density, showing for both cases that the transformed HEOM approach allows simulations of much larger system-bath coupling than would otherwise be possible.

</details>


### [240] [Electron decoherence in cylindrical holes and circular apertures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35498)
*Cruz I. Velasco,F. Javier García de Abajo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了快电子在两种典型几何结构中退相干（decoherence）的定量理论：金属中圆柱孔与薄理想导体膜圆孔，结合电磁格林张量（Green-tensor）与涨落-耗散定理（fluctuation--dissipation theorem）并保留推迟效应，给出退相干概率与弹性相位的解析及数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由电子的相干性（coherence）决定了相位敏感电子显微术（phase-sensitive electron microscopy）的分辨率与对比度，而电子在环境中留下可区分激发时相干性即丧失，因此需对快电子退相干进行定量刻画。
