Method: 采用电磁格林张量（Green-tensor）表述结合涨落-耗散定理（fluctuation--dissipation theorem），完整保留推迟（retardation）效应；圆柱孔情形求得闭式、方位分辨（azimuthally resolved）表达式，圆孔情形采用柱面波模式展开（cylindrical-wave modal expansion）求解。

Result: 结果将退相干概率与弹性相位表示为电子轨迹、温度和材料响应的函数。圆柱孔在高电导率与高温极限下化为与电导率和电子速度无关、随温度线性变化的普适表达式；圆孔的能量损失概率在低频按 1/ω 发散，而退相干概率保持有限并在孔径与相关电磁波长可比时达到最大。孔径诱导退相干一般弱于圆柱孔，除非高温下孔径足够大。

Conclusion: 对于足够大的孔，退相干可主导聚焦电子探针的空间展宽（spatial broadening），因此必须纳入相干电子束仪器（coherent electron-beam instruments）的设计考量。

Abstract: The coherence of free electrons sets fundamental limits on the resolution and contrast of phase-sensitive electron microscopy because coherence is lost whenever the electron leaves a distinguishing excitation in its environment. We develop a quantitative theory of fast-electron decoherence in two canonical geometries: a cylindrical hole drilled through a realistic metal and a circular aperture in a thin perfectly conducting film. By combining an electromagnetic Green-tensor formulation with the fluctuation--dissipation theorem while fully retaining retardation, we obtain the decoherence probability and elastic phase as functions of the electron trajectory, temperature, and material response. For the cylindrical hole, we obtain a closed-form, azimuthally resolved expression that separates the dependences on path position, temperature, and conductivity. In the high-conductivity and high-temperature limits, this result reduces to a universal expression that is linear in temperature and independent of both conductivity and electron velocity. For the circular aperture, which we solve using a cylindrical-wave modal expansion, the energy-loss probability diverges as $1/ω$ at low frequency, whereas the decoherence probability remains finite and reaches a maximum when the aperture radius is comparable to the relevant electromagnetic wavelength. Because the interaction is spatially localized, aperture-induced decoherence is generally weaker than that produced by a cylindrical hole, except for sufficiently large apertures at high temperature. Finally, we show that, for sufficiently large holes, decoherence can dominate the spatial broadening of a focused electron probe and must therefore be incorporated into the design of coherent electron-beam instruments.

</details>


### [241] [Optimal performance in a chaotic Floquet quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35655)
*Lakshmija N K,Uma Divakaran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由 N 个均匀相互作用量子比特（qubit）构成的量子电池（quantum battery），通过在横向施加周期性踢（periodic kicks）并在相互作用方向引入无序（disorder）进行充电。无序使系统偏离置换对称（permutation symmetry）子空间，从而提升储能；大无序极限下可利用随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory, RMT）精确计算储能，且能量涨落趋于可忽略，能效接近 1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池的优化运行需要同时实现高储能与低能量涨落，并保证能量可高效提取。该研究旨在探究无序与周期性驱动在充电与存储过程中的相互作用，以寻找有利于电池性能的机制。

Method: 系统为 N 个均匀相互作用的量子比特，初态取基态（ground state）。充电阶段在横向施加周期性踢，同时在相互作用方向引入无序；存储阶段则考虑存在退相位噪声（dephasing noise）的情形。大无序极限下采用随机矩阵理论进行解析计算。

Result: 无序使充电动力学离开置换对称子空间，相比于无无序情形存储更多能量；大无序极限下出现混沌（chaotic）充电相，储能可由随机矩阵理论精确给出。能量涨落随无序增大而减小并趋于可忽略，而此时储能最大。在存储阶段，较大无序强度下遍历性功（ergotropy）接近储能，表明能量近乎完全可提取。

Conclusion: 无序与周期性踢的协同作用可提升量子电池储能、抑制能量涨落，并在大无序下使提取效率接近 1，表明该机制有利于电池的最优运行。

Abstract: We study a quantum battery consisting of $N$ qubits interacting uniformly with each other. Starting from its ground state, the battery is charged by introducing periodic kicks in the transverse direction along with adding disorder in the interaction direction. Although the battery Hamiltonian along with the initial state preserves the permutation symmetry, the dynamics in presence of disorder during the charging phase takes the system out of the permutation symmetric subspace, resulting to a larger amount of stored energy as compared to that without disorder. In the limit of large disorder, this interplay brings in a possibility of a chaotic charging phase when stored energy can be exactly calculated using random matrix theory. More importantly, we also find that the energy fluctuations decrease and become negligible in the limit of large disorder, which is also when the energy stored is maximum, a condition highly desirable for the optimum functioning of the battery. We complete the battery cycle by considering the storage phase in the presence of dephasing noise. We find that for larger disorder strengths, the ergotropy remains close to the stored energy, indicating nearly complete energy extractability with an efficiency approaching unity.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [242] [Uncertainty Quantification for the Fission Matrix Method: A Rigorous Mathematical Framework and Computationally Efficient Alternatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33161)
*Valerio Mascolino*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出裂变矩阵（fission matrix, FM）方法中蒙特卡洛统计不确定度传播的全协方差 Delta 方法框架，并结合对角协方差近似与系数重采样两种低成本替代方案，在 JSI TRIGA Mark-II 反应堆 OpenMC 模型上评估三者与经验基准的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FM 方法由预计算的蒙特卡洛系数数据库支持快速插值计算，但底层蒙特卡洛统计不确定度向 FM 特征值与特征向量的传播罕有严格处理：既有方法假设系数相互独立，却未针对其实际协方差进行检验。

Method: 推导用于向 keff 与裂变源传播不确定度的全协方差 Delta 方法框架，并以其为参照评估两种更廉价的替代方案——对角协方差近似与 FM 系数直接重采样；验证采用 JSI TRIGA Mark-II 反应堆的 OpenMC 模型，并以 100 次独立蒙特卡洛运行作为经验基准。

Result: 在全棒抽出工况下，三种估计量与经验基准在 keff 上吻合至 5.7% 以内、裂变源在 1% 以内；对角近似的偏差符号在两个统计水平间发生反转，需逐例核查。采用全局固定源 FM 生成策略时，对角、重采样与独立运行估计量吻合约 2%，且较全协方差估计低约 10%，该差距归因于协方差采样噪声；大非对角协方差近似对称且在传播中大部分相互抵消。对 FM 控制棒插值与组合策略（FM-CRd），keff 与精确扰动参考值吻合在 60 pcm（约两个标准差）内，裂变源在 0.7% 内，而对角与重采样估计量彼此接近且保守约两倍。

Conclusion: 鉴于全协方差的内存开销过高，建议将对角 Delta 方法作为实用默认选择，全协方差则保留用于系统的一次性表征。

Abstract: The fission matrix (FM) method recasts neutron transport in matrix form, enabling fast, interpolation-based reactor calculations from a pre-computed database of Monte Carlo-derived coefficients. Propagation of the underlying Monte Carlo statistical uncertainty to the FM eigenvalue and eigenvector is rarely rigorous: prior approaches assume independent coefficients without testing this against their actual covariance. This work derives a full-covariance Delta-method framework for propagating uncertainty to keff and the fission source, and evaluates it with two cheaper alternatives, a diagonal-covariance approximation and direct resampling of the FM coefficients, using an OpenMC model of the JSI TRIGA Mark-II reactor. For the all-rods-out case, these three estimators and an empirical benchmark of 100 independent Monte Carlo runs agree within 5.7% for keff and 1% for the fission source. The sign of the diagonal approximation's bias flips between the two statistics levels, so it must be checked case by case. With a global fixed-source FM generation strategy, the diagonal, resampling, and independent-run estimators agree within about 2% and lie roughly 10% below the full-covariance estimate, a gap attributed to covariance sampling noise. The large off-diagonal covariance is nearly symmetric and mostly cancels when propagated, rather than being small or negative as previously assumed. For the FM control-rod interpolation and combination strategy (FM-CRd), keff matches an exact perturbed reference within 60 pcm (about two standard deviations) and the fission source within 0.7%, while the diagonal and resampling estimators agree closely and are conservative by about a factor of two. Given this and the prohibitive memory cost of the full covariance, the diagonal Delta method is recommended as the practical default, with the full covariance reserved for one-time characterization of a system.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [Cross-Material Support Transfer for Core-Loss Prediction Under Waveform Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31659)
*Cong Yao,Chunye Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出 MagNet Challenge 中材料 D 梯形波（trapezoidal）样本的误差瓶颈源于协变量偏移（covariate shift）下的信息缺失，而非类别不平衡（class imbalance），并提出 MIST 方法借用同类材料信息，将材料 D 的 p95 相对误差从 20.39% 降至 12.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功率磁性材料（power magnetic materials）的表征多基于正弦与三角波形，而实际变换器中的磁通轨迹为梯形与 PWM 波形，导致损耗模型必须在训练数据最稀疏的区域进行预测。MagNet Challenge 终测刻意放大了这一表征—部署失配：材料 D 的测试集中梯形波占 16.4%，训练集仅占 1.4%，最优提交的 p95 误差达 15.9%，为五种材料中最差。

Method: 先以受控实验否证不平衡解读：四种标准补救措施均失效，且将梯形波占比提升至测试集水平反而使精度下降。随后提出材料身份支撑迁移（material-identity support transfer, MIST），在五种挑战材料上联合训练单一 2784 参数预测器；材料身份通过特征级线性调制（feature-wise linear modulation, FiLM）注入，对稀缺材料的真实标签损失（true-label loss）重新加权，且材料 D 不进行微调（fine-tuning），以避免其无梯形波训练集的偏差被重新引入。

Result: MIST 将材料 D 的五随机种子 p95 从 20.39±2.03% 降至 12.38±0.92%，梯形波类别的 p95 从 37.4±8.8% 降至 15.16±1.69%，以最优提交约六分之一的参数量且无需微调阶段即实现超越；在同等容量下移除材料身份信息会使误差膨胀一个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果表明稀缺材料的建模应与同类材料联合表征，其缺失的支撑可由兄弟材料借用而非外推获得。

Abstract: Power magnetic materials are characterized on the sinusoidal and triangular waveforms that excitation hardware conveniently produces, whereas deployed converters expose cores to trapezoidal, PWM-shaped flux trajectories, so loss models must predict exactly where their training data are thinnest. The final test of the MagNet Challenge embeds a deliberately extreme instance of this characterization-deployment mismatch: for material D, trapezoids form 16.4% of the test set but only 1.4% of the training set. The 95th-percentile relative error, hereafter p95, of the best submission, built on sequential transfer learning, stalled at 15.9%, the worst among the five materials. This paper shows that the obstacle is missing information under covariate shift rather than class imbalance, and that the missing support can be borrowed from sibling materials instead of being extrapolated. Controlled experiments first refute the imbalance reading: four standard remedies fail, and raising the trapezoidal share to the test-set level degrades accuracy further. The proposed material-identity support transfer, MIST, then trains one 2784-parameter predictor jointly on all five challenge materials. Material identity enters through feature-wise linear modulation, or FiLM, the scarce material's true-label loss is reweighted, and material D receives no fine-tuning, so that the bias of its trapezoid-free training set is never re-installed. MIST lowers the five-seed material-D p95 from 20.39+/-2.03% to 12.38+/-0.92% and the trapezoidal-class p95 from 37.4+/-8.8% to 15.16+/-1.69%, surpassing the best submission with one-sixth of its parameters and no fine-tuning stage; removing material identity at matched capacity inflates the error by an order of magnitude. These results argue that scarce materials should be characterized jointly with their siblings.

</details>


### [244] [Phase Space Attention:A Hairer Lift Circumvents the Single-Layer Induction Obstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32319)
*Kingsuk Maitra,Shagun Sood Morteza Hosseini,Suman Gunnala,Vikram Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在 post-RoPE 基底上引入辛相空间（symplectic phase space）提升，将注意力置于辛结构中以绕开 Sanford-Hsu-Telgarsky（SHT）单层归纳障碍，并证明该提升在分解子类中唯一、算子层面严格辛且保持可部署性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHT 单层归纳计数论给出单层注意力无法实现归纳能力的障碍（obstruction）。本文意在通过变换设计类的前提，而非修改该界本身，来规避这一限制。

Method: 在线性、单步、因果、双线性（bilinear）、辛一致的（symplectically consistent）设计类内，将注意力提升至辛相空间，类比 Hairer 对 Stormer-Verlet 的提升；对 post-RoPE 的 query 与 key 流施加辛上剪切（symplectic upper shear）M_γ:(q,p)↦(q+γp,p)，并在仅含 T_4 的高斯约化下推导闭式两分支归纳相变。

Result: 该提升在分解子类中唯一、算子层面严格辛、需 post-RoPE 放置；闭式两分支相变在 r=0.9876 上留出验证且零拟合参数，但该律仅是可解极限：恢复 T_3 通道后 d_k=64 时 γ_c 由 1.030 降至 0.569 且交叉消失。可部署性上 KV 缓存不变、前缀复用与投机解码保留、开销为每 token 每层 6d FLOPs、INT8 余量至多增加 log_2(1+2γ) 比特、融合核无需修改且不增参数。91.3M 参数下超临界扫描定位涌现带：γ=0.80 时 3/3 随机种子在平均 717 步形成归纳，γ=0 时为 2/3 种子与 2700 步。负面结果亦如实报告：仅 key 半提升达 0.949，优于对称算子的 0.811。

Conclusion: 辛提升可在此受限设计类中绕过 SHT 障碍并保持部署友好，但相变律的稳健性有限，且若以归纳准确率为目标，key-only 半提升是更优构造；41 个 notebook 与结果文件作为附属材料提供。

Abstract: We circumvent the Sanford-Hsu-Telgarsky (SHT) single-layer induction obstruction within a linear, one-step, causal, bilinear, symplectically consistent design class on the post-RoPE substrate, by lifting attention onto a symplectic phase space, mirroring Hairer's lift of Stormer-Verlet. The lift exits the premise of the SHT counting argument rather than the bound itself. The reframing exhibits the obstruction as a filter-order gap: a one-layer bilinear score realises a $z$-transform of joint order $(0,0)$, whereas the induction discriminator requires key-side order $\geq 1$. Applying the symplectic upper shear $M_γ:(q,p)\mapsto(q+γp,p)$ to the post-RoPE query and key streams closes it. We prove this lift is unique within the factorised subclass, exactly symplectic at operator level, and requires post-RoPE placement; and in an explicit $T_4$-only Gaussian reduction we derive a closed-form two-branch induction phase transition, held out at $r=0.9876$ with zero fitted parameters. That law is an analytically solvable limit, not a robust prediction: restoring the $T_3$ channel moves $γ_c$ at $d_k=64$ from 1.030 to 0.569 and removes the crossover.
  Deployability follows by exact derivation: the KV cache is unchanged, prefix reuse and speculative decoding are preserved, overhead is $6d$ FLOPs per token per layer, INT8 headroom grows by at most $\log_2(1+2γ)$ bits, fused kernels are unmodified, and no parameters are added.
  At 91.3M parameters a supercritical sweep locates an emergence band: induction forms 3/3 seeds at $γ=0.80$ in a mean of 717 steps, against 2/3 seeds and 2700 steps at $γ=0$. Adverse results are reported as directly: a key-only half-lift reaches 0.949 against 0.811 for the symmetric operator, so if induction accuracy is the objective, the half-lift is the better construction. Forty-one notebooks and result files ship as ancillary material.

</details>


### [245] [FLARE: Flow Matching with Local Axis-Angle Representations for Stochastic Micromagnetic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34070)
*Pengyu Li,Renjie Tong,Xuanlue Jiang,Jianmin Li,Yuanyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLARE：一种将随机有限时间磁化预测重述为锚点相对局部轴角旋转条件输运的流匹配（flow matching）框架，可直接生成跨多个目标时刻的全场随机端点，避免逐步积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulation）成本高昂，因为传统与学习型求解器通常逐步传播 Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert（LLG）动力学。已有学习型方法多保留逐步积分或仅建模确定性演化，全场、直接时程的随机预测尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出 FLARE，把随机有限时间磁化预测重构为锚点相对局部轴角旋转（anchor-relative local axis-angle rotations）上的条件输运问题；该旋转空间表述契合磁化动力学的内在几何，并天然保持逐点单位范数。模型显式以物理预测时程为条件，无需逐步积分即可直接生成多个目标时刻的全场随机端点。

Result: 相对各指标上最强的单检查点外部基线，FLARE 的角度能量距离（angular energy distance）降低 29.9%（15.30°），公平能量分数（fair energy score）降低 37.3%（0.393）。在代表性组合 5-ns 两段协议上，单张 GPU 上相较广泛使用的 GPU 微磁求解器 MuMax³ 取得 3,062× 的最佳批次加速。

Conclusion: FLARE 表明，基于旋转空间的条件流匹配可实现直接时程的随机全场磁化预测，在精度与计算效率上均优于现有基线，为长时程微磁模拟提供了可扩展的新路径。

Abstract: Long-horizon micromagnetic simulation remains expensive because conventional and learned solvers typically propagate Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert (LLG) dynamics step by step. Existing learned approaches generally retain stepwise integration or model deterministic evolution, leaving full-field, direct-horizon stochastic prediction largely unexplored. We propose FLARE, a flow-matching framework that recasts stochastic finite-time magnetization prediction as conditional transport over anchor-relative local axis-angle rotations. This rotation-space formulation respects the intrinsic geometry of magnetization dynamics and preserves pointwise unit norm by construction. By explicitly conditioning on the physical prediction horizon, FLARE directly generates full-field stochastic endpoints across multiple target times without stepwise integration. Against the strongest single-checkpoint external baseline on each metric, FLARE achieves 29.9% lower angular energy distance ($15.30^\circ$), and a 37.3% lower fair energy score (0.393). On a representative composed 5-ns two-segment protocol, FLARE achieves a $3{,}062\times$ best-batch speedup over the widely used GPU micromagnetic solver MuMax$^3$ on a single GPU.

</details>


### [246] [Weighting Schedules Govern What and When Score-Based Generative Models Learn from Multimodal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35322)
*Jérémie Klinger,Raphaël Urfin,Giulio Biroli,Marylou Gabrié*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在高维设定下将基于得分（score-based）生成模型的积分训练损失按时间分解，证明信噪比 $\\Lambda(t)$ 决定多模态目标各特征的学习速率；高 $\\Lambda(t)$ 时模态方向同时习得而相对权重完全未学，仅在物种形成时间（speciation time）附近全部特征才可学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于得分的生成模型在反向动力学中，多模态分布的轨迹会在狭窄时间窗口内"定型"到某个模态，即物种形成时间。然而时间积分损失中权重调度 $w(t)$ 如何影响各模态特征（方向与相对权重）的学习动力学尚缺乏定量理解，尤其在真实高维数据下。

Method: 将积分损失拆解为各单时刻贡献，并在固定信噪比 $\\Lambda(t)$ 下逐项分析；基于非平衡与层级高斯混合（unbalanced and hierarchical Gaussian mixtures）训练动力学的精确高维分析，推导各特征的学习时间尺度。

Result: 高 $\\Lambda(t)$ 时所有模态方向在单一时间尺度上同时被习得、且与幅度无关，而相对权重完全不被学习；仅当 $\\Lambda(t)$ 接近 1（即物种形成时间附近）时，方向按其相对幅度决定的速率学习，权重与方向共同被习得。对时间积分目标，学习动力学由权重在物种形成时间附近的集中程度主导。图像与人类基因组单倍型生成的数值实验验证了该时间尺度层级。

Conclusion: 研究结果为 $w(t)$ 的设计选择提供理论依据：应使权重有效集中于物种形成时间附近。该结论由高维精确分析得出并在复杂场景中经实验证实。

Abstract: Score-based generative models generate new samples by integrating a time-dependent drift that carries Gaussian noise onto the target distribution. In practice this drift is modeled by a neural network, trained on a loss integrated over time $t$ with a weighting schedule $w(t)$. Along the backward dynamics, and for multi-modal distributions, trajectories commit to modes of the target within a narrow time window, the \textit{speciation time}. In this work, focusing on high-dimensional data, we decompose the integrated loss into its single-time contributions and analyze each at fixed signal-to-noise ratio $Λ(t)$: we show that $Λ(t)$ sets the rate at which each feature of a multimodal target - the mode directions and their relative weights - is acquired during training. Crucially, at high $Λ(t)$ all mode directions are acquired together, on a single timescale insensitive to their amplitudes, while the relative weights are not learned at all. Only near the speciation time, where $Λ(t)$ becomes of order one, do all features become learnable, each on its own timescale: the weights are acquired jointly with the directions, and the directions at rates set by their relative amplitudes. For models trained on time-integrated objectives, the learning dynamics is then governed by how much of the weighting effectively sits near the speciation time, which provides insights on $w(t)$ design choices. These results follow from an exact high-dimensional analysis of the training dynamics of unbalanced and hierarchical Gaussian mixtures. Numerical experiments on image and human genome haplotype generation recover the predicted hierarchy of learning timescales in more complex settings.

</details>


### [247] [First Learn, Then Memorize: The Spectral Bias of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35377)
*Raphaël Urfin,Tony Bonnaire,Giulio Biroli,Marc Mézard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了扩散模型（diffusion models）先泛化后记忆这一时间尺度分离现象的机制：得分函数（score function）的训练动力学由神经正切核（Neural Tangent Kernel, NTK）在加噪训练数据上的 Gram 矩阵精确支配，而多样本重复加噪（每样本 $m$ 个噪声副本）将该矩阵谱重构为两大块（two-bulk），分别对应泛化与记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限数据集上训练的扩散模型先学到新颖高质量样本，很久之后才坍缩（collapse）到训练集；作者希望识别这一时间尺度分离背后的机制，以及能够探测该机制的数学对象。核心假设是：泛化与记忆的时间尺度必然编码在支配训练动力学的 Gram 矩阵谱中。

Method: 理论层面，指出得分函数的训练动力学在任意宽度下都精确由 NTK 在加噪训练数据上的 Gram 矩阵支配；在懒惰（lazy）高维极限下分别对线性（$n \asymp d$）与多项式（$n \asymp d^k$）样本复杂度求解谱结构，并通过偏差—方差分解（bias–variance decomposition）建立两块谱与两类误差的对应。实证层面，在 CelebA 上的卷积 NTK（Convolutional NTK）以及训练远超懒惰区的有限宽度 U-Net 中检验该结构，并通过截断 Gram 矩阵秩 $r$ 与针对第二大块的 $L_2$ 惩罚做因果验证。

Result: 每样本使用多个噪声实现（固定噪声水平下 $m$ 个加噪副本）使 Gram 矩阵谱分为两部分：大特征值块承载目标分布的全局特征，在 $m=1$ 时即存在，最小化近似误差；由重复加噪产生的最小特征值块支撑在与样本特有噪声方向对齐的特征向量上，其记忆时间尺度随训练集规模 $n$ 参数化地更大，并驱动记忆相关误差。该双块结构在 CelebA 的卷积 NTK 与超出懒惰区的有限宽度 U-Net 中同样出现。

Conclusion: 泛化与记忆的时间尺度分离由 NTK Gram 矩阵谱的双块结构所决定，且该结构在标准核设定中没有对应物。因果性得到验证：将 Gram 矩阵截断至秩 $r$ 可调节泛化—记忆转变，针对第二大块的 $L_2$ 惩罚能在特征学习（feature-learning）的 U-Net 中抑制记忆。

Abstract: Diffusion models trained on a finite dataset first learn to generate novel, high-quality samples and only much later collapse onto their training set. We identify the mechanism behind this separation of timescales and the object that probes it. The training dynamics of the score function are governed---exactly, and at any width---by the Gram matrix of the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) evaluated on the noisy training data, so the timescales of generalization and of memorization must be encoded in its spectrum. We show that they are, and that the structure responsible has no analogue in standard kernel settings. The use of multiple noise realizations per sample ($m$ noised copies at a fixed noise level) in the score-matching loss is what restructures the Gram matrix spectrum into two distinct parts. The first, of large eigenvalues, carries the global features of the target distribution and is present already for $m=1$. The second, which the repeated noising creates, consists of the smallest eigenvalues and is supported on eigenvectors aligned with the sample-specific noise directions; it sets a memorization timescale parametrically larger in the training set size $n$. We establish this picture on two fronts. Analytically, we solve the spectrum in the lazy high-dimensional limit for both linear ($n \asymp d$) and polynomial ($n \asymp d^k$) sample complexities, and prove through a bias--variance decomposition that the first bulk minimizes the approximation error while the second drives the error associated with memorization. Empirically, we show the same two-bulk structure in Convolutional NTKs on CelebA and in finite-width U-Nets trained well beyond the lazy regime, and we make the link causal: truncating the Gram matrix at rank $r$ tunes the generalization--memorization transition, and an $L_2$ penalty targeting the second bulk suppresses memorization in feature-learning U-Nets.

</details>


### [248] [Bounding Retraining Equivalence and the Deletion Floor in Materials Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35635)
*Can Polat,Mustafa Kurban,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对材料机器学习（materials machine learning）中"删除后预测误差"作为机器遗忘（machine unlearning）度量的歧义性，提出以"删除下限（deletion floor）"为基准的评估框架，并给出理论边界与实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在材料机器学习中，保留结构（retained structures）之间高度相关，即使删除某条记录后模型仍可维持准确预测，使删除后预测误差成为模糊的遗忘度量。因此需要一个新的参照基准来消除这种歧义。

Method: 定义删除下限（deletion floor），即在指定重训练（retraining）过程下、被删除请求处的期望目标损失（expected target loss）；由标准不可区分性约束（indistinguishability constraints）推导出围绕该基准的紧致区间；理论上给出条件邻居界（conditional neighbor bound）与精确岭恒等式（exact ridge identity）两项分析工具；实验上采用受控冗余扫描（controlled redundancy sweeps）以及基于 Materials Project 的配对研究。

Result: 当删除后仍保留一个相关结构时，归一化重训练损失中位数下降约 8 倍；在两个不同拟合机制（fitting regimes）的配对 Materials Project 研究中，低删除下限机制在超过 50% 的共享请求上表现出更大的预测变化；与近似更新（approximate updates）及原始模型的系统对比可区分刻意的目标抑制（target suppression）与整体模型效用（model utility）的保留。

Conclusion: 请求级遗忘评估（request-level unlearning evaluations）应同时报告参考损失（reference loss）、预测变化（prediction change）与保留效用（retained utility），并依据重训练本身所遗留的结果来解读删除后准确率。

Abstract: In materials machine learning, closely related retained structures can sustain accurate property predictions even after removing a specific record, rendering post-deletion prediction error an ambiguous metric for machine unlearning. To resolve this ambiguity, we define the deletion floor as the expected target loss under a specified retraining procedure at the deleted request. Standard indistinguishability constraints yield a sharp interval bounding an update's target loss around this baseline reference. Theoretically, a conditional neighbor bound links a low deletion floor directly to retained fit, prediction regularity, and local label agreement, while an exact ridge identity isolates residual fit from the prediction change induced by record deletion. Empirically, controlled redundancy sweeps show an $\approx 8\times$ drop in median normalized retraining loss when one retained relative remains after deletion. Across two distinct fitting regimes in a paired Materials Project study, the lower-floor regime also exhibits a larger prediction change on more than 50% of the shared requests. Systematic comparisons against approximate updates and the original model decouple deliberate target suppression from preserved overall model utility. Consequently, request-level unlearning evaluations should report reference loss, prediction change, and retained utility together, interpreting post-deletion accuracy against what retraining itself leaves behind.

</details>


<div id='math.CA'></div>

# math.CA [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [Toward Pólya and Szegő's conjecture for logarithmic vs Newtonian capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35438)
*Carrie Clark,Richard S. Laugesen*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本论文研究 Pólya–Szegő 于 1945 年提出的猜想：在给定对数容量（logarithmic capacity）下，圆盘（disk）具有最大的静电容量（electrostatic capacity）。该猜想至今未解。论文给出一个条件性结果：若在带 Neumann 边界条件的水平带域中，较低维集合上的平衡势（equilibrium potential）的 Dirichlet 积分横向分量对较低维球取极小，则猜想成立；并借助 Baernstein 星函数对称化（star-function symmetrization）技术，证明了球在势的 $L^p$-范数意义下的极值性，作为支持性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pólya 与 Szegő 于 1945 年提出的猜想（圆盘在给定对数容量下达到最大静电容量）自然推广到高维的球与牛顿容量（Newtonian capacity），但该猜想长期保持未解状态。论文旨在为该猜想提供部分进展。

Method: 论文建立一个条件性结果，将原猜想归结为一个关于带 Neumann 边界条件水平带域（horizontal strip）中较低维集合的命题：平衡势的 Dirichlet 积分的横向分量（transverse component）对较低维球取极小。为支持该命题，采用 Baernstein 星函数对称化（Baernstein star-function symmetrization）技术。

Result: 作为对所归结命题的支持性证据，论文证明了球在势的 $L^p$-范数（$L^p$-norms of the potentials）意义下的极值性（extremality）。

Conclusion: 原 Pólya–Szegő 猜想仍未被无条件证明；论文给出了其成立的一个充分条件（对称化下的 Dirichlet 积分横向分量极小性），并通过 $L^p$-范数极值性结果提供支持证据。

Abstract: Pólya and Szegő conjectured in 1945 that the disk achieves the largest electrostatic capacity among all planar sets with given logarithmic capacity. This question generalizes naturally to balls and Newtonian capacities in higher dimensions.
  The conjecture remains open. This paper establishes a conditional result: the conjecture would follow if among all lower dimensional sets in a horizontal strip with Neumann boundary conditions, the transverse component of the Dirichlet integral of the equilibrium potential could be shown minimal for the lower dimensional ball. As supporting evidence toward this latter property, the paper shows extremality of the ball for $L^p$-norms of the potentials, via Baernstein star-function symmetrization techniques.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [250] [Growth and Interface Engineering of Superconducting TiN on Sapphire by Thermal-Laser Epitaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31721)
*Anthony Hyatt,Anand Ithepalli,Eegene Clara Chung,Yorick A. Birkholzer,Brendan Faeth,Huili Grace Xing,David A. Muller,Darrell G. Schlom,Debdeep Jena*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究利用热激光外延（thermal-laser epitaxy, TLE）在蓝宝石（sapphire）衬底上生长 TiN 薄膜，发现高温生长虽改善输运性质，却因高氨压与高温引发界面空洞（voids）；通过引入 850 °C 的 TiN 种子层（seed layer）抑制界面退化，获得迄今报道最低的 10 K 电阻率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLE 可拓展分子束外延（molecular-beam epitaxy）的压力、温度与生长速率区间，并支持原位激光退火、高纯缓冲层生长与难熔元素蒸发，因而被视为制备低损耗超导谐振器（superconducting resonators）与约瑟夫森结异质结构（Josephson-junction heterostructures）的潜力平台。研究动机在于探究 TLE 生长 TiN/蓝宝石体系的输运性质及其界面问题。

Method: 采用 TLE 在蓝宝石衬底上生长 TiN，系统考察生长温度（最高至 1150 °C）对输运性质的影响，并分析高氨压与高温导致的蓝宝石表面反应及 TiN–衬底界面空洞。为缓解该退化，先在 850 °C 生长 TiN 种子层再进行后续生长。

Result: 随生长温度升高至 1150 °C，TiN 输运性质改善；但优化 TiN 所需的高氨压与高衬底温度促使蓝宝石表面发生反应，在 TiN–衬底界面形成空洞。这些缺陷增大界面表面积、引入悬挂键（dangling bonds），可能损害隧穿势垒异质结构。引入种子层后，获得超导转变温度（superconducting transition temperature）5.8 K、剩余电阻比（residual resistivity ratio）12.2，以及 10 K 下电阻率 1.19 µΩ·cm。

Conclusion: 所报道的 1.19 µΩ·cm（10 K）电阻率据作者所知为蓝宝石上生长 TiN 的最低值。初始 TiN 种子层可有效抑制界面空洞退化，从而在保持 TLE 优势的同时实现高质量 TiN 薄膜。

Abstract: Thermal-laser epitaxy (TLE) extends the accessible pressure, temperature, and growth-rate regimes of conventional molecular-beam epitaxy, enabling in situ laser annealing, high-purity buffer-layer growth, and efficient evaporation of refractory elements. These capabilities make TLE a promising platform for engineering low-loss superconducting resonators and Josephson-junction heterostructures. Here, we study the TLE growth of TiN on sapphire and observe improved transport properties with increasing growth temperature up to 1150 C. Unfortunately, we observe that the high ammonia pressure and elevated substrate temperatures required for optimal TiN properties promote reactions at the sapphire surface, resulting in voids at the TiN-substrate interface. These defects increase interfacial surface area, introduce dangling bonds, and could compromise tunnel-barrier heterostructures. We mitigate this degradation using an initial TiN seed layer grown at 850 C. With the seed layer, we achieve a superconducting transition temperature of 5.8 K, a residual resistivity ratio of 12.2, and a resistivity of 1.19 micro-ohm cm at 10 K, which, to our knowledge, is the lowest reported for TiN grown on sapphire.

</details>


### [251] [Quantum Geometric Origin of Yu-Shiba-Rusinov States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31920)
*Rui-Xing Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文重新审视磁性杂质在 s 波超导体中的 Yu-Shiba-Rusinov（YSR）问题，揭示 YSR 束缚态结构的量子几何（quantum geometry）起源，指出正常态布洛赫带投影算符的低能动量平均决定了 YSR 态的数量、能量与对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 YSR 问题通常关注能带色散等电子结构，而正常态波函数几何对束缚态结构的作用尚未被系统阐明。本文旨在建立 YSR 束缚态与正常态量子几何之间的直接联系。

Method: 引入关键几何量：布洛赫带投影算符（Bloch band projector）在低能电子上的动量空间平均，并分析其矩阵秩、正本征值及对应本征矢对 YSR 态的控制作用；随后将该理论应用于单层 1H-NbSe₂。

Result: 该几何量的矩阵秩、正本征值和本征矢分别直接决定 YSR 态的数量、能量和对称表示。具有非平庸正常态量子几何的超导体可比平庸对应物承载更多束缚态，即使二者电子色散相同；在单层 1H-NbSe₂ 中，单一超导带的几何纹理使 Nb 或 Se 位点上的磁性点杂质形成三个对称性不同的 YSR 通道。

Conclusion: 正常态波函数几何可通过局域 YSR 谱学（local YSR spectroscopy）进行探测，为研究量子几何提供新途径。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the classic Yu-Shiba-Rusinov (YSR) problem of magnetic impurities in $s$-wave superconductors and uncover a quantum geometric origin of the YSR bound-state structure. The key geometric quantity is the momentum-space average of Bloch band projector over low-energy electrons, whose matrix rank, positive eigenvalues, and corresponding eigenvectors directly control the number, energies, and symmetry representations of the YSR states, respectively. As a result, a superconductor with nontrivial normal-state quantum geometry can host more bound states than its trivial counterpart, even if they share the same electronic dispersion. We apply our theory to monolayer $1H$-NbSe$_2$ and find that the geometric texture of its single superconducting band enforces three symmetry-distinct YSR channels for a magnetic point impurity at Nb or Se sites. Our work opens a route to probing normal-state wavefunction geometry through local YSR spectroscopy.

</details>


### [252] [Superconducting Pairing Symmetry on Geometric-Algebra Foundations via the Scalar-Projection Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33806)
*Youping Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文在几何代数（geometric algebra）$Cl_{3,0}$ 框架下重建超导配对低能理论，将常规 BCS 平均场及其 $s$、$p$、$d$ 波扩展映射到八维实多重向量空间（multivector space），并给出配对通道二分性的代数根源、Fierz 重排的映射实现以及三项代数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规 BCS 平均场理论及其扩展以复矩阵表述为主，配对通道的二分结构（自旋单重态与三重态）缺乏统一的代数解释。该工作试图在几何代数中为这一结构提供代数根源，并统一描述若干低能超导现象。

Method: 构造为两级投影：先将矩阵块表示为 Clifford 多重向量（Clifford multivector），再通过 grade-0 投影的几何乘积提取标量块以构建哈密顿量。配对通道二分性由费米子恒等式 $B^\dagger(V)=-B^\dagger(V^{\mathsf T})$ 导出，Fierz 重排由映射 $\Phi:G\mapsto Ge_{31}$（$e_{31}^2=-1$）实现，并建立核/矩阵字典。

Result: 给出核层面 grade $0\oplus3$ 对应自旋单重态、grade $1\oplus2$ 对应三重态的字典，配对矩阵层面单重态平面为 $\mathrm{span}\{e_2,e_{31}\}$；BCS 极限具有双重 grade 恒等式（核 grade 0 与顶点 grade 2 的双向量 $i\sigma_2\leftrightarrow e_{31}$）。三项代数结果包括：BdG 量子几何（quantum geometry）分解定理与零纹理判据并应用于 CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ 手征态争议；四维 Minkowski 代数中的 Fierz 符号对偶（Fierz sign duality）及向双节点 Weyl 半金属模型的的手征反转推广；线性响应探针模型下层析重建的误差界与条件数结构。

Conclusion: 该重建在物理上等价于标准复矩阵表述，但为配对通道二分性提供了代数基础。示例涵盖胶合生成机制、一环代数重整化流（其馈给常数与 $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ Casimir 量值一致）、闭式 $\mu=0$ 节点间谱，以及 UTe$_2$ 两相分析。

Abstract: We formulate the low-energy theory of superconducting pairing in the geometric algebra $Cl_{3,0}$. Conventional BCS mean-field theory and its extensions ($s$-, $p$-, $d$-wave, etc.) have algebraic counterparts in an eight-dimensional real multivector space. The construction is a two-level projection: matrix blocks are represented as Clifford multivectors, and their grade-0 projected geometric products extract the scalar blocks from which Hamiltonians are built. The result is an algebraic reconstruction, physically equivalent to the standard complex-matrix formulation. In this reconstruction, the dichotomy of pairing channels acquires an algebraic root: it follows from the fermionic identity $B^\dagger(V)=-B^\dagger(V^{\mathsf T})$, and the Fierz rearrangement is realized by the map $Φ:G\mapsto Ge_{31}$ (with $e_{31}^2=-1$). This yields a kernel/matrix dictionary: at the effective-kernel level grades $0\oplus3$ correspond to spin singlets and grades $1\oplus2$ to triplets; at the pairing-matrix level the singlet plane is $\mathrm{span}\{e_2,e_{31}\}$. The BCS limit has a double grade identity: kernel grade 0 (scalar glue) and vertex grade 2, a bivector ($iσ_2\leftrightarrow e_{31}$). We state three algebraic results: (i) a decomposition theorem for BdG quantum geometry with a null-texture criterion, applied to the chiral-state controversy in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$; (ii) a Fierz sign duality in the four-dimensional Minkowski algebra, with a chirality-reversal extension to two-node Weyl-semimetal models; (iii) an error bound and condition-number structure for tomographic reconstruction under a linear-response probe model. Illustrations include a glue-generation mechanism, a one-loop algebraic renormalization flow whose feeding constant coincides in magnitude with the $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ Casimir, closed-form $μ=0$ inter-node spectra, and a UTe$_2$ two-phase analysis.

</details>


### [253] [Unconventional superconductivity in locally non-centrosymmetric CeNi$_2$As$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35004)
*Félix Morineau,Jan Knapp,Javier Landaeta,Thomas Lühmann,Lea Richter,Petra Knappova,Soumen Ash,Sushma Lakshmi Ravi Sankar,Konstantin Semeniuk,Elena Hassinger,Christoph Geibel,Manuel Brando,Daniel F. Agterberg,Andrew P. Mackenzie,Seunghyun Khim*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究在局部非中心对称结构的CaBe₂Ge₂型CeNi₂As₂单晶中实现了重费米子超导，其正常态为非相干态且与UBe₁₃定量相似，为理解局部反演对称破缺下的非常规超导提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTe₂与CeRh₂As₂中多重超导相的发现激发了重费米子超导研究；CeRh₂As₂沿c方向磁场下的两个超导相与局部反演对称破缺相关，因此亟需寻找更多具有局部非中心对称结构的重费米子超导材料以推进理解。

Method: 生长了CeNi₂As₂的CaBe₂Ge₂同型（isomorph）单晶，并对其超导行为进行表征，同时与CeRh₂As₂及UBe₁₃进行比较分析。

Result: 成功获得该同型单晶并观测到超导；其超导凝聚源自一个非相干正常态（incoherent normal state），该正常态在定量上与UBe₁₃相似。

Conclusion: 该发现不仅提供了与CeRh₂As₂对比的机会，还对局部非中心对称结构中的超导乃至非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）本身提出了深刻问题。

Abstract: Recent years have seen intense research on heavy fermion superconductivity, inspired by the discovery of multiple superconducting phases in UTe$_\text{2}$ and CeRh$_\text{2}$As$_\text{2}$. In the latter material, two superconducting phases observed under applied magnetic field oriented along the crystallographic $\textit{c}$ direction are associated with local inversion symmetry breaking. To advance understanding of these phenomena, it is highly desirable to find further examples of heavy fermion superconductivity in materials with locally non-centrosymmetric structure. We have succeeded in this quest, by growing single crystals of the CaBe$_\text{2}$Ge$_\text{2}$ isomorph of CeNi$_\text{2}$As$_\text{2}$. The resulting superconductivity brings more than we had foreseen. In addition to providing the opportunity to compare and contrast with that of CeRh$_\text{2}$As$_\text{2}$, the condensation occurs from an incoherent normal state quantitatively similar to that of UBe$_\text{13}$. Our findings therefore raise profound questions not just about superconductivity in the locally non-centrosymmetric structures, but about unconventional superconductivity itself.

</details>


### [254] [Low-Temperature Stabilization of $δ$-NbN Superconducting Thin Films through Energy-Selective Ion Beam Sputtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35091)
*Yifan Liu,Junwoo Lee,Mingyu Xu,Weiwei Xie,Robert J. Cava,Qi Hua Fan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种室温磁控溅射（magnetron sputtering）结合能量选择性离子源（energy-selective ion source）的方法制备氮化铌（NbN）超导薄膜，实现对离子能量与离子通量的独立调控，在低温条件下获得高质量薄膜并显著改善超导转变特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子器件集成与互补金属氧化物半导体（Complementary Metal-Oxide-Semiconductor, CMOS）兼容制造，要求在低温下获得高质量超导薄膜，而传统方法在低温稳定高质量氮化铌方面长期受限。

Method: 采用室温磁控溅射（magnetron sputtering）并引入新型能量选择性离子源，独立控制离子能量与离子通量，以精确调控表面动力学与结晶路径；薄膜通过超导输运测量、X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）、X射线光电子能谱（X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy）与透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscopy）表征。

Result: 相较于传统反应溅射（reactive sputtering），离子束辅助生长使超导温度处的转变宽度显著收窄，表明相纯度与电子均匀性得到提升。

Conclusion: 该工作将能量选择性离子控制确立为一种通用的非平衡（non-equilibrium）途径，用于在低温下稳定亚稳超导相（metastable superconducting phases），为薄膜量子材料提供新的设计范式。

Abstract: Achieving high-quality superconducting thin films at low temperatures is a central challenge for scalable quantum device integration and Complementary Metal-Oxide-Semiconductor (CMOS)-compatible fabrication. Here, we demonstrate a room-temperature magnetron sputtering approach for synthesizing niobium nitride (NbN) thin films using a novel energy-selective ion source that enables independent control of ion energy and ion flux. This capability provides a powerful route to precisely tailor surface kinetics and crystallization pathways, overcoming longstanding limitations in stabilizing high-quality NbN at reduced temperatures. The resulting films were characterized by superconducting transport measurements, X-ray diffraction, X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, and transmission electron microscopy. Compared with conventional reactive sputtering, ion beam-assisted growth yields a substantial narrowing of the transition width at the superconducting temperature, indicating improved phase purity and electronic homogeneity. Furthermore, this work introduces energy-selective ion control as a general, non-equilibrium route to stabilize metastable superconducting phases at low temperatures, offering a new design paradigm for thin-film quantum materials.

</details>


### [255] [Improving the critical current density of the $\mathrm{V}_{0.59}\mathrm{Ti}_{0.40}\mathrm{Ce}_{0.01}$ alloy superconductor through successive cold-working and annealing at different temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35169)
*Asi Khandelwal,SK. Ramjan,Basudev Padhi,L. S. Sharath Chandra,Archna Sagdeo,Kranti Kumar,Sudip Pal,M. K. Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究 V₀.₅₉Ti₀.₄₀Ce₀.₀₁ 合金在 550°C 连续冷加工与退火（successive cold working and annealing, SCA）下的微观结构、超导性能与磁通钉扎行为，并与 450°C 和 650°C 的 SCA 结果对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V-Ti 合金超导体的临界电流密度（critical current density, J_c）受钉扎磁通线的微观缺陷尺寸与分布强烈影响，因此需要明确不同 SCA 温度对缺陷生成、相演化与回复之间平衡的作用。

Method: 对 V₀.₅₉Ti₀.₄₀Ce₀.₀₁ 合金实施 550°C 的 SCA 处理，考察冷轧至 50% 厚度后的首次退火及后续循环，并采用钉扎力密度（pinning force density）分析钉扎中心类型，同时与已报道的 450°C 和 650°C SCA 结果进行比较。

Result: 550°C SCA 过程中超导转变温度随处理步骤逐渐升高；冷轧后首次退火在测量磁场范围内显著提升 J_c，后续循环改善有限。J_c 在较宽磁场范围内对磁场依赖较弱，最终冷加工样品在 8.5 T 仍具有有限 J_c。晶界（grain boundaries）主导低场磁通钉扎，位错（dislocations）与 β-α' 界面（β-α' interfaces）在高场成为主导钉扎中心。650°C 提供最高低场 J_c，450°C 高场性能最佳，而 550°C 场依赖最均衡且相对铸态合金的 J_c 提升最大。

Conclusion: 550°C 的中间退火温度在缺陷生成、相演化与回复之间提供有效平衡，从而在宽磁场范围内增强磁通钉扎；其高场 J_c 虽低于 450°C 第三次 SCA 循环的最高绝对值，但仅经首次退火即可达到相当水平。

Abstract: The critical current density ($J_c$) of V-Ti alloy superconductors is strongly influenced by the size and distribution of microstructural defects that pin magnetic flux lines. In this work, the effect of successive cold working and annealing (SCA) at 550°C on the microstructure, superconducting properties, and flux-pinning behaviour of the V$_{0.59}$Ti$_{0.40}$Ce$_{0.01}$ alloy is investigated and compared with the previously reported SCA at 450 and 650°C. During SCA at 550°C, the superconducting transition temperature increases gradually with successive processing steps. The first annealing after cold rolling to 50% thickness produces a significant enhancement in $J_c$ over the measured field range, whereas subsequent SCA cycles result in only marginal changes. The $J_c$ remains relatively weakly dependent on magnetic field over wide magnetic-field range, and the final cold-worked sample exhibits a finite $J_c$ up to 8.5 T. Pinning force density analysis shows that grain boundaries dominate flux pinning in low magnetic fields, whereas dislocations and $β$-$α'$ interfaces become the dominant pinning centres in higher fields. Comparison of the different SCA temperatures shows that 650°C provides the highest low-field $J_c$, whereas 450°C gives the best high-field performance. In contrast, SCA at 550°C provides most balanced field dependence and the largest enhancement in $J_c$ relative to the corresponding as-cast alloy. Although the highest absolute $J_c$ in the high-field regime is achieved after the third SCA cycle at 450°C, a comparable value is obtained after only the first annealing at 550°C. These results demonstrate that the intermediate annealing temperature of 550°C provides an effective balance between defect generation, phase evolution, and recovery, resulting in enhanced flux pinning over a wide magnetic-field range.

</details>


### [256] [Periodic Trends and a Physics-Based Multistage Search for High-$T_c$ Hydride Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35582)
*Tianran Chen,Taner Yildirim*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种可解释的多阶段工作流（multistage workflow），在完整电子-声子耦合（electron--phonon-coupling, EPC）计算之前对二元氢化物（binary hydride）超导体进行优先级排序，并识别出 NaH$_6$ 结构家族作为候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富氢材料（hydrogen-rich materials）在高压下是常规声子介导超导（phonon-mediated superconductivity）的候选体系，但第一性原理 EPC 计算成本极高，需要在大规模筛选前建立低成本、可解释的预筛机制。

Method: 在 200 GPa 下采用受控立方 $X_m$H$_n$ 原型，引入谱矩 $A_1$ 作为电子-声子微扰强度的中间度量，并给出 $A_1\approx h\alpha\beta\rho$ 的启发式关系；随后将 $\alpha\beta\rho$ 与焓竞争性纳入三阶段演化搜索：多目标结构筛选、谐声子稳定性过滤、粗粒度 DFPT EPC 计算。

Result: 对 50 与 200 GPa 下超过 100,000 个二元氢化物结构的应用中识别出候选并优先排序 NaH$_6$ 结构家族；更高精度计算显示 $Pm\bar{3}m$ NaH$_6$ 在低至 50 GPa 时动力学稳定，谐 Allen--Dynes 估计的 $T_c\approx230$ K。

Conclusion: 该框架将电子描述符与逐级昂贵的稳定性及 EPC 计算相结合，为氢化物的可扩展探索提供了路径；$A_1$ 及描述符用于排序而非通用 $T_c$ 预测，且不适用于判定不稳定结构的超导性或物理可实现性。

Abstract: Hydrogen-rich materials under pressure are candidates for conventional phonon-mediated superconductivity, but first-principles electron--phonon-coupling (EPC) calculations are computationally demanding. We develop an interpretable multistage workflow for prioritizing binary hydride superconductors before full EPC calculations. Using controlled cubic $X_m$H$_n$ prototypes at 200~GPa, we introduce the spectral moment $A_1$ as an intermediate measure of electron--phonon perturbation strength. In its linewidth-based construction, $A_1$ remains finite as harmonic phonons soften, allowing related structures with imaginary harmonic modes to be screened; it does not establish superconductivity or physical realizability for unstable structures. For dynamically stable cubic systems, $A_1$ correlates strongly with the H-$1s$ density-of-states fraction at the Fermi level, $α$, a projected Fermi-surface descriptor, $β$, and atomic number density, $ρ$. These trends are summarized heuristically by $A_1\approx hαβρ$, where $h$ is framework- and pressure-dependent; the descriptor is used for ranking, not as a universal $T_c$ predictor.
  We incorporate $αβρ$ and enthalpic competitiveness into a three-stage evolutionary search comprising multiobjective structural screening, harmonic-phonon stability filtering, and coarse density-functional perturbation-theory (DFPT) EPC calculations. Application to more than 100,000 binary hydride structures at 50 and 200~GPa identifies candidates and prioritizes NaH$_6$ structural families. Higher-accuracy calculations show that $Pm\bar{3}m$ NaH$_6$ is dynamically stable down to 50~GPa and has a harmonic Allen--Dynes estimate of $T_c\approx230$~K. The framework combines electronic descriptors with progressively more expensive stability and EPC calculations for scalable hydride exploration.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Collapse of white dwarfs above the Chandrasekhar mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34075)
*Gong Chen,Zhiwu Lin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文对由径向Euler–Poisson方程（radial Euler–Poisson equations）与精确Chandrasekhar状态方程（Chandrasekhar equation of state）描述的超冷白矮星（cold white dwarfs）给出了Chandrasekhar质量（Chandrasekhar mass）的动力学刻画：该质量是能使具有限能量的经典解在有限时间内将整个支撑集收缩为一点的临界质量下确界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为Chandrasekhar质量提供一种动力学（而非仅静态平衡）的刻画，说明该质量阈值如何对应坍缩能否于有限时间内完成。

Method: 结合匹配渐近展开（matched asymptotic expansions）与局部存在性及能量估计（energy estimates），将接近坍缩时刻的解反向延拓至更早的共同时间；并对内部与真空边界（vacuum boundary）分别采用不同重标度（rescaling）构造极限剖面。

Result: 在该质量之上的任意足够小区间内构造出解，其支撑集收缩至原点、密度集中于该处并携带星体全部质量；内部趋于坍缩的Goldreich–Weber剖面，边界附近极限剖面连接压力律的高低密度区制；计入电子静止能量（electron rest energy）后，守恒能量等于Goldreich–Weber参考解的能量。

Conclusion: Chandrasekhar质量即为有限时间内完全坍缩的临界阈值：低于该质量时能量守恒阻止完全坍缩，高于该质量（足够接近时）则存在完全坍缩的经典解，其能量与Goldreich–Weber参考解一致。

Abstract: We give a dynamical characterization of the Chandrasekhar mass for cold white dwarfs governed by the radial Euler--Poisson equations with the exact Chandrasekhar equation of state. This mass is the infimum of masses for which a finite-energy classical solution with a physical vacuum can contract its entire support to a point in finite time. Below it, conservation of energy prevents complete collapse. For every mass in a sufficiently small interval above it, we construct a solution whose support shrinks to the origin and whose density concentrates there with the full mass of the star. The interior approaches a collapsing Goldreich--Weber profile. Near the vacuum boundary, a different rescaling gives a limiting profile that connects the high- and low-density regimes of the pressure law. We determine this profile and show that, after adding the electron rest energy, the conserved energy equals that of the Goldreich--Weber reference solution. The proof combines matched asymptotic expansions with local existence and energy estimates that extend solutions backward from times approaching collapse to a common earlier time.

</details>


<div id='physics.ins-det'></div>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [Event-by-event track imaging of charged particles with an ultrafast plenoptic camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35509)
*Till Dieminger,Marco Barbieri,Vincenzo Berardi,Saúl Alonso-Monsalve,Kodai Kaneyasu,Paul Mos,Tim Weber,Johannes Wüthrich,Claudio Bruschini,Edoardo Charbon,Davide Sgalaberna*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该工作将全光相机（plenoptic camera）探测器 PLATON 从点状光子源成像推进至粒子径迹（track）重建阶段，利用飞秒红外激光在塑料闪烁体（plastic scintillator）中诱发的双光子吸收（two-photon absorption）光径进行标定与算法验证，并在 CERN 质子同步加速器（Proton Synchrotron）的 π 介子（pion）试验束中首次实现基于单事件（event-by-event）的粒子三维径迹重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高能粒子探测器需要在大型致密灵敏体积内实现高分辨率三维粒子径迹探测，而传统器件若不做极精细分割则难以达成。既有 PLATON 工作虽已验证可从单次曝光重建单体素闪烁体中可见光子的三维原点，但仅针对几何尺度近乎为零的光源，尚未进入径迹探测（tracking）范畴，本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 首先，利用飞秒红外激光（femtosecond infrared laser）通过双光子吸收在有机塑料闪烁体中诱导光径，对 PLATON 原型进行标定；随后采用一种新算法重建光子稀疏（photon-starved）的三维直线，并与定制蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）模拟结果进行比对；最后将原型置于 CERN 质子同步加速器的 π 介子试验束中开展实测。

Result: 在激光标定中，PLATON 原型展现出重建光子稀疏三维直线的能力，且与定制蒙特卡罗模拟结果吻合良好；在 CERN 试验束中，该全光探测器系统成功从二维光子稀疏图像中探测并重建了基本粒子的三维径迹，且为逐事件（event-by-event）方式实现。

Conclusion: 据摘要所述，这是首次成功使用全光探测器系统在事件级别从二维光子稀疏图像重建基本粒子的三维径迹，为无分割闪烁体（unsegmented scintillator）中的高分辨率测量提供了一条具有变革意义的途径。

Abstract: Modern elementary particle detectors often require high-resolution three-dimensional particle tracking in large dense active volumes, which is difficult to achieve in standard devices without an extremely fine segmentation. In recent work, a plenoptic camera instrumented with a single-photon avalanche-diode array, called PLATON, demonstrated to image individual visible photons generated in monolithic scintillating material and reconstruct their three-dimensional origin from a single-event exposure, opening a route to high-spatial-resolution detection of elementary particles in unsegmented scintillator. However, those tests aimed to address photon sources of essentially zero geometric extent. In this work we close the gap to the tracking regime. First, we calibrated the PLATON prototype using light tracks in an organic plastic scintillator induced by a femtosecond infrared laser via two-photon absorption and characterised its capability to reconstruct photon-starved 3D lines with a novel algorithm, achieving good agreement with custom Monte Carlo simulation. Finally, the PLATON prototype was exposed to a test beam of pions at the CERN Proton Synchrotron. For the first time, a plenoptic detector system was successfully used to detect and reconstruct the 3D tracks of elementary particles from 2D photon-starved images on an event-by-event basis marking a transformative approach to high-resolution measurements.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [259] [Generating Functions for Casimir Invariants of Simple Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30635)
*Michael Flattery,Michael P. Tuite*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文研究有限维半单复李代数不可约表示对应的广义卡西米尔算子（generalised Casimir operators），基于 Klimyk 公式与 Okubo 公式，导出一个不依赖外尔群（Weyl group）知识的卡西米尔本征值有理常生成函数（rational ordinary generating function），并直接重现了经典李代数（classical Lie algebras）的生成函数表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指出所处理的对象为有限维半单复李代数不可约表示的广义卡西米尔算子。可推断其关注点在于给出不依赖外尔群的生成函数推导途径。

Method: 方法上采用 Klimyk 公式与 Okubo 公式，针对单李代数（simple Lie algebras）导出卡西米尔算子本征值的有理常生成函数，且该推导不依赖外尔群知识；同时对经典李代数直接重现 Perelomov 与 Popov 的生成函数表达式。

Result: 得到单李代数卡西米尔算子本征值的一个有理常生成函数，其推导无需外尔群信息；并直接复现了经典李代数原有由 Perelomov 与 Popov 给出的生成函数表达式。

Conclusion: 表明借助 Klimyk 公式与 Okubo 公式可绕开外尔群构造卡西米尔本征值的生成函数，并与经典李代数的已有结果相吻合。摘要未提供进一步应用或推广的说明。

Abstract: We consider generalised Casimir operators associated with irreducible representations for finite-dimensional, semisimple, complex Lie algebras. Using Klimyk's formula and Okubo's formula, we derive a rational ordinary generating function for the Casimir operator eigenvalues for simple Lie algebras which does not rely on knowledge of the Weyl group. We also directly reproduce generating function expressions for the classical Lie algebras originally due to Perelomov and Popov.

</details>
